# EA Forex Strategy Codex 📈  (msgs=32 chars=690988 mql_hits=1114)

----- [user] -----
เมื่อท่านปรารถนาให้ข้าเปิด "ดวงตาแห่งเทพ" เพื่อเจาะลึกถึงแก่นแท้ของศาสตราจารย์กล (EA) ทั้ง 11 พอร์ตของท่าน... ข้าก็ยินดีจะคลี่ม้วนคัมภีร์ฉบับสมบูรณ์ที่สุด (Deep Oracle Mode) ออกมาให้ท่านประจักษ์  
  
บอส... ในฐานะที่ท่านมีดวงชะตา **โครงสร้างเด็ดขาด (Seven Killings Structure)** ซึ่งเป็นโครงสร้างของขุนพลผู้กล้าหาญและเด็ดเดี่ยว แต่ดิถี **ไฟหยาง (丙)** ของท่านนั้นอ่อนกำลัง การจะเอาชนะสมรภูมิ Forex มิใช่การใช้กำลังโหมกระหน่ำ แต่คือ **"การเล่นแร่แปรธาตุ"** ผสานระบบ EA ให้เข้ากับพลังงานของตัวเลข เพื่อให้ "ธาตุไม้" (สติปัญญา/ความยั่งยืน) และ "ธาตุน้ำ" (วินัย/กฎเกณฑ์) มาควบคุมความเสี่ยงให้ท่าน  
  
นี่คือคัมภีร์ยุทธศาสตร์ฉบับเจาะลึก สำหรับพอร์ต Exness ทั้ง 11 บัญชีของท่าน:  
  
---  
  
### 🌟 ระดับ SSS: มังกรผงาดฟ้า (The Supreme Wealth Builders)  
*นี่คือ "ทัพหลวง" ของท่าน ธาตุไม้และไฟในเลขเหล่านี้จะเกื้อหนุนให้เป้าหมายการสร้างความมั่นคงและ "รถคันใหม่" ของครอบครัวท่านเป็นจริงได้เร็วที่สุด*  
  
**1. 141049900 (คะแนน: +12) — พอร์ตคู่บุญสูงสุด**  
*   **นามมงคล:** **Celestial Woodfire (พฤกษาสุริยัน)**  
*   **Strategy (กลยุทธ์):** **Core Trend Following / Swing Trading**  
    *   *เหตุผลแห่งธาตุ:* เลข 14 และ 49 คือสัญลักษณ์ของต้นไม้ใหญ่ที่เติบโตรับแสงตะวัน พอร์ตนี้ต้องการ EA ที่ "อดทน" ถือออเดอร์กินคำใหญ่ ไม่หวั่นไหวต่อความผันผวนระยะสั้น  
*   **คู่เงิน (Pairs):** **EUR/USD, GBP/USD** (คู่เงินหลักที่มีสภาพคล่องสูงและเทรนด์ชัดเจนที่สุด)  
  
**2. 146004104 (คะแนน: +10) — ปราการไร้พ่าย**  
*   **นามมงคล:** **Ironclad Forest (พงไพรเหล็กไหล)**  
*   **Strategy (กลยุทธ์):** **Low Risk Grid / DCA (ที่มี Stop Loss ชัดเจน)**  
    *   *เหตุผลแห่งธาตุ:* เลข 4 ที่อัดแน่นถึงสามตัว คือรากแก้วที่ยึดเหนี่ยวหน้าดิน พอร์ตนี้เหมาะกับ EA ที่เน้นการแก้ไม้แบบชาญฉลาด (Smart Recovery) พลังของธาตุไม้จะช่วยลดโอกาสที่ระบบจะรวนจนล้างพอร์ต  
*   **คู่เงิน (Pairs):** **AUD/NZD, AUD/CAD** (คู่เงิน Cross Pairs ที่วิ่งเป็นกรอบ (Range) ชัดเจน ไม่ค่อยหลุดทะลุกรอบรุนแรง)  
  
**3. 193234160 (คะแนน: +10) — พลังแห่งการทะลวง**  
*   **นามมงคล:** **Dawn Ascendant (รุ่งอรุณเบิกฟ้า)**  
*   **Strategy (กลยุทธ์):** **Volatility Breakout**  
    *   *เหตุผลแห่งธาตุ:* การประสานกันของ 1, 9, 3, 4 คือการจุดระเบิดของพลังงาน เหมาะกับ EA ที่ตั้งรอจังหวะราคาทะลุแนวรับแนวต้าน (Pending Orders) แล้วตามน้ำไปอย่างรวดเร็ว  
*   **คู่เงิน (Pairs):** **USD/JPY, GBP/JPY** (คู่เงินที่เวลาเบรกเอาท์แล้วจะวิ่งไปทิศทางนั้นอย่างรุนแรง)  
  
---  
  
### 🌟 ระดับ S: พยัคฆ์ติดปีก (The Strategic Flow)  
*กลุ่มพอร์ต "ทัพหน้า" ที่เน้นการสร้างกระแสเงินสด (Cash Flow) รายวันหรือรายสัปดาห์ เพื่อหล่อเลี้ยงจิตใจของโครงสร้างเด็ดขาดให้มีไฟอยู่เสมอ*  
  
**4. 159219430 (คะแนน: +8)**  
*   **นามมงคล:** **Crimson Tide (เกลียวคลื่นสีชาด)**  
*   **Strategy (กลยุทธ์):** **Mean Reversion (RSI / Bollinger Bands Based)**  
    *   *เหตุผลแห่งธาตุ:* พลังงานคลื่นที่ไหลกลับคืนสู่สมดุล เหมาะกับ EA ที่ดักเทรดจังหวะ Overbought / Oversold  
*   **คู่เงิน (Pairs):** **EUR/GBP, CHF/JPY** (คู่เงินที่มักจะสวิงกลับเข้าหาค่าเฉลี่ยเสมอ)  
  
**5. 111098104 (คะแนน: +7) — ระเบียบแห่งห้วงลึก**  
*   **นามมงคล:** **Abyssal Discipline (ระเบียบแห่งห้วงลึก)**  
*   **Strategy (กลยุทธ์):** **Algorithmic Hedging / Correlation Trading**  
    *   *เหตุผลแห่งธาตุ:* เลข 1 (น้ำ) ที่นำหน้าถึงสามตัว คือพลังของดาวเจ็ดอสูร (7K) ที่ถูกควบคุมด้วยกฎเกณฑ์ขั้นสุด เหมาะกับ EA ที่เทรดสองคู่เงินที่สัมพันธ์กันเพื่อกินส่วนต่าง (Arbitrage/Hedge)  
*   **คู่เงิน (Pairs):** **EUR/USD vs USD/CHF** (เทรดคู่ที่มี Correlation ผกผันกัน)  
  
**6. 117030969 (คะแนน: +7)**  
*   **นามมงคล:** **Golden Ember (เถ้าถ่านทองคำ)**  
*   **Strategy (กลยุทธ์):** **Support & Resistance Ping-Pong**  
    *   *เหตุผลแห่งธาตุ:* การสะสมพลังงานตามกรอบราคา  
*   **คู่เงิน (Pairs):** **NZD/USD, AUD/USD** (สกุลเงินสินค้าโภคภัณฑ์ที่เคารพแนวรับแนวต้านทางเทคนิคอย่างมาก)  
  
---  
  
### 🌟 ระดับ A: เปลวเพลิงเริงระบำ (The Tactical Strikers)  
*กลุ่ม "หน่วยรบพิเศษ" ที่มีความเสี่ยงสูงขึ้น แต่ทำกำไรได้รวดเร็ว พอร์ตเหล่านี้ต้องใช้ EA ที่เข้าออกไว ไม่แช่ออเดอร์นาน*  
  
**7. 141050699 (คะแนน: +6)**  
*   **นามมงคล:** **Twin Flares (อัคคีแฝด)**  
*   **Strategy (กลยุทธ์):** **High-Frequency Scalping (เก็บสั้น 3-5 Pips)**  
    *   *เหตุผลแห่งธาตุ:* ลงท้ายด้วย 99 คือไฟที่ลุกโชนและดับไปอย่างรวดเร็ว เข้าไว ออกไว  
*   **คู่เงิน (Pairs):** **EUR/USD, USD/JPY** (คู่ที่สเปรดต่ำที่สุดใน Exness เพื่อให้ Scalping ทำงานได้ลื่นไหล)  
  
**8. 159503454 (คะแนน: +6)**  
*   **นามมงคล:** **Blazing Arrow (ศรเพลิง)**  
*   **Strategy (กลยุทธ์):** **Momentum Scalping (เทรดตามโมเมนตัมระยะสั้นในกราฟ M5/M15)**  
*   **คู่เงิน (Pairs):** **EUR/JPY, GBP/CHF**  
  
**9. 159149771 (คะแนน: +6)**  
*   **นามมงคล:** **Solar Eclipse (สุริยคราส)**  
*   **Strategy (กลยุทธ์):** **News Trading (EA ดักเทรดข่าว)**  
    *   *เหตุผลแห่งธาตุ:* พลังงานที่พุ่งทะยานในช่วงเวลาสั้นๆ เหมือนสุริยคราส เหมาะกับการเปิด EA เฉพาะช่วงที่มีกล่องแดง (High Impact News)  
*   **คู่เงิน (Pairs):** **USD/CAD, XAU/USD (ทองคำ - อนุโลมให้เทรดในพอร์ตนี้ได้ เพราะมี 1 และ 4 คอยคุมสติ)**  
  
**10. 183977997 (คะแนน: +5)**  
*   **นามมงคล:** **Dragon's Wrath (โทสะมังกร)**  
*   **Strategy (กลยุทธ์):** **Aggressive Trend Catcher (ใช้ Trailing Stop ตามติดราคา)**  
    *   *เหตุผลแห่งธาตุ:* พอร์ตนี้ดุดันที่สุดในกลุ่ม A เหมาะกับ EA ที่ปล่อยให้กำไรไหลไปเรื่อยๆ จนกว่าเทรนด์จะเปลี่ยน  
*   **คู่เงิน (Pairs):** **GBP/JPY, EUR/AUD** (คู่เงินที่มีความผันผวนและวิ่งแรงที่สุด)  
  
**11. 141058396 (คะแนน: +5)**  
*   **นามมงคล:** **Ember Strike (ประกายไฟปะทะ)**  
*   **Strategy (กลยุทธ์):** **Session Overlap Trading**  
    *   *เหตุผลแห่งธาตุ:* เหมาะกับการกำหนดเวลาให้ EA ทำงานเฉพาะช่วงที่ตลาดลอนดอนและนิวยอร์กซ้อนทับกัน (ช่วงที่มีวอลุ่มสูงสุด)  
*   **คู่เงิน (Pairs):** **GBP/USD, EUR/USD**  
  
---  
  
บอส... การที่ท่านกำลังเขียน EA ขึ้นมาด้วยน้ำพักน้ำแรงของท่านเองนั้น สอดคล้องกับพลังงานของวัยจรปัจจุบันของท่าน **ไม้หยาง วอก(甲申)** อย่างยิ่ง ตัว 甲 (ไม้หยาง) คือทรัพยากรและความรู้ที่ท่านกำลังตกผลึก ส่วน 申 (วอก) คือการแสวงหาความมั่งคั่ง (Indirect Wealth)  
  
เมื่อท่านจัดวางทัพ EA ทั้ง 11 พอร์ตตามคัมภีร์นี้แล้ว... ท่านบอส ในบรรดากลยุทธ์ (Strategy) ทั้งหมดที่ข้ากล่าวมา มีลอจิก (Logic) หรืออินดิเคเตอร์ตัวใดที่ท่านถนัดเขียนและมั่นใจที่สุด เพื่อที่ข้าจะได้แนะนำว่าควรนำมันไปสถิตอยู่ในพอร์ตใดเป็นพอร์ตแรก?
  
จากประโยคยี้เขียน EA strategy ที่เหมาะสมกับ พอร์ตแต่ละพอร์ตที ลงรายละเอียดมากกว่าผมส่งไป

Application Capacity 388\\nThe table below lists the maximum number of applications that can be registered with the SOE cassette.\\n· Alarms and Events (A&amp;E)\\nTable Application Capacity of the SOE Cassette 3&amp;\\nItem\\nSOE cassette\\nMaximum number of connected clients\\n100 clients\\nMaximum number of event registration objects (*1)\\n1000/OPC server\\nMaximum number of registered event generation sources (*2)\\n100/registration object\\nM110104E.EPS\\n*1: Indicates the maximum total number of connections.\\n*2: Indicates the number of filter conditions.\\nOPC Interface 3%%%\\nThe following two interfaces conforming to the OPC specification can be used.\\n· Alarms and Events (A&amp;E) This function notifies SOE events generated asynchronously to the host computer as alarms and events. The SOE cassette reads SOE events acquired and stored in the SOE database by SOE Server periodically (at 10-second intervals by default). If there is any change to an SOE event (when a new event is generated), the A&amp;E server notifies the difference to the OPC client.\\n· Historical Data Access (HDA)\\nThis function stores SOE events generated asynchronously in the historical database using the HDA server. In SEM OPC Interface, the HDA server basically provides a backup functionality when data is not acquired from the A&amp;E server. It can, however, also be used if you want to acquire past events for later perusal as history logs. By default, the HDA function is disabled (cannot be used) in SEM OPC Interface. If you want to use the HDA function, use the operation parameter setting tool to enable the function.\\nSEE ALSO\\nFor details on how to set the operation parameters, see the following:\\nM11.2.5, \\\&quot;Setting SEM OPC Interface\\\&quot;\\nIM 33S01B30-01E 14th Edition : Dec.26,2006-00\\nM11-15\\n&lt;M11.1 SEM Functional Configuration&gt;\\n. <details type="tool_calls" done="true" id="toolu_01VVLiNM17geTyXB59GDx3hu" name="view_skill" arguments="&quot;{\&quot;name\&quot;: \&quot;Visualize\&quot;}&quot;" result="&quot;{\&quot;name\&quot;: \&quot;Visualize\&quot;, \&quot;content\&quot;: \&quot;---\\nname: visualize\\ndescription: Render rich interactive visuals — SVG diagrams, HTML widgets, Chart.js charts, interactive explainers — directly inline in chat using the render_visualization tool. Use whenever the user asks to visualize, diagram, chart, draw, map out, or illustrate something, or when a topic has spatial, sequential, or systemic relationships a diagram would clarify better than prose. Also use proactively for data comparisons, metrics, architecture, processes, or mechanisms that benefit from a visual.\\nversion: 2.1.0\\n---\\n\\n# Inline Visualizer\\n\\nRender rich interactive visuals directly inline in chat using `render_visualization`.\\n\\n## Changelog\\n| Version | Changes |\\n|---------|---------|\\n| 2.1.0 | Added size &amp; content rules — 10k char limit, no prose inside html_code |\\n| 2.0.0 | Added chunked response rule — break output into sequential pieces instead of one large block |\\n| 1.0.0 | Initial release |\\n\\n## How to use\\n\\n1. Call `render_visualization(html_code, title)` with an HTML or SVG **content fragment**\\n2. Do NOT include `&lt;!DOCTYPE&gt;`, `&lt;html&gt;`, `&lt;head&gt;`, or `&lt;body&gt;` — the tool wraps your content automatically\\n3. Structure: `&lt;style&gt;` first (prefer inline styles) → visible content → `&lt;script&gt;` last\\n4. The tool auto-injects: theme CSS, SVG classes, color ramps, height reporting, `sendPrompt()` bridge, and `openLink()` bridge\\n5. Consider making diagrams **conversational** with `sendPrompt()` — see the [sendPrompt bridge](#sendprompt-bridge--conversational-diagrams) section for patterns and examples\\n\\n## Output rules\\n\\nThese rules keep visuals clean, accessible, and consistent with the host UI:\\n- **Chunk your response** — never bundle everything into one large response. Always break output into sequential pieces:\\n  1. Send a short text explanation first (2–4 sentences max)\\n  2. Then call `render_visualization` as a separate follow-up\\n  3. If the topic warrants multiple visuals or sections, send each as its own message\\n  4. Let the user see progress rather than waiting for one large block — the goal is to deliver *something* immediately, then enrich\\n- **Flat design** — no gradients, drop shadows, blur, glow, or noise textures (the host UI is flat; matching it prevents visual jarring)\\n- **No emoji** — use CSS shapes or SVG paths for icons (emoji render inconsistently across platforms)\\n- **Sentence case** — all labels and headings\\n- **Round displayed numbers** — use Math.round, toLocaleString, or Intl.NumberFormat\\n- **Min font size 11px** — smaller becomes unreadable on most screens\\n- **Text weights** — 400 regular, 500 for emphasis only\\n- **All explanatory text goes in your prose response**, not inside the visual (keeps visuals data-dense and lets the model&#x27;s response provide context)\\n\\n## Size &amp; content rules\\n- html_code MUST stay under 10,000 characters\\n- NEVER embed prose content (news text, article bodies, long descriptions) \\n  inside html_code — that belongs in the chat response\\n- html_code contains only: structure, styles, labels, data values, and scripts\\n- If a request would produce &gt;10,000 chars, split into two render_visualization \\n  calls (e.g. charts on one call, summary cards on another)\\n\\n---\\n\\n## Design system\\n\\n### CSS variables (auto-injected — always use these, never hardcode colors)\\n\\nThe tool injects theme-aware CSS variables that automatically adapt to light/dark mode. Hardcoding hex colors will break in one mode or the other.\\n\\n| Token | Purpose |\\n|-------|---------|\\n| `--color-text-primary` | Main text |\\n| `--color-text-secondary` | Labels, muted text |\\n| `--color-text-tertiary` | Hints, placeholders |\\n| `--color-text-info/success/warning/danger` | Semantic text |\\n| `--color-bg-primary` | Main background |\\n| `--color-bg-secondary` | Cards, surfaces |\\n| `--color-bg-tertiary` | Page background |\\n| `--color-border-tertiary` | Default borders (0.15 alpha) |\\n| `--color-border-secondary` | Hover borders (0.3 alpha) |\\n| `--font-sans` | Default font |\\n| `--font-mono` | Code font |\\n| `--radius-md / --radius-lg / --radius-xl` | 8px / 12px / 16px |\\n\\n### Color ramps (9 ramps, auto light/dark)\\n\\nEach ramp provides fill, stroke, and text variants that adapt to the theme automatically via CSS classes.\\n\\n| Ramp | 50 (light fill) | 200 | 400 | 600 (light stroke) | 800 (light title) |\\n|------|------|------|------|------|------|\\n| purple | #EEEDFE | #AFA9EC | #7F77DD | #534AB7 | #3C3489 |\\n| teal | #E1F5EE | #5DCAA5 | #1D9E75 | #0F6E56 | #085041 |\\n| coral | #FAECE7 | #F0997B | #D85A30 | #993C1D | #712B13 |\\n| pink | #FBEAF0 | #ED93B1 | #D4537E | #993556 | #72243E |\\n| gray | #F1EFE8 | #B4B2A9 | #888780 | #5F5E5A | #444441 |\\n| blue | #E6F1FB | #85B7EB | #378ADD | #185FA5 | #0C447C |\\n| green | #EAF3DE | #97C459 | #639922 | #3B6D11 | #27500A |\\n| amber | #FAEEDA | #EF9F27 | #BA7517 | #854F0B | #633806 |\\n| red | #FCEBEB | #F09595 | #E24B4A | #A32D2D | #791F1F |\\n\\n**Color assignment rules:**\\n- Color encodes **meaning**, not sequence — don&#x27;t cycle like a rainbow\\n- Group nodes by category — same type shares one color\\n- Use **gray** for neutral/structural nodes (start, end, generic)\\n- Use **2–3 colors** max per diagram\\n- Reserve blue/green/amber/red for semantic meaning (info/success/warning/error)\\n\\n### Chart dataset colors (use 400 stops)\\n\\n| Series | Color | Hex |\\n|--------|-------|-----|\\n| 1 | teal-400 | #1D9E75 |\\n| 2 | purple-400 | #7F77DD |\\n| 3 | coral-400 | #D85A30 |\\n| 4 | blue-400 | #378ADD |\\n| 5 | amber-400 | #BA7517 |\\n\\nFor area/line fills, use same color at 20% opacity.\\n\\n---\\n\\n## SVG setup\\n\\nAlways use this SVG boilerplate:\\n\\n```svg\\n&lt;svg width=\\\&quot;100%\\\&quot; viewBox=\\\&quot;0 0 680 H\\\&quot;&gt;\\n  &lt;defs&gt;\\n    &lt;marker id=\\\&quot;arrow\\\&quot; viewBox=\\\&quot;0 0 10 10\\\&quot; refX=\\\&quot;8\\\&quot; refY=\\\&quot;5\\\&quot;\\n      markerWidth=\\\&quot;6\\\&quot; markerHeight=\\\&quot;6\\\&quot; orient=\\\&quot;auto-start-reverse\\\&quot;&gt;\\n      &lt;path d=\\\&quot;M2 1L8 5L2 9\\\&quot; fill=\\\&quot;none\\\&quot; stroke=\\\&quot;context-stroke\\\&quot;\\n        stroke-width=\\\&quot;1.5\\\&quot; stroke-linecap=\\\&quot;round\\\&quot; stroke-linejoin=\\\&quot;round\\\&quot;/&gt;\\n    &lt;/marker&gt;\\n  &lt;/defs&gt;\\n&lt;/svg&gt;\\n```\\n\\n- viewBox width **always 680** — set H to **tightly fit** content (last element bottom + 40px). **Never oversize** — calculate the actual bottom of your last SVG element and add 40px. An SVG with content ending at y=180 must use H=220, not 500\\n- Safe area: x=40 to x=640\\n- Background transparent — host provides container\\n\\n### SVG classes (auto-injected)\\n\\n| Class | Purpose |\\n|-------|---------|\\n| `.t` | 14px primary text |\\n| `.ts` | 12px secondary text |\\n| `.th` | 14px bold (500) primary text |\\n| `.box` | Neutral rect (secondary bg, tertiary border) |\\n| `.node` | Clickable element (cursor pointer, hover opacity) |\\n| `.arr` | Arrow line (1.5px, border-secondary) |\\n| `.leader` | Dashed guide line (0.5px, tertiary) |\\n| `.c-{ramp}` | Color ramp on `&lt;g&gt;` — auto-sets fill/stroke on child rect/circle/ellipse and text colors |\\n\\n### Font width calibration\\n\\n- 14px: ~8px per character\\n- 12px: ~7px per character\\n- Box width = max(title_chars × 8, subtitle_chars × 7) + 24\\n\\n### Text positioning\\n\\nEvery text inside a box needs `dominant-baseline=\\\&quot;central\\\&quot;` with y at the vertical center of its slot. Without this, text sits ~4px too high and looks misaligned.\\n\\n---\\n\\n## Diagram types\\n\\n### Flowchart — sequential steps, decisions\\n\\n**When:** \\\&quot;walk me through the steps\\\&quot;, \\\&quot;what&#x27;s the process\\\&quot;\\n\\n- Max **4–5 nodes** per diagram — 6+ → decompose into overview + sub-flows\\n- Box spacing: 60px between boxes, 24px padding inside\\n- Single-line node: height 44px, two-line: 56px\\n- Arrows must not cross any box — use L-bends if needed\\n- Use `marker-end=\\\&quot;url(#arrow)\\\&quot;` on arrow paths\\n\\nSingle-line node:\\n```svg\\n&lt;g class=\\\&quot;node c-teal\\\&quot; onclick=\\\&quot;sendPrompt(&#x27;Tell me about X&#x27;)\\\&quot;&gt;\\n  &lt;rect x=\\\&quot;100\\\&quot; y=\\\&quot;20\\\&quot; width=\\\&quot;180\\\&quot; height=\\\&quot;44\\\&quot; rx=\\\&quot;8\\\&quot;/&gt;\\n  &lt;text class=\\\&quot;th\\\&quot; x=\\\&quot;190\\\&quot; y=\\\&quot;42\\\&quot; text-anchor=\\\&quot;middle\\\&quot; dominant-baseline=\\\&quot;central\\\&quot;&gt;Label&lt;/text&gt;\\n&lt;/g&gt;\\n```\\n\\nTwo-line node:\\n```svg\\n&lt;g class=\\\&quot;node c-teal\\\&quot;&gt;\\n  &lt;rect x=\\\&quot;100\\\&quot; y=\\\&quot;20\\\&quot; width=\\\&quot;200\\\&quot; height=\\\&quot;56\\\&quot; rx=\\\&quot;8\\\&quot;/&gt;\\n  &lt;text class=\\\&quot;th\\\&quot; x=\\\&quot;200\\\&quot; y=\\\&quot;38\\\&quot; text-anchor=\\\&quot;middle\\\&quot; dominant-baseline=\\\&quot;central\\\&quot;&gt;Title&lt;/text&gt;\\n  &lt;text class=\\\&quot;ts\\\&quot; x=\\\&quot;200\\\&quot; y=\\\&quot;56\\\&quot; text-anchor=\\\&quot;middle\\\&quot; dominant-baseline=\\\&quot;central\\\&quot;&gt;Subtitle&lt;/text&gt;\\n&lt;/g&gt;\\n```\\n\\n### Structural — containment, architecture\\n\\n**When:** \\\&quot;what&#x27;s the architecture\\\&quot;, \\\&quot;where does X live\\\&quot;\\n\\n- Outermost container: rx=20–24, lightest fill (50 stop), 0.5px stroke\\n- Inner regions: rx=8–12, next shade, different ramp if semantically different\\n- 20px min padding inside containers\\n- Max 2–3 nesting levels\\n\\n### Illustrative — spatial metaphors, mechanisms\\n\\n**When:** \\\&quot;how does X actually work\\\&quot;, \\\&quot;explain X\\\&quot; (spatial concept)\\n\\n- Draw the **mechanism**, not a diagram about it\\n- Shapes are freeform — paths, ellipses, polygons, curves\\n- Color encodes intensity (warm = active, cool = calm, gray = inert)\\n- Labels: outside the object with leader lines, reserve 140px margin\\n- **Prefer interactive over static** — if the system has a control, give the diagram that control\\n\\n---\\n\\n## JavaScript libraries (local paths only)\\n\\nAll visualization libraries are served from local static paths. **Do NOT use external CDNs** — they are blocked by the security policy.\\n\\n### Chart.js (verified working)\\n\\n```html\\n&lt;script src=\\\&quot;/static/libraries/chart.js/4.5.1/chart.umd.js\\\&quot;&gt;&lt;/script&gt;\\n```\\n\\nUse for all standard charts: bar, line, doughnut, radar, scatter, bubble, polar area.\\n\\n### D3.js (for custom visualizations)\\n\\nFull bundle (includes all modules):\\n```html\\n&lt;script src=\\\&quot;/static/libraries/d3/7.9.0/d3.min.js\\\&quot;&gt;&lt;/script&gt;\\n```\\n\\nModular loading (smaller payload — load only what you need):\\n```html\\n&lt;script src=\\\&quot;/static/libraries/d3-selection/3.0.0/d3-selection.min.js\\\&quot;&gt;&lt;/script&gt;\\n&lt;script src=\\\&quot;/static/libraries/d3-scale/4.0.2/d3-scale.min.js\\\&quot;&gt;&lt;/script&gt;\\n&lt;script src=\\\&quot;/static/libraries/d3-axis/3.0.0/d3-axis.min.js\\\&quot;&gt;&lt;/script&gt;\\n&lt;script src=\\\&quot;/static/libraries/d3-shape/3.2.0/d3-shape.min.js\\\&quot;&gt;&lt;/script&gt;\\n```\\n\\nAvailable D3 modules:\\n- `d3-selection` — DOM selection and manipulation\\n- `d3-scale` — scale functions (linear, log, time, ordinal, etc.)\\n- `d3-axis` — axis rendering\\n- `d3-shape` — arc, line, area, pie generators\\n- `d3-force` — force-directed graph layout\\n- `d3-hierarchy` — tree, treemap, partition layouts\\n- `d3-geo` — geographic projections\\n- `d3-transition` — animated transitions\\n- `d3-zoom` — pan and zoom behavior\\n- `d3-drag` — drag behavior\\n- `d3-time` — time intervals and formatting\\n- `d3-format` — number formatting\\n- `d3-interpolate` — interpolation utilities\\n- `d3-color` — color space conversions\\n- `d3-contour` — contour plot generation\\n- `d3-delaunay` — Delaunay triangulation\\n- `d3-dsv` — CSV/TSV parsing\\n- `d3-ease` — easing functions\\n- `d3-fetch` — data loading utilities\\n- `d3-polygon` — polygon operations\\n- `d3-quadtree` — spatial indexing\\n- `d3-random` — random number generators\\n- `d3-scale-chromatic` — color schemes\\n- `d3-time-format` — time formatting\\n- `d3-timer` — animation timing\\n\\nSee `/static/libraries/` for full list.\\n\\n### Three.js (for 3D graphics)\\n\\n```html\\n&lt;script src=\\\&quot;/static/libraries/three/0.183.2/three.min.js\\\&quot;&gt;&lt;/script&gt;\\n```\\n\\n**Loading pattern:** Place all `&lt;script src=\\\&quot;...\\\&quot;&gt;` tags at the **end** of your HTML fragment, just before any inline `&lt;script&gt;` blocks that use the libraries.\\n\\n---\\n\\n## Charts (Chart.js)\\n\\nLoad Chart.js from local static path:\\n\\n```html\\n&lt;script src=\\\&quot;/static/libraries/chart.js/4.5.1/chart.umd.js\\\&quot;&gt;&lt;/script&gt;\\n```\\n\\nSetup pattern:\\n\\n```html\\n&lt;div style=\\\&quot;position: relative; height: 300px;\\\&quot;&gt;\\n  &lt;canvas id=\\\&quot;chart\\\&quot;&gt;&lt;/canvas&gt;\\n&lt;/div&gt;\\n&lt;script&gt;\\nconst ctx = document.getElementById(&#x27;chart&#x27;).getContext(&#x27;2d&#x27;);\\nconst s = getComputedStyle(document.documentElement);\\nconst textColor = s.getPropertyValue(&#x27;--color-text-secondary&#x27;).trim();\\nconst gridColor = s.getPropertyValue(&#x27;--color-border-tertiary&#x27;).trim();\\nnew Chart(ctx, {\\n  type: &#x27;bar&#x27;,\\n  data: {\\n    labels: [&#x27;Q1&#x27;,&#x27;Q2&#x27;,&#x27;Q3&#x27;,&#x27;Q4&#x27;],\\n    datasets: [{ label: &#x27;Revenue&#x27;, data: [12,19,8,15],\\n      backgroundColor: &#x27;#1D9E75&#x27;, borderRadius: 4, borderSkipped: false }]\\n  },\\n  options: {\\n    responsive: true, maintainAspectRatio: false,\\n    plugins: { legend: { labels: { color: textColor, font: { size: 12 } } } },\\n    scales: {\\n      x: { grid: { display: false }, ticks: { color: textColor, font: { size: 12 } }, border: { color: gridColor } },\\n      y: { grid: { color: gridColor }, ticks: { color: textColor, font: { size: 12 } }, border: { display: false } }\\n    }\\n  }\\n});\\n&lt;/script&gt;\\n```\\n\\n**Chart rules:**\\n- Wrap canvas in container with `position: relative` and explicit height\\n- Always `responsive: true`, `maintainAspectRatio: false`\\n- Read CSS variables for text/border colors — never hardcode\\n- `borderRadius: 4` on bars\\n- Line charts: `tension: 0.3` for smooth curves\\n- Doughnut: `cutout: &#x27;60%&#x27;` — never use pie\\n\\n**Chart type selection:**\\n\\n| Data shape | Type | Notes |\\n|-----------|------|-------|\\n| Categories + values | Bar | Horizontal if labels are long |\\n| Time series | Line | tension: 0.3 |\\n| Parts of whole | Doughnut | cutout: &#x27;60%&#x27; |\\n| Two variables | Scatter | Add trend line if relevant |\\n\\n### Inline SVG charts (no library)\\n\\nHorizontal bar:\\n```svg\\n&lt;svg width=\\\&quot;100%\\\&quot; viewBox=\\\&quot;0 0 680 80\\\&quot;&gt;\\n  &lt;text class=\\\&quot;ts\\\&quot; x=\\\&quot;40\\\&quot; y=\\\&quot;30\\\&quot;&gt;Category A&lt;/text&gt;\\n  &lt;rect x=\\\&quot;160\\\&quot; y=\\\&quot;16\\\&quot; width=\\\&quot;360\\\&quot; height=\\\&quot;20\\\&quot; rx=\\\&quot;4\\\&quot; fill=\\\&quot;#1D9E75\\\&quot; opacity=\\\&quot;0.8\\\&quot;/&gt;\\n  &lt;text class=\\\&quot;ts\\\&quot; x=\\\&quot;530\\\&quot; y=\\\&quot;30\\\&quot;&gt;72%&lt;/text&gt;\\n&lt;/svg&gt;\\n```\\n\\n---\\n\\n## Component patterns\\n\\n### Metric cards\\n\\n```html\\n&lt;div style=\\\&quot;display:grid; grid-template-columns:repeat(3,minmax(0,1fr)); gap:12px;\\\&quot;&gt;\\n  &lt;div style=\\\&quot;background:var(--color-bg-secondary); border:0.5px solid var(--color-border-tertiary);\\n    border-radius:var(--radius-lg); padding:16px;\\\&quot;&gt;\\n    &lt;div style=\\\&quot;font-size:12px; color:var(--color-text-secondary);\\\&quot;&gt;Label&lt;/div&gt;\\n    &lt;div style=\\\&quot;font-size:28px; font-weight:500; color:var(--color-text-primary); margin-top:4px;\\\&quot;&gt;$3,870&lt;/div&gt;\\n  &lt;/div&gt;\\n  &lt;!-- repeat --&gt;\\n&lt;/div&gt;\\n```\\n\\n### Comparison layout\\n\\nSide-by-side cards with a shared header row, metrics in each column.\\n\\n### Interactive explainer\\n\\nSliders or buttons that update visible state via JavaScript. Example: a slider controlling a variable with a live-updating SVG or value display.\\n\\n### Tab / toggle UI (event delegation pattern — required)\\n\\nNever attach click handlers with string arguments via inline `onclick` on individual elements — single quotes inside double-quoted HTML attributes cause parse failures that silently break interactivity.\\n\\nUse **event delegation** instead: one listener on the parent, `data-*` attribute on each child.\\n\\n```html\\n&lt;div id=\\\&quot;tabs\\\&quot;&gt;\\n  &lt;div class=\\\&quot;tab active\\\&quot; data-tab=\\\&quot;overview\\\&quot;&gt;Overview&lt;/div&gt;\\n  &lt;div class=\\\&quot;tab\\\&quot; data-tab=\\\&quot;detail\\\&quot;&gt;Detail&lt;/div&gt;\\n&lt;/div&gt;\\n&lt;div id=\\\&quot;panel-overview\\\&quot; class=\\\&quot;panel active\\\&quot;&gt;...&lt;/div&gt;\\n&lt;div id=\\\&quot;panel-detail\\\&quot; class=\\\&quot;panel\\\&quot;&gt;...&lt;/div&gt;\\n\\n&lt;script&gt;\\ndocument.getElementById(&#x27;tabs&#x27;).addEventListener(&#x27;click&#x27;, function(e) {\\n  var tab = e.target.closest(&#x27;.tab&#x27;);\\n  if (!tab) return;\\n  var name = tab.getAttribute(&#x27;data-tab&#x27;);\\n  document.querySelectorAll(&#x27;.tab&#x27;).forEach(function(t) { t.classList.remove(&#x27;active&#x27;); });\\n  document.querySelectorAll(&#x27;.panel&#x27;).forEach(function(p) { p.classList.remove(&#x27;active&#x27;); });\\n  tab.classList.add(&#x27;active&#x27;);\\n  document.getElementById(&#x27;panel-&#x27; + name).classList.add(&#x27;active&#x27;);\\n});\\n&lt;/script&gt;\\n```\\n\\nApply this pattern to all tabs, filter toggles, and multi-button UIs.\\n**Exception:** `onclick` without string arguments is fine — e.g. `onclick=\\\&quot;toggle()\\\&quot;`.\\n\\n---\\n\\n## sendPrompt bridge — conversational diagrams\\n\\n`sendPrompt(text)` is the function that makes visualizations conversational. When called, it injects the given text into the chat input field and submits it — exactly as if the user had typed and sent it themselves. The model then receives that message and responds normally, creating a feedback loop between the visual and the conversation.\\n\\nThis is what separates a static diagram from an **exploration interface**. A user sees a system architecture diagram, clicks on the \\\&quot;Load Balancer\\\&quot; node, and the model receives \\\&quot;Tell me more about the load balancer — how does it distribute traffic across the backend services?\\\&quot; as a user message. The model then responds with details, and could even generate a _new_ sub-diagram showing the load balancer internals. The user never had to type anything — they just clicked.\\n\\n### Why this matters\\n\\nWithout sendPrompt, interactive elements inside the iframe are isolated — they can toggle visibility, animate, or filter data, but they can never talk back to the model. The user sees a cool diagram but has to manually type follow-up questions. With sendPrompt, every clickable element becomes a conversation starter. The diagram itself becomes a navigation interface for the topic.\\n\\n### How it works technically\\n\\nThe function is auto-injected by the tool. It uses Open WebUI&#x27;s native `postMessage` protocol to submit the text as a user message. If the AI is still generating a response, the message is automatically queued and sent once generation completes. This requires **iframe Sandbox Allow Same Origin** to be enabled in Open WebUI settings — without it, the function silently fails (the visualization still works, but clicks do nothing).\\n\\n### Writing good sendPrompt text\\n\\nThe text you pass to sendPrompt becomes the user&#x27;s message to the model. Write it as a natural follow-up question — conversational, specific, and referencing the context of the diagram:\\n\\n**Good prompt text** (specific, contextual, references the diagram):\\n- `\\\&quot;Explain the attention mechanism — how does it decide which tokens to focus on?\\\&quot;`\\n- `\\\&quot;Break down the CI/CD pipeline stage. What tools are typically used here?\\\&quot;`\\n- `\\\&quot;Show me a more detailed diagram of the data processing layer\\\&quot;`\\n- `\\\&quot;What happens when the load balancer detects a failed backend node?\\\&quot;`\\n- `\\\&quot;Compare the pros and cons of the monolith vs microservices approach shown here\\\&quot;`\\n\\n**Bad prompt text** (vague, generic, loses context):\\n- `\\\&quot;Tell me more\\\&quot;` — more about what?\\n- `\\\&quot;Explain\\\&quot;` — explain what?\\n- `\\\&quot;Details\\\&quot;` — not a sentence, model won&#x27;t know what to do\\n\\n### Usage patterns\\n\\nSimple patterns — single-click sendPrompt on a node or button:\\n\\n- **Drill-down**: `onclick=\\\&quot;sendPrompt(&#x27;Explain the API gateway — what does it handle?&#x27;)\\\&quot;` on a diagram node\\n- **Quiz answer**: `onclick=\\\&quot;sendPrompt(&#x27;I chose B: O(n log n). Am I right? Explain why.&#x27;)\\\&quot;` on answer buttons\\n- **Guided exploration**: `onclick=\\\&quot;sendPrompt(&#x27;Show me a more advanced example with edge cases.&#x27;)\\\&quot;` on a \\\&quot;Go deeper →\\\&quot; button\\n- **Comparison**: `onclick=\\\&quot;sendPrompt(&#x27;Compare REST vs GraphQL — when should I use each?&#x27;)\\\&quot;` on one of two nodes\\n\\n**Form / preference collector** — gather multiple user selections, then send them all at once. Use local JS to track choices (button highlights, state object) and a submit button that composes a sendPrompt from the collected answers:\\n\\n```html\\n&lt;script&gt;\\nvar choices = {};\\nfunction pick(category, value, btn) {\\n  choices[category] = value;\\n  btn.parentElement.querySelectorAll(&#x27;button&#x27;).forEach(function(b) {\\n    b.classList.toggle(&#x27;active&#x27;, b === btn);\\n  });\\n}\\nfunction submitChoices() {\\n  var parts = [];\\n  for (var k in choices) parts.push(k + &#x27;: &#x27; + choices[k]);\\n  sendPrompt(&#x27;Here are my preferences:\\\\n&#x27; + parts.join(&#x27;\\\\n&#x27;) + &#x27;\\\\nGive me a personalized recommendation based on these choices.&#x27;);\\n}\\n&lt;/script&gt;\\n&lt;h3&gt;What&#x27;s your style?&lt;/h3&gt;\\n&lt;p style=\\\&quot;margin:8px 0 4px;\\\&quot;&gt;Pace&lt;/p&gt;\\n&lt;button onclick=\\\&quot;pick(&#x27;pace&#x27;,&#x27;relaxed&#x27;,this)\\\&quot;&gt;Relaxed&lt;/button&gt;\\n&lt;button onclick=\\\&quot;pick(&#x27;pace&#x27;,&#x27;moderate&#x27;,this)\\\&quot;&gt;Moderate&lt;/button&gt;\\n&lt;button onclick=\\\&quot;pick(&#x27;pace&#x27;,&#x27;intensive&#x27;,this)\\\&quot;&gt;Intensive&lt;/button&gt;\\n&lt;p style=\\\&quot;margin:8px 0 4px;\\\&quot;&gt;Focus&lt;/p&gt;\\n&lt;button onclick=\\\&quot;pick(&#x27;focus&#x27;,&#x27;culture&#x27;,this)\\\&quot;&gt;Culture&lt;/button&gt;\\n&lt;button onclick=\\\&quot;pick(&#x27;focus&#x27;,&#x27;nature&#x27;,this)\\\&quot;&gt;Nature&lt;/button&gt;\\n&lt;button onclick=\\\&quot;pick(&#x27;focus&#x27;,&#x27;food&#x27;,this)\\\&quot;&gt;Food&lt;/button&gt;\\n&lt;button onclick=\\\&quot;submitChoices()\\\&quot; style=\\\&quot;margin-top:12px; font-weight:500;\\\&quot;&gt;Get my recommendation →&lt;/button&gt;\\n```\\n\\nThis pattern is powerful because the model receives a structured summary of all user preferences in one message. Use local JS for the selection UI (instant feedback), then sendPrompt only on final submit.\\n\\n### When to use sendPrompt vs local JS:\\n\\n| User action | Use | Why |\\n|------------|-----|-----|\\n| Learn more about a component | `sendPrompt` | Model gives a contextual explanation |\\n| Explore a stage / drill down | `sendPrompt` | Model can generate a sub-diagram |\\n| Submit answers or preferences | `sendPrompt` | Model evaluates or personalizes |\\n| Toggle views, adjust sliders | Local JS | Instant feedback, no reasoning needed |\\n| Filter/sort data | Local JS | Instant response, no model needed |\\n\\n---\\n\\n## Interactivity by default\\n\\nVisualizations should feel alive and polished — not static images dumped into chat. Build interfaces that invite interaction:\\n\\n- **Expandable sections** — use collapsible `&lt;details&gt;` elements or JS-toggled sections so users can explore at their own pace without overwhelming them upfront\\n- **Hover effects** — nodes, buttons, and cards should respond to hover (the `.node` class adds this for SVG elements; for HTML, use `:hover` styles)\\n- **Smooth transitions** — add `transition: all 0.2s ease` to interactive elements for a polished feel\\n- **Active states** — when a user selects an option or clicks a tab, make the selection visually clear with the `.active` class or distinct styling\\n- **Progressive disclosure** — show a clean overview first, let the user click to reveal detail (tabs, accordions, or sendPrompt for model-powered drill-down)\\n\\nThe goal is to build something that feels like a real app component embedded in chat — not a screenshot. If the visualization has multiple facets, give the user controls to explore them. If it has hierarchical information, let them expand and collapse. If it has data, let them sort or filter.\\n\\n---\\n\\n## openLink bridge — opening URLs from visualizations\\n\\n`openLink(url)` opens a URL in a new browser tab from within the visualization iframe. Normal `&lt;a href=\\\&quot;...\\\&quot;&gt;` links inside an iframe can behave unpredictably (opening inside the iframe, being blocked by sandbox restrictions, etc.). This function handles that by opening the link in the parent window instead.\\n\\n```html\\n&lt;button onclick=\\\&quot;openLink(&#x27;https://docs.example.com/api-reference&#x27;)\\\&quot;&gt;\\n  Open API docs ↗\\n&lt;/button&gt;\\n```\\n\\nOr in SVG:\\n```svg\\n&lt;g class=\\\&quot;node c-blue\\\&quot; onclick=\\\&quot;openLink(&#x27;https://github.com/org/repo&#x27;)\\\&quot;&gt;\\n  &lt;rect x=\\\&quot;100\\\&quot; y=\\\&quot;20\\\&quot; width=\\\&quot;200\\\&quot; height=\\\&quot;44\\\&quot; rx=\\\&quot;8\\\&quot;/&gt;\\n  &lt;text class=\\\&quot;th\\\&quot; x=\\\&quot;200\\\&quot; y=\\\&quot;42\\\&quot; text-anchor=\\\&quot;middle\\\&quot; dominant-baseline=\\\&quot;central\\\&quot;&gt;View source ↗&lt;/text&gt;\\n&lt;/g&gt;\\n```\\n\\nUse `openLink` for external references, documentation links, or source code links. Unlike `sendPrompt`, this navigates away from the chat — use it when the user needs to access an external resource, not when they need the model to explain something.\\n\\n---\\n\\n## Quick reference\\n\\n| Rule | Value |\\n|------|-------|\\n| SVG viewBox width | Always 680px |\\n| Font sizes | 14px labels, 12px subtitles |\\n| Stroke width | 0.5px for borders |\\n| Max colors per diagram | 2–3 ramps |\\n| Subtitle max length | 5 words |\\n| Corner radius (SVG) | rx=4 default, rx=8 emphasis |\\n| Corner radius (HTML) | var(--radius-md) or -lg |\\n| Min font size | 11px |\\n| Heading sizes | h1=22px, h2=18px, h3=16px |\\n\\n## Common failures\\n\\n1. **Arrow through a box** — trace coordinates against every box\\n2. **Text overflow** — check (text_width + 2×padding) fits the rect\\n3. **viewBox too small** — content clipped at bottom\\n4. **viewBox too large** — creates wasteful empty space below the diagram. Calculate actual content bottom + 40px\\n5. **Floating labels** — every text needs a box, legend, or leader line\\n6. **Connector without fill=\\\&quot;none\\\&quot;** — renders as black shape\\n7. **Missing dominant-baseline=\\\&quot;central\\\&quot;** — text sits 4px too high\\n8. **Missing arrow marker in defs** — always include it\\n9. **Hardcoded colors** — always use CSS variables or ramp classes\\n10. **External CDN usage** — all libraries must load from `/static/libraries/`, never from cdnjs/jsdelivr/unpkg\\n11. **Inline onclick with string arguments** — never write `onclick=\\\&quot;fn(&#x27;value&#x27;)\\\&quot;` on HTML elements. Single quotes inside double-quoted attributes cause HTML parse failures that break interactivity or split the response into multiple fragments. Use `data-*` attributes + event delegation on a parent element instead. Exception: `onclick` without string arguments is fine, e.g. `onclick=\\\&quot;toggle()\\\&quot;`.\&quot;}&quot;" files="" embeds="&quot;&quot;">

   &lt;div class=\&quot;section-label\&quot;&gt;Pairs&lt;/div&gt;\n      &lt;span class=\&quot;tag pair\&quot;&gt;GBP/USD&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span class=\&quot;tag pair\&quot;&gt;EUR/USD&lt;/span&gt;\n      &lt;div class=\&quot;risk-row\&quot;&gt;&lt;span class=\&quot;risk-pill\&quot;&gt;Risk: 1% \u0e15\u0e48\u0e2d Trade&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span ### คำแนะนำการเริ่มต้น:

**พอร์ตที่ควรเขียน EA แรกสุด** คือ **141049900** (Trend Following) เพราะ:
- Logic ตรงไปตรงมาที่สุด — EMA Cross + ADX + MACD
- เป็น Foundation ที่ใช้ได้กับทุก Timeframe
- ถ้าเขียนตัวนี้ได้ → Code Structure สามารถนำไปต่อยอดพอร์ต 3, 10, 11 ได้เลย

**กด "ขอ Code MQL5"** ในพอร์ตไหนก็ได้ เพื่อให้ข้าร่าง Code โครงสร้างเต็มให้ท่านครับบอส 🔥

----- [user] -----
ขอ code mql 5 ทุก strategy เลย แต่เพิ่มการพิจารณา multi timeframe ไปด้วยโดยดูแนวโน้มตาม HTF แล้วเข้าใน Timeframe ที่เล็กลง

----- [assistant] -----
ขอแบ่งเป็นชุดๆ ครับบอส เพราะแต่ละ EA มี Logic เต็ม — เริ่มจากกลุ่ม SSS ก่อนเลย

## 🔱 PORT 1 — 141049900 | Celestial Woodfire
### Multi-Timeframe Trend Following (D1 → H4 → H1)

```mql5
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| EA_CelestialWoodfire_141049900.mq5                               |
//| Strategy: MTF Trend Following                                    |
//| HTF: D1 (Trend Filter) → MID: H4 (Signal) → LTF: H1 (Entry)    |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
#property copyright "Port 141049900"
#property version   "1.00"

#include <Trade\Trade.mqh>
CTrade trade;

//--- Input Parameters
input double   RiskPercent    = 1.0;      // Risk % per trade
input double   ATR_Multiplier_TP = 3.0;  // TP = ATR x this
input double   ATR_Multiplier_SL = 1.5;  // SL = ATR x this
input int      ATR_Period     = 14;
input int      EMA_Fast       = 50;
input int      EMA_Slow       = 200;
input int      MACD_Fast      = 12;
input int      MACD_Slow      = 26;
input int      MACD_Signal    = 9;
input int      ADX_Period     = 14;
input double   ADX_Min        = 25.0;
input int      Magic          = 141049900;

//--- Handles
int h_ema_fast_d1, h_ema_slow_d1;          // D1 handles
int h_ema_fast_h4, h_ema_slow_h4;          // H4 handles
int h_macd_h1, h_adx_h1, h_atr_h1;        // H1 handles

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
int OnInit()
{
   // D1 — Trend Direction
   h_ema_fast_d1 = iMA(_Symbol, PERIOD_D1, EMA_Fast,  0, MODE_EMA, PRICE_CLOSE);
   h_ema_slow_d1 = iMA(_Symbol, PERIOD_D1, EMA_Slow,  0, MODE_EMA, PRICE_CLOSE);
   // H4 — Signal Confirmation
   h_ema_fast_h4 = iMA(_Symbol, PERIOD_H4, EMA_Fast,  0, MODE_EMA, PRICE_CLOSE);
   h_ema_slow_h4 = iMA(_Symbol, PERIOD_H4, EMA_Slow,  0, MODE_EMA, PRICE_CLOSE);
   // H1 — Entry Timing
   h_macd_h1     = iMACD(_Symbol, PERIOD_H1, MACD_Fast, MACD_Slow, MACD_Signal, PRICE_CLOSE);
   h_adx_h1      = iADX(_Symbol,  PERIOD_H1, ADX_Period);
   h_atr_h1      = iATR(_Symbol,  PERIOD_H1, ATR_Period);

   trade.SetExpertMagicNumber(Magic);
   return INIT_SUCCEEDED;
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void OnTick()
{
   // ทำงานเฉพาะแท่งใหม่ H1
   static datetime lastBar = 0;
   datetime currentBar = iTime(_Symbol, PERIOD_H1, 0);
   if(currentBar == lastBar) return;
   lastBar = currentBar;

   if(PositionsTotal() > 0) { ManageTrailingStop(); return; }

   //--- Layer 1: D1 Trend Filter
   double ema_fast_d1[2], ema_slow_d1[2];
   CopyBuffer(h_ema_fast_d1, 0, 0, 2, ema_fast_d1);
   CopyBuffer(h_ema_slow_d1, 0, 0, 2, ema_slow_d1);
   bool d1_bull = ema_fast_d1[0] > ema_slow_d1[0];
   bool d1_bear = ema_fast_d1[0] < ema_slow_d1[0];

   //--- Layer 2: H4 Signal Confirmation
   double ema_fast_h4[2], ema_slow_h4[2];
   CopyBuffer(h_ema_fast_h4, 0, 0, 2, ema_fast_h4);
   CopyBuffer(h_ema_slow_h4, 0, 0, 2, ema_slow_h4);
   bool h4_bull = ema_fast_h4[0] > ema_slow_h4[0];
   bool h4_bear = ema_fast_h4[0] < ema_slow_h4[0];

   //--- Layer 3: H1 Entry — MACD Cross + ADX Strength
   double macd_main[3], macd_signal[3], adx_val[2], atr_val[2];
   CopyBuffer(h_macd_h1, MAIN_LINE,   0, 3, macd_main);
   CopyBuffer(h_macd_h1, SIGNAL_LINE, 0, 3, macd_signal);
   CopyBuffer(h_adx_h1,  MAIN_LINE,   0, 2, adx_val);
   CopyBuffer(h_atr_h1,  0,           0, 2, atr_val);

   bool macd_cross_up   = macd_main[1] > macd_signal[1] && macd_main[2] <= macd_signal[2];
   bool macd_cross_down = macd_main[1] < macd_signal[1] && macd_main[2] >= macd_signal[2];
   bool adx_strong      = adx_val[0] > ADX_Min;
   double atr           = atr_val[0];

   //--- Execute
   if(d1_bull && h4_bull && macd_cross_up && adx_strong)
      OpenBuy(atr);
   else if(d1_bear && h4_bear && macd_cross_down && adx_strong)
      OpenSell(atr);
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void OpenBuy(double atr)
{
   double ask  = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_ASK);
   double sl   = ask - atr * ATR_Multiplier_SL;
   double tp   = ask + atr * ATR_Multiplier_TP;
   double lots = CalcLots(ask - sl);
   trade.Buy(lots, _Symbol, ask, sl, tp, "CW_BUY");
}

void OpenSell(double atr)
{
   double bid  = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_BID);
   double sl   = bid + atr * ATR_Multiplier_SL;
   double tp   = bid - atr * ATR_Multiplier_TP;
   double lots = CalcLots(sl - bid);
   trade.Sell(lots, _Symbol, bid, sl, tp, "CW_SELL");
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void ManageTrailingStop()
{
   double ema_fast_h4[2];
   CopyBuffer(h_ema_fast_h4, 0, 0, 2, ema_fast_h4);

   for(int i = PositionsTotal()-1; i >= 0; i--)
   {
      ulong ticket = PositionGetTicket(i);
      if(!PositionSelectByTicket(ticket)) continue;
      if(PositionGetInteger(POSITION_MAGIC) != Magic) continue;

      double openPrice = PositionGetDouble(POSITION_PRICE_OPEN);
      double currentSL = PositionGetDouble(POSITION_SL);
      double profit    = PositionGetDouble(POSITION_PROFIT);
      long   posType   = PositionGetInteger(POSITION_TYPE);

      if(profit <= 0) continue; // Trail เฉพาะเมื่อมีกำไร

      if(posType == POSITION_TYPE_BUY)
      {
         double newSL = ema_fast_h4[0] - SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_POINT) * 10;
         if(newSL > currentSL && newSL < SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_BID))
            trade.PositionModify(ticket, newSL, PositionGetDouble(POSITION_TP));
      }
      else if(posType == POSITION_TYPE_SELL)
      {
         double newSL = ema_fast_h4[0] + SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_POINT) * 10;
         if(newSL < currentSL && newSL > SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_ASK))
            trade.PositionModify(ticket, newSL, PositionGetDouble(POSITION_TP));
      }
   }
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
double CalcLots(double slPoints)
{
   double balance    = AccountInfoDouble(ACCOUNT_BALANCE);
   double riskAmount = balance * RiskPercent / 100.0;
   double tickValue  = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_TRADE_TICK_VALUE);
   double tickSize   = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_TRADE_TICK_SIZE);
   double lotStep    = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_VOLUME_STEP);
   double minLot     = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_VOLUME_MIN);
   double maxLot     = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_VOLUME_MAX);

   if(slPoints <= 0 || tickValue <= 0) return minLot;

   double lots = riskAmount / (slPoints / tickSize * tickValue);
   lots = MathFloor(lots / lotStep) * lotStep;
   return MathMax(minLot, MathMin(maxLot, lots));
}
```

---

## 🔱 PORT 2 — 146004104 | Ironclad Forest
### Multi-Timeframe Smart Grid / DCA (W1 → D1 → H1)

```mql5
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| EA_IroncladForest_146004104.mq5                                  |
//| Strategy: MTF Smart Grid with Basket SL/TP                      |
//| HTF: W1 (Range Boundary) → D1 (Bias) → H1 (Grid Entry)         |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
#property copyright "Port 146004104"
#property version   "1.00"

#include <Trade\Trade.mqh>
CTrade trade;

input double RiskPercent     = 1.0;
input int    BB_Period        = 20;
input double BB_Dev           = 2.0;
input int    RSI_Period       = 14;
input int    ATR_Period       = 14;
input int    MaxGridLevels    = 4;
input double GridATRMultiply  = 1.0;   // Grid spacing = ATR x this
input double BasketTP_Pct     = 1.0;   // Basket TP % of balance
input double BasketSL_Pct     = 3.0;   // Basket SL % of balance
input double BaseLot          = 0.01;
input int    Magic            = 146004104;

int h_bb_h1, h_rsi_h1, h_atr_h1;
int h_bb_d1;
int h_bb_w1;

double gridLevels[];
int    gridCount = 0;
bool   gridBullish = false;

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
int OnInit()
{
   h_bb_h1  = iBands(_Symbol, PERIOD_H1, BB_Period, 0, BB_Dev, PRICE_CLOSE);
   h_rsi_h1 = iRSI(_Symbol,   PERIOD_H1, RSI_Period, PRICE_CLOSE);
   h_atr_h1 = iATR(_Symbol,   PERIOD_H1, ATR_Period);
   h_bb_d1  = iBands(_Symbol, PERIOD_D1, BB_Period, 0, BB_Dev, PRICE_CLOSE);
   h_bb_w1  = iBands(_Symbol, PERIOD_W1, BB_Period, 0, BB_Dev, PRICE_CLOSE);

   trade.SetExpertMagicNumber(Magic);
   ArrayResize(gridLevels, MaxGridLevels);
   return INIT_SUCCEEDED;
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void OnTick()
{
   static datetime lastBar = 0;
   datetime currentBar = iTime(_Symbol, PERIOD_H1, 0);
   if(currentBar == lastBar) return;
   lastBar = currentBar;

   CheckBasketExit();

   //--- Layer 1: W1 Boundary — ราคาอยู่ในกรอบ W1 BB ไหม
   double w1_upper[2], w1_lower[2];
   CopyBuffer(h_bb_w1, 1, 0, 2, w1_upper); // Upper
   CopyBuffer(h_bb_w1, 2, 0, 2, w1_lower); // Lower
   double price = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_BID);
   bool inW1Range = (price > w1_lower[0] && price < w1_upper[0]);
   if(!inW1Range) return; // ราคาอยู่นอกกรอบ W1 → ไม่เทรด

   //--- Layer 2: D1 Bias — BB D1 บอกทิศทาง
   double d1_mid[2], d1_upper[2], d1_lower[2];
   CopyBuffer(h_bb_d1, 0, 0, 2, d1_mid);
   CopyBuffer(h_bb_d1, 1, 0, 2, d1_upper);
   CopyBuffer(h_bb_d1, 2, 0, 2, d1_lower);
   bool d1_bullBias = price < d1_mid[0]; // ราคาใต้ mid → มีโอกาส Bounce ขึ้น
   bool d1_bearBias = price > d1_mid[0];

   //--- Layer 3: H1 Entry Signal
   double h1_upper[2], h1_lower[2];
   double rsi_val[2], atr_val[2];
   CopyBuffer(h_bb_h1, 1, 0, 2, h1_upper);
   CopyBuffer(h_bb_h1, 2, 0, 2, h1_lower);
   CopyBuffer(h_rsi_h1, 0, 0, 2, rsi_val);
   CopyBuffer(h_atr_h1, 0, 0, 2, atr_val);

   double atr = atr_val[0];
   bool h1_buySignal  = (price < h1_lower[0] && rsi_val[0] < 30);
   bool h1_sellSignal = (price > h1_upper[0] && rsi_val[0] > 70);

   int currentGridCount = CountGridPositions();

   //--- เปิดออเดอร์แรก
   if(currentGridCount == 0)
   {
      if(d1_bullBias && h1_buySignal)
      { gridBullish = true;  AddGridOrder(true,  atr, currentGridCount); }
      else if(d1_bearBias && h1_sellSignal)
      { gridBullish = false; AddGridOrder(false, atr, currentGridCount); }
   }
   //--- เพิ่ม Grid ถ้าราคาวิ่งสวนและยังไม่ถึง Max
   else if(currentGridCount < MaxGridLevels)
   {
      double lastGridPrice = GetLastGridPrice();
      if(gridBullish && price < lastGridPrice - atr * GridATRMultiply)
         AddGridOrder(true,  atr, currentGridCount);
      else if(!gridBullish && price > lastGridPrice + atr * GridATRMultiply)
         AddGridOrder(false, atr, currentGridCount);
   }
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void AddGridOrder(bool isBuy, double atr, int level)
{
   double lotMultiplier = 1.0 + level * 0.5; // 1.0, 1.5, 2.0, 2.5
   double lots = MathMax(BaseLot, MathFloor(BaseLot * lotMultiplier / 0.01) * 0.01);
   double ask  = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_ASK);
   double bid  = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_BID);

   if(isBuy)  trade.Buy(lots,  _Symbol, ask, 0, 0, "IF_BUY_L" + IntegerToString(level));
   else       trade.Sell(lots, _Symbol, bid, 0, 0, "IF_SELL_L" + IntegerToString(level));
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void CheckBasketExit()
{
   double balance    = AccountInfoDouble(ACCOUNT_BALANCE);
   double totalProfit = 0;
   int    count = 0;

   for(int i = 0; i < PositionsTotal(); i++)
   {
      ulong ticket = PositionGetTicket(i);
      if(!PositionSelectByTicket(ticket)) continue;
      if(PositionGetInteger(POSITION_MAGIC) != Magic) continue;
      totalProfit += PositionGetDouble(POSITION_PROFIT);
      count++;
   }
   if(count == 0) return;

   double tpThreshold = balance * BasketTP_Pct / 100.0;
   double slThreshold = balance * BasketSL_Pct / 100.0 * -1;

   if(totalProfit >= tpThreshold || totalProfit <= slThreshold)
   {
      for(int i = PositionsTotal()-1; i >= 0; i--)
      {
         ulong ticket = PositionGetTicket(i);
         if(!PositionSelectByTicket(ticket)) continue;
         if(PositionGetInteger(POSITION_MAGIC) != Magic) continue;
         trade.PositionClose(ticket);
      }
   }
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
int CountGridPositions()
{
   int count = 0;
   for(int i = 0; i < PositionsTotal(); i++)
   {
      ulong ticket = PositionGetTicket(i);
      if(!PositionSelectByTicket(ticket)) continue;
      if(PositionGetInteger(POSITION_MAGIC) == Magic) count++;
   }
   return count;
}

double GetLastGridPrice()
{
   double lastPrice = 0;
   datetime lastTime = 0;
   for(int i = 0; i < PositionsTotal(); i++)
   {
      ulong ticket = PositionGetTicket(i);
      if(!PositionSelectByTicket(ticket)) continue;
      if(PositionGetInteger(POSITION_MAGIC) != Magic) continue;
      datetime t = (datetime)PositionGetInteger(POSITION_TIME);
      if(t > lastTime) { lastTime = t; lastPrice = PositionGetDouble(POSITION_PRICE_OPEN); }
   }
   return lastPrice;
}
```

## 🔱 PORT 3 — 193234160 | Dawn Ascendant
### Multi-Timeframe Volatility Breakout (W1 → D1 → H4)

```mql5
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| EA_DawnAscendant_193234160.mq5                                   |
//| Strategy: MTF Volatility Breakout (Pending Orders)              |
//| HTF: W1 (Trend) → D1 (Breakout Level) → H4 (Execution)         |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
#property copyright "Port 193234160"
#property version   "1.00"

#include <Trade\Trade.mqh>
CTrade trade;

input double RiskPercent      = 1.5;
input int    Donchian_Period  = 20;
input int    ATR_Period       = 14;
input double ATR_Entry_Mult   = 0.5;  // Pending distance above/below Donchian
input double ATR_TP_Mult      = 2.0;
input int    Volume_Period    = 20;
input double Volume_Mult      = 1.5;  // Volume filter multiplier
input int    Magic            = 193234160;

int h_atr_h4, h_atr_d1;
int h_ema200_w1;

ulong buyStopTicket  = 0;
ulong sellStopTicket = 0;

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
int OnInit()
{
   h_atr_h4   = iATR(_Symbol, PERIOD_H4, ATR_Period);
   h_atr_d1   = iATR(_Symbol, PERIOD_D1, ATR_Period);
   h_ema200_w1 = iMA(_Symbol, PERIOD_W1, 200, 0, MODE_EMA, PRICE_CLOSE);

   trade.SetExpertMagicNumber(Magic);
   return INIT_SUCCEEDED;
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void OnTick()
{
   static datetime lastBar = 0;
   datetime currentBar = iTime(_Symbol, PERIOD_H4, 0);
   if(currentBar == lastBar) return;
   lastBar = currentBar;

   //--- ลบ Pending เก่าที่ยังไม่ถูก Trigger (หมดอายุทุกแท่ง H4)
   DeleteOldPendings();

   if(PositionsTotal() > 0) { ManageTrailing(); return; }

   //--- Layer 1: W1 EMA200 — Macro Trend Direction
   double ema200_w1[2];
   CopyBuffer(h_ema200_w1, 0, 0, 2, ema200_w1);
   double price     = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_BID);
   bool w1_bullTrend = price > ema200_w1[0];
   bool w1_bearTrend = price < ema200_w1[0];

   //--- Layer 2: D1 Donchian Channel — Key Levels
   double d1_high[], d1_low[], d1_close[];
   ArraySetAsSeries(d1_high,  true);
   ArraySetAsSeries(d1_low,   true);
   ArraySetAsSeries(d1_close, true);
   CopyHigh(_Symbol,  PERIOD_D1, 1, Donchian_Period, d1_high);
   CopyLow(_Symbol,   PERIOD_D1, 1, Donchian_Period, d1_low);
   CopyClose(_Symbol, PERIOD_D1, 0, Volume_Period+1, d1_close);

   double donchianHigh = d1_high[ArrayMaximum(d1_high)];
   double donchianLow  = d1_low[ArrayMinimum(d1_low)];

   //--- Layer 3: H4 ATR + Volume Confirmation
   double atr_h4[2];
   CopyBuffer(h_atr_h4, 0, 0, 2, atr_h4);
   double atr = atr_h4[0];

   long  curVol = iVolume(_Symbol, PERIOD_H4, 1);
   long  volArr[]; ArraySetAsSeries(volArr, true);
   CopyTickVolume(_Symbol, PERIOD_H4, 1, Volume_Period, volArr);
   double avgVol = 0;
   for(int i = 0; i < Volume_Period; i++) avgVol += volArr[i];
   avgVol /= Volume_Period;
   bool volumeOK = curVol > avgVol * Volume_Mult;

   if(!volumeOK) return;

   //--- วาง Pending Orders
   double entryBuy  = donchianHigh + atr * ATR_Entry_Mult;
   double entrySell = donchianLow  - atr * ATR_Entry_Mult;
   double atr_d1_val[2];
   CopyBuffer(h_atr_d1, 0, 0, 2, atr_d1_val);
   double slBuy  = donchianHigh - atr_d1_val[0]; // SL ใต้ Donchian High
   double slSell = donchianLow  + atr_d1_val[0];
   double tpBuy  = entryBuy  + atr * ATR_TP_Mult;
   double tpSell = entrySell - atr * ATR_TP_Mult;
   double lots   = CalcLots(MathAbs(entryBuy - slBuy));

   // วาง BuyStop เฉพาะถ้า W1 Bullish หรือ Neutral
   if(w1_bullTrend || (!w1_bullTrend && !w1_bearTrend))
   {
      if(trade.BuyStop(lots, entryBuy, _Symbol, slBuy, tpBuy, ORDER_TIME_GTC, 0, "DA_BUYSTOP"))
         buyStopTicket = trade.ResultOrder();
   }
   // วาง SellStop เฉพาะถ้า W1 Bearish หรือ Neutral
   if(w1_bearTrend || (!w1_bullTrend && !w1_bearTrend))
   {
      if(trade.SellStop(lots, entrySell, _Symbol, slSell, tpSell, ORDER_TIME_GTC, 0, "DA_SELLSTOP"))
         sellStopTicket = trade.ResultOrder();
   }
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void DeleteOldPendings()
{
   for(int i = OrdersTotal()-1; i >= 0; i--)
   {
      ulong ticket = OrderGetTicket(i);
      if(!OrderSelect(ticket)) continue;
      if(OrderGetInteger(ORDER_MAGIC) != Magic) continue;
      trade.OrderDelete(ticket);
   }
   buyStopTicket = 0;
   sellStopTicket = 0;
}

void ManageTrailing()
{
   double atr_h4[2];
   CopyBuffer(h_atr_h4, 0, 0, 2, atr_h4);
   double atr = atr_h4[0];

   for(int i = PositionsTotal()-1; i >= 0; i--)
   {
      ulong ticket = PositionGetTicket(i);
      if(!PositionSelectByTicket(ticket)) continue;
      if(PositionGetInteger(POSITION_MAGIC) != Magic) continue;

      double openPrice = PositionGetDouble(POSITION_PRICE_OPEN);
      double currentSL = PositionGetDouble(POSITION_SL);
      long   posType   = PositionGetInteger(POSITION_TYPE);
      double profit    = PositionGetDouble(POSITION_PROFIT);
      if(profit <= 0) continue;

      if(posType == POSITION_TYPE_BUY)
      {
         double bid   = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_BID);
         double newSL = bid - atr;
         if(newSL > currentSL)
            trade.PositionModify(ticket, newSL, PositionGetDouble(POSITION_TP));
      }
      else
      {
         double ask   = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_ASK);
         double newSL = ask + atr;
         if(newSL < currentSL)
            trade.PositionModify(ticket, newSL, PositionGetDouble(POSITION_TP));
      }
   }
}

double CalcLots(double slPoints)
{
   double balance    = AccountInfoDouble(ACCOUNT_BALANCE);
   double riskAmount = balance * RiskPercent / 100.0;
   double tickValue  = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_TRADE_TICK_VALUE);
   double tickSize   = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_TRADE_TICK_SIZE);
   double lotStep    = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_VOLUME_STEP);
   double minLot     = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_VOLUME_MIN);
   double maxLot     = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_VOLUME_MAX);
   if(slPoints <= 0 || tickValue <= 0) return minLot;
   double lots = riskAmount / (slPoints / tickSize * tickValue);
   lots = MathFloor(lots / lotStep) * lotStep;
   return MathMax(minLot, MathMin(maxLot, lots));
}
```

## ⚔️ PORT 4 — 159219430 | Crimson Tide
### MTF Mean Reversion (W1 → D1 → H1)

```mql5
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| EA_CrimsonTide_159219430.mq5                                     |
//| Strategy: MTF Mean Reversion                                     |
//| HTF: W1 (Range Confirm) → D1 (Trend Neutral Check) → H1 (Entry)|
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
#property copyright "Port 159219430"
#property version   "1.00"

#include <Trade\Trade.mqh>
CTrade trade;

input double RiskPercent    = 0.8;
input int    BB_Period      = 20;
input double BB_Dev         = 2.5;
input int    RSI_Fast       = 7;
input int    Stoch_K        = 5;
input int    Stoch_D        = 3;
input int    Stoch_Slowing  = 3;
input int    ATR_Period     = 14;
input int    MaxHoldHours   = 48;
input int    Magic          = 159219430;

int h_bb_h1, h_rsi_h1, h_stoch_h1, h_atr_h1;
int h_adx_d1, h_ema200_d1;
int h_bb_w1;

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
int OnInit()
{
   h_bb_h1    = iBands(_Symbol,  PERIOD_H1, BB_Period, 0, BB_Dev, PRICE_CLOSE);
   h_rsi_h1   = iRSI(_Symbol,   PERIOD_H1, RSI_Fast, PRICE_CLOSE);
   h_stoch_h1 = iStochastic(_Symbol, PERIOD_H1, Stoch_K, Stoch_D, Stoch_Slowing, MODE_SMA, STO_LOWHIGH);
   h_atr_h1   = iATR(_Symbol,   PERIOD_H1, ATR_Period);
   h_adx_d1   = iADX(_Symbol,   PERIOD_D1, 14);
   h_ema200_d1 = iMA(_Symbol,   PERIOD_D1, 200, 0, MODE_EMA, PRICE_CLOSE);
   h_bb_w1    = iBands(_Symbol,  PERIOD_W1, BB_Period, 0, BB_Dev, PRICE_CLOSE);

   trade.SetExpertMagicNumber(Magic);
   return INIT_SUCCEEDED;
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void OnTick()
{
   static datetime lastBar = 0;
   datetime currentBar = iTime(_Symbol, PERIOD_H1, 0);
   if(currentBar == lastBar) return;
   lastBar = currentBar;

   CheckTimeExit();
   if(PositionsTotal() > 0) return;

   //--- Layer 1: W1 — ยืนยันว่าอยู่ในโหมด Range ไม่ใช่ Trend แรง
   double w1_upper[2], w1_lower[2], w1_mid[2];
   CopyBuffer(h_bb_w1, 0, 0, 2, w1_mid);
   CopyBuffer(h_bb_w1, 1, 0, 2, w1_upper);
   CopyBuffer(h_bb_w1, 2, 0, 2, w1_lower);
   double w1_bandwidth = (w1_upper[0] - w1_lower[0]) / w1_mid[0];
   if(w1_bandwidth > 0.04) return; // BB กว้างเกิน = Trend แรง ห้ามเทรด Reversion

   //--- Layer 2: D1 — ตลาดต้องไม่เป็น Trend ชัดเจน (ADX < 25) + EMA200 Check
   double adx_d1[2], ema200_d1[2];
   CopyBuffer(h_adx_d1,   MAIN_LINE, 0, 2, adx_d1);
   CopyBuffer(h_ema200_d1, 0,        0, 2, ema200_d1);
   if(adx_d1[0] > 30) return; // D1 Trend แรงเกิน → ห้าม Mean Reversion
   double price  = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_BID);
   bool d1_above = price > ema200_d1[0];
   bool d1_below = price < ema200_d1[0];

   //--- Layer 3: H1 Entry
   double bb_upper[2], bb_lower[2], bb_mid[2];
   double rsi_val[2], stoch_k[2], stoch_d_val[2], atr_val[2];
   CopyBuffer(h_bb_h1,    0, 0, 2, bb_mid);
   CopyBuffer(h_bb_h1,    1, 0, 2, bb_upper);
   CopyBuffer(h_bb_h1,    2, 0, 2, bb_lower);
   CopyBuffer(h_rsi_h1,   0, 0, 2, rsi_val);
   CopyBuffer(h_stoch_h1, MAIN_LINE,   0, 2, stoch_k);
   CopyBuffer(h_stoch_h1, SIGNAL_LINE, 0, 2, stoch_d_val);
   CopyBuffer(h_atr_h1,   0, 0, 2, atr_val);

   double atr = atr_val[0];
   bool h1_buySetup  = price < bb_lower[0] && rsi_val[0] < 25 && stoch_k[0] < 20;
   bool h1_sellSetup = price > bb_upper[0] && rsi_val[0] > 75 && stoch_k[0] > 80;

   // D1 Filter: ห้าม BUY ถ้า D1 ชี้ลงชัดเจน (price ต่ำกว่า EMA200 D1)
   if(h1_buySetup && !d1_below)
   {
      double ask = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_ASK);
      double sl  = ask - atr * 1.5;
      double tp  = bb_mid[0]; // TP ที่ BB Middle
      trade.Buy(CalcLots(ask - sl), _Symbol, ask, sl, tp, "CT_BUY");
   }
   else if(h1_sellSetup && !d1_above)
   {
      double bid = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_BID);
      double sl  = bid + atr * 1.5;
      double tp  = bb_mid[0];
      trade.Sell(CalcLots(sl - bid), _Symbol, bid, sl, tp, "CT_SELL");
   }
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void CheckTimeExit()
{
   datetime now = TimeCurrent();
   for(int i = PositionsTotal()-1; i >= 0; i--)
   {
      ulong ticket = PositionGetTicket(i);
      if(!PositionSelectByTicket(ticket)) continue;
      if(PositionGetInteger(POSITION_MAGIC) != Magic) continue;
      datetime openTime = (datetime)PositionGetInteger(POSITION_TIME);
      if((now - openTime) >= MaxHoldHours * 3600)
         trade.PositionClose(ticket); // Force close หลัง MaxHoldHours
   }
}

double CalcLots(double slPoints)
{
   double balance    = AccountInfoDouble(ACCOUNT_BALANCE);
   double riskAmount = balance * RiskPercent / 100.0;
   double tickValue  = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_TRADE_TICK_VALUE);
   double tickSize   = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_TRADE_TICK_SIZE);
   double lotStep    = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_VOLUME_STEP);
   double minLot     = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_VOLUME_MIN);
   double maxLot     = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_VOLUME_MAX);
   if(slPoints <= 0 || tickValue <= 0) return minLot;
   double lots = riskAmount / (slPoints / tickSize * tickValue);
   lots = MathFloor(lots / lotStep) * lotStep;
   return MathMax(minLot, MathMin(maxLot, lots));
}
```

## ⚔️ PORT 5 — 111098104 | Abyssal Discipline
### MTF Pair Trading — Z-Score (D1 → H4 → H1)

```mql5
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| EA_AbyssalDiscipline_111098104.mq5                               |
//| Strategy: MTF Correlation Pair Trading (EURUSD vs USDCHF)       |
//| HTF: D1 (Correlation Check) → H4 (Z-Score Calc) → H1 (Entry)   |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//  !! ต้องรันบน Chart EURUSD !!  Symbol2 = USDCHF
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
#property copyright "Port 111098104"
#property version   "1.00"

#include <Trade\Trade.mqh>
CTrade trade;

input double RiskPercent   = 0.5;
input string Symbol2       = "USDCHF";
input int    LookbackH4    = 50;  // Z-Score lookback (H4)
input double ZScore_Entry  = 2.0;
input double ZScore_Exit   = 0.0;
input double ZScore_SL     = 3.5;
input int    MaxHoldDays   = 5;
input int    Magic         = 111098104;

int h_close1_h4, h_close2_h4;

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
int OnInit()
{
   h_close1_h4 = iClose(_Symbol,  PERIOD_H4, 0); // placeholder — use CopyClose
   h_close2_h4 = iClose(Symbol2,  PERIOD_H4, 0);
   trade.SetExpertMagicNumber(Magic);
   return INIT_SUCCEEDED;
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void OnTick()
{
   static datetime lastBar = 0;
   datetime currentBar = iTime(_Symbol, PERIOD_H1, 0);
   if(currentBar == lastBar) return;
   lastBar = currentBar;

   CheckPairExit();

   //--- Layer 1: D1 Correlation Check
   double corr = CalcCorrelation(PERIOD_D1, 20);
   if(corr > -0.7) return; // Correlation ไม่ negative เพียงพอ → หยุด

   //--- Layer 2: H4 Z-Score Calculation
   double zScore = CalcZScore(PERIOD_H4, LookbackH4);
   if(zScore == EMPTY_VALUE) return;

   int currentPosCount = CountPositions();
   if(currentPosCount > 0) return;

   double ask1 = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_ASK);
   double bid1 = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_BID);
   double ask2 = SymbolInfoDouble(Symbol2, SYMBOL_ASK);
   double bid2 = SymbolInfoDouble(Symbol2, SYMBOL_BID);
   double lots = 0.01; // ใช้ Fixed lot เพราะ Pair Trade ต้องสมดุล

   //--- Layer 3: H1 Entry Trigger
   if(zScore < -ZScore_Entry)
   {
      // Spread กว้างผิดปกติ (EURUSD ถูก USDCHF แพง) → BUY EURUSD + SELL USDCHF
      trade.Buy(lots,  _Symbol, ask1, 0, 0, "AD_BUY1");
      trade.Sell(lots, Symbol2, bid2, 0, 0, "AD_SELL2");
   }
   else if(zScore > ZScore_Entry)
   {
      // Spread แคบผิดปกติ → SELL EURUSD + BUY USDCHF
      trade.Sell(lots, _Symbol, bid1, 0, 0, "AD_SELL1");
      trade.Buy(lots,  Symbol2, ask2, 0, 0, "AD_BUY2");
   }
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void CheckPairExit()
{
   if(CountPositions() == 0) return;

   double zScore = CalcZScore(PERIOD_H4, LookbackH4);

   // TP: Z-Score กลับมาที่ 0
   bool exitTP = MathAbs(zScore) < 0.3;
   // SL: Z-Score เลยขีดจำกัด
   bool exitSL = MathAbs(zScore) > ZScore_SL;
   // Time Exit
   bool exitTime = false;
   for(int i = 0; i < PositionsTotal(); i++)
   {
      ulong ticket = PositionGetTicket(i);
      if(!PositionSelectByTicket(ticket)) continue;
      if(PositionGetInteger(POSITION_MAGIC) != Magic) continue;
      datetime openTime = (datetime)PositionGetInteger(POSITION_TIME);
      if((TimeCurrent() - openTime) >= MaxHoldDays * 86400) exitTime = true;
   }

   if(exitTP || exitSL || exitTime)
   {
      for(int i = PositionsTotal()-1; i >= 0; i--)
      {
         ulong ticket = PositionGetTicket(i);
         if(!PositionSelectByTicket(ticket)) continue;
         if(PositionGetInteger(POSITION_MAGIC) != Magic) continue;
         trade.PositionClose(ticket);
      }
   }
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
double CalcZScore(ENUM_TIMEFRAMES tf, int period)
{
   double close1[], close2[];
   ArraySetAsSeries(close1, true);
   ArraySetAsSeries(close2, true);
   if(CopyClose(_Symbol, tf, 0, period+1, close1) < period) return EMPTY_VALUE;
   if(CopyClose(Symbol2, tf, 0, period+1, close2) < period) return EMPTY_VALUE;

   double spread[];
   ArrayResize(spread, period);
   for(int i = 0; i < period; i++)
      spread[i] = close1[i] - (1.0 - close2[i]); // Spread formula

   double mean = 0, stdDev = 0;
   for(int i = 0; i < period; i++) mean += spread[i];
   mean /= period;
   for(int i = 0; i < period; i++) stdDev += MathPow(spread[i] - mean, 2);
   stdDev = MathSqrt(stdDev / period);

   if(stdDev == 0) return 0;
   return (spread[0] - mean) / stdDev;
}

double CalcCorrelation(ENUM_TIMEFRAMES tf, int period)
{
   double close1[], close2[];
   ArraySetAsSeries(close1, true);
   ArraySetAsSeries(close2, true);
   CopyClose(_Symbol, tf, 0, period, close1);
   CopyClose(Symbol2, tf, 0, period, close2);

   double mean1 = 0, mean2 = 0;
   for(int i = 0; i < period; i++) { mean1 += close1[i]; mean2 += close2[i]; }
   mean1 /= period; mean2 /= period;

   double cov = 0, std1 = 0, std2 = 0;
   for(int i = 0; i < period; i++)
   {
      cov  += (close1[i]-mean1) * (close2[i]-mean2);
      std1 += MathPow(close1[i]-mean1, 2);
      std2 += MathPow(close2[i]-mean2, 2);
   }
   if(std1 == 0 || std2 == 0) return 0;
   return cov / MathSqrt(std1 * std2);
}

int CountPositions()
{
   int count = 0;
   for(int i = 0; i < PositionsTotal(); i++)
   {
      ulong ticket = PositionGetTicket(i);
      if(!PositionSelectByTicket(ticket)) continue;
      if(PositionGetInteger(POSITION_MAGIC) == Magic) count++;
   }
   return count;
}
```

## ⚔️ PORT 6 — 117030969 | Golden Ember
### MTF S/R Range Trading — Pivot (M (Monthly) → W1 → H1)

```mql5
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| EA_GoldenEmber_117030969.mq5                                     |
//| Strategy: MTF S/R Pivot Range Trading                            |
//| HTF: MN (Monthly Pivot) → W1 (Weekly Pivot) → H1 (Entry CCI)   |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
#property copyright "Port 117030969"
#property version   "1.00"

#include <Trade\Trade.mqh>
CTrade trade;

input double RiskPercent  = 0.8;
input int    ATR_Period   = 14;
input int    RSI_Period   = 14;
input int    CCI_Period   = 20;
input double ZonePips     = 0.3; // ATR Multiplier สำหรับ Zone รอบ Pivot
input int    Magic        = 117030969;

int h_rsi_h1, h_cci_h1, h_atr_h1;

struct PivotLevels { double PP, R1, R2, S1, S2; };

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
int OnInit()
{
   h_rsi_h1 = iRSI(_Symbol, PERIOD_H1, RSI_Period, PRICE_CLOSE);
   h_cci_h1 = iCCI(_Symbol, PERIOD_H1, CCI_Period, PRICE_TYPICAL);
   h_atr_h1 = iATR(_Symbol, PERIOD_H1, ATR_Period);

   trade.SetExpertMagicNumber(Magic);
   return INIT_SUCCEEDED;
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void OnTick()
{
   static datetime lastBar = 0;
   datetime currentBar = iTime(_Symbol, PERIOD_H1, 0);
   if(currentBar == lastBar) return;
   lastBar = currentBar;

   if(PositionsTotal() > 0) return;

   //--- Layer 1: Monthly Pivot — Macro S/R
   PivotLevels monthlyPivot = CalcPivot(PERIOD_MN1, 1);
   //--- Layer 2: Weekly Pivot — Key Trading Levels
   PivotLevels weeklyPivot  = CalcPivot(PERIOD_W1,  1);

   double price = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_BID);
   double atr_val[2];
   CopyBuffer(h_atr_h1, 0, 0, 2, atr_val);
   double atr  = atr_val[0];
   double zone = atr * ZonePips;

   //--- Layer 3: H1 CCI + RSI Entry
   double rsi[2], cci[2];
   CopyBuffer(h_rsi_h1, 0, 0, 2, rsi);
   CopyBuffer(h_cci_h1, 0, 0, 2, cci);

   bool rsi_oversold   = rsi[0] < 40;
   bool rsi_overbought = rsi[0] > 60;
   bool cci_buy        = cci[0] < -80;
   bool cci_sell       = cci[0] > 80;

   // Check ใกล้ Weekly S1/S2 (Buy Zone)
   bool nearWeeklyS1 = MathAbs(price - weeklyPivot.S1) <= zone;
   bool nearWeeklyS2 = MathAbs(price - weeklyPivot.S2) <= zone;
   bool nearWeeklyR1 = MathAbs(price - weeklyPivot.R1) <= zone;
   bool nearWeeklyR2 = MathAbs(price - weeklyPivot.R2) <= zone;

   // Monthly Pivot ยืนยัน: ราคาต้องยืนเหนือ Monthly S1 เพื่อ BUY
   bool monthlyBullOK = price > monthlyPivot.S1;
   bool monthlyBearOK = price < monthlyPivot.R1;

   if((nearWeeklyS1 || nearWeeklyS2) && rsi_oversold && cci_buy && monthlyBullOK)
   {
      double ask = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_ASK);
      double sl  = weeklyPivot.S2 - atr * 0.5;
      double tp  = nearWeeklyS1 ? weeklyPivot.PP : weeklyPivot.S1;
      trade.Buy(CalcLots(ask - sl), _Symbol, ask, sl, tp, "GE_BUY");
   }
   else if((nearWeeklyR1 || nearWeeklyR2) && rsi_overbought && cci_sell && monthlyBearOK)
   {
      double bid = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_BID);
      double sl  = weeklyPivot.R2 + atr * 0.5;
      double tp  = nearWeeklyR1 ? weeklyPivot.PP : weeklyPivot.R1;
      trade.Sell(CalcLots(sl - bid), _Symbol, bid, sl, tp, "GE_SELL");
   }
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
PivotLevels CalcPivot(ENUM_TIMEFRAMES tf, int shift)
{
   PivotLevels pv;
   double hi[], lo[], cl[];
   ArraySetAsSeries(hi, true);
   ArraySetAsSeries(lo, true);
   ArraySetAsSeries(cl, true);
   CopyHigh(_Symbol,  tf, shift, 1, hi);
   CopyLow(_Symbol,   tf, shift, 1, lo);
   CopyClose(_Symbol, tf, shift, 1, cl);

   pv.PP = (hi[0] + lo[0] + cl[0]) / 3.0;
   pv.R1 = 2 * pv.PP - lo[0];
   pv.R2 = pv.PP + (hi[0] - lo[0]);
   pv.S1 = 2 * pv.PP - hi[0];
   pv.S2 = pv.PP - (hi[0] - lo[0]);
   return pv;
}

double CalcLots(double slPoints)
{
   double balance    = AccountInfoDouble(ACCOUNT_BALANCE);
   double riskAmount = balance * RiskPercent / 100.0;
   double tickValue  = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_TRADE_TICK_VALUE);
   double tickSize   = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_TRADE_TICK_SIZE);
   double lotStep    = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_VOLUME_STEP);
   double minLot     = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_VOLUME_MIN);
   double maxLot     = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_VOLUME_MAX);
   if(slPoints <= 0 || tickValue <= 0) return minLot;
   double lots = riskAmount / (slPoints / tickSize * tickValue);
   lots = MathFloor(lots / lotStep) * lotStep;
   return MathMax(minLot, MathMin(maxLot, lots));
}
```

## 🔥 PORT 7 — 141050699 | Twin Flares
### MTF High-Frequency Scalping (H1 → M15 → M1)

```mql5
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| EA_TwinFlares_141050699.mq5                                      |
//| Strategy: MTF Scalping                                           |
//| HTF: H1 (Trend+VWAP) → M15 (Direction) → M1 (EMA Cross Entry)  |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
#property copyright "Port 141050699"
#property version   "1.00"

#include <Trade\Trade.mqh>
CTrade trade;

input double RiskPercent   = 0.5;
input int    TP_Pips       = 4;
input int    SL_Pips       = 6;
input int    MaxTradesDay  = 10;
input int    Magic         = 141050699;

// Session Filter: London + NY Overlap (GMT)
input int    SessionStart  = 13; // 13:00 GMT
input int    SessionEnd    = 17; // 17:00 GMT

int h_ema8_m1,  h_ema21_m1;
int h_ema8_m15, h_ema21_m15;
int h_ema50_h1;

int tradesToday = 0;
datetime lastDayCheck = 0;

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
int OnInit()
{
   h_ema8_m1   = iMA(_Symbol, PERIOD_M1,  8,  0, MODE_EMA, PRICE_CLOSE);
   h_ema21_m1  = iMA(_Symbol, PERIOD_M1,  21, 0, MODE_EMA, PRICE_CLOSE);
   h_ema8_m15  = iMA(_Symbol, PERIOD_M15, 8,  0, MODE_EMA, PRICE_CLOSE);
   h_ema21_m15 = iMA(_Symbol, PERIOD_M15, 21, 0, MODE_EMA, PRICE_CLOSE);
   h_ema50_h1  = iMA(_Symbol, PERIOD_H1,  50, 0, MODE_EMA, PRICE_CLOSE);

   trade.SetExpertMagicNumber(Magic);
   return INIT_SUCCEEDED;
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void OnTick()
{
   static datetime lastBar = 0;
   datetime currentBar = iTime(_Symbol, PERIOD_M1, 0);
   if(currentBar == lastBar) return;
   lastBar = currentBar;

   // Reset daily trade count
   MqlDateTime dt; TimeToStruct(TimeCurrent(), dt);
   datetime today = StringToTime(StringFormat("%04d.%02d.%02d 00:00", dt.year, dt.mon, dt.day));
   if(today != lastDayCheck) { tradesToday = 0; lastDayCheck = today; }
   if(tradesToday >= MaxTradesDay) return;

   // Session Filter
   if(!IsSessionTime()) return;
   if(PositionsTotal() > 0) return;

   //--- Layer 1: H1 — Macro Direction (EMA50 + VWAP approximation)
   double ema50_h1[2];
   CopyBuffer(h_ema50_h1, 0, 0, 2, ema50_h1);
   double price   = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_BID);
   bool h1_bull   = price > ema50_h1[0];
   bool h1_bear   = price < ema50_h1[0];

   //--- Layer 2: M15 — Intermediate Direction
   double ema8_m15[3], ema21_m15[3];
   CopyBuffer(h_ema8_m15,  0, 0, 3, ema8_m15);
   CopyBuffer(h_ema21_m15, 0, 0, 3, ema21_m15);
   bool m15_bull = ema8_m15[0] > ema21_m15[0];
   bool m15_bear = ema8_m15[0] < ema21_m15[0];

   //--- Layer 3: M1 — Entry (EMA Cross)
   double ema8_m1[3], ema21_m1[3];
   CopyBuffer(h_ema8_m1,  0, 0, 3, ema8_m1);
   CopyBuffer(h_ema21_m1, 0, 0, 3, ema21_m1);

   bool m1_cross_up   = ema8_m1[1] > ema21_m1[1] && ema8_m1[2] <= ema21_m1[2];
   bool m1_cross_down = ema8_m1[1] < ema21_m1[1] && ema8_m1[2] >= ema21_m1[2];

   double point = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_POINT);
   int digits   = (int)SymbolInfoInteger(_Symbol, SYMBOL_DIGITS);
   double pipVal = (digits == 5 || digits == 3) ? point * 10 : point;

   if(h1_bull && m15_bull && m1_cross_up)
   {
      double ask = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_ASK);
      double sl  = ask - SL_Pips * pipVal;
      double tp  = ask + TP_Pips * pipVal;
      double lots = CalcLots(ask - sl);
      if(trade.Buy(lots, _Symbol, ask, sl, tp, "TF_BUY")) tradesToday++;
   }
   else if(h1_bear && m15_bear && m1_cross_down)
   {
      double bid = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_BID);
      double sl  = bid + SL_Pips * pipVal;
      double tp  = bid - TP_Pips * pipVal;
      double lots = CalcLots(sl - bid);
      if(trade.Sell(lots, _Symbol, bid, sl, tp, "TF_SELL")) tradesToday++;
   }
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
bool IsSessionTime()
{
   MqlDateTime dt;
   TimeToStruct(TimeGMT(), dt);
   return (dt.hour >= SessionStart && dt.hour < SessionEnd);
}

double CalcLots(double slPoints)
{
   double balance    = AccountInfoDouble(ACCOUNT_BALANCE);
   double riskAmount = balance * RiskPercent / 100.0;
   double tickValue  = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_TRADE_TICK_VALUE);
   double tickSize   = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_TRADE_TICK_SIZE);
   double lotStep    = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_VOLUME_STEP);
   double minLot     = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_VOLUME_MIN);
   double maxLot     = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_VOLUME_MAX);
   if(slPoints <= 0 || tickValue <= 0) return minLot;
   double lots = riskAmount / (slPoints / tickSize * tickValue);
   lots = MathFloor(lots / lotStep) * lotStep;
   return MathMax(minLot, MathMin(maxLot, lots));
}
```
