## 🔥 PORT 8 — 159503454 | Blazing Arrow
### MTF Momentum Scalping + Partial Close (H4 → M15 → M5)

```mql5
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| EA_BlazingArrow_159503454.mq5                                    |
//| Strategy: MTF Momentum Scalping + Partial Close                  |
//| HTF: H4 (Trend) → M15 (Momentum Dir) → M5 (Entry Engulfing)    |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
#property copyright "Port 159503454"
#property version   "1.00"

#include <Trade\Trade.mqh>
CTrade trade;

input double RiskPercent     = 1.0;
input int    TP_Pips         = 12;
input int    SL_Pips         = 8;
input int    PartialClose_Pips = 6;
input double PartialClose_Pct  = 50.0;
input int    Magic           = 159503454;

int h_ema50_h4;
int h_rsi3_m15, h_mom10_m15;
int h_ema50_m5;

bool partialDone = false;

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
int OnInit()
{
   h_ema50_h4  = iMA(_Symbol, PERIOD_H4, 50, 0, MODE_EMA, PRICE_CLOSE);
   h_rsi3_m15  = iRSI(_Symbol, PERIOD_M15, 3, PRICE_CLOSE);
   h_mom10_m15 = iMomentum(_Symbol, PERIOD_M15, 10, PRICE_CLOSE);
   h_ema50_m5  = iMA(_Symbol, PERIOD_M5, 50, 0, MODE_EMA, PRICE_CLOSE);

   trade.SetExpertMagicNumber(Magic);
   return INIT_SUCCEEDED;
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void OnTick()
{
   static datetime lastBar = 0;
   datetime currentBar = iTime(_Symbol, PERIOD_M5, 0);
   if(currentBar == lastBar) return;
   lastBar = currentBar;

   if(PositionsTotal() > 0) { ManagePartialClose(); return; }
   partialDone = false;

   //--- Layer 1: H4 — Trend Direction
   double ema50_h4[2];
   CopyBuffer(h_ema50_h4, 0, 0, 2, ema50_h4);
   double price  = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_BID);
   bool h4_bull  = price > ema50_h4[0];
   bool h4_bear  = price < ema50_h4[0];

   //--- Layer 2: M15 — Momentum Confirmation
   double rsi3[2], mom10[3];
   CopyBuffer(h_rsi3_m15,  0, 0, 2, rsi3);
   CopyBuffer(h_mom10_m15, 0, 0, 3, mom10);
   bool m15_bull_mom = rsi3[0] > 50 && mom10[0] > 100 && mom10[1] <= 100;
   bool m15_bear_mom = rsi3[0] < 50 && mom10[0] < 100 && mom10[1] >= 100;

   //--- Layer 3: M5 — Engulfing Candle + EMA50
   double ema50_m5[2];
   CopyBuffer(h_ema50_m5, 0, 0, 2, ema50_m5);

   double open1  = iOpen(_Symbol,  PERIOD_M5, 1);
   double close1 = iClose(_Symbol, PERIOD_M5, 1);
   double open2  = iOpen(_Symbol,  PERIOD_M5, 2);
   double close2 = iClose(_Symbol, PERIOD_M5, 2);
   bool bullEngulf = close1 > open1 && close2 < open2 && close1 > open2 && open1 < close2;
   bool bearEngulf = close1 < open1 && close2 > open2 && close1 < open2 && open1 > close2;

   double point  = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_POINT);
   int digits    = (int)SymbolInfoInteger(_Symbol, SYMBOL_DIGITS);
   double pipVal = (digits == 5 || digits == 3) ? point * 10 : point;

   if(h4_bull && m15_bull_mom && bullEngulf && price > ema50_m5[0])
   {
      double ask = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_ASK);
      double sl  = ask - SL_Pips * pipVal;
      double tp  = ask + TP_Pips * pipVal;
      trade.Buy(CalcLots(ask - sl), _Symbol, ask, sl, tp, "BA_BUY");
   }
   else if(h4_bear && m15_bear_mom && bearEngulf && price < ema50_m5[0])
   {
      double bid = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_BID);
      double sl  = bid + SL_Pips * pipVal;
      double tp  = bid - TP_Pips * pipVal;
      trade.Sell(CalcLots(sl - bid), _Symbol, bid, sl, tp, "BA_SELL");
   }
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void ManagePartialClose()
{
   if(partialDone) return;
   double point  = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_POINT);
   int digits    = (int)SymbolInfoInteger(_Symbol, SYMBOL_DIGITS);
   double pipVal = (digits == 5 || digits == 3) ? point * 10 : point;

   for(int i = PositionsTotal()-1; i >= 0; i--)
   {
      ulong ticket = PositionGetTicket(i);
      if(!PositionSelectByTicket(ticket)) continue;
      if(PositionGetInteger(POSITION_MAGIC) != Magic) continue;

      double openPrice = PositionGetDouble(POSITION_PRICE_OPEN);
      double volume    = PositionGetDouble(POSITION_VOLUME);
      double currentPrice = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_BID);
      long   posType   = PositionGetInteger(POSITION_TYPE);

      double profitPips = 0;
      if(posType == POSITION_TYPE_BUY)
         profitPips = (currentPrice - openPrice) / pipVal;
      else
         profitPips = (openPrice - currentPrice) / pipVal;

      if(profitPips >= PartialClose_Pips)
      {
         double closeLots = MathFloor(volume * PartialClose_Pct / 100.0 / 0.01) * 0.01;
         double minLot = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_VOLUME_MIN);
         if(closeLots >= minLot)
         {
            trade.PositionClosePartial(ticket, closeLots);
            // Move SL to Breakeven
            trade.PositionModify(ticket, openPrice, PositionGetDouble(POSITION_TP));
            partialDone = true;
         }
      }
   }
}

double CalcLots(double slPoints)
{
   double balance    = AccountInfoDouble(ACCOUNT_BALANCE);
   double riskAmount = balance * RiskPercent / 100.0;
   double tickValue  = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_TRADE_TICK_VALUE);
   double tickSize   = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_TRADE_TICK_SIZE);
   double lotStep    = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_VOLUME_STEP);
   double minLot     = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_VOLUME_MIN);
   double maxLot     = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_VOLUME_MAX);
   if(slPoints <= 0 || tickValue <= 0) return minLot;
   double lots = riskAmount / (slPoints / tickSize * tickValue);
   lots = MathFloor(lots / lotStep) * lotStep;
   return MathMax(minLot, MathMin(maxLot, lots));
}
```

## 🔥 PORT 9 — 159149771 | Solar Eclipse
### MTF News Straddle (D1 → H1 → M15)

```mql5
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| EA_SolarEclipse_159149771.mq5                                    |
//| Strategy: MTF News Straddle                                      |
//| HTF: D1 (Daily Bias) → H1 (Pre-News Range) → M15 (Execution)   |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
#property copyright "Port 159149771"
#property version   "1.00"

#include <Trade\Trade.mqh>
CTrade trade;

input double RiskPercent     = 1.0;
input int    ATR_Period      = 14;
input double ATR_Entry_Mult  = 0.8;
input double ATR_TP_Mult     = 2.0;
input double ATR_SL_Mult     = 1.0;
input int    NewsMinutesBefore = 15; // วาง Pending กี่นาทีก่อนข่าว
input int    TimeStop_Minutes  = 30; // ปิด Position ถ้าไม่ถึง TP ใน X นาที
input double MaxSpreadMult     = 3.0;
input int    MaxEventsPerDay   = 2;
input int    Magic             = 159149771;

// *** Manual News Time Input (ตั้งเวลาข่าวด้วยตนเอง) ***
input string NewsTime1 = "08:30"; // เวลาข่าวที่ 1 (GMT)
input string NewsTime2 = "14:00"; // เวลาข่าวที่ 2 (GMT)

int h_atr_m15, h_atr_h1;
int h_ema200_d1;

int eventsToday = 0;
datetime lastDayReset = 0;
datetime entryTime1 = 0, entryTime2 = 0;

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
int OnInit()
{
   h_atr_m15   = iATR(_Symbol, PERIOD_M15, ATR_Period);
   h_atr_h1    = iATR(_Symbol, PERIOD_H1,  ATR_Period);
   h_ema200_d1 = iMA(_Symbol,  PERIOD_D1, 200, 0, MODE_EMA, PRICE_CLOSE);

   trade.SetExpertMagicNumber(Magic);
   return INIT_SUCCEEDED;
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void OnTick()
{
   // Reset daily counter
   MqlDateTime dt; TimeToStruct(TimeGMT(), dt);
   datetime today = StringToTime(StringFormat("%04d.%02d.%02d 00:00", dt.year, dt.mon, dt.day));
   if(today != lastDayReset) { eventsToday = 0; lastDayReset = today; entryTime1 = 0; entryTime2 = 0; }

   CheckTimeStop();
   DeleteExpiredPendings();

   if(eventsToday >= MaxEventsPerDay) return;

   // สร้าง News Time สำหรับวันนี้
   if(entryTime1 == 0) entryTime1 = StringToTime(StringFormat("%04d.%02d.%02d %s", dt.year, dt.mon, dt.day, NewsTime1));
   if(entryTime2 == 0) entryTime2 = StringToTime(StringFormat("%04d.%02d.%02d %s", dt.year, dt.mon, dt.day, NewsTime2));

   datetime gmtNow   = TimeGMT();
   datetime triggerT1 = entryTime1 - NewsMinutesBefore * 60;
   datetime triggerT2 = entryTime2 - NewsMinutesBefore * 60;

   if((gmtNow >= triggerT1 && gmtNow < triggerT1 + 60) ||
      (gmtNow >= triggerT2 && gmtNow < triggerT2 + 60))
   {
      if(OrdersTotal() == 0 && PositionsTotal() == 0)
         PlaceStraddle();
   }
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void PlaceStraddle()
{
   // Layer 1: D1 Bias
   double ema200[2];
   CopyBuffer(h_ema200_d1, 0, 0, 2, ema200);
   double price = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_BID);
   bool d1_bull = price > ema200[0];

   // Layer 2: H1 ATR for Range
   double atr_h1[2];
   CopyBuffer(h_atr_h1, 0, 0, 2, atr_h1);

   // Layer 3: M15 ATR for Precise Entry
   double atr_m15[2];
   CopyBuffer(h_atr_m15, 0, 0, 2, atr_m15);
   double atr = atr_m15[0];

   // Spread Filter
   double spread = SymbolInfoInteger(_Symbol, SYMBOL_SPREAD) * SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_POINT);
   double avgSpread = atr * 0.05; // Approx normal spread
   if(spread > avgSpread * MaxSpreadMult) return;

   double ask = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_ASK);
   double bid = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_BID);

   double buyEntry  = ask + atr * ATR_Entry_Mult;
   double sellEntry = bid - atr * ATR_Entry_Mult;
   double buyTP     = buyEntry  + atr * ATR_TP_Mult;
   double buysSL    = buyEntry  - atr * ATR_SL_Mult;
   double sellTP    = sellEntry - atr * ATR_TP_Mult;
   double sellSL    = sellEntry + atr * ATR_SL_Mult;
   double lots      = CalcLots(atr * ATR_SL_Mult);

   // D1 Bias: ถ้า Bullish วาง BuyStop ก่อน / ถ้า Bearish วาง SellStop ก่อน
   if(d1_bull || true) // Straddle ทั้งคู่เสมอ แต่ D1 กำหนด Lot size
   {
      double buyLots  = d1_bull ? lots * 1.2 : lots;
      double sellLots = d1_bull ? lots : lots * 1.2;
      trade.BuyStop(buyLots,  buyEntry,  _Symbol, buysSL,  buyTP,  ORDER_TIME_GTC, 0, "SE_BUYSTOP");
      trade.SellStop(sellLots, sellEntry, _Symbol, sellSL, sellTP, ORDER_TIME_GTC, 0, "SE_SELLSTOP");
      eventsToday++;
   }
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void CheckTimeStop()
{
   datetime now = TimeCurrent();
   for(int i = PositionsTotal()-1; i >= 0; i--)
   {
      ulong ticket = PositionGetTicket(i);
      if(!PositionSelectByTicket(ticket)) continue;
      if(PositionGetInteger(POSITION_MAGIC) != Magic) continue;
      datetime openTime = (datetime)PositionGetInteger(POSITION_TIME);
      if((now - openTime) >= TimeStop_Minutes * 60)
         trade.PositionClose(ticket);
   }
}

void DeleteExpiredPendings()
{
   for(int i = OrdersTotal()-1; i >= 0; i--)
   {
      ulong ticket = OrderGetTicket(i);
      if(!OrderSelect(ticket)) continue;
      if(OrderGetInteger(ORDER_MAGIC) != Magic) continue;
      // ลบ Pending ที่เก่ากว่า 1 ชั่วโมง
      datetime placeTime = (datetime)OrderGetInteger(ORDER_TIME_SETUP);
      if((TimeCurrent() - placeTime) > 3600)
         trade.OrderDelete(ticket);
   }
}

double CalcLots(double slPoints)
{
   double balance    = AccountInfoDouble(ACCOUNT_BALANCE);
   double riskAmount = balance * RiskPercent / 100.0;
   double tickValue  = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_TRADE_TICK_VALUE);
   double tickSize   = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_TRADE_TICK_SIZE);
   double lotStep    = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_VOLUME_STEP);
   double minLot     = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_VOLUME_MIN);
   double maxLot     = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_VOLUME_MAX);
   if(slPoints <= 0 || tickValue <= 0) return minLot;
   double lots = riskAmount / (slPoints / tickSize * tickValue);
   lots = MathFloor(lots / lotStep) * lotStep;
   return MathMax(minLot, MathMin(maxLot, lots));
}
```

## 🔥 PORT 10 — 183977997 | Dragon's Wrath
### MTF Aggressive Trend Catcher + Supertrend Trailing (W1 → D1 → H4)

```mql5
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| EA_DragonsWrath_183977997.mq5                                    |
//| Strategy: MTF Aggressive Trend + Supertrend Trailing             |
//| HTF: W1 (Macro Trend) → D1 (ADX Confirm) → H4 (Supertrend)     |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
#property copyright "Port 183977997"
#property version   "1.00"

#include <Trade\Trade.mqh>
CTrade trade;

input double RiskPercent      = 1.5;
input int    ATR_Period       = 14;
input double ST_Multiplier    = 3.0;  // Supertrend ATR Multiplier
input int    ST_Period        = 10;   // Supertrend ATR Period
input int    ADX_Period       = 14;
input double ADX_Min          = 30.0;
input double HardSL_ATR_Mult  = 2.0;
input int    Magic            = 183977997;

int h_ema50_w1, h_ema200_w1;
int h_adx_d1;
int h_atr_h4, h_atr_d1;

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
int OnInit()
{
   h_ema50_w1  = iMA(_Symbol, PERIOD_W1, 50,  0, MODE_EMA, PRICE_CLOSE);
   h_ema200_w1 = iMA(_Symbol, PERIOD_W1, 200, 0, MODE_EMA, PRICE_CLOSE);
   h_adx_d1    = iADX(_Symbol, PERIOD_D1, ADX_Period);
   h_atr_h4    = iATR(_Symbol, PERIOD_H4, ATR_Period);
   h_atr_d1    = iATR(_Symbol, PERIOD_D1, ATR_Period);

   trade.SetExpertMagicNumber(Magic);
   return INIT_SUCCEEDED;
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void OnTick()
{
   static datetime lastBar = 0;
   datetime currentBar = iTime(_Symbol, PERIOD_H4, 0);
   if(currentBar == lastBar) return;
   lastBar = currentBar;

   if(PositionsTotal() > 0) { ManageSupertrendTrail(); return; }

   //--- Layer 1: W1 Macro Trend
   double ema50_w1[2], ema200_w1[2];
   CopyBuffer(h_ema50_w1,  0, 0, 2, ema50_w1);
   CopyBuffer(h_ema200_w1, 0, 0, 2, ema200_w1);
   bool w1_bull = ema50_w1[0] > ema200_w1[0];
   bool w1_bear = ema50_w1[0] < ema200_w1[0];

   //--- Layer 2: D1 ADX Strength
   double adx_d1[2];
   CopyBuffer(h_adx_d1, MAIN_LINE, 0, 2, adx_d1);
   if(adx_d1[0] < ADX_Min) return; // Trend ไม่แข็งพอ

   //--- Layer 3: H4 Supertrend Signal
   double atr_h4[3];
   CopyBuffer(h_atr_h4, 0, 0, 3, atr_h4);
   double st_signal = CalcSupertrendSignal(atr_h4[1]);

   double atr_d1[2];
   CopyBuffer(h_atr_d1, 0, 0, 2, atr_d1);

   if(w1_bull && st_signal > 0) // Supertrend Bullish Flip
   {
      double ask = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_ASK);
      double sl  = ask - atr_d1[0] * HardSL_ATR_Mult;
      double lots = CalcLots(ask - sl);
      trade.Buy(lots, _Symbol, ask, sl, 0, "DW_BUY"); // No fixed TP
   }
   else if(w1_bear && st_signal < 0)
   {
      double bid = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_BID);
      double sl  = bid + atr_d1[0] * HardSL_ATR_Mult;
      double lots = CalcLots(sl - bid);
      trade.Sell(lots, _Symbol, bid, sl, 0, "DW_SELL");
   }
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
// Supertrend Signal Calculation (+1 = Bullish Flip, -1 = Bearish Flip, 0 = No change)
double CalcSupertrendSignal(double atr)
{
   double high1 = iHigh(_Symbol, PERIOD_H4, 1);
   double low1  = iLow(_Symbol,  PERIOD_H4, 1);
   double high2 = iHigh(_Symbol, PERIOD_H4, 2);
   double low2  = iLow(_Symbol,  PERIOD_H4, 2);
   double close1 = iClose(_Symbol, PERIOD_H4, 1);
   double close2 = iClose(_Symbol, PERIOD_H4, 2);

   double hl2_1 = (high1 + low1) / 2.0;
   double hl2_2 = (high2 + low2) / 2.0;
   double upperBand1 = hl2_1 + ST_Multiplier * atr;
   double lowerBand1 = hl2_1 - ST_Multiplier * atr;

   static double prevUpperBand = 0, prevLowerBand = 0;
   static int prevTrend = 0;

   double finalUpper = (prevUpperBand == 0) ? upperBand1 : ((upperBand1 < prevUpperBand || close2 > prevUpperBand) ? upperBand1 : prevUpperBand);
   double finalLower = (prevLowerBand == 0) ? lowerBand1 : ((lowerBand1 > prevLowerBand || close2 < prevLowerBand) ? lowerBand1 : prevLowerBand);

   int trend = (close1 > finalUpper) ? 1 : (close1 < finalLower ? -1 : prevTrend);
   double signal = 0;
   if(trend == 1 && prevTrend != 1) signal = 1;  // Bullish Flip
   if(trend == -1 && prevTrend != -1) signal = -1; // Bearish Flip

   prevUpperBand = finalUpper;
   prevLowerBand = finalLower;
   prevTrend = trend;
   return signal;
}

void ManageSupertrendTrail()
{
   double atr_h4[2];
   CopyBuffer(h_atr_h4, 0, 0, 2, atr_h4);
   double adx_d1[2];
   CopyBuffer(h_adx_d1, MAIN_LINE, 0, 2, adx_d1);

   // ถ้า ADX ดิ่งต่ำกว่า 20 → เทรนด์อ่อนแรง ปิดทันที
   if(adx_d1[0] < 20)
   {
      for(int i = PositionsTotal()-1; i >= 0; i--)
      {
         ulong ticket = PositionGetTicket(i);
         if(!PositionSelectByTicket(ticket)) continue;
         if(PositionGetInteger(POSITION_MAGIC) != Magic) continue;
         trade.PositionClose(ticket);
      }
      return;
   }

   // Supertrend Trailing
   double high_h4 = iHigh(_Symbol, PERIOD_H4, 1);
   double low_h4  = iLow(_Symbol,  PERIOD_H4, 1);
   double trailBuy  = ((high_h4 + low_h4) / 2.0) - ST_Multiplier * atr_h4[1];
   double trailSell = ((high_h4 + low_h4) / 2.0) + ST_Multiplier * atr_h4[1];

   for(int i = PositionsTotal()-1; i >= 0; i--)
   {
      ulong ticket = PositionGetTicket(i);
      if(!PositionSelectByTicket(ticket)) continue;
      if(PositionGetInteger(POSITION_MAGIC) != Magic) continue;

      double currentSL = PositionGetDouble(POSITION_SL);
      long   posType   = PositionGetInteger(POSITION_TYPE);
      double profit    = PositionGetDouble(POSITION_PROFIT);
      if(profit <= 0) continue;

      if(posType == POSITION_TYPE_BUY && trailBuy > currentSL)
         trade.PositionModify(ticket, trailBuy, 0);
      else if(posType == POSITION_TYPE_SELL && trailSell < currentSL)
         trade.PositionModify(ticket, trailSell, 0);
   }
}

double CalcLots(double slPoints)
{
   double balance    = AccountInfoDouble(ACCOUNT_BALANCE);
   double riskAmount = balance * RiskPercent / 100.0;
   double tickValue  = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_TRADE_TICK_VALUE);
   double tickSize   = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_TRADE_TICK_SIZE);
   double lotStep    = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_VOLUME_STEP);
   double minLot     = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_VOLUME_MIN);
   double maxLot     = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_VOLUME_MAX);
   if(slPoints <= 0 || tickValue <= 0) return minLot;
   double lots = riskAmount / (slPoints / tickSize * tickValue);
   lots = MathFloor(lots / lotStep) * lotStep;
   return MathMax(minLot, MathMin(maxLot, lots));
}
```

## 🔥 PORT 11 — 141058396 | Ember Strike
### MTF Session Overlap Trading (D1 → H1 → M30)

```mql5
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| EA_EmberStrike_141058396.mq5                                     |
//| Strategy: MTF Session Overlap Trading                            |
//| HTF: D1 (Daily Bias) → H1 (EMA Trend) → M30 (Entry Signal)     |
//| Session: London-NY Overlap 13:00-17:00 GMT                      |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
#property copyright "Port 141058396"
#property version   "1.00"

#include <Trade\Trade.mqh>
CTrade trade;

input double RiskPercent   = 1.0;
input int    TP_Pips       = 20;
input int    SL_Pips       = 10;
input int    SessionStart  = 13; // GMT
input int    SessionEnd    = 17; // GMT
input int    Magic         = 141058396;

int h_ema21_m30, h_ema50_m30;
int h_ema21_h1,  h_ema50_h1;
int h_rsi14_m30;
int h_ema200_d1;

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
int OnInit()
{
   h_ema21_m30  = iMA(_Symbol, PERIOD_M30, 21, 0, MODE_EMA, PRICE_CLOSE);
   h_ema50_m30  = iMA(_Symbol, PERIOD_M30, 50, 0, MODE_EMA, PRICE_CLOSE);
   h_ema21_h1   = iMA(_Symbol, PERIOD_H1,  21, 0, MODE_EMA, PRICE_CLOSE);
   h_ema50_h1   = iMA(_Symbol, PERIOD_H1,  50, 0, MODE_EMA, PRICE_CLOSE);
   h_rsi14_m30  = iRSI(_Symbol, PERIOD_M30, 14, PRICE_CLOSE);
   h_ema200_d1  = iMA(_Symbol, PERIOD_D1, 200, 0, MODE_EMA, PRICE_CLOSE);

   trade.SetExpertMagicNumber(Magic);
   return INIT_SUCCEEDED;
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void OnTick()
{
   static datetime lastBar = 0;
   datetime currentBar = iTime(_Symbol, PERIOD_M30, 0);
   if(currentBar == lastBar) return;
   lastBar = currentBar;

   // Force Close เมื่อ Session สิ้นสุด
   if(!IsSessionTime())
   {
      CloseAllPositions();
      return;
   }
   if(PositionsTotal() > 0) return;

   //--- Layer 1: D1 Daily Bias
   double ema200_d1[2];
   CopyBuffer(h_ema200_d1, 0, 0, 2, ema200_d1);
   double price  = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_BID);
   bool d1_bull  = price > ema200_d1[0];
   bool d1_bear  = price < ema200_d1[0];

   //--- Layer 2: H1 Intermediate Trend
   double ema21_h1[2], ema50_h1[2];
   CopyBuffer(h_ema21_h1, 0, 0, 2, ema21_h1);
   CopyBuffer(h_ema50_h1, 0, 0, 2, ema50_h1);
   bool h1_bull = ema21_h1[0] > ema50_h1[0];
   bool h1_bear = ema21_h1[0] < ema50_h1[0];

   //--- Layer 3: M30 Entry Signal
   double ema21_m30[3], ema50_m30[3], rsi_val[2];
   CopyBuffer(h_ema21_m30, 0, 0, 3, ema21_m30);
   CopyBuffer(h_ema50_m30, 0, 0, 3, ema50_m30);
   CopyBuffer(h_rsi14_m30, 0, 0, 2, rsi_val);

   bool m30_cross_up   = ema21_m30[1] > ema50_m30[1] && ema21_m30[2] <= ema50_m30[2];
   bool m30_cross_down = ema21_m30[1] < ema50_m30[1] && ema21_m30[2] >= ema50_m30[2];
   bool rsi_bull = rsi_val[0] > 50;
   bool rsi_bear = rsi_val[0] < 50;

   double point  = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_POINT);
   int digits    = (int)SymbolInfoInteger(_Symbol, SYMBOL_DIGITS);
   double pipVal = (digits == 5 || digits == 3) ? point * 10 : point;

   // ต้องผ่านทุก 3 Layer
   if(d1_bull && h1_bull && m30_cross_up && rsi_bull)
   {
      double ask = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_ASK);
      double sl  = ask - SL_Pips * pipVal;
      double tp  = ask + TP_Pips * pipVal;
      trade.Buy(CalcLots(ask - sl), _Symbol, ask, sl, tp, "ES_BUY");
   }
   else if(d1_bear && h1_bear && m30_cross_down && rsi_bear)
   {
      double bid = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_BID);
      double sl  = bid + SL_Pips * pipVal;
      double tp  = bid - TP_Pips * pipVal;
      trade.Sell(CalcLots(sl - bid), _Symbol, bid, sl, tp, "ES_SELL");
   }
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
bool IsSessionTime()
{
   MqlDateTime dt;
   TimeToStruct(TimeGMT(), dt);
   return (dt.hour >= SessionStart && dt.hour < SessionEnd);
}

void CloseAllPositions()
{
   for(int i = PositionsTotal()-1; i >= 0; i--)
   {
      ulong ticket = PositionGetTicket(i);
      if(!PositionSelectByTicket(ticket)) continue;
      if(PositionGetInteger(POSITION_MAGIC) != Magic) continue;
      trade.PositionClose(ticket);
   }
}

double CalcLots(double slPoints)
{
   double balance    = AccountInfoDouble(ACCOUNT_BALANCE);
   double riskAmount = balance * RiskPercent / 100.0;
   double tickValue  = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_TRADE_TICK_VALUE);
   double tickSize   = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_TRADE_TICK_SIZE);
   double lotStep    = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_VOLUME_STEP);
   double minLot     = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_VOLUME_MIN);
   double maxLot     = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_VOLUME_MAX);
   if(slPoints <= 0 || tickValue <= 0) return minLot;
   double lots = riskAmount / (slPoints / tickSize * tickValue);
   lots = MathFloor(lots / lotStep) * lotStep;
   return MathMax(minLot, MathMin(maxLot, lots));
}
```

| พอร์ต | HTF (กรอง) | MID (ยืนยัน) | LTF (Entry) |
|---|---|---|---|
| 141049900 | D1 EMA Cross | H4 EMA Align | H1 MACD+ADX |
| 146004104 | W1 BB Range | D1 BB Bias | H1 Grid+RSI |
| 193234160 | W1 EMA200 | D1 Donchian | H4 Pending+Vol |
| 159219430 | W1 BB Width | D1 ADX<25 | H1 RSI+BB+Stoch |

| 117030969 | Monthly Pivot | Weekly Pivot | H1 CCI+RSI |
| 141050699 | H1 EMA50 | M15 EMA Cross | M1 EMA+Volume |
| 159503454 | H4 EMA50 | M15 Momentum | M5 Engulfing |

| 141058396 | D1 EMA200 | H1 EMA21/50 | M30 Cross+RSI |

---

### ⚠️ คำแนะนำก่อน Deploy ครับบอส

1. **Backtest ก่อนทุก EA** ใน MT5 Strategy Tester อย่างน้อย 2 ปี
2. **Port 5 (Z-Score)** ต้องรันบน Chart EURUSD เท่านั้น และต้องมี USDCHF ใน Market Watch
3. **Port 9 (News)** ต้องตั้ง `NewsTime1` / `NewsTime2` ให้ตรงกับเวลาข่าว High Impact จริง
4. **Port 10 (Supertrend)** มี Static Variable — ถ้า Restart EA ต้อง Allow `prevTrend` reset ใหม่
5. **CalcLots** ใช้ `ACCOUNT_BALANCE` — แนะนำเปลี่ยนเป็น `ACCOUNT_EQUITY` ถ้าต้องการ Dynamic Risk

----- [user] -----
แต่ละ EA ที่เป็น Multi Timeframe ให้สามารถ configure ได้ด้วยว่าจะเอา TF ไหน Lot ที่เปิดให้เป็น Dynamic lot ได้ แล้วก็มีการ money management ได้ด้วย แต่ไม่เกิน 5 ไม้ ทุก EA

----- [assistant] -----
ผมจะเขียน EA ทั้ง 11 ตัวพร้อม Configurable MTF + Dynamic Lot + Money Management ครบถ้วนครับบอส

## 🔧 COMMON INCLUDE FILE — MTF_Base.mqh
### (ใช้ร่วมกันทุก EA — สร้างไฟล์นี้ใน MQL5/Include/)

```mql5
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| MTF_Base.mqh — Shared Functions for All 11 EAs                  |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
#ifndef MTF_BASE_MQH
#define MTF_BASE_MQH

#include <Trade\Trade.mqh>

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Money Management Structure                                        |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
struct MoneyManagement
{
   double   RiskPercent;       // % Risk per trade
   double   MaxDailyLossPct;   // Max daily loss % ของ Balance
   double   MaxDrawdownPct;    // Max total drawdown % ของ Balance
   int      MaxPositions;      // Max open positions (hard cap = 5)
   double   MinLot;            // Min lot override
   double   MaxLot;            // Max lot override
   bool     UseEquity;         // true=Equity, false=Balance for calc
};

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Dynamic Lot Calculation                                           |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
double DynamicLot(double slDistance, MoneyManagement &mm, int magic)
{
   double accountBase  = mm.UseEquity
                         ? AccountInfoDouble(ACCOUNT_EQUITY)
                         : AccountInfoDouble(ACCOUNT_BALANCE);
   double riskAmount   = accountBase * mm.RiskPercent / 100.0;
   double tickValue    = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_TRADE_TICK_VALUE);
   double tickSize     = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_TRADE_TICK_SIZE);
   double lotStep      = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_VOLUME_STEP);
   double brokerMinLot = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_VOLUME_MIN);
   double brokerMaxLot = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_VOLUME_MAX);

   double minL = MathMax(mm.MinLot, brokerMinLot);
   double maxL = MathMin(mm.MaxLot > 0 ? mm.MaxLot : brokerMaxLot, brokerMaxLot);

   if(slDistance <= 0 || tickValue <= 0 || tickSize <= 0) return minL;

   double lots = riskAmount / (slDistance / tickSize * tickValue);
   lots = MathFloor(lots / lotStep) * lotStep;
   return MathMax(minL, MathMin(maxL, lots));
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Count open positions for this EA (by magic)                      |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
int CountMagicPositions(int magic)
{
   int count = 0;
   for(int i = 0; i < PositionsTotal(); i++)
   {
      ulong ticket = PositionGetTicket(i);
      if(!PositionSelectByTicket(ticket)) continue;
      if((int)PositionGetInteger(POSITION_MAGIC) == magic) count++;
   }
   return count;
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Daily Loss Check — คืน true ถ้าเกิน MaxDailyLoss                |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
bool IsDailyLossExceeded(MoneyManagement &mm, int magic)
{
   double balance     = AccountInfoDouble(ACCOUNT_BALANCE);
   double equity      = AccountInfoDouble(ACCOUNT_EQUITY);
   double maxLoss     = balance * mm.MaxDailyLossPct / 100.0;

   // คำนวณกำไร/ขาดทุนวันนี้
   datetime startOfDay = StringToTime(TimeToString(TimeCurrent(), TIME_DATE));
   double todayPL = 0;

   // Open positions P&L
   for(int i = 0; i < PositionsTotal(); i++)
   {
      ulong ticket = PositionGetTicket(i);
      if(!PositionSelectByTicket(ticket)) continue;
      if((int)PositionGetInteger(POSITION_MAGIC) != magic) continue;
      todayPL += PositionGetDouble(POSITION_PROFIT)
               + PositionGetDouble(POSITION_SWAP)
               + PositionGetDouble(POSITION_COMMISSION);
   }

   // Closed trades today (History)
   HistorySelect(startOfDay, TimeCurrent());
   for(int i = 0; i < HistoryDealsTotal(); i++)
   {
      ulong dealTicket = HistoryDealGetTicket(i);
      if((int)HistoryDealGetInteger(dealTicket, DEAL_MAGIC) != magic) continue;
      if(HistoryDealGetInteger(dealTicket, DEAL_ENTRY) == DEAL_ENTRY_OUT)
         todayPL += HistoryDealGetDouble(dealTicket, DEAL_PROFIT)
                  + HistoryDealGetDouble(dealTicket, DEAL_SWAP)
                  + HistoryDealGetDouble(dealTicket, DEAL_COMMISSION);
   }

   return (todayPL <= -maxLoss);
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Drawdown Check — คืน true ถ้า Drawdown เกิน Max                 |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
bool IsDrawdownExceeded(MoneyManagement &mm)
{
   double balance = AccountInfoDouble(ACCOUNT_BALANCE);
   double equity  = AccountInfoDouble(ACCOUNT_EQUITY);
   double dd      = (balance - equity) / balance * 100.0;
   return (dd >= mm.MaxDrawdownPct);
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Master Gate — ผ่านทุก check ถึงจะเทรดได้                        |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
bool CanTrade(MoneyManagement &mm, int magic)
{
   if(CountMagicPositions(magic) >= MathMin(mm.MaxPositions, 5)) return false;
   if(IsDailyLossExceeded(mm, magic)) return false;
   if(IsDrawdownExceeded(mm)) return false;
   return true;
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Close All Positions for this magic                               |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void CloseAllByMagic(CTrade &trade, int magic)
{
   for(int i = PositionsTotal()-1; i >= 0; i--)
   {
      ulong ticket = PositionGetTicket(i);
      if(!PositionSelectByTicket(ticket)) continue;
      if((int)PositionGetInteger(POSITION_MAGIC) == magic)
         trade.PositionClose(ticket);
   }
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Pip Value Helper                                                  |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
double GetPipValue()
{
   double point  = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_POINT);
   int    digits = (int)SymbolInfoInteger(_Symbol, SYMBOL_DIGITS);
   return (digits == 5 || digits == 3) ? point * 10.0 : point;
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Session Time Check (GMT)                                         |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
bool IsInSession(int startHour, int endHour)
{
   MqlDateTime dt;
   TimeToStruct(TimeGMT(), dt);
   return (dt.hour >= startHour && dt.hour < endHour);
}

#endif
```

## 🌟 PORT 1 — 141049900 | Celestial Woodfire
### Configurable MTF Trend Following

```mql5
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| EA_CelestialWoodfire_141049900.mq5                               |
//| MTF Trend Following — Fully Configurable                         |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
#property copyright "Port 141049900"
#property version   "2.00"

#include <Trade\Trade.mqh>
#include <MTF_Base.mqh>

//--- ══ TIMEFRAME CONFIGURATION ══
input group "═══ Timeframe Settings ═══"
input ENUM_TIMEFRAMES TF_Higher   = PERIOD_D1;   // HTF — Trend Direction
input ENUM_TIMEFRAMES TF_Middle   = PERIOD_H4;   // MTF — Signal Confirm
input ENUM_TIMEFRAMES TF_Entry    = PERIOD_H1;   // LTF — Entry Timing

//--- ══ INDICATOR SETTINGS ══
input group "═══ Indicator Settings ═══"
input int    EMA_Fast      = 50;
input int    EMA_Slow      = 200;
input int    MACD_Fast     = 12;
input int    MACD_Slow     = 26;
input int    MACD_Signal   = 9;
input int    ADX_Period    = 14;
input double ADX_Min       = 25.0;
input int    ATR_Period    = 14;

//--- ══ MONEY MANAGEMENT ══
input group "═══ Money Management ═══"
input double Risk_Percent      = 1.0;    // Risk % per trade
input double MaxDailyLoss_Pct  = 3.0;   // Max daily loss %
input double MaxDrawdown_Pct   = 10.0;  // Max total drawdown %
input int    Max_Positions     = 2;     // Max positions (1-5)
input double ATR_SL_Mult       = 1.5;   // SL = ATR x this
input double ATR_TP_Mult       = 3.0;   // TP = ATR x this
input double Override_MinLot   = 0.01;  // Min lot override
input double Override_MaxLot   = 5.0;   // Max lot override
input bool   Use_Equity        = false; // Use Equity for lot calc

//--- ══ TRAILING STOP ══
input group "═══ Trailing Stop ═══"
input bool   Enable_Trail     = true;
input double Trail_ATR_Mult   = 1.0;  // Trail = ATR x this

input int    Magic = 141049900;

//--- Handles
int h_ema_fast_htf, h_ema_slow_htf;
int h_ema_fast_mtf, h_ema_slow_mtf;
int h_macd_entry, h_adx_entry, h_atr_entry;

CTrade trade;
MoneyManagement mm;

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
int OnInit()
{
   // Validate Max_Positions
   int maxPos = (int)MathMin(Max_Positions, 5);

   // Init MM struct
   mm.RiskPercent      = Risk_Percent;
   mm.MaxDailyLossPct  = MaxDailyLoss_Pct;
   mm.MaxDrawdownPct   = MaxDrawdown_Pct;
   mm.MaxPositions     = maxPos;
   mm.MinLot           = Override_MinLot;
   mm.MaxLot           = Override_MaxLot;
   mm.UseEquity        = Use_Equity;

   // Create handles
   h_ema_fast_htf = iMA(_Symbol, TF_Higher, EMA_Fast, 0, MODE_EMA, PRICE_CLOSE);
   h_ema_slow_htf = iMA(_Symbol, TF_Higher, EMA_Slow, 0, MODE_EMA, PRICE_CLOSE);
   h_ema_fast_mtf = iMA(_Symbol, TF_Middle, EMA_Fast, 0, MODE_EMA, PRICE_CLOSE);
   h_ema_slow_mtf = iMA(_Symbol, TF_Middle, EMA_Slow, 0, MODE_EMA, PRICE_CLOSE);
   h_macd_entry   = iMACD(_Symbol, TF_Entry, MACD_Fast, MACD_Slow, MACD_Signal, PRICE_CLOSE);
   h_adx_entry    = iADX(_Symbol,  TF_Entry, ADX_Period);
   h_atr_entry    = iATR(_Symbol,  TF_Entry, ATR_Period);

   trade.SetExpertMagicNumber(Magic);

   Print("CelestialWoodfire Init OK | HTF:", EnumToString(TF_Higher),
         " MTF:", EnumToString(TF_Middle), " LTF:", EnumToString(TF_Entry));
   return INIT_SUCCEEDED;
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void OnTick()
{
   static datetime lastBar = 0;
   datetime currentBar = iTime(_Symbol, TF_Entry, 0);
   if(currentBar == lastBar) return;
   lastBar = currentBar;

   if(Enable_Trail) ManageTrailing();
   if(!CanTrade(mm, Magic)) return;

   //--- HTF: Trend Direction
   double ema_fast_h[], ema_slow_h[];
   CopyBuffer(h_ema_fast_htf, 0, 0, 2, ema_fast_h);
   CopyBuffer(h_ema_slow_htf, 0, 0, 2, ema_slow_h);
   bool htf_bull = ema_fast_h[0] > ema_slow_h[0];
   bool htf_bear = ema_fast_h[0] < ema_slow_h[0];

   //--- MTF: Signal Confirm
   double ema_fast_m[], ema_slow_m[];
   CopyBuffer(h_ema_fast_mtf, 0, 0, 2, ema_fast_m);
   CopyBuffer(h_ema_slow_mtf, 0, 0, 2, ema_slow_m);
   bool mtf_bull = ema_fast_m[0] > ema_slow_m[0];
   bool mtf_bear = ema_fast_m[0] < ema_slow_m[0];

   //--- LTF: Entry — MACD Cross + ADX
   double macd_main[], macd_sig[], adx_val[], atr_val[];
   CopyBuffer(h_macd_entry, MAIN_LINE,   0, 3, macd_main);
   CopyBuffer(h_macd_entry, SIGNAL_LINE, 0, 3, macd_sig);
   CopyBuffer(h_adx_entry,  MAIN_LINE,   0, 2, adx_val);
   CopyBuffer(h_atr_entry,  0,           0, 2, atr_val);

   bool macd_up   = macd_main[1] > macd_sig[1] && macd_main[2] <= macd_sig[2];
   bool macd_down = macd_main[1] < macd_sig[1] && macd_main[2] >= macd_sig[2];
   bool adx_ok    = adx_val[0] > ADX_Min;
   double atr     = atr_val[0];

   if(htf_bull && mtf_bull && macd_up && adx_ok)   ExecuteBuy(atr);
   if(htf_bear && mtf_bear && macd_down && adx_ok) ExecuteSell(atr);
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void ExecuteBuy(double atr)
{
   double ask  = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_ASK);
   double sl   = ask - atr * ATR_SL_Mult;
   double tp   = ask + atr * ATR_TP_Mult;
   double lots = DynamicLot(ask - sl, mm, Magic);
   trade.Buy(lots, _Symbol, ask, sl, tp,
             StringFormat("CW|R%.1f%%|SL%.1f|TP%.1f", Risk_Percent, ATR_SL_Mult, ATR_TP_Mult));
}

void ExecuteSell(double atr)
{
   double bid  = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_BID);
   double sl   = bid + atr * ATR_SL_Mult;
   double tp   = bid - atr * ATR_TP_Mult;
   double lots = DynamicLot(sl - bid, mm, Magic);
   trade.Sell(lots, _Symbol, bid, sl, tp,
              StringFormat("CW|R%.1f%%|SL%.1f|TP%.1f", Risk_Percent, ATR_SL_Mult, ATR_TP_Mult));
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void ManageTrailing()
{
   double ema_fast_m[], atr_val[];
   CopyBuffer(h_ema_fast_mtf, 0, 0, 2, ema_fast_m);
   CopyBuffer(h_atr_entry,    0, 0, 2, atr_val);
   double atr = atr_val[0];

   for(int i = PositionsTotal()-1; i >= 0; i--)
   {
      ulong ticket = PositionGetTicket(i);
      if(!PositionSelectByTicket(ticket)) continue;
      if((int)PositionGetInteger(POSITION_MAGIC) != Magic) continue;
      if(PositionGetDouble(POSITION_PROFIT) <= 0) continue;

      double sl  = PositionGetDouble(POSITION_SL);
      double tp  = PositionGetDouble(POSITION_TP);
      long   typ = PositionGetInteger(POSITION_TYPE);

      if(typ == POSITION_TYPE_BUY)
      {
         double newSL = ema_fast_m[0] - atr * Trail_ATR_Mult;
         if(newSL > sl && newSL < SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_BID))
            trade.PositionModify(ticket, newSL, tp);
      }
      else
      {
         double newSL = ema_fast_m[0] + atr * Trail_ATR_Mult;
         if(newSL < sl && newSL > SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_ASK))
            trade.PositionModify(ticket, newSL, tp);
      }
   }
}
```

## 🌟 PORT 2 — 146004104 | Ironclad Forest
### Configurable MTF Smart Grid + MM

```mql5
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| EA_IroncladForest_146004104.mq5                                  |
//| MTF Smart Grid / DCA — Fully Configurable                        |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
#property copyright "Port 146004104"
#property version   "2.00"

#include <Trade\Trade.mqh>
#include <MTF_Base.mqh>

input group "═══ Timeframe Settings ═══"
input ENUM_TIMEFRAMES TF_Higher   = PERIOD_W1;  // HTF — Range Boundary
input ENUM_TIMEFRAMES TF_Middle   = PERIOD_D1;  // MTF — BB Bias
input ENUM_TIMEFRAMES TF_Entry    = PERIOD_H1;  // LTF — Grid Entry

input group "═══ Indicator Settings ═══"
input int    BB_Period     = 20;
input double BB_Dev_HTF    = 2.0;
input double BB_Dev_Entry  = 2.0;
input int    RSI_Period    = 14;
input int    ATR_Period    = 14;

input group "═══ Grid Settings ═══"
input int    MaxGridLevels    = 4;          // Max grid levels (สูงสุด 4)
input double GridATR_Mult     = 1.0;        // Grid spacing = ATR x this
input double LotMultiplier    = 1.5;        // แต่ละ Level เพิ่ม Lot เท่าไหร่

input group "═══ Money Management ═══"
input double Risk_Percent     = 1.0;
input double MaxDailyLoss_Pct = 3.0;
input double MaxDrawdown_Pct  = 10.0;
input int    Max_Positions    = 4;          // ≤ 5 เสมอ (Grid นับทุก Level)
input double BaseLot          = 0.01;
input double Override_MaxLot  = 2.0;
input double BasketTP_Pct     = 1.0;       // Basket TP % of balance
input double BasketSL_Pct     = 3.0;       // Basket SL % of balance
input bool   Use_Equity       = false;

input int Magic = 146004104;

int h_bb_htf, h_bb_mtf, h_bb_entry;
int h_rsi_entry, h_atr_entry;

CTrade trade;
MoneyManagement mm;
bool gridBullish = false;

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
int OnInit()
{
   mm.RiskPercent     = Risk_Percent;
   mm.MaxDailyLossPct = MaxDailyLoss_Pct;
   mm.MaxDrawdownPct  = MaxDrawdown_Pct;
   mm.MaxPositions    = (int)MathMin(Max_Positions, 5);
   mm.MinLot          = BaseLot;
   mm.MaxLot          = Override_MaxLot;
   mm.UseEquity       = Use_Equity;

   h_bb_htf   = iBands(_Symbol, TF_Higher, BB_Period, 0, BB_Dev_HTF,   PRICE_CLOSE);
   h_bb_mtf   = iBands(_Symbol, TF_Middle, BB_Period, 0, BB_Dev_HTF,   PRICE_CLOSE);
   h_bb_entry = iBands(_Symbol, TF_Entry,  BB_Period, 0, BB_Dev_Entry, PRICE_CLOSE);
   h_rsi_entry = iRSI(_Symbol,  TF_Entry,  RSI_Period, PRICE_CLOSE);
   h_atr_entry = iATR(_Symbol,  TF_Entry,  ATR_Period);

   trade.SetExpertMagicNumber(Magic);
   return INIT_SUCCEEDED;
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void OnTick()
{
   static datetime lastBar = 0;
   datetime currentBar = iTime(_Symbol, TF_Entry, 0);
   if(currentBar == lastBar) return;
   lastBar = currentBar;

   CheckBasketExit();
   if(IsDrawdownExceeded(mm)) { CloseAllByMagic(trade, Magic); return; }
   if(IsDailyLossExceeded(mm, Magic)) return;

   double price = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_BID);

   //--- HTF: ราคาต้องอยู่ในกรอบ BB HTF
   double htf_upper[], htf_lower[];
   CopyBuffer(h_bb_htf, 1, 0, 2, htf_upper);
   CopyBuffer(h_bb_htf, 2, 0, 2, htf_lower);
   if(price < htf_lower[0] || price > htf_upper[0]) return;

   //--- MTF: BB Middle Bias
   double mtf_mid[];
   CopyBuffer(h_bb_mtf, 0, 0, 2, mtf_mid);
   bool mtf_bullBias = price < mtf_mid[0];
   bool mtf_bearBias = price > mtf_mid[0];

   //--- LTF: BB Touch + RSI
   double entry_upper[], entry_lower[];
   double rsi[], atr[];
   CopyBuffer(h_bb_entry, 1, 0, 2, entry_upper);
   CopyBuffer(h_bb_entry, 2, 0, 2, entry_lower);
   CopyBuffer(h_rsi_entry, 0, 0, 2, rsi);
   CopyBuffer(h_atr_entry, 0, 0, 2, atr);
   double atrVal = atr[0];

   bool buySignal  = (price < entry_lower[0] && rsi[0] < 30);
   bool sellSignal = (price > entry_upper[0] && rsi[0] > 70);

   int gridCount = CountMagicPositions(Magic);

   if(gridCount == 0)
   {
      if(gridCount >= MathMin(Max_Positions, 5)) return;
      if(mtf_bullBias && buySignal)
      { gridBullish = true;  AddGrid(true,  atrVal, 0); }
      else if(mtf_bearBias && sellSignal)
      { gridBullish = false; AddGrid(false, atrVal, 0); }
   }
   else if(gridCount < MathMin(MaxGridLevels, 4) &&
           gridCount < MathMin(Max_Positions, 5))
   {
      double lastPrice = GetLastGridPrice();
      if(gridBullish  && price < lastPrice - atrVal * GridATR_Mult)
         AddGrid(true,  atrVal, gridCount);
      else if(!gridBullish && price > lastPrice + atrVal * GridATR_Mult)
         AddGrid(false, atrVal, gridCount);
   }
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void AddGrid(bool isBuy, double atr, int level)
{
   double account = mm.UseEquity
                    ? AccountInfoDouble(ACCOUNT_EQUITY)
                    : AccountInfoDouble(ACCOUNT_BALANCE);
   double riskAmt = account * mm.RiskPercent / 100.0;

   double mult    = MathPow(LotMultiplier, level); // 1x, 1.5x, 2.25x, 3.375x
   double lots    = BaseLot * mult;
   double lotStep = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_VOLUME_STEP);
   lots = MathFloor(lots / lotStep) * lotStep;
   lots = MathMax(mm.MinLot, MathMin(mm.MaxLot, lots));

   double ask = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_ASK);
   double bid = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_BID);
   string comment = StringFormat("IF|L%d|%.2f", level, lots);

   if(isBuy)  trade.Buy(lots,  _Symbol, ask, 0, 0, comment);
   else       trade.Sell(lots, _Symbol, bid, 0, 0, comment);
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void CheckBasketExit()
{
   double balance = AccountInfoDouble(ACCOUNT_BALANCE);
   double totalPL = 0;
   int    count   = 0;

   for(int i = 0; i < PositionsTotal(); i++)
   {
      ulong t = PositionGetTicket(i);
      if(!PositionSelectByTicket(t)) continue;
      if((int)PositionGetInteger(POSITION_MAGIC) != Magic) continue;
      totalPL += PositionGetDouble(POSITION_PROFIT)
               + PositionGetDouble(POSITION_SWAP)
               + PositionGetDouble(POSITION_COMMISSION);
      count++;
   }
   if(count == 0) return;

   double tp_thresh =  balance * BasketTP_Pct / 100.0;
   double sl_thresh = -balance * BasketSL_Pct / 100.0;

   if(totalPL >= tp_thresh || totalPL <= sl_thresh)
      CloseAllByMagic(trade, Magic);
}

double GetLastGridPrice()
{
   double lastPrice = 0; datetime lastTime = 0;
   for(int i = 0; i < PositionsTotal(); i++)
   {
      ulong t = PositionGetTicket(i);
      if(!PositionSelectByTicket(t)) continue;
      if((int)PositionGetInteger(POSITION_MAGIC) != Magic) continue;
      datetime posTime = (datetime)PositionGetInteger(POSITION_TIME);
      if(posTime > lastTime)
      { lastTime = posTime; lastPrice = PositionGetDouble(POSITION_PRICE_OPEN); }
   }
   return lastPrice;
}
```

## 🌟 PORT 3 — 193234160 | Dawn Ascendant
### Configurable MTF Breakout

```mql5
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| EA_DawnAscendant_193234160.mq5                                   |
//| MTF Volatility Breakout — Fully Configurable                     |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
#property copyright "Port 193234160"
#property version   "2.00"

#include <Trade\Trade.mqh>
#include <MTF_Base.mqh>

input group "═══ Timeframe Settings ═══"
input ENUM_TIMEFRAMES TF_Higher   = PERIOD_W1;   // HTF — Macro Trend
input ENUM_TIMEFRAMES TF_Middle   = PERIOD_D1;   // MTF — Donchian Level
input ENUM_TIMEFRAMES TF_Entry    = PERIOD_H4;   // LTF — Execution

input group "═══ Indicator Settings ═══"
input int    Donchian_Period  = 20;
input int    ATR_Period       = 14;
input double ATR_Entry_Mult   = 0.5;
input double ATR_TP_Mult      = 2.0;
input double ATR_SL_Mult      = 1.0;
input int    EMA_HTF_Period   = 200;
input int    Vol_Period       = 20;
input double Vol_Mult         = 1.5;

input group "═══ Money Management ═══"
input double Risk_Percent      = 1.5;
input double MaxDailyLoss_Pct  = 4.0;
input double MaxDrawdown_Pct   = 12.0;
input int    Max_Positions     = 2;
input double Override_MinLot   = 0.01;
input double Override_MaxLot   = 5.0;
input bool   Use_Equity        = false;

input group "═══ Trailing Stop ═══"
input bool   Enable_Trail    = true;
input double Trail_ATR_Mult  = 1.0;

input int Magic = 193234160;

int h_ema_htf, h_atr_mtf, h_atr_entry;

CTrade trade;
MoneyManagement mm;

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
int OnInit()
{
   mm.RiskPercent     = Risk_Percent;
   mm.MaxDailyLossPct = MaxDailyLoss_Pct;
   mm.MaxDrawdownPct  = MaxDrawdown_Pct;
   mm.MaxPositions    = (int)MathMin(Max_Positions, 5);
   mm.MinLot          = Override_MinLot;
   mm.MaxLot          = Override_MaxLot;
   mm.UseEquity       = Use_Equity;

   h_ema_htf    = iMA(_Symbol,  TF_Higher, EMA_HTF_Period, 0, MODE_EMA, PRICE_CLOSE);
   h_atr_mtf    = iATR(_Symbol, TF_Middle, ATR_Period);
   h_atr_entry  = iATR(_Symbol, TF_Entry,  ATR_Period);

   trade.SetExpertMagicNumber(Magic);
   return INIT_SUCCEEDED;
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void OnTick()
{
   static datetime lastBar = 0;
   datetime currentBar = iTime(_Symbol, TF_Entry, 0);
   if(currentBar == lastBar) return;
   lastBar = currentBar;

   DeleteExpiredPendings();
   if(Enable_Trail && PositionsTotal() > 0) { ManageTrailing(); return; }
   if(!CanTrade(mm, Magic)) return;

   //--- HTF: EMA Macro Trend
   double ema_htf[]; CopyBuffer(h_ema_htf, 0, 0, 2, ema_htf);
   double price   = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_BID);
   bool htf_bull  = price > ema_htf[0];
   bool htf_bear  = price < ema_htf[0];

   //--- MTF: Donchian Channel
   double hi[], lo[];
   ArraySetAsSeries(hi, true); ArraySetAsSeries(lo, true);
   CopyHigh(_Symbol, TF_Middle, 1, Donchian_Period, hi);
   CopyLow(_Symbol,  TF_Middle, 1, Donchian_Period, lo);
   double dHigh = hi[ArrayMaximum(hi)];
   double dLow  = lo[ArrayMinimum(lo)];

   //--- LTF: ATR + Volume
   double atr_e[]; CopyBuffer(h_atr_entry, 0, 0, 2, atr_e);
   double atr_m[]; CopyBuffer(h_atr_mtf,   0, 0, 2, atr_m);
   double atr     = atr_e[0];

   long  volArr[]; ArraySetAsSeries(volArr, true);
   CopyTickVolume(_Symbol, TF_Entry, 1, Vol_Period, volArr);
   double avgVol = 0;
   for(int i = 0; i < Vol_Period; i++) avgVol += volArr[i];
   avgVol /= Vol_Period;
   long curVol = iVolume(_Symbol, TF_Entry, 1);
   if(curVol < avgVol * Vol_Mult) return;

   double entryBuy  = dHigh + atr * ATR_Entry_Mult;
   double entrySell = dLow  - atr * ATR_Entry_Mult;
   double lots      = DynamicLot(atr * ATR_SL_Mult, mm, Magic);

   if(OrdersTotal() == 0)
   {
      if(htf_bull || (!htf_bull && !htf_bear))
         trade.BuyStop(lots, entryBuy,
                       _Symbol,
                       entryBuy - atr_m[0],
                       entryBuy + atr * ATR_TP_Mult,
                       ORDER_TIME_GTC, 0, "DA_BS");

      if(htf_bear || (!htf_bull && !htf_bear))
         trade.SellStop(lots, entrySell,
                        _Symbol,
                        entrySell + atr_m[0],
                        entrySell - atr * ATR_TP_Mult,
                        ORDER_TIME_GTC, 0, "DA_SS");
   }
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void DeleteExpiredPendings()
{
   // ลบ Pending ที่อายุเกิน 1 แท่ง TF_Entry
   int tfSeconds = PeriodSeconds(TF_Entry);
   for(int i = OrdersTotal()-1; i >= 0; i--)
   {
      ulong ticket = OrderGetTicket(i);
      if(!OrderSelect(ticket)) continue;
      if((int)OrderGetInteger(ORDER_MAGIC) != Magic) continue;
      datetime setupTime = (datetime)OrderGetInteger(ORDER_TIME_SETUP);
      if((TimeCurrent() - setupTime) >= tfSeconds)
         trade.OrderDelete(ticket);
   }
}

void ManageTrailing()
{
   double atr_e[]; CopyBuffer(h_atr_entry, 0, 0, 2, atr_e);
   double atr = atr_e[0];

   for(int i = PositionsTotal()-1; i >= 0; i--)
   {
      ulong ticket = PositionGetTicket(i);
      if(!PositionSelectByTicket(ticket)) continue;
      if((int)PositionGetInteger(POSITION_MAGIC) != Magic) continue;
      if(PositionGetDouble(POSITION_PROFIT) <= 0) continue;

      double sl  = PositionGetDouble(POSITION_SL);
      double tp  = PositionGetDouble(POSITION_TP);
      long   typ = PositionGetInteger(POSITION_TYPE);

      if(typ == POSITION_TYPE_BUY)
      {
         double newSL = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_BID) - atr * Trail_ATR_Mult;
         if(newSL > sl) trade.PositionModify(ticket, newSL, tp);
      }
      else
      {
         double newSL = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_ASK) + atr * Trail_ATR_Mult;
         if(newSL < sl) trade.PositionModify(ticket, newSL, tp);
      }
   }
}
```

## ⚔️ PORT 4 — 159219430 | Crimson Tide
### Configurable MTF Mean Reversion

```mql5
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| EA_CrimsonTide_159219430.mq5                                     |
//| MTF Mean Reversion — Fully Configurable                          |

#property copyright "Port 159219430"
#property version   "2.00"

#include <MTF_Base.mqh>

input group "═══ Timeframe Settings ═══"

input ENUM_TIMEFRAMES TF_Middle   = PERIOD_D1;   // MTF — ADX Trend Check
input ENUM_TIMEFRAMES TF_Entry    = PERIOD_H1;   // LTF — RSI+BB Entry

input group "═══ Indicator Settings ═══"
input int    BB_Period     = 20;
input double BB_Dev_HTF    = 2.0;
input double BB_Dev_Entry  = 2.5;
input int    RSI_Period    = 7;

input int    Stoch_D       = 3;
input int    Stoch_Slow    = 3;
input int    ADX_Period    = 14;
input double ADX_Max       = 30.0;   // ถ้า ADX เกินนี้ = Trend แรง ห้ามเทรด
input int    ATR_Period    = 14;

input group "═══ Money Management ═══"
input double Risk_Percent      = 0.8;
input double MaxDailyLoss_Pct  = 2.5;
input double MaxDrawdown_Pct   = 8.0;
input int    Max_Positions     = 3;
input double ATR_SL_Mult       = 1.5;
input double Override_MinLot   = 0.01;
input double Override_MaxLot   = 3.0;
input int    MaxHoldHours      = 48;
input bool   Use_Equity        = false;

input int Magic = 159219430;

int h_bb_htf, h_adx_mtf, h_ema200_mtf;
int h_bb_entry, h_rsi_entry, h_stoch_entry, h_atr_entry;

CTrade trade;
MoneyManagement mm;

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
int OnInit()
{
   mm.RiskPercent     = Risk_Percent;
   mm.MaxDailyLossPct = MaxDailyLoss_Pct;
   mm.MaxDrawdownPct  = MaxDrawdown_Pct;
   mm.MaxPositions    = (int)MathMin(Max_Positions, 5);
   mm.MinLot          = Override_MinLot;
   mm.MaxLot          = Override_MaxLot;
   mm.UseEquity       = Use_Equity;

   h_bb_htf      = iBands(_Symbol, TF_Higher, BB_Period, 0, BB_Dev_HTF,   PRICE_CLOSE);
   h_adx_mtf     = iADX(_Symbol,   TF_Middle, ADX_Period);
   h_ema200_mtf  = iMA(_Symbol,    TF_Middle, 200, 0, MODE_EMA, PRICE_CLOSE);
   h_bb_entry    = iBands(_Symbol,  TF_Entry, BB_Period, 0, BB_Dev_Entry, PRICE_CLOSE);
   h_rsi_entry   = iRSI(_Symbol,    TF_Entry, RSI_Period, PRICE_CLOSE);
   h_stoch_entry = iStochastic(_Symbol, TF_Entry, Stoch_K, Stoch_D, Stoch_Slow, MODE_SMA, STO_LOWHIGH);
   h_atr_entry   = iATR(_Symbol,    TF_Entry, ATR_Period);

   trade.SetExpertMagicNumber(Magic);
   return INIT_SUCCEEDED;
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void OnTick()
{
   static datetime lastBar = 0;
   datetime currentBar = iTime(_Symbol, TF_Entry, 0);
   if(currentBar == lastBar) return;
   lastBar = currentBar;

   CheckTimeExit();
   if(!CanTrade(mm, Magic)) return;

   //--- HTF: BB Width — Range ไหมเปล่า
   double htf_u[], htf_l[], htf_m[];
   CopyBuffer(h_bb_htf, 0, 0, 2, htf_m);
   CopyBuffer(h_bb_htf, 1, 0, 2, htf_u);
   CopyBuffer(h_bb_htf, 2, 0, 2, htf_l);
   double bbWidth = (htf_u[0] - htf_l[0]) / (htf_m[0] > 0 ? htf_m[0] : 1);
   if(bbWidth > BB_Width_Max) return; // กว้างเกิน = Trend

   //--- MTF: ADX + EMA200 Direction
   double adx_m[], ema200_m[];
   CopyBuffer(h_adx_mtf,    MAIN_LINE, 0, 2, adx_m);
   CopyBuffer(h_ema200_mtf, 0,         0, 2, ema200_m);
   if(adx_m[0] > ADX_Max) return;
   double price   = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_BID);
   bool mtf_above = price > ema200_m[0]; // ราคาเหนือ EMA200
   bool mtf_below = price < ema200_m[0];

   //--- LTF: BB + RSI + Stochastic
   double bb_u[], bb_l[], bb_m2[], rsi[], stk[], std_[], atr_[];
   CopyBuffer(h_bb_entry,    0, 0, 2, bb_m2);
   CopyBuffer(h_bb_entry,    1, 0, 2, bb_u);
   CopyBuffer(h_bb_entry,    2, 0, 2, bb_l);
   CopyBuffer(h_rsi_entry,   0, 0, 2, rsi);
   CopyBuffer(h_stoch_entry, MAIN_LINE, 0, 2, stk);
   CopyBuffer(h_atr_entry,   0, 0, 2, atr_);
   double atr = atr_[0];

   bool buySetup  = price < bb_l[0] && rsi[0] < 25 && stk[0] < 20;
   bool sellSetup = price > bb_u[0] && rsi[0] > 75 && stk[0] > 80;

   if(buySetup && !mtf_below)  // ห้าม BUY ถ้า MTF ชี้ลง
   {
      double ask = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_ASK);
      double sl  = ask - atr * ATR_SL_Mult;
      double tp  = bb_m2[0];
      trade.Buy(DynamicLot(ask-sl, mm, Magic), _Symbol, ask, sl, tp, "CT_BUY");
   }
   else if(sellSetup && !mtf_above)
   {
      double bid = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_BID);
      double sl  = bid + atr * ATR_SL_Mult;
      double tp  = bb_m2[0];
      trade.Sell(DynamicLot(sl-bid, mm, Magic), _Symbol, bid, sl, tp, "CT_SELL");
   }
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void CheckTimeExit()
{
   datetime now = TimeCurrent();
   for(int i = PositionsTotal()-1; i >= 0; i--)
   {
      ulong t = PositionGetTicket(i);
      if(!PositionSelectByTicket(t)) continue;
      if((int)PositionGetInteger(POSITION_MAGIC) != Magic) continue;
      datetime openTime = (datetime)PositionGetInteger(POSITION_TIME);
      if((now - openTime) >= (datetime)(MaxHoldHours * 3600))
         trade.PositionClose(t);
   }
}
```

## ⚔️ PORT 5 — 111098104 | Abyssal Discipline
### Configurable MTF Z-Score Pair Trading

```mql5
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| EA_AbyssalDiscipline_111098104.mq5                               |
//| MTF Z-Score Pair Trading — Fully Configurable                    |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
#property copyright "Port 111098104"
#property version   "2.00"

#include <Trade\Trade.mqh>
#include <MTF_Base.mqh>

input group "═══ Timeframe Settings ═══"
input ENUM_TIMEFRAMES TF_Higher   = PERIOD_D1;   // HTF — Correlation Check
input ENUM_TIMEFRAMES TF_Middle   = PERIOD_H4;   // MTF — Z-Score Calc
input ENUM_TIMEFRAMES TF_Entry    = PERIOD_H1;   // LTF — Entry Trigger

input group "═══ Pair Settings ═══"
input string Symbol2          = "USDCHF";  // Second symbol (run on EURUSD)

input group "═══ Z-Score Settings ═══"
input int    LookbackHTF      = 20;   // Correlation lookback (HTF bars)
input int    LookbackMTF      = 50;   // Z-Score lookback (MTF bars)
input double ZScore_Entry     = 2.0;
input double ZScore_Exit      = 0.3;
input double ZScore_HardSL    = 3.5;
input double Corr_Min         = -0.7; // Correlation ต้องต่ำกว่านี้

input group "═══ Money Management ═══"
input double Risk_Percent      = 0.5;
input double MaxDailyLoss_Pct  = 2.0;
input double MaxDrawdown_Pct   = 8.0;
input int    Max_Positions     = 2;   // 2 positions = 1 pair (BUY+SELL)
input int    MaxHoldDays       = 5;
input double Fixed_Lot         = 0.01;
input bool   Use_Equity        = false;

input int Magic = 111098104;

CTrade trade;
MoneyManagement mm;

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
int OnInit()
{
   mm.RiskPercent     = Risk_Percent;
   mm.MaxDailyLossPct = MaxDailyLoss_Pct;
   mm.MaxDrawdownPct  = MaxDrawdown_Pct;
   mm.MaxPositions    = (int)MathMin(Max_Positions, 5);
   mm.MinLot          = Fixed_Lot;
   mm.MaxLot          = Fixed_Lot * 5;
   mm.UseEquity       = Use_Equity;

   trade.SetExpertMagicNumber(Magic);
   return INIT_SUCCEEDED;
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void OnTick()
{
   static datetime lastBar = 0;
   datetime currentBar = iTime(_Symbol, TF_Entry, 0);
   if(currentBar == lastBar) return;
   lastBar = currentBar;

   CheckPairExit();
   if(!CanTrade(mm, Magic)) return;
   if(CountMagicPositions(Magic) >= 2) return; // Pair = 2 positions max

   //--- HTF: Correlation Check
   double corr = CalcCorrelation(TF_Higher, LookbackHTF);
   if(corr > Corr_Min) return;

   //--- MTF: Z-Score
   double zScore = CalcZScore(TF_Middle, LookbackMTF);
   if(zScore == EMPTY_VALUE) return;

   double ask1 = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_ASK);
   double bid1 = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_BID);
   double ask2 = SymbolInfoDouble(Symbol2, SYMBOL_ASK);
   double bid2 = SymbolInfoDouble(Symbol2, SYMBOL_BID);

   //--- LTF: Confirm Z-Score still holding at entry TF
   double zEntry = CalcZScore(TF_Entry, LookbackMTF / 2);

   if(zScore < -ZScore_Entry && zEntry < -ZScore_Entry)
   {
      trade.Buy(Fixed_Lot,  _Symbol, ask1, 0, 0, "AD_BUY1");
      trade.Sell(Fixed_Lot, Symbol2, bid2, 0, 0, "AD_SELL2");
   }
   else if(zScore > ZScore_Entry && zEntry > ZScore_Entry)
   {
      trade.Sell(Fixed_Lot, _Symbol, bid1, 0, 0, "AD_SELL1");
      trade.Buy(Fixed_Lot,  Symbol2, ask2, 0, 0, "AD_BUY2");
   }
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void CheckPairExit()
{
   if(CountMagicPositions(Magic) == 0) return;

   double zScore = CalcZScore(TF_Middle, LookbackMTF);
   bool exitTP   = MathAbs(zScore) < ZScore_Exit;
   bool exitSL   = MathAbs(zScore) > ZScore_HardSL;
   bool exitDD   = IsDrawdownExceeded(mm);

   bool exitTime = false;
   for(int i = 0; i < PositionsTotal(); i++)
   {
      ulong t = PositionGetTicket(i);
      if(!PositionSelectByTicket(t)) continue;
      if((int)PositionGetInteger(POSITION_MAGIC) != Magic) continue;
      datetime ot = (datetime)PositionGetInteger(POSITION_TIME);
      if((TimeCurrent() - ot) >= (datetime)(MaxHoldDays * 86400)) exitTime = true;
   }

   if(exitTP || exitSL || exitTime || exitDD)
      CloseAllByMagic(trade, Magic);
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
double CalcZScore(ENUM_TIMEFRAMES tf, int period)
{
   double c1[], c2[];
   ArraySetAsSeries(c1, true); ArraySetAsSeries(c2, true);
   if(CopyClose(_Symbol, tf, 0, period+1, c1) < period+1) return EMPTY_VALUE;
   if(CopyClose(Symbol2, tf, 0, period+1, c2) < period+1) return EMPTY_VALUE;

   double spread[];  ArrayResize(spread, period);
   for(int i = 0; i < period; i++) spread[i] = c1[i] - (1.0 - c2[i]);

   double mean = 0;
   for(int i = 0; i < period; i++) mean += spread[i];
   mean /= period;

   double stdDev = 0;
   for(int i = 0; i < period; i++) stdDev += MathPow(spread[i]-mean, 2);
   stdDev = MathSqrt(stdDev / period);

   return (stdDev == 0) ? 0 : (spread[0] - mean) / stdDev;
}

double CalcCorrelation(ENUM_TIMEFRAMES tf, int period)
{
   double c1[], c2[];
   ArraySetAsSeries(c1, true); ArraySetAsSeries(c2, true);
   CopyClose(_Symbol, tf, 0, period, c1);
   CopyClose(Symbol2, tf, 0, period, c2);

   double m1=0, m2=0, cov=0, s1=0, s2=0;
   for(int i=0;i<period;i++){m1+=c1[i];m2+=c2[i];}
   m1/=period; m2/=period;
   for(int i=0;i<period;i++)
   { cov+=(c1[i]-m1)*(c2[i]-m2); s1+=MathPow(c1[i]-m1,2); s2+=MathPow(c2[i]-m2,2); }
   return (s1>0&&s2>0) ? cov/MathSqrt(s1*s2) : 0;
}
```

## ⚔️ PORT 6 — 117030969 | Golden Ember
### Configurable MTF Pivot Range

```mql5
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| EA_GoldenEmber_117030969.mq5                                     |
//| MTF Pivot S/R Range — Fully Configurable                         |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
#property copyright "Port 117030969"
#property version   "2.00"

#include <Trade\Trade.mqh>
#include <MTF_Base.mqh>

input group "═══ Timeframe Settings ═══"
input ENUM_TIMEFRAMES TF_Higher   = PERIOD_MN1;  // HTF — Monthly Pivot
input ENUM_TIMEFRAMES TF_Middle   = PERIOD_W1;   // MTF — Weekly Pivot
input ENUM_TIMEFRAMES TF_Entry    = PERIOD_H1;   // LTF — CCI+RSI Entry

input group "═══ Indicator Settings ═══"
input int    RSI_Period    = 14;
input int    CCI_Period    = 20;
input int    ATR_Period    = 14;
input double Zone_ATR_Mult = 0.3;  // Zone ± รอบ Pivot

input group "═══ Money Management ═══"
input double Risk_Percent      = 0.8;
input double MaxDailyLoss_Pct  = 2.5;
input double MaxDrawdown_Pct   = 8.0;
input int    Max_Positions     = 3;
input double ATR_SL_Mult       = 0.5;
input double Override_MinLot   = 0.01;
input double Override_MaxLot   = 3.0;
input bool   Use_Equity        = false;

input int Magic = 117030969;

struct PivotSet { double PP, R1, R2, S1, S2; };

int h_rsi, h_cci, h_atr;
CTrade trade;
MoneyManagement mm;

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
int OnInit()
{
   mm.RiskPercent     = Risk_Percent;
   mm.MaxDailyLossPct = MaxDailyLoss_Pct;
   mm.MaxDrawdownPct  = MaxDrawdown_Pct;
   mm.MaxPositions    = (int)MathMin(Max_Positions, 5);
   mm.MinLot          = Override_MinLot;
   mm.MaxLot          = Override_MaxLot;
   mm.UseEquity       = Use_Equity;

   h_rsi = iRSI(_Symbol, TF_Entry, RSI_Period, PRICE_CLOSE);
   h_cci = iCCI(_Symbol, TF_Entry, CCI_Period, PRICE_TYPICAL);
   h_atr = iATR(_Symbol, TF_Entry, ATR_Period);

   trade.SetExpertMagicNumber(Magic);
   return INIT_SUCCEEDED;
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void OnTick()
{
   static datetime lastBar = 0;
   datetime currentBar = iTime(_Symbol, TF_Entry, 0);
   if(currentBar == lastBar) return;
   lastBar = currentBar;

   if(!CanTrade(mm, Magic)) return;

   //--- HTF: Monthly Pivot — Macro Boundary
   PivotSet monthly = CalcPivot(TF_Higher, 1);
   //--- MTF: Weekly Pivot — Trading Levels
   PivotSet weekly  = CalcPivot(TF_Middle, 1);

   double price = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_BID);
   double atr_v[]; CopyBuffer(h_atr, 0, 0, 2, atr_v);
   double atr   = atr_v[0];
   double zone  = atr * Zone_ATR_Mult;

   double rsi_v[], cci_v[];
   CopyBuffer(h_rsi, 0, 0, 2, rsi_v);
   CopyBuffer(h_cci, 0, 0, 2, cci_v);

   bool nearS1 = MathAbs(price - weekly.S1) <= zone;
   bool nearS2 = MathAbs(price - weekly.S2) <= zone;
   bool nearR1 = MathAbs(price - weekly.R1) <= zone;
   bool nearR2 = MathAbs(price - weekly.R2) <= zone;

   //--- Monthly confirms: BUY ต้องเหนือ monthly.S1 / SELL ต้องใต้ monthly.R1
   bool mthBullOK = price > monthly.S1;
   bool mthBearOK = price < monthly.R1;

   bool buySignal  = (nearS1||nearS2) && rsi_v[0]<40 && cci_v[0]<-80 && mthBullOK;
   bool sellSignal = (nearR1||nearR2) && rsi_v[0]>60 && cci_v[0]> 80 && mthBearOK;

   if(buySignal)
   {
      double ask = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_ASK);
      double sl  = (nearS1 ? weekly.S2 : weekly.S2 - atr) - atr * ATR_SL_Mult;
      double tp  = nearS1 ? weekly.PP : weekly.S1;
      if(sl < ask)
         trade.Buy(DynamicLot(ask-sl, mm, Magic), _Symbol, ask, sl, tp, "GE_BUY");
   }
   else if(sellSignal)
   {
      double bid = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_BID);
      double sl  = (nearR1 ? weekly.R2 : weekly.R2 + atr) + atr * ATR_SL_Mult;
      double tp  = nearR1 ? weekly.PP : weekly.R1;
      if(sl > bid)
         trade.Sell(DynamicLot(sl-bid, mm, Magic), _Symbol, bid, sl, tp, "GE_SELL");
   }
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
PivotSet CalcPivot(ENUM_TIMEFRAMES tf, int shift)
{
   PivotSet pv = {0,0,0,0,0};
   double hi[], lo[], cl[];
   ArraySetAsSeries(hi, true);
   ArraySetAsSeries(lo, true);
   ArraySetAsSeries(cl, true);
   if(CopyHigh(_Symbol, tf, shift, 1, hi) < 1) return pv;
   if(CopyLow(_Symbol,  tf, shift, 1, lo) < 1) return pv;
   if(CopyClose(_Symbol,tf, shift, 1, cl) < 1) return pv;

   pv.PP = (hi[0] + lo[0] + cl[0]) / 3.0;
   pv.R1 = 2*pv.PP - lo[0];
   pv.R2 = pv.PP + (hi[0] - lo[0]);
   pv.S1 = 2*pv.PP - hi[0];
   pv.S2 = pv.PP - (hi[0] - lo[0]);
   return pv;
}
```

## 🔥 PORT 7 — 141050699 | Twin Flares
### Configurable MTF Scalping

```mql5
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| EA_TwinFlares_141050699.mq5                                      |
//| MTF Scalping — Fully Configurable                                |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
#property copyright "Port 141050699"
#property version   "2.00"

#include <Trade\Trade.mqh>
#include <MTF_Base.mqh>

input group "═══ Timeframe Settings ═══"
input ENUM_TIMEFRAMES TF_Higher   = PERIOD_H1;   // HTF — Direction
input ENUM_TIMEFRAMES TF_Middle   = PERIOD_M15;  // MTF — Intermediate
input ENUM_TIMEFRAMES TF_Entry    = PERIOD_M1;   // LTF — Scalp Entry

input group "═══ Indicator Settings ═══"
input int    EMA_Fast      = 8;
input int    EMA_Slow      = 21;
input int    EMA_HTF       = 50;   // EMA Period สำหรับ HTF Direction
input int    Vol_Period    = 20;
input double Vol_Mult      = 1.3;

input group "═══ Trade Settings ═══"
input int    TP_Pips        = 4;
input int    SL_Pips        = 6;
input int    MaxTradesDay   = 10;
input int    SessionStart   = 13;  // GMT
input int    SessionEnd     = 17;  // GMT

input group "═══ Money Management ═══"
input double Risk_Percent      = 0.5;
input double MaxDailyLoss_Pct  = 2.0;
input double MaxDrawdown_Pct   = 6.0;
input int    Max_Positions     = 3;
input double Override_MinLot   = 0.01;
input double Override_MaxLot   = 2.0;
input bool   Use_Equity        = true;  // Scalping ใช้ Equity

input int Magic = 141050699;

int h_ema_htf;
int h_ema_fast_mtf, h_ema_slow_mtf;
int h_ema_fast_entry, h_ema_slow_entry;

CTrade trade;
MoneyManagement mm;

int   tradesToday   = 0;
datetime lastDayReset = 0;

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
int OnInit()
{
   mm.RiskPercent     = Risk_Percent;
   mm.MaxDailyLossPct = MaxDailyLoss_Pct;
   mm.MaxDrawdownPct  = MaxDrawdown_Pct;
   mm.MaxPositions    = (int)MathMin(Max_Positions, 5);
   mm.MinLot          = Override_MinLot;
   mm.MaxLot          = Override_MaxLot;
   mm.UseEquity       = Use_Equity;

   h_ema_htf        = iMA(_Symbol, TF_Higher, EMA_HTF,  0, MODE_EMA, PRICE_CLOSE);
   h_ema_fast_mtf   = iMA(_Symbol, TF_Middle, EMA_Fast, 0, MODE_EMA, PRICE_CLOSE);
   h_ema_slow_mtf   = iMA(_Symbol, TF_Middle, EMA_Slow, 0, MODE_EMA, PRICE_CLOSE);
   h_ema_fast_entry = iMA(_Symbol, TF_Entry,  EMA_Fast, 0, MODE_EMA, PRICE_CLOSE);
   h_ema_slow_entry = iMA(_Symbol, TF_Entry,  EMA_Slow, 0, MODE_EMA, PRICE_CLOSE);

   trade.SetExpertMagicNumber(Magic);
   return INIT_SUCCEEDED;
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void OnTick()
{
   static datetime lastBar = 0;
   datetime currentBar = iTime(_Symbol, TF_Entry, 0);
   if(currentBar == lastBar) return;
   lastBar = currentBar;

   // Daily reset
   MqlDateTime dt; TimeToStruct(TimeCurrent(), dt);
   datetime today = StringToTime(StringFormat("%04d.%02d.%02d", dt.year, dt.mon, dt.day));
   if(today != lastDayReset) { tradesToday = 0; lastDayReset = today; }

   if(!IsInSession(SessionStart, SessionEnd)) return;
   if(tradesToday >= MaxTradesDay) return;
   if(!CanTrade(mm, Magic)) return;

   //--- HTF
   double ema_htf[]; CopyBuffer(h_ema_htf, 0, 0, 2, ema_htf);
   double price  = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_BID);
   bool htf_bull = price > ema_htf[0];
   bool htf_bear = price < ema_htf[0];

   //--- MTF
   double ef_m[], es_m[];
   CopyBuffer(h_ema_fast_mtf, 0, 0, 2, ef_m);
   CopyBuffer(h_ema_slow_mtf, 0, 0, 2, es_m);
   bool mtf_bull = ef_m[0] > es_m[0];
   bool mtf_bear = ef_m[0] < es_m[0];

   //--- LTF: EMA Cross
   double ef_e[], es_e[];
   CopyBuffer(h_ema_fast_entry, 0, 0, 3, ef_e);
   CopyBuffer(h_ema_slow_entry, 0, 0, 3, es_e);
   bool cross_up   = ef_e[1] > es_e[1] && ef_e[2] <= es_e[2];
   bool cross_down = ef_e[1] < es_e[1] && ef_e[2] >= es_e[2];

   // Volume
   long vol = iVolume(_Symbol, TF_Entry, 1);
   long volArr[]; ArraySetAsSeries(volArr, true);
   CopyTickVolume(_Symbol, TF_Entry, 1, Vol_Period, volArr);
   double avgVol = 0;
   for(int i=0;i<Vol_Period;i++) avgVol += volArr[i];
   avgVol /= Vol_Period;
   bool volOK = vol > avgVol * Vol_Mult;

   double pip = GetPipValue();
   if(htf_bull && mtf_bull && cross_up && volOK)
   {
      double ask = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_ASK);
      double sl  = ask - SL_Pips * pip;
      double tp  = ask + TP_Pips * pip;
      if(trade.Buy(DynamicLot(ask-sl,mm,Magic), _Symbol, ask, sl, tp, "TF_BUY"))
         tradesToday++;
   }
   else if(htf_bear && mtf_bear && cross_down && volOK)
   {
      double bid = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_BID);
      double sl  = bid + SL_Pips * pip;
      double tp  = bid - TP_Pips * pip;
      if(trade.Sell(DynamicLot(sl-bid,mm,Magic), _Symbol, bid, sl, tp, "TF_SELL"))
         tradesToday++;
   }
}
```

## 🔥 PORT 8 — 159503454 | Blazing Arrow
### Configurable MTF Momentum + Partial Close

```mql5
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| EA_BlazingArrow_159503454.mq5                                    |
//| MTF Momentum Scalping + Partial Close — Configurable             |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
#property copyright "Port 159503454"
#property version   "2.00"

#include <Trade\Trade.mqh>
#include <MTF_Base.mqh>

input group "═══ Timeframe Settings ═══"
input ENUM_TIMEFRAMES TF_Higher   = PERIOD_H4;   // HTF — Trend
input ENUM_TIMEFRAMES TF_Middle   = PERIOD_M15;  // MTF — Momentum
input ENUM_TIMEFRAMES TF_Entry    = PERIOD_M5;   // LTF — Engulfing

input group "═══ Indicator Settings ═══"
input int    EMA_HTF_Period    = 50;
input int    EMA_Entry_Period  = 50;
input int    RSI_Period        = 3;
input int    Mom_Period        = 10;

input group "═══ Trade Settings ═══"
input int    TP_Pips             = 12;
input int    SL_Pips             = 8;
input int    PartialClose_Pips   = 6;
input double PartialClose_Pct    = 50.0;

input group "═══ Money Management ═══"
input double Risk_Percent      = 1.0;
input double MaxDailyLoss_Pct  = 3.0;
input double MaxDrawdown_Pct   = 10.0;
input int    Max_Positions     = 3;
input double Override_MinLot   = 0.01;
input double Override_MaxLot   = 5.0;
input bool   Use_Equity        = true;

input int Magic = 159503454;

int h_ema_htf, h_ema_entry;
int h_rsi_mtf, h_mom_mtf;

   mm.MaxDailyLossPct = MaxDailyLoss_Pct;

   mm.MaxPositions    = (int)MathMin(Max_Positions, 5);

   mm.MaxLot          = Override_MaxLot;
   mm.UseEquity       = Use_Equity;

   h_ema_htf   = iMA(_Symbol,  TF_Higher, EMA_HTF_Period,   0, MODE_EMA, PRICE_CLOSE);
   h_ema_entry = iMA(_Symbol,  TF_Entry,  EMA_Entry_Period, 0, MODE_EMA, PRICE_CLOSE);
   h_rsi_mtf   = iRSI(_Symbol, TF_Middle, RSI_Period, PRICE_CLOSE);
   h_mom_mtf   = iMomentum(_Symbol, TF_Middle, Mom_Period, PRICE_CLOSE);

   trade.SetExpertMagicNumber(Magic);
   return INIT_SUCCEEDED;
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void OnTick()
{
   static datetime lastBar = 0;
   datetime currentBar = iTime(_Symbol, TF_Entry, 0);
   if(currentBar == lastBar) return;
   lastBar = currentBar;

   ManagePartialClose();
   if(!CanTrade(mm, Magic)) return;

   //--- HTF
   double ema_h[]; CopyBuffer(h_ema_htf, 0, 0, 2, ema_h);
   double price  = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_BID);
   bool htf_bull = price > ema_h[0];
   bool htf_bear = price < ema_h[0];

   //--- MTF: RSI3 + Momentum
   double rsi_m[], mom_m[];
   CopyBuffer(h_rsi_mtf, 0, 0, 2, rsi_m);
   CopyBuffer(h_mom_mtf, 0, 0, 3, mom_m);
   bool mtf_bull = rsi_m[0] > 50 && mom_m[0] > 100 && mom_m[1] <= 100;
   bool mtf_bear = rsi_m[0] < 50 && mom_m[0] < 100 && mom_m[1] >= 100;

   //--- LTF: Engulfing + EMA50
   double ema_e[]; CopyBuffer(h_ema_entry, 0, 0, 2, ema_e);
   double o1 = iOpen(_Symbol,  TF_Entry, 1), c1 = iClose(_Symbol, TF_Entry, 1);
   double o2 = iOpen(_Symbol,  TF_Entry, 2), c2 = iClose(_Symbol, TF_Entry, 2);
   bool bullEngulf = c1>o1 && c2<o2 && c1>o2 && o1<c2;
   bool bearEngulf = c1<o1 && c2>o2 && c1<o2 && o1>c2;

   double pip = GetPipValue();

   if(htf_bull && mtf_bull && bullEngulf && price > ema_e[0])
   {
      double ask = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_ASK);
      double sl  = ask - SL_Pips * pip;
      double tp  = ask + TP_Pips * pip;
      trade.Buy(DynamicLot(ask-sl, mm, Magic), _Symbol, ask, sl, tp, "BA_BUY");
   }
   else if(htf_bear && mtf_bear && bearEngulf && price < ema_e[0])
   {
      double bid = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_BID);
      double sl  = bid + SL_Pips * pip;
      double tp  = bid - TP_Pips * pip;
      trade.Sell(DynamicLot(sl-bid, mm, Magic), _Symbol, bid, sl, tp, "BA_SELL");
   }
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void ManagePartialClose()
{
   double pip = GetPipValue();
   for(int i = PositionsTotal()-1; i >= 0; i--)
   {
      ulong ticket = PositionGetTicket(i);
      if(!PositionSelectByTicket(ticket)) continue;
      if((int)PositionGetInteger(POSITION_MAGIC) != Magic) continue;

      string comment = PositionGetString(POSITION_COMMENT);
      if(StringFind(comment, "PC") >= 0) continue; // Already partial closed

      double openP = PositionGetDouble(POSITION_PRICE_OPEN);
      double vol   = PositionGetDouble(POSITION_VOLUME);
      long   typ   = PositionGetInteger(POSITION_TYPE);
      double curP  = (typ==POSITION_TYPE_BUY)
                     ? SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_BID)
                     : SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_ASK);

      double profitPips = (typ==POSITION_TYPE_BUY)
                          ? (curP - openP) / pip
                          : (openP - curP) / pip;

      if(profitPips >= PartialClose_Pips)
      {
         double lotStep  = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_VOLUME_STEP);
         double minLot   = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_VOLUME_MIN);
         double closeLot = MathFloor(vol * PartialClose_Pct/100.0 / lotStep) * lotStep;
         if(closeLot >= minLot && (vol - closeLot) >= minLot)
         {
            trade.PositionClosePartial(ticket, closeLot);
            trade.PositionModify(ticket, openP, PositionGetDouble(POSITION_TP));
         }
      }
   }
}
```
