## 🔥 PORT 9 — 159149771 | Solar Eclipse
### Configurable MTF News Straddle

```mql5
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| EA_SolarEclipse_159149771.mq5                                    |
//| MTF News Straddle — Fully Configurable                           |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
#property copyright "Port 159149771"
#property version   "2.00"

#include <Trade\Trade.mqh>
#include <MTF_Base.mqh>

input group "═══ Timeframe Settings ═══"
input ENUM_TIMEFRAMES TF_Higher   = PERIOD_D1;   // HTF — Daily Bias
input ENUM_TIMEFRAMES TF_Middle   = PERIOD_H1;   // MTF — Pre-News Range
input ENUM_TIMEFRAMES TF_Entry    = PERIOD_M15;  // LTF — Straddle Exec

input group "═══ News Settings ═══"
input string NewsTime1          = "08:30";  // GMT
input string NewsTime2          = "14:00";  // GMT
input int    MinutesBefore      = 15;
input int    TimeStop_Mins      = 30;
input double MaxSpread_Mult     = 3.0;
input int    MaxEventsPerDay    = 2;

input group "═══ Indicator Settings ═══"
input int    ATR_Period         = 14;
input double ATR_Entry_Mult     = 0.8;
input double ATR_TP_Mult        = 2.0;
input double ATR_SL_Mult        = 1.0;
input int    EMA_HTF_Period     = 200;

input group "═══ Money Management ═══"
input double Risk_Percent      = 1.0;
input double MaxDailyLoss_Pct  = 3.0;
input double MaxDrawdown_Pct   = 8.0;
input int    Max_Positions     = 2;    // Straddle = max 2
input double Override_MinLot   = 0.01;
input double Override_MaxLot   = 5.0;
input bool   Use_Equity        = false;

input int Magic = 159149771;

int h_ema_htf, h_atr_mtf, h_atr_entry;
CTrade trade;
MoneyManagement mm;

int      eventsToday = 0;
datetime lastDayReset = 0;
datetime newsT1 = 0, newsT2 = 0;

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
int OnInit()
{
   mm.RiskPercent     = Risk_Percent;
   mm.MaxDailyLossPct = MaxDailyLoss_Pct;
   mm.MaxDrawdownPct  = MaxDrawdown_Pct;
   mm.MaxPositions    = (int)MathMin(Max_Positions, 5);
   mm.MinLot          = Override_MinLot;
   mm.MaxLot          = Override_MaxLot;
   mm.UseEquity       = Use_Equity;

   h_ema_htf   = iMA(_Symbol,  TF_Higher, EMA_HTF_Period, 0, MODE_EMA, PRICE_CLOSE);
   h_atr_mtf   = iATR(_Symbol, TF_Middle, ATR_Period);
   h_atr_entry = iATR(_Symbol, TF_Entry,  ATR_Period);

   trade.SetExpertMagicNumber(Magic);
   return INIT_SUCCEEDED;
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void OnTick()
{
   MqlDateTime dt; TimeToStruct(TimeGMT(), dt);
   datetime today = StringToTime(StringFormat("%04d.%02d.%02d", dt.year, dt.mon, dt.day));
   if(today != lastDayReset)
   {
      eventsToday = 0; lastDayReset = today; newsT1 = 0; newsT2 = 0;
   }

   CheckTimeStop();
   CancelOppositePending();
   if(eventsToday >= MaxEventsPerDay) return;
   if(IsDrawdownExceeded(mm)) { CloseAllByMagic(trade, Magic); return; }
   if(IsDailyLossExceeded(mm, Magic)) return;

   if(newsT1 == 0) newsT1 = StringToTime(StringFormat("%04d.%02d.%02d %s", dt.year, dt.mon, dt.day, NewsTime1));
   if(newsT2 == 0) newsT2 = StringToTime(StringFormat("%04d.%02d.%02d %s", dt.year, dt.mon, dt.day, NewsTime2));

   datetime gmtNow = TimeGMT();
   bool shouldPlace = ((gmtNow >= newsT1 - MinutesBefore*60 && gmtNow < newsT1 - (MinutesBefore-1)*60) ||
                       (gmtNow >= newsT2 - MinutesBefore*60 && gmtNow < newsT2 - (MinutesBefore-1)*60));

   if(shouldPlace && OrdersTotal() == 0 && PositionsTotal() == 0)
      PlaceStraddle();
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void PlaceStraddle()
{
   // HTF Bias
   double ema_h[]; CopyBuffer(h_ema_htf, 0, 0, 2, ema_h);
   double price   = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_BID);
   bool d1_bull   = price > ema_h[0];

   // MTF: Pre-news range size
   double atr_m[]; CopyBuffer(h_atr_mtf,   0, 0, 2, atr_m);
   double atr_e[]; CopyBuffer(h_atr_entry, 0, 0, 2, atr_e);
   double atr      = atr_e[0];

   // Spread Filter
   double spread = SymbolInfoInteger(_Symbol, SYMBOL_SPREAD) * SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_POINT);
   if(spread > atr * 0.05 * MaxSpread_Mult) return;

   double ask = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_ASK);
   double bid = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_BID);

   double buyEntry  = ask + atr * ATR_Entry_Mult;
   double sellEntry = bid - atr * ATR_Entry_Mult;
   double lots      = DynamicLot(atr * ATR_SL_Mult, mm, Magic);
   // D1 Bias → ปรับ Lot
   double buyLots   = d1_bull ? lots * 1.2 : lots;
   double sellLots  = d1_bull ? lots : lots * 1.2;
   buyLots  = MathMin(buyLots,  mm.MaxLot);
   sellLots = MathMin(sellLots, mm.MaxLot);

   trade.BuyStop(buyLots,  buyEntry,  _Symbol,
                 buyEntry  - atr * ATR_SL_Mult,
                 buyEntry  + atr * ATR_TP_Mult,
                 ORDER_TIME_GTC, 0, "SE_BS");

   trade.SellStop(sellLots, sellEntry, _Symbol,
                  sellEntry + atr * ATR_SL_Mult,
                  sellEntry - atr * ATR_TP_Mult,
                  ORDER_TIME_GTC, 0, "SE_SS");
   eventsToday++;
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void CancelOppositePending()
{
   // เมื่อ Position เปิดแล้ว ลบ Pending ฝั่งตรงข้าม
   if(PositionsTotal() == 0) return;
   long posType = -1;
   for(int i = 0; i < PositionsTotal(); i++)
   {
      ulong t = PositionGetTicket(i);
      if(!PositionSelectByTicket(t)) continue;
      if((int)PositionGetInteger(POSITION_MAGIC) == Magic)
      { posType = PositionGetInteger(POSITION_TYPE); break; }
   }
   if(posType < 0) return;

   for(int i = OrdersTotal()-1; i >= 0; i--)
   {
      ulong t = OrderGetTicket(i);
      if(!OrderSelect(t)) continue;
      if((int)OrderGetInteger(ORDER_MAGIC) != Magic) continue;
      long ordType = OrderGetInteger(ORDER_TYPE);
      // ถ้าเป็น BUY Position → ลบ SellStop / ถ้า SELL → ลบ BuyStop
      if((posType == POSITION_TYPE_BUY  && ordType == ORDER_TYPE_SELL_STOP) ||
         (posType == POSITION_TYPE_SELL && ordType == ORDER_TYPE_BUY_STOP))
         trade.OrderDelete(t);
   }
}

void CheckTimeStop()
{
   datetime now = TimeCurrent();
   for(int i = PositionsTotal()-1; i >= 0; i--)
   {
      ulong t = PositionGetTicket(i);
      if(!PositionSelectByTicket(t)) continue;
      if((int)PositionGetInteger(POSITION_MAGIC) != Magic) continue;
      datetime ot = (datetime)PositionGetInteger(POSITION_TIME);
      if((now - ot) >= (datetime)(TimeStop_Mins * 60))
         trade.PositionClose(t);
   }
}
```

## 🔥 PORT 10 — 183977997 | Dragon's Wrath
### Configurable MTF Supertrend

```mql5
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| EA_DragonsWrath_183977997.mq5                                    |
//| MTF Supertrend Trend Catcher — Fully Configurable                |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
#property copyright "Port 183977997"
#property version   "2.00"

#include <Trade\Trade.mqh>
#include <MTF_Base.mqh>

input group "═══ Timeframe Settings ═══"
input ENUM_TIMEFRAMES TF_Higher   = PERIOD_W1;   // HTF — Macro Trend
input ENUM_TIMEFRAMES TF_Middle   = PERIOD_D1;   // MTF — ADX Strength
input ENUM_TIMEFRAMES TF_Entry    = PERIOD_H4;   // LTF — Supertrend Signal

input group "═══ Indicator Settings ═══"
input int    EMA_HTF_Fast     = 50;
input int    EMA_HTF_Slow     = 200;
input int    ADX_Period       = 14;
input double ADX_Min          = 30.0;
input double ADX_Exit         = 20.0;
input int    ST_Period        = 10;    // Supertrend ATR Period
input double ST_Multiplier    = 3.0;   // Supertrend Multiplier
input int    ATR_SL_Period    = 14;
input double HardSL_ATR_Mult  = 2.0;

input group "═══ Money Management ═══"
input double Risk_Percent      = 1.5;
input double MaxDailyLoss_Pct  = 4.5;
input double MaxDrawdown_Pct   = 15.0;
input int    Max_Positions     = 2;
input double Override_MinLot   = 0.01;
input double Override_MaxLot   = 10.0;
input bool   Use_Equity        = false;

input int Magic = 183977997;

int h_ema_fast_htf, h_ema_slow_htf;
int h_adx_mtf;
int h_atr_entry, h_atr_sl;

CTrade trade;
MoneyManagement mm;

// Supertrend state
double prevUpperBand = 0, prevLowerBand = 0;
int    prevTrend     = 0;

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
int OnInit()
{
   mm.RiskPercent     = Risk_Percent;
   mm.MaxDailyLossPct = MaxDailyLoss_Pct;
   mm.MaxDrawdownPct  = MaxDrawdown_Pct;
   mm.MaxPositions    = (int)MathMin(Max_Positions, 5);
   mm.MinLot          = Override_MinLot;
   mm.MaxLot          = Override_MaxLot;
   mm.UseEquity       = Use_Equity;

   h_ema_fast_htf = iMA(_Symbol,  TF_Higher, EMA_HTF_Fast, 0, MODE_EMA, PRICE_CLOSE);
   h_ema_slow_htf = iMA(_Symbol,  TF_Higher, EMA_HTF_Slow, 0, MODE_EMA, PRICE_CLOSE);
   h_adx_mtf      = iADX(_Symbol, TF_Middle, ADX_Period);
   h_atr_entry    = iATR(_Symbol, TF_Entry,  ST_Period);
   h_atr_sl       = iATR(_Symbol, TF_Middle, ATR_SL_Period);

   trade.SetExpertMagicNumber(Magic);
   // Warmup Supertrend
   prevUpperBand = 0; prevLowerBand = 0; prevTrend = 0;
   return INIT_SUCCEEDED;
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void OnTick()
{
   static datetime lastBar = 0;
   datetime currentBar = iTime(_Symbol, TF_Entry, 0);
   if(currentBar == lastBar) return;
   lastBar = currentBar;

   if(CountMagicPositions(Magic) > 0) { ManageSupertrend(); return; }
   if(!CanTrade(mm, Magic)) return;

   //--- HTF: EMA Cross
   double ef_h[], es_h[];
   CopyBuffer(h_ema_fast_htf, 0, 0, 2, ef_h);
   CopyBuffer(h_ema_slow_htf, 0, 0, 2, es_h);
   bool htf_bull = ef_h[0] > es_h[0];
   bool htf_bear = ef_h[0] < es_h[0];

   //--- MTF: ADX
   double adx_m[];
   CopyBuffer(h_adx_mtf, MAIN_LINE, 0, 2, adx_m);
   if(adx_m[0] < ADX_Min) return;

   //--- LTF: Supertrend Signal
   double atr_e[];
   CopyBuffer(h_atr_entry, 0, 0, 3, atr_e);
   int stSignal = CalcSupertrend(atr_e[1]);

   double atr_sl[];
   CopyBuffer(h_atr_sl, 0, 0, 2, atr_sl);

   if(htf_bull && stSignal == 1)
   {
      double ask = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_ASK);
      double sl  = ask - atr_sl[0] * HardSL_ATR_Mult;
      trade.Buy(DynamicLot(ask-sl, mm, Magic), _Symbol, ask, sl, 0, "DW_BUY");
   }
   else if(htf_bear && stSignal == -1)
   {
      double bid = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_BID);
      double sl  = bid + atr_sl[0] * HardSL_ATR_Mult;
      trade.Sell(DynamicLot(sl-bid, mm, Magic), _Symbol, bid, sl, 0, "DW_SELL");
   }
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
int CalcSupertrend(double atr)
{
   double hi1 = iHigh(_Symbol,  TF_Entry, 1), lo1 = iLow(_Symbol,  TF_Entry, 1);
   double cl1 = iClose(_Symbol, TF_Entry, 1), cl2 = iClose(_Symbol, TF_Entry, 2);
   double hl2 = (hi1 + lo1) / 2.0;

   double upper = hl2 + ST_Multiplier * atr;
   double lower = hl2 - ST_Multiplier * atr;

   double fUpper = (prevUpperBand > 0 && (upper < prevUpperBand || cl2 > prevUpperBand)) ? upper : prevUpperBand;
   double fLower = (prevLowerBand > 0 && (lower > prevLowerBand || cl2 < prevLowerBand)) ? lower : prevLowerBand;
   if(prevUpperBand == 0) fUpper = upper;
   if(prevLowerBand == 0) fLower = lower;

   int trend  = (cl1 > fUpper) ? 1 : (cl1 < fLower ? -1 : prevTrend);
   int signal = 0;
   if(trend ==  1 && prevTrend != 1)  signal =  1;
   if(trend == -1 && prevTrend != -1) signal = -1;

   prevUpperBand = fUpper;
   prevLowerBand = fLower;
   prevTrend     = trend;
   return signal;
}

void ManageSupertrend()
{
   double adx_m[];
   CopyBuffer(h_adx_mtf, MAIN_LINE, 0, 2, adx_m);
   if(adx_m[0] < ADX_Exit || IsDrawdownExceeded(mm))
   { CloseAllByMagic(trade, Magic); return; }

   double atr_e[];
   CopyBuffer(h_atr_entry, 0, 0, 2, atr_e);
   double hi1 = iHigh(_Symbol, TF_Entry, 1), lo1 = iLow(_Symbol, TF_Entry, 1);
   double hl2  = (hi1 + lo1) / 2.0;
   double trailBuy  = hl2 - ST_Multiplier * atr_e[0];
   double trailSell = hl2 + ST_Multiplier * atr_e[0];

   for(int i = PositionsTotal()-1; i >= 0; i--)
   {
      ulong t = PositionGetTicket(i);
      if(!PositionSelectByTicket(t)) continue;
      if((int)PositionGetInteger(POSITION_MAGIC) != Magic) continue;
      if(PositionGetDouble(POSITION_PROFIT) <= 0) continue;

      double sl  = PositionGetDouble(POSITION_SL);
      long   typ = PositionGetInteger(POSITION_TYPE);

      if(typ == POSITION_TYPE_BUY  && trailBuy  > sl)
         trade.PositionModify(t, trailBuy,  0);
      else if(typ == POSITION_TYPE_SELL && trailSell < sl)
         trade.PositionModify(t, trailSell, 0);
   }
}
```

## 🔥 PORT 11 — 141058396 | Ember Strike
### Configurable MTF Session Trading

```mql5
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| EA_EmberStrike_141058396.mq5                                     |
//| MTF Session Overlap Trading — Fully Configurable                 |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
#property copyright "Port 141058396"
#property version   "2.00"

#include <Trade\Trade.mqh>
#include <MTF_Base.mqh>

input group "═══ Timeframe Settings ═══"
input ENUM_TIMEFRAMES TF_Higher   = PERIOD_D1;   // HTF — Daily Bias
input ENUM_TIMEFRAMES TF_Middle   = PERIOD_H1;   // MTF — H1 Trend
input ENUM_TIMEFRAMES TF_Entry    = PERIOD_M30;  // LTF — M30 Entry

input group "═══ Indicator Settings ═══"
input int    EMA_Fast_HTF    = 200;   // HTF EMA
input int    EMA_Fast_MTF    = 21;    // MTF EMA Fast
input int    EMA_Slow_MTF    = 50;    // MTF EMA Slow
input int    EMA_Fast_Entry  = 21;    // Entry EMA Fast
input int    EMA_Slow_Entry  = 50;    // Entry EMA Slow
input int    RSI_Period      = 14;

input group "═══ Session Settings ═══"
input int    SessionStart    = 13;   // GMT — London-NY Open
input int    SessionEnd      = 17;   // GMT — Force Close Hour

input group "═══ Trade Settings ═══"
input int    TP_Pips         = 20;
input int    SL_Pips         = 10;

input group "═══ Money Management ═══"
input double Risk_Percent      = 1.0;
input double MaxDailyLoss_Pct  = 3.0;
input double MaxDrawdown_Pct   = 8.0;
input int    Max_Positions     = 3;
input double Override_MinLot   = 0.01;
input double Override_MaxLot   = 5.0;
input bool   Use_Equity        = true;

input int Magic = 141058396;

int h_ema_htf;
int h_ema_fast_mtf, h_ema_slow_mtf;
int h_ema_fast_entry, h_ema_slow_entry;
int h_rsi_entry;

CTrade trade;
MoneyManagement mm;
bool forceClosed = false;

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
int OnInit()
{
   mm.RiskPercent     = Risk_Percent;
   mm.MaxDailyLossPct = MaxDailyLoss_Pct;
   mm.MaxDrawdownPct  = MaxDrawdown_Pct;
   mm.MaxPositions    = (int)MathMin(Max_Positions, 5);
   mm.MinLot          = Override_MinLot;
   mm.MaxLot          = Override_MaxLot;
   mm.UseEquity       = Use_Equity;

   h_ema_htf         = iMA(_Symbol, TF_Higher, EMA_Fast_HTF,   0, MODE_EMA, PRICE_CLOSE);
   h_ema_fast_mtf    = iMA(_Symbol, TF_Middle, EMA_Fast_MTF,   0, MODE_EMA, PRICE_CLOSE);
   h_ema_slow_mtf    = iMA(_Symbol, TF_Middle, EMA_Slow_MTF,   0, MODE_EMA, PRICE_CLOSE);
   h_ema_fast_entry  = iMA(_Symbol, TF_Entry,  EMA_Fast_Entry, 0, MODE_EMA, PRICE_CLOSE);
   h_ema_slow_entry  = iMA(_Symbol, TF_Entry,  EMA_Slow_Entry, 0, MODE_EMA, PRICE_CLOSE);
   h_rsi_entry       = iRSI(_Symbol, TF_Entry, RSI_Period, PRICE_CLOSE);

   trade.SetExpertMagicNumber(Magic);
   return INIT_SUCCEEDED;
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void OnTick()
{
   static datetime lastBar = 0;
   datetime currentBar = iTime(_Symbol, TF_Entry, 0);
   if(currentBar == lastBar) return;
   lastBar = currentBar;

   // ── Force Close เมื่อหมด Session ──
   if(!IsInSession(SessionStart, SessionEnd))
   {
      if(!forceClosed)
      {
         CloseAllByMagic(trade, Magic);
         forceClosed = true;
      }
      return;
   }
   forceClosed = false;

   if(!CanTrade(mm, Magic)) return;

   //--- HTF: Daily EMA200 Bias
   double ema_h[]; CopyBuffer(h_ema_htf, 0, 0, 2, ema_h);
   double price  = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_BID);
   bool htf_bull = price > ema_h[0];
   bool htf_bear = price < ema_h[0];

   //--- MTF: H1 EMA Trend
   double ef_m[], es_m[];
   CopyBuffer(h_ema_fast_mtf, 0, 0, 2, ef_m);
   CopyBuffer(h_ema_slow_mtf, 0, 0, 2, es_m);
   bool mtf_bull = ef_m[0] > es_m[0];
   bool mtf_bear = ef_m[0] < es_m[0];

   //--- LTF: M30 EMA Cross + RSI
   double ef_e[], es_e[], rsi_v[];
   CopyBuffer(h_ema_fast_entry, 0, 0, 3, ef_e);
   CopyBuffer(h_ema_slow_entry, 0, 0, 3, es_e);
   CopyBuffer(h_rsi_entry,      0, 0, 2, rsi_v);

   bool cross_up   = ef_e[1] > es_e[1] && ef_e[2] <= es_e[2] && rsi_v[0] > 50;
   bool cross_down = ef_e[1] < es_e[1] && ef_e[2] >= es_e[2] && rsi_v[0] < 50;

   double pip = GetPipValue();

   if(htf_bull && mtf_bull && cross_up)
   {
      double ask = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_ASK);
      double sl  = ask - SL_Pips * pip;
      double tp  = ask + TP_Pips * pip;
      trade.Buy(DynamicLot(ask-sl, mm, Magic), _Symbol, ask, sl, tp, "ES_BUY");
   }
   else if(htf_bear && mtf_bear && cross_down)
   {
      double bid = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_BID);
      double sl  = bid + SL_Pips * pip;
      double tp  = bid - TP_Pips * pip;
      trade.Sell(DynamicLot(sl-bid, mm, Magic), _Symbol, bid, sl, tp, "ES_SELL");
   }
}
```

---

## 📦 สรุป Configuration ที่ Customize ได้ทุก EA

| Feature | ทุก EA รองรับ |
|---|---|
| **TF_Higher / TF_Middle / TF_Entry** | ✅ เปลี่ยน TF ได้ทุกตัว |
| **Risk_Percent** | ✅ Dynamic Lot ตาม % Risk |
| **Max_Positions** | ✅ จำกัด 1-5 ไม้ (Hard cap = 5) |
| **MaxDailyLoss_Pct** | ✅ หยุดเทรดเมื่อขาดทุนเกินวันนี้ |
| **MaxDrawdown_Pct** | ✅ ปิดทั้งหมดเมื่อ DD เกิน |
| **Use_Equity** | ✅ เลือก Balance หรือ Equity คำนวณ Lot |
| **Override_MinLot / MaxLot** | ✅ กำหนด Lot Range เอง |
| **MTF_Base.mqh** | ✅ ใช้ร่วมกันทุก EA ไม่ต้องเขียนซ้ำ |

> **วิธีใช้:** วาง `MTF_Base.mqh` ไว้ที่ `MQL5/Include/` แล้ว Compile แต่ละ EA ได้เลยครับบอส 🔥

----- [user] -----
ขอแก้เป็น stand alone ดีกว่า ให้ไปอยู่ใน EA แต่ละตัวให้หน่อย

----- [assistant] -----
ได้เลยครับบอส — รวม MTF_Base เข้าไปใน EA แต่ละตัว Standalone 100% ไม่ต้องพึ่ง include ไฟล์ภายนอก

---

## 🌟 PORT 1 — 141049900 | Celestial Woodfire

```mql5
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| EA_CelestialWoodfire_141049900.mq5                               |
//| MTF Trend Following — Standalone                                 |
//| HTF: Trend Direction | MTF: Confirm | LTF: Entry                 |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
#property copyright "Port 141049900"
#property version   "2.00"
#property strict

#include <Trade\Trade.mqh>

//────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
//  INPUT GROUPS
//────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
input group "═══ Timeframe Settings ═══"
input ENUM_TIMEFRAMES TF_Higher  = PERIOD_D1;
input ENUM_TIMEFRAMES TF_Middle  = PERIOD_H4;
input ENUM_TIMEFRAMES TF_Entry   = PERIOD_H1;

input group "═══ Indicator Settings ═══"
input int    EMA_Fast      = 50;
input int    EMA_Slow      = 200;
input int    MACD_Fast     = 12;
input int    MACD_Slow     = 26;
input int    MACD_Signal   = 9;
input int    ADX_Period    = 14;
input double ADX_Min       = 25.0;
input int    ATR_Period    = 14;

input group "═══ Money Management ═══"
input double Risk_Percent      = 1.0;
input double MaxDailyLoss_Pct  = 3.0;
input double MaxDrawdown_Pct   = 10.0;
input int    Max_Positions     = 2;
input double ATR_SL_Mult       = 1.5;
input double ATR_TP_Mult       = 3.0;
input double Min_Lot           = 0.01;
input double Max_Lot           = 5.0;
input bool   Use_Equity        = false;

input group "═══ Trailing Stop ═══"
input bool   Enable_Trail    = true;
input double Trail_ATR_Mult  = 1.0;

input int Magic = 141049900;

//────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
//  GLOBALS
//────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
CTrade trade;
int h_ema_fast_htf, h_ema_slow_htf;
int h_ema_fast_mtf, h_ema_slow_mtf;
int h_macd_entry, h_adx_entry, h_atr_entry;

//────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
//  MONEY MANAGEMENT FUNCTIONS
//────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
double CalcDynamicLot(double slDistance)
{
   double base      = Use_Equity ? AccountInfoDouble(ACCOUNT_EQUITY)
                                 : AccountInfoDouble(ACCOUNT_BALANCE);
   double riskAmt   = base * Risk_Percent / 100.0;
   double tickVal   = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_TRADE_TICK_VALUE);
   double tickSize  = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_TRADE_TICK_SIZE);
   double lotStep   = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_VOLUME_STEP);
   double brokerMin = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_VOLUME_MIN);
   double brokerMax = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_VOLUME_MAX);
   double minL      = MathMax(Min_Lot, brokerMin);
   double maxL      = MathMin(Max_Lot, brokerMax);
   if(slDistance <= 0 || tickVal <= 0 || tickSize <= 0) return minL;
   double lots = riskAmt / (slDistance / tickSize * tickVal);
   lots = MathFloor(lots / lotStep) * lotStep;
   return MathMax(minL, MathMin(maxL, lots));
}

int CountMyPositions()
{
   int n = 0;
   for(int i = 0; i < PositionsTotal(); i++)
   {
      ulong t = PositionGetTicket(i);
      if(PositionSelectByTicket(t) &&
         (int)PositionGetInteger(POSITION_MAGIC) == Magic) n++;
   }
   return n;
}

bool IsDailyLossBreached()
{
   double balance  = AccountInfoDouble(ACCOUNT_BALANCE);
   double maxLoss  = balance * MaxDailyLoss_Pct / 100.0;
   datetime dayStart = StringToTime(TimeToString(TimeCurrent(), TIME_DATE));
   double pl = 0;
   for(int i = 0; i < PositionsTotal(); i++)
   {
      ulong t = PositionGetTicket(i);
      if(!PositionSelectByTicket(t)) continue;
      if((int)PositionGetInteger(POSITION_MAGIC) != Magic) continue;
      pl += PositionGetDouble(POSITION_PROFIT)
          + PositionGetDouble(POSITION_SWAP)
          + PositionGetDouble(POSITION_COMMISSION);
   }
   HistorySelect(dayStart, TimeCurrent());
   for(int i = 0; i < HistoryDealsTotal(); i++)
   {
      ulong d = HistoryDealGetTicket(i);
      if((int)HistoryDealGetInteger(d, DEAL_MAGIC) != Magic) continue;
      if(HistoryDealGetInteger(d, DEAL_ENTRY) == DEAL_ENTRY_OUT)
         pl += HistoryDealGetDouble(d, DEAL_PROFIT)
             + HistoryDealGetDouble(d, DEAL_SWAP)
             + HistoryDealGetDouble(d, DEAL_COMMISSION);
   }
   return (pl <= -maxLoss);
}

bool IsDrawdownBreached()
{
   double bal = AccountInfoDouble(ACCOUNT_BALANCE);
   double eq  = AccountInfoDouble(ACCOUNT_EQUITY);
   return (bal > 0 && (bal - eq) / bal * 100.0 >= MaxDrawdown_Pct);
}

bool CanTrade()
{
   int cap = (int)MathMin(Max_Positions, 5);
   if(CountMyPositions() >= cap)  return false;
   if(IsDailyLossBreached())      return false;
   if(IsDrawdownBreached())       return false;
   return true;
}

void CloseAll()
{
   for(int i = PositionsTotal()-1; i >= 0; i--)
   {
      ulong t = PositionGetTicket(i);
      if(PositionSelectByTicket(t) &&
         (int)PositionGetInteger(POSITION_MAGIC) == Magic)
         trade.PositionClose(t);
   }
}

double GetPip()
{
   double point = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_POINT);
   int    digs  = (int)SymbolInfoInteger(_Symbol, SYMBOL_DIGITS);
   return (digs == 5 || digs == 3) ? point * 10.0 : point;
}

//────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
//  ONINIT
//────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
int OnInit()
{
   h_ema_fast_htf = iMA(_Symbol, TF_Higher, EMA_Fast,  0, MODE_EMA, PRICE_CLOSE);
   h_ema_slow_htf = iMA(_Symbol, TF_Higher, EMA_Slow,  0, MODE_EMA, PRICE_CLOSE);
   h_ema_fast_mtf = iMA(_Symbol, TF_Middle, EMA_Fast,  0, MODE_EMA, PRICE_CLOSE);
   h_ema_slow_mtf = iMA(_Symbol, TF_Middle, EMA_Slow,  0, MODE_EMA, PRICE_CLOSE);
   h_macd_entry   = iMACD(_Symbol, TF_Entry, MACD_Fast, MACD_Slow, MACD_Signal, PRICE_CLOSE);
   h_adx_entry    = iADX(_Symbol,  TF_Entry, ADX_Period);
   h_atr_entry    = iATR(_Symbol,  TF_Entry, ATR_Period);
   if(h_ema_fast_htf < 0 || h_ema_slow_htf < 0 || h_macd_entry < 0)
   { Alert("Handle error — check symbol/TF"); return INIT_FAILED; }
   trade.SetExpertMagicNumber(Magic);
   Print("CelestialWoodfire Ready | TF:", EnumToString(TF_Higher),
         "/", EnumToString(TF_Middle), "/", EnumToString(TF_Entry));
   return INIT_SUCCEEDED;
}

void OnDeinit(const int reason)
{
   IndicatorRelease(h_ema_fast_htf); IndicatorRelease(h_ema_slow_htf);
   IndicatorRelease(h_ema_fast_mtf); IndicatorRelease(h_ema_slow_mtf);
   IndicatorRelease(h_macd_entry);   IndicatorRelease(h_adx_entry);
   IndicatorRelease(h_atr_entry);
}

//────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
//  ONTICK
//────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
void OnTick()
{
   static datetime lastBar = 0;
   datetime curBar = iTime(_Symbol, TF_Entry, 0);
   if(curBar == lastBar) return;
   lastBar = curBar;

   if(IsDrawdownBreached()) { CloseAll(); return; }
   if(Enable_Trail) ManageTrailing();
   if(!CanTrade()) return;

   //--- HTF
   double ef_h[2], es_h[2];
   CopyBuffer(h_ema_fast_htf, 0, 0, 2, ef_h);
   CopyBuffer(h_ema_slow_htf, 0, 0, 2, es_h);
   bool htf_bull = ef_h[0] > es_h[0];
   bool htf_bear = ef_h[0] < es_h[0];

   //--- MTF
   double ef_m[2], es_m[2];
   CopyBuffer(h_ema_fast_mtf, 0, 0, 2, ef_m);
   CopyBuffer(h_ema_slow_mtf, 0, 0, 2, es_m);
   bool mtf_bull = ef_m[0] > es_m[0];
   bool mtf_bear = ef_m[0] < es_m[0];

   //--- LTF
   double macd_m[3], macd_s[3], adx_v[2], atr_v[2];
   CopyBuffer(h_macd_entry, MAIN_LINE,   0, 3, macd_m);
   CopyBuffer(h_macd_entry, SIGNAL_LINE, 0, 3, macd_s);
   CopyBuffer(h_adx_entry,  MAIN_LINE,   0, 2, adx_v);
   CopyBuffer(h_atr_entry,  0,           0, 2, atr_v);

   bool macd_up   = macd_m[1] > macd_s[1] && macd_m[2] <= macd_s[2];
   bool macd_dn   = macd_m[1] < macd_s[1] && macd_m[2] >= macd_s[2];
   bool adx_ok    = adx_v[0] > ADX_Min;
   double atr     = atr_v[0];

   if(htf_bull && mtf_bull && macd_up && adx_ok)
   {
      double ask = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_ASK);
      double sl  = ask - atr * ATR_SL_Mult;
      double tp  = ask + atr * ATR_TP_Mult;
      trade.Buy(CalcDynamicLot(ask - sl), _Symbol, ask, sl, tp, "CW_BUY");
   }
   else if(htf_bear && mtf_bear && macd_dn && adx_ok)
   {
      double bid = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_BID);
      double sl  = bid + atr * ATR_SL_Mult;
      double tp  = bid - atr * ATR_TP_Mult;
      trade.Sell(CalcDynamicLot(sl - bid), _Symbol, bid, sl, tp, "CW_SELL");
   }
}

//────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
void ManageTrailing()
{
   double ef_m[2], atr_v[2];
   CopyBuffer(h_ema_fast_mtf, 0, 0, 2, ef_m);
   CopyBuffer(h_atr_entry,    0, 0, 2, atr_v);
   double atr = atr_v[0];

   for(int i = PositionsTotal()-1; i >= 0; i--)
   {
      ulong t = PositionGetTicket(i);
      if(!PositionSelectByTicket(t)) continue;
      if((int)PositionGetInteger(POSITION_MAGIC) != Magic) continue;
      if(PositionGetDouble(POSITION_PROFIT) <= 0) continue;

      double sl  = PositionGetDouble(POSITION_SL);
      double tp  = PositionGetDouble(POSITION_TP);
      long   typ = PositionGetInteger(POSITION_TYPE);

      if(typ == POSITION_TYPE_BUY)
      {
         double nsl = ef_m[0] - atr * Trail_ATR_Mult;
         if(nsl > sl && nsl < SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_BID))
            trade.PositionModify(t, nsl, tp);
      }
      else
      {
         double nsl = ef_m[0] + atr * Trail_ATR_Mult;
         if(nsl < sl && nsl > SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_ASK))
            trade.PositionModify(t, nsl, tp);
      }
   }
}
```

## 🌟 PORT 2 — 146004104 | Ironclad Forest

```mql5
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| EA_IroncladForest_146004104.mq5                                  |
//| MTF Smart Grid/DCA — Standalone                                  |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
#property copyright "Port 146004104"
#property version   "2.00"
#property strict

#include <Trade\Trade.mqh>

input group "═══ Timeframe Settings ═══"
input ENUM_TIMEFRAMES TF_Higher  = PERIOD_W1;
input ENUM_TIMEFRAMES TF_Middle  = PERIOD_D1;
input ENUM_TIMEFRAMES TF_Entry   = PERIOD_H1;

input group "═══ Indicator Settings ═══"
input int    BB_Period      = 20;
input double BB_Dev_HTF     = 2.0;
input double BB_Dev_Entry   = 2.0;
input int    RSI_Period     = 14;
input int    ATR_Period     = 14;

input group "═══ Grid Settings ═══"
input int    MaxGridLevels   = 4;
input double GridATR_Mult    = 1.0;
input double LotMultiplier   = 1.5;

input group "═══ Money Management ═══"
input double Risk_Percent      = 1.0;
input double MaxDailyLoss_Pct  = 3.0;
input double MaxDrawdown_Pct   = 10.0;
input int    Max_Positions     = 4;
input double Base_Lot          = 0.01;
input double Max_Lot           = 2.0;
input double BasketTP_Pct      = 1.0;
input double BasketSL_Pct      = 3.0;
input bool   Use_Equity        = false;

input int Magic = 146004104;

CTrade trade;
int h_bb_htf, h_bb_mtf, h_bb_entry, h_rsi_entry, h_atr_entry;
bool gridBullish = false;

//────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
int CountMyPositions()
{
   int n = 0;
   for(int i = 0; i < PositionsTotal(); i++)
   {
      ulong t = PositionGetTicket(i);
      if(PositionSelectByTicket(t) &&
         (int)PositionGetInteger(POSITION_MAGIC) == Magic) n++;
   }
   return n;
}

bool IsDailyLossBreached()
{
   double balance = AccountInfoDouble(ACCOUNT_BALANCE);
   double maxLoss = balance * MaxDailyLoss_Pct / 100.0;
   datetime dayStart = StringToTime(TimeToString(TimeCurrent(), TIME_DATE));
   double pl = 0;
   for(int i = 0; i < PositionsTotal(); i++)
   {
      ulong t = PositionGetTicket(i);
      if(!PositionSelectByTicket(t)) continue;
      if((int)PositionGetInteger(POSITION_MAGIC) != Magic) continue;
      pl += PositionGetDouble(POSITION_PROFIT)
          + PositionGetDouble(POSITION_SWAP)
          + PositionGetDouble(POSITION_COMMISSION);
   }
   HistorySelect(dayStart, TimeCurrent());
   for(int i = 0; i < HistoryDealsTotal(); i++)
   {
      ulong d = HistoryDealGetTicket(i);
      if((int)HistoryDealGetInteger(d, DEAL_MAGIC) != Magic) continue;
      if(HistoryDealGetInteger(d, DEAL_ENTRY) == DEAL_ENTRY_OUT)
         pl += HistoryDealGetDouble(d, DEAL_PROFIT)
             + HistoryDealGetDouble(d, DEAL_SWAP)
             + HistoryDealGetDouble(d, DEAL_COMMISSION);
   }
   return (pl <= -maxLoss);
}

bool IsDrawdownBreached()
{
   double bal = AccountInfoDouble(ACCOUNT_BALANCE);
   double eq  = AccountInfoDouble(ACCOUNT_EQUITY);
   return (bal > 0 && (bal - eq) / bal * 100.0 >= MaxDrawdown_Pct);
}

void CloseAll()
{
   for(int i = PositionsTotal()-1; i >= 0; i--)
   {
      ulong t = PositionGetTicket(i);
      if(PositionSelectByTicket(t) &&
         (int)PositionGetInteger(POSITION_MAGIC) == Magic)
         trade.PositionClose(t);
   }
}

double GetLastGridPrice()
{
   double lp = 0; datetime lt = 0;
   for(int i = 0; i < PositionsTotal(); i++)
   {
      ulong t = PositionGetTicket(i);
      if(!PositionSelectByTicket(t)) continue;
      if((int)PositionGetInteger(POSITION_MAGIC) != Magic) continue;
      datetime pt = (datetime)PositionGetInteger(POSITION_TIME);
      if(pt > lt) { lt = pt; lp = PositionGetDouble(POSITION_PRICE_OPEN); }
   }
   return lp;
}

double CalcGridLot(int level)
{
   double base    = Use_Equity ? AccountInfoDouble(ACCOUNT_EQUITY)
                               : AccountInfoDouble(ACCOUNT_BALANCE);
   double lots    = Base_Lot * MathPow(LotMultiplier, level);
   double lotStep = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_VOLUME_STEP);
   double minL    = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_VOLUME_MIN);
   double maxL    = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_VOLUME_MAX);
   lots = MathFloor(lots / lotStep) * lotStep;
   return MathMax(MathMax(Base_Lot, minL), MathMin(MathMin(Max_Lot, maxL), lots));
}

void CheckBasketExit()
{
   double balance = AccountInfoDouble(ACCOUNT_BALANCE);
   double pl = 0; int cnt = 0;
   for(int i = 0; i < PositionsTotal(); i++)
   {
      ulong t = PositionGetTicket(i);
      if(!PositionSelectByTicket(t)) continue;
      if((int)PositionGetInteger(POSITION_MAGIC) != Magic) continue;
      pl += PositionGetDouble(POSITION_PROFIT)
          + PositionGetDouble(POSITION_SWAP)
          + PositionGetDouble(POSITION_COMMISSION);
      cnt++;
   }
   if(cnt == 0) return;
   if(pl >=  balance * BasketTP_Pct / 100.0 ||
      pl <= -balance * BasketSL_Pct / 100.0) CloseAll();
}

//────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
int OnInit()
{
   h_bb_htf    = iBands(_Symbol, TF_Higher, BB_Period, 0, BB_Dev_HTF,   PRICE_CLOSE);
   h_bb_mtf    = iBands(_Symbol, TF_Middle, BB_Period, 0, BB_Dev_HTF,   PRICE_CLOSE);
   h_bb_entry  = iBands(_Symbol, TF_Entry,  BB_Period, 0, BB_Dev_Entry, PRICE_CLOSE);
   h_rsi_entry = iRSI(_Symbol,   TF_Entry,  RSI_Period, PRICE_CLOSE);
   h_atr_entry = iATR(_Symbol,   TF_Entry,  ATR_Period);
   trade.SetExpertMagicNumber(Magic);
   return INIT_SUCCEEDED;
}

void OnDeinit(const int reason)
{
   IndicatorRelease(h_bb_htf);   IndicatorRelease(h_bb_mtf);
   IndicatorRelease(h_bb_entry); IndicatorRelease(h_rsi_entry);
   IndicatorRelease(h_atr_entry);
}

void OnTick()
{
   static datetime lastBar = 0;
   datetime curBar = iTime(_Symbol, TF_Entry, 0);
   if(curBar == lastBar) return;
   lastBar = curBar;

   if(IsDrawdownBreached()) { CloseAll(); return; }
   CheckBasketExit();
   if(IsDailyLossBreached()) return;

   double price = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_BID);

   //--- HTF: ราคาต้องอยู่ในกรอบ BB
   double htf_u[2], htf_l[2];
   CopyBuffer(h_bb_htf, 1, 0, 2, htf_u);
   CopyBuffer(h_bb_htf, 2, 0, 2, htf_l);
   if(price < htf_l[0] || price > htf_u[0]) return;

   //--- MTF: BB Middle Bias
   double mtf_m[2];
   CopyBuffer(h_bb_mtf, 0, 0, 2, mtf_m);
   bool mtf_bull = price < mtf_m[0];
   bool mtf_bear = price > mtf_m[0];

   //--- LTF
   double en_u[2], en_l[2], rsi_v[2], atr_v[2];
   CopyBuffer(h_bb_entry,  1, 0, 2, en_u);
   CopyBuffer(h_bb_entry,  2, 0, 2, en_l);
   CopyBuffer(h_rsi_entry, 0, 0, 2, rsi_v);
   CopyBuffer(h_atr_entry, 0, 0, 2, atr_v);
   double atr = atr_v[0];
   bool buyOK  = price < en_l[0] && rsi_v[0] < 30;
   bool sellOK = price > en_u[0] && rsi_v[0] > 70;

   int maxPos  = (int)MathMin(Max_Positions, 5);
   int maxGrid = (int)MathMin(MaxGridLevels, 4);
   int cnt     = CountMyPositions();

   if(cnt == 0)
   {
      if(mtf_bull && buyOK)
      { gridBullish = true;  double ask=SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol,SYMBOL_ASK);
        trade.Buy(CalcGridLot(0),  _Symbol, ask, 0, 0, "IF_BUY_L0"); }
      else if(mtf_bear && sellOK)
      { gridBullish = false; double bid=SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol,SYMBOL_BID);
        trade.Sell(CalcGridLot(0), _Symbol, bid, 0, 0, "IF_SELL_L0"); }
   }
   else if(cnt < maxGrid && cnt < maxPos)
   {
      double lastP = GetLastGridPrice();
      if(gridBullish  && price < lastP - atr * GridATR_Mult)
      { double ask=SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol,SYMBOL_ASK);
        trade.Buy(CalcGridLot(cnt),  _Symbol, ask, 0, 0, "IF_BUY_L"+IntegerToString(cnt)); }
      else if(!gridBullish && price > lastP + atr * GridATR_Mult)
      { double bid=SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol,SYMBOL_BID);
        trade.Sell(CalcGridLot(cnt), _Symbol, bid, 0, 0, "IF_SELL_L"+IntegerToString(cnt)); }
   }
}
```

## 🌟 PORT 3 — 193234160 | Dawn Ascendant

```mql5
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| EA_DawnAscendant_193234160.mq5                                   |
//| MTF Volatility Breakout — Standalone                             |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
#property copyright "Port 193234160"
#property version   "2.00"
#property strict

#include <Trade\Trade.mqh>

input group "═══ Timeframe Settings ═══"
input ENUM_TIMEFRAMES TF_Higher  = PERIOD_W1;
input ENUM_TIMEFRAMES TF_Middle  = PERIOD_D1;
input ENUM_TIMEFRAMES TF_Entry   = PERIOD_H4;

input group "═══ Indicator Settings ═══"
input int    Donchian_Period  = 20;
input int    ATR_Period       = 14;
input double ATR_Entry_Mult   = 0.5;
input double ATR_TP_Mult      = 2.0;
input double ATR_SL_Mult      = 1.0;
input int    EMA_HTF_Period   = 200;
input int    Vol_Period       = 20;
input double Vol_Mult         = 1.5;

input group "═══ Money Management ═══"
input double Risk_Percent      = 1.5;
input double MaxDailyLoss_Pct  = 4.0;
input double MaxDrawdown_Pct   = 12.0;
input int    Max_Positions     = 2;
input double Min_Lot           = 0.01;
input double Max_Lot           = 5.0;
input bool   Use_Equity        = false;

input group "═══ Trailing Stop ═══"
input bool   Enable_Trail    = true;
input double Trail_ATR_Mult  = 1.0;

input int Magic = 193234160;

CTrade trade;
int h_ema_htf, h_atr_mtf, h_atr_entry;

//────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
double CalcDynamicLot(double slDist)
{
   double base     = Use_Equity ? AccountInfoDouble(ACCOUNT_EQUITY)
                                : AccountInfoDouble(ACCOUNT_BALANCE);
   double riskAmt  = base * Risk_Percent / 100.0;
   double tickVal  = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_TRADE_TICK_VALUE);
   double tickSize = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_TRADE_TICK_SIZE);
   double lotStep  = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_VOLUME_STEP);
   double minL     = MathMax(Min_Lot, SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_VOLUME_MIN));
   double maxL     = MathMin(Max_Lot, SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_VOLUME_MAX));
   if(slDist <= 0 || tickVal <= 0 || tickSize <= 0) return minL;
   double lots = riskAmt / (slDist / tickSize * tickVal);
   lots = MathFloor(lots / lotStep) * lotStep;
   return MathMax(minL, MathMin(maxL, lots));
}

int CountMyPositions()
{
   int n = 0;
   for(int i = 0; i < PositionsTotal(); i++)
   {
      ulong t = PositionGetTicket(i);
      if(PositionSelectByTicket(t) &&
         (int)PositionGetInteger(POSITION_MAGIC) == Magic) n++;
   }
   return n;
}

bool IsDailyLossBreached()
{
   double balance = AccountInfoDouble(ACCOUNT_BALANCE);
   double maxLoss = balance * MaxDailyLoss_Pct / 100.0;
   datetime dayStart = StringToTime(TimeToString(TimeCurrent(), TIME_DATE));
   double pl = 0;
   for(int i = 0; i < PositionsTotal(); i++)
   {
      ulong t = PositionGetTicket(i);
      if(!PositionSelectByTicket(t)) continue;
      if((int)PositionGetInteger(POSITION_MAGIC) != Magic) continue;
      pl += PositionGetDouble(POSITION_PROFIT)
          + PositionGetDouble(POSITION_SWAP)
          + PositionGetDouble(POSITION_COMMISSION);
   }
   HistorySelect(dayStart, TimeCurrent());
   for(int i = 0; i < HistoryDealsTotal(); i++)
   {
      ulong d = HistoryDealGetTicket(i);
      if((int)HistoryDealGetInteger(d, DEAL_MAGIC) != Magic) continue;
      if(HistoryDealGetInteger(d, DEAL_ENTRY) == DEAL_ENTRY_OUT)
         pl += HistoryDealGetDouble(d, DEAL_PROFIT)
             + HistoryDealGetDouble(d, DEAL_SWAP)
             + HistoryDealGetDouble(d, DEAL_COMMISSION);
   }
   return (pl <= -maxLoss);
}

bool IsDrawdownBreached()
{
   double bal = AccountInfoDouble(ACCOUNT_BALANCE);
   double eq  = AccountInfoDouble(ACCOUNT_EQUITY);
   return (bal > 0 && (bal - eq) / bal * 100.0 >= MaxDrawdown_Pct);
}

void CloseAll()
{
   for(int i = PositionsTotal()-1; i >= 0; i--)
   {
      ulong t = PositionGetTicket(i);
      if(PositionSelectByTicket(t) &&
         (int)PositionGetInteger(POSITION_MAGIC) == Magic)
         trade.PositionClose(t);
   }
}

void DeleteMyPendings(int maxAgeSeconds)
{
   for(int i = OrdersTotal()-1; i >= 0; i--)
   {
      ulong t = OrderGetTicket(i);
      if(!OrderSelect(t)) continue;
      if((int)OrderGetInteger(ORDER_MAGIC) != Magic) continue;
      datetime setup = (datetime)OrderGetInteger(ORDER_TIME_SETUP);
      if((TimeCurrent() - setup) >= maxAgeSeconds)
         trade.OrderDelete(t);
   }
}

void ManageTrailing(double atr)
{
   for(int i = PositionsTotal()-1; i >= 0; i--)
   {
      ulong t = PositionGetTicket(i);
      if(!PositionSelectByTicket(t)) continue;
      if((int)PositionGetInteger(POSITION_MAGIC) != Magic) continue;
      if(PositionGetDouble(POSITION_PROFIT) <= 0) continue;
      double sl = PositionGetDouble(POSITION_SL);
      double tp = PositionGetDouble(POSITION_TP);
      long  typ = PositionGetInteger(POSITION_TYPE);
      if(typ == POSITION_TYPE_BUY)
      {
         double nsl = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_BID) - atr * Trail_ATR_Mult;
         if(nsl > sl) trade.PositionModify(t, nsl, tp);
      }
      else
      {
         double nsl = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_ASK) + atr * Trail_ATR_Mult;
         if(nsl < sl) trade.PositionModify(t, nsl, tp);
      }
   }
}

//────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
int OnInit()
{
   h_ema_htf   = iMA(_Symbol,  TF_Higher, EMA_HTF_Period, 0, MODE_EMA, PRICE_CLOSE);
   h_atr_mtf   = iATR(_Symbol, TF_Middle, ATR_Period);
   h_atr_entry = iATR(_Symbol, TF_Entry,  ATR_Period);
   trade.SetExpertMagicNumber(Magic);
   return INIT_SUCCEEDED;
}

void OnDeinit(const int reason)
{
   IndicatorRelease(h_ema_htf);
   IndicatorRelease(h_atr_mtf);
   IndicatorRelease(h_atr_entry);
}

void OnTick()
{
   static datetime lastBar = 0;
   datetime curBar = iTime(_Symbol, TF_Entry, 0);
   if(curBar == lastBar) return;
   lastBar = curBar;

   if(IsDrawdownBreached()) { CloseAll(); return; }
   int tfSec = PeriodSeconds(TF_Entry);
   DeleteMyPendings(tfSec);

   double atr_e[2]; CopyBuffer(h_atr_entry, 0, 0, 2, atr_e);
   if(Enable_Trail && CountMyPositions() > 0) { ManageTrailing(atr_e[0]); return; }
   if(IsDailyLossBreached()) return;
   if(CountMyPositions() >= (int)MathMin(Max_Positions, 5)) return;

   //--- HTF
   double ema_h[2]; CopyBuffer(h_ema_htf, 0, 0, 2, ema_h);
   double price  = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_BID);
   bool htf_bull = price > ema_h[0];
   bool htf_bear = price < ema_h[0];

   //--- MTF: Donchian
   double hi[], lo[];
   ArraySetAsSeries(hi, true); ArraySetAsSeries(lo, true);
   CopyHigh(_Symbol, TF_Middle, 1, Donchian_Period, hi);
   CopyLow(_Symbol,  TF_Middle, 1, Donchian_Period, lo);
   double dHigh = hi[ArrayMaximum(hi)];
   double dLow  = lo[ArrayMinimum(lo)];

   //--- LTF: Volume
   double atr_m[2]; CopyBuffer(h_atr_mtf, 0, 0, 2, atr_m);
   double atr = atr_e[0];
   long volArr[]; ArraySetAsSeries(volArr, true);
   CopyTickVolume(_Symbol, TF_Entry, 1, Vol_Period, volArr);
   double avgVol = 0;
   for(int i = 0; i < Vol_Period; i++) avgVol += volArr[i];
   avgVol /= Vol_Period;
   if(iVolume(_Symbol, TF_Entry, 1) < avgVol * Vol_Mult) return;

   double ask = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_ASK);
   double bid = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_BID);
   double lots = CalcDynamicLot(atr * ATR_SL_Mult);

   if(OrdersTotal() == 0)
   {
      if(htf_bull || (!htf_bull && !htf_bear))
      {
         double eB = dHigh + atr * ATR_Entry_Mult;
         trade.BuyStop(lots, eB, _Symbol,
                       eB - atr_m[0], eB + atr * ATR_TP_Mult,
                       ORDER_TIME_GTC, 0, "DA_BS");
      }
      if(htf_bear || (!htf_bull && !htf_bear))
      {
         double eS = dLow - atr * ATR_Entry_Mult;
         trade.SellStop(lots, eS, _Symbol,
                        eS + atr_m[0], eS - atr * ATR_TP_Mult,
                        ORDER_TIME_GTC, 0, "DA_SS");
      }
   }
}
```

```mql5
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+

double CalcDynamicLot(double slDist)
{
   double base     = Use_Equity ? AccountInfoDouble(ACCOUNT_EQUITY)
                                : AccountInfoDouble(ACCOUNT_BALANCE);
   double riskAmt  = base * Risk_Percent / 100.0;
   double tickVal  = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_TRADE_TICK_VALUE);
   double tickSize = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_TRADE_TICK_SIZE);
   double lotStep  = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_VOLUME_STEP);
   double minL     = MathMax(Min_Lot, SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_VOLUME_MIN));
   double maxL     = MathMin(Max_Lot, SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_VOLUME_MAX));
   if(slDist <= 0 || tickVal <= 0 || tickSize <= 0) return minL;
   double lots = riskAmt / (slDist / tickSize * tickVal);
   lots = MathFloor(lots / lotStep) * lotStep;
   return MathMax(minL, MathMin(maxL, lots));
}

int CountMyPositions()
{

   {
      ulong t = PositionGetTicket(i);
      if(PositionSelectByTicket(t) &&
         (int)PositionGetInteger(POSITION_MAGIC) == Magic) n++;
   }
   return n;
}

         pl += HistoryDealGetDouble(d, DEAL_PROFIT)
             + HistoryDealGetDouble(d, DEAL_SWAP)

   return (pl <= -maxLoss);

void CheckTimeExit()
{
   datetime now = TimeCurrent();
   for(int i = PositionsTotal()-1; i >= 0; i--)
   {
      ulong t = PositionGetTicket(i);
      if(!PositionSelectByTicket(t)) continue;
      if((int)PositionGetInteger(POSITION_MAGIC) != Magic) continue;
      datetime ot = (datetime)PositionGetInteger(POSITION_TIME);
      if((now - ot) >= (datetime)(MaxHoldHours * 3600))
         trade.PositionClose(t);
   }
}

//────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
int OnInit()
{
   h_bb_htf      = iBands(_Symbol, TF_Higher, BB_Period, 0, BB_Dev_HTF,   PRICE_CLOSE);
   h_adx_mtf     = iADX(_Symbol,   TF_Middle, ADX_Period);
   h_ema200_mtf  = iMA(_Symbol,    TF_Middle, 200, 0, MODE_EMA, PRICE_CLOSE);
   h_bb_entry    = iBands(_Symbol,  TF_Entry, BB_Period, 0, BB_Dev_Entry, PRICE_CLOSE);
   h_rsi_entry   = iRSI(_Symbol,    TF_Entry, RSI_Period, PRICE_CLOSE);
   h_stoch_entry = iStochastic(_Symbol, TF_Entry, Stoch_K, Stoch_D, Stoch_Slow,
                                MODE_SMA, STO_LOWHIGH);
   h_atr_entry   = iATR(_Symbol,    TF_Entry, ATR_Period);
   trade.SetExpertMagicNumber(Magic);
   return INIT_SUCCEEDED;

void OnDeinit(const int reason)
{
   IndicatorRelease(h_bb_htf);    IndicatorRelease(h_adx_mtf);
   IndicatorRelease(h_ema200_mtf);IndicatorRelease(h_bb_entry);
   IndicatorRelease(h_rsi_entry); IndicatorRelease(h_stoch_entry);
   IndicatorRelease(h_atr_entry);
}

void OnTick()
{
   static datetime lastBar = 0;
   datetime curBar = iTime(_Symbol, TF_Entry, 0);
   if(curBar == lastBar) return;
   lastBar = curBar;

   if(IsDrawdownBreached()) { CloseAll(); return; }
   CheckTimeExit();
   if(IsDailyLossBreached()) return;
   if(CountMyPositions() >= (int)MathMin(Max_Positions, 5)) return;

   //--- HTF: BB Width Check
   double htf_u[2], htf_l[2], htf_m[2];
   CopyBuffer(h_bb_htf, 0, 0, 2, htf_m);
   CopyBuffer(h_bb_htf, 1, 0, 2, htf_u);
   CopyBuffer(h_bb_htf, 2, 0, 2, htf_l);
   double bbW = (htf_m[0] > 0) ? (htf_u[0] - htf_l[0]) / htf_m[0] : 999;
   if(bbW > BB_Width_Max) return;

   //--- MTF: ADX + EMA200
   double adx_m[2], ema200[2];
   CopyBuffer(h_adx_mtf,    MAIN_LINE, 0, 2, adx_m);
   CopyBuffer(h_ema200_mtf, 0,         0, 2, ema200);
   if(adx_m[0] > ADX_Max) return;
   double price   = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_BID);
   bool mtf_above = price > ema200[0];
   bool mtf_below = price < ema200[0];

   //--- LTF: BB + RSI + Stoch
   double bb_u[2], bb_l[2], bb_m2[2], rsi_v[2], stk[2], atr_v[2];
   CopyBuffer(h_bb_entry,    0, 0, 2, bb_m2);
   CopyBuffer(h_bb_entry,    1, 0, 2, bb_u);
   CopyBuffer(h_bb_entry,    2, 0, 2, bb_l);
   CopyBuffer(h_rsi_entry,   0, 0, 2, rsi_v);
   CopyBuffer(h_stoch_entry, MAIN_LINE, 0, 2, stk);
   CopyBuffer(h_atr_entry,   0, 0, 2, atr_v);
   double atr = atr_v[0];

   bool buySetup  = price < bb_l[0] && rsi_v[0] < 25 && stk[0] < 20;
   bool sellSetup = price > bb_u[0] && rsi_v[0] > 75 && stk[0] > 80;

   if(buySetup && !mtf_below)
   {
      double ask = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_ASK);
      double sl  = ask - atr * ATR_SL_Mult;
      trade.Buy(CalcDynamicLot(ask - sl), _Symbol, ask, sl, bb_m2[0], "CT_BUY");
   }
   else if(sellSetup && !mtf_above)
   {
      double bid = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_BID);
      double sl  = bid + atr * ATR_SL_Mult;
      trade.Sell(CalcDynamicLot(sl - bid), _Symbol, bid, sl, bb_m2[0], "CT_SELL");
   }
}
```

## ⚔️ PORT 5 — 111098104 | Abyssal Discipline

```mql5
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| EA_AbyssalDiscipline_111098104.mq5                               |
//| MTF Z-Score Pair Trading — Standalone                            |
//| !! รันบน Chart EURUSD !!                                         |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
#property copyright "Port 111098104"
#property version   "2.00"
#property strict

#include <Trade\Trade.mqh>

input group "═══ Timeframe Settings ═══"
input ENUM_TIMEFRAMES TF_Higher  = PERIOD_D1;
input ENUM_TIMEFRAMES TF_Middle  = PERIOD_H4;
input ENUM_TIMEFRAMES TF_Entry   = PERIOD_H1;

input group "═══ Pair Settings ═══"
input string Symbol2         = "USDCHF";

input group "═══ Z-Score Settings ═══"
input int    LookbackHTF     = 20;
input int    LookbackMTF     = 50;
input double ZScore_Entry    = 2.0;
input double ZScore_Exit     = 0.3;
input double ZScore_HardSL   = 3.5;
input double Corr_Min        = -0.7;

input group "═══ Money Management ═══"
input double MaxDailyLoss_Pct  = 2.0;
input double MaxDrawdown_Pct   = 8.0;
input int    Max_Positions     = 2;
input int    MaxHoldDays       = 5;
input double Fixed_Lot         = 0.01;
input bool   Use_Equity        = false;

input int Magic = 111098104;

CTrade trade;

//────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
int CountMyPositions()
{
   int n = 0;
   for(int i = 0; i < PositionsTotal(); i++)
   {
      ulong t = PositionGetTicket(i);
      if(PositionSelectByTicket(t) &&
         (int)PositionGetInteger(POSITION_MAGIC) == Magic) n++;
   }
   return n;
}

bool IsDailyLossBreached()
{
   double balance = AccountInfoDouble(ACCOUNT_BALANCE);
   double maxLoss = balance * MaxDailyLoss_Pct / 100.0;
   datetime dayStart = StringToTime(TimeToString(TimeCurrent(), TIME_DATE));
   double pl = 0;
   for(int i = 0; i < PositionsTotal(); i++)
   {
      ulong t = PositionGetTicket(i);
      if(!PositionSelectByTicket(t)) continue;
      if((int)PositionGetInteger(POSITION_MAGIC) != Magic) continue;
      pl += PositionGetDouble(POSITION_PROFIT)
          + PositionGetDouble(POSITION_SWAP)
          + PositionGetDouble(POSITION_COMMISSION);
   }
   HistorySelect(dayStart, TimeCurrent());
   for(int i = 0; i < HistoryDealsTotal(); i++)
   {
      ulong d = HistoryDealGetTicket(i);
      if((int)HistoryDealGetInteger(d, DEAL_MAGIC) != Magic) continue;
      if(HistoryDealGetInteger(d, DEAL_ENTRY) == DEAL_ENTRY_OUT)
         pl += HistoryDealGetDouble(d, DEAL_PROFIT)
             + HistoryDealGetDouble(d, DEAL_SWAP)
             + HistoryDealGetDouble(d, DEAL_COMMISSION);
   }
   return (pl <= -maxLoss);
}

bool IsDrawdownBreached()
{
   double bal = AccountInfoDouble(ACCOUNT_BALANCE);
   double eq  = AccountInfoDouble(ACCOUNT_EQUITY);
   return (bal > 0 && (bal - eq) / bal * 100.0 >= MaxDrawdown_Pct);
}

void CloseAll()
{
   for(int i = PositionsTotal()-1; i >= 0; i--)
   {
      ulong t = PositionGetTicket(i);
      if(PositionSelectByTicket(t) &&
         (int)PositionGetInteger(POSITION_MAGIC) == Magic)
         trade.PositionClose(t);
   }
}

double CalcZScore(ENUM_TIMEFRAMES tf, int period)
{
   double c1[], c2[];
   ArraySetAsSeries(c1, true); ArraySetAsSeries(c2, true);
   if(CopyClose(_Symbol, tf, 0, period+1, c1) < period+1) return EMPTY_VALUE;
   if(CopyClose(Symbol2, tf, 0, period+1, c2) < period+1) return EMPTY_VALUE;
   double spread[]; ArrayResize(spread, period);
   for(int i = 0; i < period; i++) spread[i] = c1[i] - (1.0 - c2[i]);
   double mean = 0, std = 0;
   for(int i = 0; i < period; i++) mean += spread[i];
   mean /= period;
   for(int i = 0; i < period; i++) std += MathPow(spread[i] - mean, 2);
   std = MathSqrt(std / period);
   return (std == 0) ? 0 : (spread[0] - mean) / std;
}

double CalcCorrelation(ENUM_TIMEFRAMES tf, int period)
{
   double c1[], c2[];
   ArraySetAsSeries(c1, true); ArraySetAsSeries(c2, true);
   CopyClose(_Symbol, tf, 0, period, c1);
   CopyClose(Symbol2, tf, 0, period, c2);
   double m1=0, m2=0, cov=0, s1=0, s2=0;
   for(int i=0;i<period;i++){m1+=c1[i];m2+=c2[i];}
   m1/=period; m2/=period;
   for(int i=0;i<period;i++)
   { cov+=(c1[i]-m1)*(c2[i]-m2); s1+=MathPow(c1[i]-m1,2); s2+=MathPow(c2[i]-m2,2); }
   return (s1>0&&s2>0) ? cov/MathSqrt(s1*s2) : 0;
}

void CheckPairExit()
{
   if(CountMyPositions() == 0) return;
   double z = CalcZScore(TF_Middle, LookbackMTF);
   bool exitTP   = MathAbs(z) < ZScore_Exit;
   bool exitSL   = MathAbs(z) > ZScore_HardSL;
   bool exitDD   = IsDrawdownBreached();
   bool exitTime = false;
   for(int i = 0; i < PositionsTotal(); i++)
   {
      ulong t = PositionGetTicket(i);
      if(!PositionSelectByTicket(t)) continue;
      if((int)PositionGetInteger(POSITION_MAGIC) != Magic) continue;
      datetime ot = (datetime)PositionGetInteger(POSITION_TIME);
      if((TimeCurrent() - ot) >= (datetime)(MaxHoldDays * 86400)) exitTime = true;
   }
   if(exitTP || exitSL || exitTime || exitDD) CloseAll();
}

//────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
int OnInit()
{
   trade.SetExpertMagicNumber(Magic);
   return INIT_SUCCEEDED;
}

void OnTick()
{
   static datetime lastBar = 0;
   datetime curBar = iTime(_Symbol, TF_Entry, 0);
   if(curBar == lastBar) return;
   lastBar = curBar;

   if(IsDrawdownBreached()) { CloseAll(); return; }
   CheckPairExit();
   if(IsDailyLossBreached()) return;
   if(CountMyPositions() >= (int)MathMin(Max_Positions, 5)) return;
   if(CountMyPositions() >= 2) return;

   //--- HTF: Correlation
   double corr = CalcCorrelation(TF_Higher, LookbackHTF);
   if(corr > Corr_Min) return;

   //--- MTF + LTF: Z-Score Dual Confirm
   double zMTF = CalcZScore(TF_Middle, LookbackMTF);
   double zLTF = CalcZScore(TF_Entry,  LookbackMTF / 2);
   if(zMTF == EMPTY_VALUE || zLTF == EMPTY_VALUE) return;

   double ask1 = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_ASK);
   double bid1 = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_BID);
   double ask2 = SymbolInfoDouble(Symbol2, SYMBOL_ASK);
   double bid2 = SymbolInfoDouble(Symbol2, SYMBOL_BID);

   if(zMTF < -ZScore_Entry && zLTF < -ZScore_Entry)
   {
      trade.Buy(Fixed_Lot,  _Symbol, ask1, 0, 0, "AD_BUY1");
      trade.Sell(Fixed_Lot, Symbol2, bid2, 0, 0, "AD_SELL2");
   }
   else if(zMTF > ZScore_Entry && zLTF > ZScore_Entry)
   {
      trade.Sell(Fixed_Lot, _Symbol, bid1, 0, 0, "AD_SELL1");
      trade.Buy(Fixed_Lot,  Symbol2, ask2, 0, 0, "AD_BUY2");
   }
}
```

## ⚔️ PORT 6 — 117030969 | Golden Ember

```mql5
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| EA_GoldenEmber_117030969.mq5                                     |
//| MTF Pivot Range Trading — Standalone                             |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
#property copyright "Port 117030969"
#property version   "2.00"
#property strict

#include <Trade\Trade.mqh>

input group "═══ Timeframe Settings ═══"
input ENUM_TIMEFRAMES TF_Higher  = PERIOD_MN1;
input ENUM_TIMEFRAMES TF_Middle  = PERIOD_W1;
input ENUM_TIMEFRAMES TF_Entry   = PERIOD_H1;

input group "═══ Indicator Settings ═══"
input int    RSI_Period     = 14;
input int    CCI_Period     = 20;
input int    ATR_Period     = 14;
input double Zone_ATR_Mult  = 0.3;
input double ATR_SL_Mult    = 0.5;

input group "═══ Money Management ═══"
input double Risk_Percent      = 0.8;
input double MaxDailyLoss_Pct  = 2.5;
input double MaxDrawdown_Pct   = 8.0;
input int    Max_Positions     = 3;
input double Min_Lot           = 0.01;
input double Max_Lot           = 3.0;
input bool   Use_Equity        = false;

input int Magic = 117030969;

CTrade trade;
int h_rsi, h_cci, h_atr;

struct PivotSet { double PP, R1, R2, S1, S2; };

//────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
double CalcDynamicLot(double slDist)
{
   double base     = Use_Equity ? AccountInfoDouble(ACCOUNT_EQUITY)
                                : AccountInfoDouble(ACCOUNT_BALANCE);
   double riskAmt  = base * Risk_Percent / 100.0;
   double tickVal  = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_TRADE_TICK_VALUE);
   double tickSize = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_TRADE_TICK_SIZE);
   double lotStep  = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_VOLUME_STEP);
   double minL     = MathMax(Min_Lot, SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_VOLUME_MIN));
   double maxL     = MathMin(Max_Lot, SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_VOLUME_MAX));
   if(slDist <= 0 || tickVal <= 0 || tickSize <= 0) return minL;
   double lots = riskAmt / (slDist / tickSize * tickVal);
   lots = MathFloor(lots / lotStep) * lotStep;
   return MathMax(minL, MathMin(maxL, lots));
}

int CountMyPositions()
{
   int n = 0;
   for(int i = 0; i < PositionsTotal(); i++)
   {
      ulong t = PositionGetTicket(i);
      if(PositionSelectByTicket(t) &&
         (int)PositionGetInteger(POSITION_MAGIC) == Magic) n++;
   }
   return n;
}

bool IsDailyLossBreached()
{
   double balance = AccountInfoDouble(ACCOUNT_BALANCE);
   double maxLoss = balance * MaxDailyLoss_Pct / 100.0;
   datetime dayStart = StringToTime(TimeToString(TimeCurrent(), TIME_DATE));
   double pl = 0;
   for(int i = 0; i < PositionsTotal(); i++)
   {
      ulong t = PositionGetTicket(i);
      if(!PositionSelectByTicket(t)) continue;
      if((int)PositionGetInteger(POSITION_MAGIC) != Magic) continue;
      pl += PositionGetDouble(POSITION_PROFIT)
          + PositionGetDouble(POSITION_SWAP)
          + PositionGetDouble(POSITION_COMMISSION);
   }
   HistorySelect(dayStart, TimeCurrent());
   for(int i = 0; i < HistoryDealsTotal(); i++)
   {
      ulong d = HistoryDealGetTicket(i);
      if((int)HistoryDealGetInteger(d, DEAL_MAGIC) != Magic) continue;
      if(HistoryDealGetInteger(d, DEAL_ENTRY) == DEAL_ENTRY_OUT)
         pl += HistoryDealGetDouble(d, DEAL_PROFIT)
             + HistoryDealGetDouble(d, DEAL_SWAP)
             + HistoryDealGetDouble(d, DEAL_COMMISSION);
   }
   return (pl <= -maxLoss);
}

bool IsDrawdownBreached()
{
   double bal = AccountInfoDouble(ACCOUNT_BALANCE);
   double eq  = AccountInfoDouble(ACCOUNT_EQUITY);
   return (bal > 0 && (bal - eq) / bal * 100.0 >= MaxDrawdown_Pct);
}

void CloseAll()
{
   for(int i = PositionsTotal()-1; i >= 0; i--)
   {
      ulong t = PositionGetTicket(i);
      if(PositionSelectByTicket(t) &&
         (int)PositionGetInteger(POSITION_MAGIC) == Magic)
         trade.PositionClose(t);
   }
}

PivotSet CalcPivot(ENUM_TIMEFRAMES tf, int shift)
{
   PivotSet pv = {0,0,0,0,0};
   double hi[1], lo[1], cl[1];
   ArraySetAsSeries(hi,true); ArraySetAsSeries(lo,true); ArraySetAsSeries(cl,true);
   if(CopyHigh(_Symbol,tf,shift,1,hi)<1) return pv;
   if(CopyLow(_Symbol, tf,shift,1,lo)<1) return pv;
   if(CopyClose(_Symbol,tf,shift,1,cl)<1) return pv;
   pv.PP = (hi[0]+lo[0]+cl[0])/3.0;
   pv.R1 = 2*pv.PP - lo[0];
   pv.R2 = pv.PP + (hi[0]-lo[0]);
   pv.S1 = 2*pv.PP - hi[0];
   pv.S2 = pv.PP - (hi[0]-lo[0]);
   return pv;
}

//────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
int OnInit()
{
   h_rsi = iRSI(_Symbol, TF_Entry, RSI_Period, PRICE_CLOSE);
   h_cci = iCCI(_Symbol, TF_Entry, CCI_Period, PRICE_TYPICAL);
   h_atr = iATR(_Symbol, TF_Entry, ATR_Period);
   trade.SetExpertMagicNumber(Magic);
   return INIT_SUCCEEDED;
}

void OnDeinit(const int reason)
{
   IndicatorRelease(h_rsi);
   IndicatorRelease(h_cci);
   IndicatorRelease(h_atr);
}

void OnTick()
{
   static datetime lastBar = 0;
   datetime curBar = iTime(_Symbol, TF_Entry, 0);
   if(curBar == lastBar) return;
   lastBar = curBar;

   if(IsDrawdownBreached()) { CloseAll(); return; }
   if(IsDailyLossBreached()) return;
   if(CountMyPositions() >= (int)MathMin(Max_Positions, 5)) return;

   PivotSet monthly = CalcPivot(TF_Higher, 1);
   PivotSet weekly  = CalcPivot(TF_Middle, 1);

   double price = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_BID);
   double atr_v[2]; CopyBuffer(h_atr, 0, 0, 2, atr_v);
   double atr  = atr_v[0];
   double zone = atr * Zone_ATR_Mult;

   double rsi_v[2], cci_v[2];
   CopyBuffer(h_rsi, 0, 0, 2, rsi_v);
   CopyBuffer(h_cci, 0, 0, 2, cci_v);

   bool nearS1  = MathAbs(price - weekly.S1) <= zone;
   bool nearS2  = MathAbs(price - weekly.S2) <= zone;
   bool nearR1  = MathAbs(price - weekly.R1) <= zone;
   bool nearR2  = MathAbs(price - weekly.R2) <= zone;
   bool mthBull = price > monthly.S1;
   bool mthBear = price < monthly.R1;

   if((nearS1||nearS2) && rsi_v[0]<40 && cci_v[0]<-80 && mthBull)
   {
      double ask = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_ASK);
      double sl  = (nearS2 ? weekly.S2 : weekly.S2 - atr) - atr * ATR_SL_Mult;
      double tp  = nearS1 ? weekly.PP : weekly.S1;
      if(sl < ask && tp > ask)
         trade.Buy(CalcDynamicLot(ask-sl), _Symbol, ask, sl, tp, "GE_BUY");
   }
   else if((nearR1||nearR2) && rsi_v[0]>60 && cci_v[0]>80 && mthBear)
   {
      double bid = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_BID);
      double sl  = (nearR2 ? weekly.R2 : weekly.R2 + atr) + atr * ATR_SL_Mult;
      double tp  = nearR1 ? weekly.PP : weekly.R1;
      if(sl > bid && tp < bid)
         trade.Sell(CalcDynamicLot(sl-bid), _Symbol, bid, sl, tp, "GE_SELL");
   }
}
```

## 🔥 PORT 7 — 141050699 | Twin Flares

```mql5
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| EA_TwinFlares_141050699.mq5                                      |
//| MTF High-Frequency Scalping — Standalone                         |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
#property copyright "Port 141050699"
#property version   "2.00"
#property strict

#include <Trade\Trade.mqh>

input group "═══ Timeframe Settings ═══"
input ENUM_TIMEFRAMES TF_Higher  = PERIOD_H1;
input ENUM_TIMEFRAMES TF_Middle  = PERIOD_M15;
input ENUM_TIMEFRAMES TF_Entry   = PERIOD_M1;

input group "═══ Indicator Settings ═══"
input int    EMA_Fast      = 8;
input int    EMA_Slow      = 21;
input int    EMA_HTF       = 50;
input int    Vol_Period    = 20;
input double Vol_Mult      = 1.3;

input group "═══ Trade Settings ═══"
input int    TP_Pips        = 4;
input int    SL_Pips        = 6;
input int    MaxTradesDay   = 10;
input int    SessionStart   = 13;
input int    SessionEnd     = 17;

input group "═══ Money Management ═══"
input double Risk_Percent      = 0.5;
input double MaxDailyLoss_Pct  = 2.0;
input double MaxDrawdown_Pct   = 6.0;
input int    Max_Positions     = 3;
input double Min_Lot           = 0.01;
input double Max_Lot           = 2.0;
input bool   Use_Equity        = true;

input int Magic = 141050699;

CTrade trade;
int h_ema_htf, h_ema_fast_mtf, h_ema_slow_mtf;
int h_ema_fast_entry, h_ema_slow_entry;
int tradesToday = 0;
datetime lastDayReset = 0;

//────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
double CalcDynamicLot(double slDist)
{
   double base     = Use_Equity ? AccountInfoDouble(ACCOUNT_EQUITY)
                                : AccountInfoDouble(ACCOUNT_BALANCE);
   double riskAmt  = base * Risk_Percent / 100.0;
   double tickVal  = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_TRADE_TICK_VALUE);
   double tickSize = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_TRADE_TICK_SIZE);
   double lotStep  = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_VOLUME_STEP);
   double minL     = MathMax(Min_Lot, SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_VOLUME_MIN));
   double maxL     = MathMin(Max_Lot, SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_VOLUME_MAX));
   if(slDist <= 0 || tickVal <= 0 || tickSize <= 0) return minL;
   double lots = riskAmt / (slDist / tickSize * tickVal);
   lots = MathFloor(lots / lotStep) * lotStep;
   return MathMax(minL, MathMin(maxL, lots));

   {
      ulong t = PositionGetTicket(i);

   }

bool IsDailyLossBreached()

   double balance = AccountInfoDouble(ACCOUNT_BALANCE);

          + PositionGetDouble(POSITION_SWAP)
          + PositionGetDouble(POSITION_COMMISSION);
   }
   HistorySelect(dayStart, TimeCurrent());
   for(int i = 0; i < HistoryDealsTotal(); i++)
   {
      ulong d = HistoryDealGetTicket(i);
      if((int)HistoryDealGetInteger(d, DEAL_MAGIC) != Magic) continue;
      if(HistoryDealGetInteger(d, DEAL_ENTRY) == DEAL_ENTRY_OUT)
         pl += HistoryDealGetDouble(d, DEAL_PROFIT)
             + HistoryDealGetDouble(d, DEAL_SWAP)

bool IsDrawdownBreached()

}

void CloseAll()
{
   for(int i = PositionsTotal()-1; i >= 0; i--)
   {
      ulong t = PositionGetTicket(i);
      if(PositionSelectByTicket(t) &&
         (int)PositionGetInteger(POSITION_MAGIC) == Magic)
         trade.PositionClose(t);
   }
}

bool IsSessionTime()
{
   MqlDateTime dt; TimeToStruct(TimeGMT(), dt);
   return (dt.hour >= SessionStart && dt.hour < SessionEnd);
}

double GetPip()
{
   double point = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_POINT);
   int    digs  = (int)SymbolInfoInteger(_Symbol, SYMBOL_DIGITS);
   return (digs == 5 || digs == 3) ? point * 10.0 : point;
}

//────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
int OnInit()
{
   h_ema_htf        = iMA(_Symbol, TF_Higher, EMA_HTF,  0, MODE_EMA, PRICE_CLOSE);
   h_ema_fast_mtf   = iMA(_Symbol, TF_Middle, EMA_Fast, 0, MODE_EMA, PRICE_CLOSE);
   h_ema_slow_mtf   = iMA(_Symbol, TF_Middle, EMA_Slow, 0, MODE_EMA, PRICE_CLOSE);
   h_ema_fast_entry = iMA(_Symbol, TF_Entry,  EMA_Fast, 0, MODE_EMA, PRICE_CLOSE);
   h_ema_slow_entry = iMA(_Symbol, TF_Entry,  EMA_Slow, 0, MODE_EMA, PRICE_CLOSE);
   trade.SetExpertMagicNumber(Magic);
   return INIT_SUCCEEDED;
}

void OnDeinit(const int reason)
{
   IndicatorRelease(h_ema_htf);
   IndicatorRelease(h_ema_fast_mtf); IndicatorRelease(h_ema_slow_mtf);
   IndicatorRelease(h_ema_fast_entry); IndicatorRelease(h_ema_slow_entry);
}

void OnTick()
{
   static datetime lastBar = 0;
   datetime curBar = iTime(_Symbol, TF_Entry, 0);
   if(curBar == lastBar) return;
   lastBar = curBar;

   if(IsDrawdownBreached()) { CloseAll(); return; }

   MqlDateTime dt; TimeToStruct(TimeCurrent(), dt);
   datetime today = StringToTime(StringFormat("%04d.%02d.%02d", dt.year, dt.mon, dt.day));
   if(today != lastDayReset) { tradesToday = 0; lastDayReset = today; }

   if(!IsSessionTime()) return;
   if(tradesToday >= MaxTradesDay) return;
   if(IsDailyLossBreached()) return;
   if(CountMyPositions() >= (int)MathMin(Max_Positions, 5)) return;

   //--- HTF
   double ema_h[2]; CopyBuffer(h_ema_htf, 0, 0, 2, ema_h);
   double price  = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_BID);
   bool htf_bull = price > ema_h[0];
   bool htf_bear = price < ema_h[0];

   //--- MTF
   double ef_m[2], es_m[2];
   CopyBuffer(h_ema_fast_mtf, 0, 0, 2, ef_m);
   CopyBuffer(h_ema_slow_mtf, 0, 0, 2, es_m);
   bool mtf_bull = ef_m[0] > es_m[0];
   bool mtf_bear = ef_m[0] < es_m[0];

   //--- LTF
   double ef_e[3], es_e[3];
   CopyBuffer(h_ema_fast_entry, 0, 0, 3, ef_e);
   CopyBuffer(h_ema_slow_entry, 0, 0, 3, es_e);
   bool cross_up   = ef_e[1] > es_e[1] && ef_e[2] <= es_e[2];
   bool cross_down = ef_e[1] < es_e[1] && ef_e[2] >= es_e[2];

   // Volume
   long volArr[]; ArraySetAsSeries(volArr, true);
   CopyTickVolume(_Symbol, TF_Entry, 1, Vol_Period, volArr);
   double avgVol = 0;
   for(int i = 0; i < Vol_Period; i++) avgVol += volArr[i];
   avgVol /= Vol_Period;
   bool volOK = (iVolume(_Symbol, TF_Entry, 1) > avgVol * Vol_Mult);

   double pip = GetPip();
   if(htf_bull && mtf_bull && cross_up && volOK)
   {
      double ask = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_ASK);
      if(trade.Buy(CalcDynamicLot(SL_Pips*pip), _Symbol, ask,
                   ask - SL_Pips*pip, ask + TP_Pips*pip, "TF_BUY"))
         tradesToday++;
   }
   else if(htf_bear && mtf_bear && cross_down && volOK)
   {
      double bid = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_BID);
      if(trade.Sell(CalcDynamicLot(SL_Pips*pip), _Symbol, bid,
                    bid + SL_Pips*pip, bid - TP_Pips*pip, "TF_SELL"))
         tradesToday++;
   }
}
```

## 🔥 PORT 8 — 159503454 | Blazing Arrow

```mql5
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| EA_BlazingArrow_159503454.mq5                                    |
//| MTF Momentum Scalping + Partial Close — Standalone               |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
#property copyright "Port 159503454"
#property version   "2.00"
#property strict

#include <Trade\Trade.mqh>

input group "═══ Timeframe Settings ═══"
input ENUM_TIMEFRAMES TF_Higher  = PERIOD_H4;
input ENUM_TIMEFRAMES TF_Middle  = PERIOD_M15;
input ENUM_TIMEFRAMES TF_Entry   = PERIOD_M5;

input group "═══ Indicator Settings ═══"
input int    EMA_HTF_Period    = 50;
input int    EMA_Entry_Period  = 50;
input int    RSI_Period        = 3;
input int    Mom_Period        = 10;

input group "═══ Trade Settings ═══"
input int    TP_Pips             = 12;
input int    SL_Pips             = 8;
input int    PartialClose_Pips   = 6;
input double PartialClose_Pct    = 50.0;

input group "═══ Money Management ═══"
input double Risk_Percent      = 1.0;
input double MaxDailyLoss_Pct  = 3.0;
input double MaxDrawdown_Pct   = 10.0;
input int    Max_Positions     = 3;
input double Min_Lot           = 0.01;
input double Max_Lot           = 5.0;
input bool   Use_Equity        = true;

input int Magic = 159503454;

CTrade trade;
int h_ema_htf, h_ema_entry, h_rsi_mtf, h_mom_mtf;

//────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
double CalcDynamicLot(double slDist)
{
   double base     = Use_Equity ? AccountInfoDouble(ACCOUNT_EQUITY)
                                : AccountInfoDouble(ACCOUNT_BALANCE);
   double riskAmt  = base * Risk_Percent / 100.0;
   double tickVal  = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_TRADE_TICK_VALUE);
   double tickSize = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_TRADE_TICK_SIZE);
   double lotStep  = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_VOLUME_STEP);
   double minL     = MathMax(Min_Lot, SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_VOLUME_MIN));
   double maxL     = MathMin(Max_Lot, SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_VOLUME_MAX));
   if(slDist <= 0 || tickVal <= 0 || tickSize <= 0) return minL;
   double lots = riskAmt / (slDist / tickSize * tickVal);
   lots = MathFloor(lots / lotStep) * lotStep;
   return MathMax(minL, MathMin(maxL, lots));
}

int CountMyPositions()
{
   int n = 0;
   for(int i = 0; i < PositionsTotal(); i++)
   {
      ulong t = PositionGetTicket(i);
      if(PositionSelectByTicket(t) &&
         (int)PositionGetInteger(POSITION_MAGIC) == Magic) n++;
   }
   return n;
}

bool IsDailyLossBreached()
{
   double balance = AccountInfoDouble(ACCOUNT_BALANCE);
   double maxLoss = balance * MaxDailyLoss_Pct / 100.0;
   datetime dayStart = StringToTime(TimeToString(TimeCurrent(), TIME_DATE));
   double pl = 0;
   for(int i = 0; i < PositionsTotal(); i++)
   {
      ulong t = PositionGetTicket(i);
      if(!PositionSelectByTicket(t)) continue;
      if((int)PositionGetInteger(POSITION_MAGIC) != Magic) continue;
      pl += PositionGetDouble(POSITION_PROFIT)
          + PositionGetDouble(POSITION_SWAP)
          + PositionGetDouble(POSITION_COMMISSION);
   }
   HistorySelect(dayStart, TimeCurrent());
   for(int i = 0; i < HistoryDealsTotal(); i++)
   {
      ulong d = HistoryDealGetTicket(i);
      if((int)HistoryDealGetInteger(d, DEAL_MAGIC) != Magic) continue;
      if(HistoryDealGetInteger(d, DEAL_ENTRY) == DEAL_ENTRY_OUT)
         pl += HistoryDealGetDouble(d, DEAL_PROFIT)
             + HistoryDealGetDouble(d, DEAL_SWAP)
             + HistoryDealGetDouble(d, DEAL_COMMISSION);
   }
   return (pl <= -maxLoss);
}

bool IsDrawdownBreached()
{
   double bal = AccountInfoDouble(ACCOUNT_BALANCE);
   double eq  = AccountInfoDouble(ACCOUNT_EQUITY);
   return (bal > 0 && (bal - eq) / bal * 100.0 >= MaxDrawdown_Pct);
}

void CloseAll()
{
   for(int i = PositionsTotal()-1; i >= 0; i--)
   {
      ulong t = PositionGetTicket(i);
      if(PositionSelectByTicket(t) &&
         (int)PositionGetInteger(POSITION_MAGIC) == Magic)
         trade.PositionClose(t);
   }
}

double GetPip()
{
   double point = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_POINT);
   int    digs  = (int)SymbolInfoInteger(_Symbol, SYMBOL_DIGITS);
   return (digs == 5 || digs == 3) ? point * 10.0 : point;
}

void ManagePartialClose()
{
   double pip = GetPip();
   for(int i = PositionsTotal()-1; i >= 0; i--)
   {
      ulong t = PositionGetTicket(i);
      if(!PositionSelectByTicket(t)) continue;
      if((int)PositionGetInteger(POSITION_MAGIC) != Magic) continue;
      string cmt = PositionGetString(POSITION_COMMENT);
      if(StringFind(cmt,"_PC") >= 0) continue;

      double openP = PositionGetDouble(POSITION_PRICE_OPEN);
      double vol   = PositionGetDouble(POSITION_VOLUME);
      long   typ   = PositionGetInteger(POSITION_TYPE);
      double curP  = (typ==POSITION_TYPE_BUY)
                     ? SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_BID)
                     : SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_ASK);
      double profPips = (typ==POSITION_TYPE_BUY)
                        ? (curP-openP)/pip : (openP-curP)/pip;

      if(profPips >= PartialClose_Pips)
      {
         double lotStep = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_VOLUME_STEP);
         double minLot  = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_VOLUME_MIN);
         double closeLot = MathFloor(vol*PartialClose_Pct/100.0/lotStep)*lotStep;
         if(closeLot >= minLot && (vol-closeLot) >= minLot)
         {
            if(trade.PositionClosePartial(t, closeLot))
               trade.PositionModify(t, openP, PositionGetDouble(POSITION_TP));
         }
      }
   }
}

//────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
int OnInit()
{
   h_ema_htf   = iMA(_Symbol,  TF_Higher, EMA_HTF_Period,   0, MODE_EMA, PRICE_CLOSE);
   h_ema_entry = iMA(_Symbol,  TF_Entry,  EMA_Entry_Period, 0, MODE_EMA, PRICE_CLOSE);
   h_rsi_mtf   = iRSI(_Symbol, TF_Middle, RSI_Period, PRICE_CLOSE);
   h_mom_mtf   = iMomentum(_Symbol, TF_Middle, Mom_Period, PRICE_CLOSE);
   trade.SetExpertMagicNumber(Magic);
   return INIT_SUCCEEDED;
}

void OnDeinit(const int reason)
{
   IndicatorRelease(h_ema_htf);   IndicatorRelease(h_ema_entry);
   IndicatorRelease(h_rsi_mtf);   IndicatorRelease(h_mom_mtf);
}

void OnTick()
{
   static datetime lastBar = 0;
   datetime curBar = iTime(_Symbol, TF_Entry, 0);
   if(curBar == lastBar) return;
   lastBar = curBar;

   if(IsDrawdownBreached()) { CloseAll(); return; }
   ManagePartialClose();
   if(IsDailyLossBreached()) return;
   if(CountMyPositions() >= (int)MathMin(Max_Positions, 5)) return;

   //--- HTF
   double ema_h[2]; CopyBuffer(h_ema_htf, 0, 0, 2, ema_h);
   double price  = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_BID);
   bool htf_bull = price > ema_h[0];
   bool htf_bear = price < ema_h[0];

   //--- MTF
   double rsi_m[2], mom_m[3];
   CopyBuffer(h_rsi_mtf, 0, 0, 2, rsi_m);
   CopyBuffer(h_mom_mtf, 0, 0, 3, mom_m);
   bool mtf_bull = rsi_m[0] > 50 && mom_m[0] > 100 && mom_m[1] <= 100;
   bool mtf_bear = rsi_m[0] < 50 && mom_m[0] < 100 && mom_m[1] >= 100;

   //--- LTF: Engulfing + EMA
   double ema_e[2]; CopyBuffer(h_ema_entry, 0, 0, 2, ema_e);
   double o1=iOpen(_Symbol,TF_Entry,1), c1=iClose(_Symbol,TF_Entry,1);
   double o2=iOpen(_Symbol,TF_Entry,2), c2=iClose(_Symbol,TF_Entry,2);
   bool bullEng = c1>o1 && c2<o2 && c1>o2 && o1<c2;
   bool bearEng = c1<o1 && c2>o2 && c1<o2 && o1>c2;

   double pip = GetPip();
   if(htf_bull && mtf_bull && bullEng && price > ema_e[0])
   {
      double ask = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_ASK);
      double sl  = ask - SL_Pips*pip;
      double tp  = ask + TP_Pips*pip;
      trade.Buy(CalcDynamicLot(ask-sl), _Symbol, ask, sl, tp, "BA_BUY");
   }
   else if(htf_bear && mtf_bear && bearEng && price < ema_e[0])
   {
      double bid = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_BID);
      double sl  = bid + SL_Pips*pip;
      double tp  = bid - TP_Pips*pip;
      trade.Sell(CalcDynamicLot(sl-bid), _Symbol, bid, sl, tp, "BA_SELL");
   }
}
```
