## 🔥 PORT 9 — 159149771 | Solar Eclipse

```mql5
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| EA_SolarEclipse_159149771.mq5                                    |
//| MTF News Straddle — Standalone                                   |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
#property copyright "Port 159149771"
#property version   "2.00"
#property strict

#include <Trade\Trade.mqh>

input group "═══ Timeframe Settings ═══"
input ENUM_TIMEFRAMES TF_Higher  = PERIOD_D1;
input ENUM_TIMEFRAMES TF_Middle  = PERIOD_H1;
input ENUM_TIMEFRAMES TF_Entry   = PERIOD_M15;

input group "═══ News Settings ═══"
input string NewsTime1         = "08:30";
input string NewsTime2         = "14:00";
input int    MinutesBefore     = 15;
input int    TimeStop_Mins     = 30;
input double MaxSpread_Mult    = 3.0;
input int    MaxEventsPerDay   = 2;

input group "═══ Indicator Settings ═══"
input int    ATR_Period        = 14;
input double ATR_Entry_Mult    = 0.8;
input double ATR_TP_Mult       = 2.0;
input double ATR_SL_Mult       = 1.0;
input int    EMA_HTF_Period    = 200;

input group "═══ Money Management ═══"
input double Risk_Percent      = 1.0;
input double MaxDailyLoss_Pct  = 3.0;
input double MaxDrawdown_Pct   = 8.0;
input int    Max_Positions     = 2;
input double Min_Lot           = 0.01;
input double Max_Lot           = 5.0;
input bool   Use_Equity        = false;

input int Magic = 159149771;

CTrade trade;
int h_ema_htf, h_atr_mtf, h_atr_entry;
int eventsToday = 0;
datetime lastDayReset = 0;
datetime newsT1 = 0, newsT2 = 0;

//────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
double CalcDynamicLot(double slDist)
{
   double base     = Use_Equity ? AccountInfoDouble(ACCOUNT_EQUITY)
                                : AccountInfoDouble(ACCOUNT_BALANCE);
   double riskAmt  = base * Risk_Percent / 100.0;
   double tickVal  = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_TRADE_TICK_VALUE);
   double tickSize = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_TRADE_TICK_SIZE);
   double lotStep  = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_VOLUME_STEP);
   double minL     = MathMax(Min_Lot, SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_VOLUME_MIN));
   double maxL     = MathMin(Max_Lot, SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_VOLUME_MAX));
   if(slDist <= 0 || tickVal <= 0 || tickSize <= 0) return minL;
   double lots = riskAmt / (slDist / tickSize * tickVal);
   lots = MathFloor(lots / lotStep) * lotStep;
   return MathMax(minL, MathMin(maxL, lots));
}

int CountMyPositions()
{
   int n = 0;
   for(int i = 0; i < PositionsTotal(); i++)
   {
      ulong t = PositionGetTicket(i);
      if(PositionSelectByTicket(t) &&
         (int)PositionGetInteger(POSITION_MAGIC) == Magic) n++;
   }
   return n;
}

bool IsDailyLossBreached()
{
   double balance = AccountInfoDouble(ACCOUNT_BALANCE);
   double maxLoss = balance * MaxDailyLoss_Pct / 100.0;
   datetime dayStart = StringToTime(TimeToString(TimeCurrent(), TIME_DATE));
   double pl = 0;
   for(int i = 0; i < PositionsTotal(); i++)
   {
      ulong t = PositionGetTicket(i);
      if(!PositionSelectByTicket(t)) continue;
      if((int)PositionGetInteger(POSITION_MAGIC) != Magic) continue;
      pl += PositionGetDouble(POSITION_PROFIT)
          + PositionGetDouble(POSITION_SWAP)
          + PositionGetDouble(POSITION_COMMISSION);
   }
   HistorySelect(dayStart, TimeCurrent());
   for(int i = 0; i < HistoryDealsTotal(); i++)
   {
      ulong d = HistoryDealGetTicket(i);
      if((int)HistoryDealGetInteger(d, DEAL_MAGIC) != Magic) continue;
      if(HistoryDealGetInteger(d, DEAL_ENTRY) == DEAL_ENTRY_OUT)
         pl += HistoryDealGetDouble(d, DEAL_PROFIT)
             + HistoryDealGetDouble(d, DEAL_SWAP)
             + HistoryDealGetDouble(d, DEAL_COMMISSION);
   }
   return (pl <= -maxLoss);
}

bool IsDrawdownBreached()
{
   double bal = AccountInfoDouble(ACCOUNT_BALANCE);
   double eq  = AccountInfoDouble(ACCOUNT_EQUITY);
   return (bal > 0 && (bal - eq) / bal * 100.0 >= MaxDrawdown_Pct);
}

void CloseAll()
{
   for(int i = PositionsTotal()-1; i >= 0; i--)
   {
      ulong t = PositionGetTicket(i);
      if(PositionSelectByTicket(t) &&
         (int)PositionGetInteger(POSITION_MAGIC) == Magic)
         trade.PositionClose(t);
   }
}

void CheckTimeStop()
{
   datetime now = TimeCurrent();
   for(int i = PositionsTotal()-1; i >= 0; i--)
   {
      ulong t = PositionGetTicket(i);
      if(!PositionSelectByTicket(t)) continue;
      if((int)PositionGetInteger(POSITION_MAGIC) != Magic) continue;
      datetime ot = (datetime)PositionGetInteger(POSITION_TIME);
      if((now - ot) >= (datetime)(TimeStop_Mins * 60))
         trade.PositionClose(t);
   }
}

void CancelOpposite()
{
   if(PositionsTotal() == 0) return;
   long posType = -1;
   for(int i = 0; i < PositionsTotal(); i++)
   {
      ulong t = PositionGetTicket(i);
      if(!PositionSelectByTicket(t)) continue;
      if((int)PositionGetInteger(POSITION_MAGIC) == Magic)
      { posType = PositionGetInteger(POSITION_TYPE); break; }
   }
   if(posType < 0) return;
   for(int i = OrdersTotal()-1; i >= 0; i--)
   {
      ulong t = OrderGetTicket(i);
      if(!OrderSelect(t)) continue;
      if((int)OrderGetInteger(ORDER_MAGIC) != Magic) continue;
      long ot = OrderGetInteger(ORDER_TYPE);
      if((posType==POSITION_TYPE_BUY  && ot==ORDER_TYPE_SELL_STOP) ||
         (posType==POSITION_TYPE_SELL && ot==ORDER_TYPE_BUY_STOP))
         trade.OrderDelete(t);
   }
}

void PlaceStraddle()
{
   double ema_h[2]; CopyBuffer(h_ema_htf, 0, 0, 2, ema_h);
   double price = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_BID);
   bool d1_bull = price > ema_h[0];

   double atr_m[2]; CopyBuffer(h_atr_mtf,   0, 0, 2, atr_m);
   double atr_e[2]; CopyBuffer(h_atr_entry, 0, 0, 2, atr_e);
   double atr = atr_e[0];

   double spread = SymbolInfoInteger(_Symbol,SYMBOL_SPREAD)
                 * SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol,SYMBOL_POINT);
   if(spread > atr * 0.05 * MaxSpread_Mult) return;

   double ask = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_ASK);
   double bid = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_BID);
   double lots = CalcDynamicLot(atr * ATR_SL_Mult);
   double buyLots  = MathMin(d1_bull ? lots*1.2 : lots, Max_Lot);
   double sellLots = MathMin(d1_bull ? lots : lots*1.2, Max_Lot);

   double eB = ask + atr * ATR_Entry_Mult;
   double eS = bid - atr * ATR_Entry_Mult;

   trade.BuyStop(buyLots,  eB, _Symbol,
                 eB - atr*ATR_SL_Mult, eB + atr*ATR_TP_Mult,
                 ORDER_TIME_GTC, 0, "SE_BS");
   trade.SellStop(sellLots, eS, _Symbol,
                  eS + atr*ATR_SL_Mult, eS - atr*ATR_TP_Mult,
                  ORDER_TIME_GTC, 0, "SE_SS");
   eventsToday++;
}

//────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
int OnInit()
{
   h_ema_htf   = iMA(_Symbol,  TF_Higher, EMA_HTF_Period, 0, MODE_EMA, PRICE_CLOSE);
   h_atr_mtf   = iATR(_Symbol, TF_Middle, ATR_Period);
   h_atr_entry = iATR(_Symbol, TF_Entry,  ATR_Period);
   trade.SetExpertMagicNumber(Magic);
   return INIT_SUCCEEDED;
}

void OnDeinit(const int reason)
{
   IndicatorRelease(h_ema_htf);
   IndicatorRelease(h_atr_mtf);
   IndicatorRelease(h_atr_entry);
}

void OnTick()
{
   MqlDateTime dt; TimeToStruct(TimeGMT(), dt);
   datetime today = StringToTime(StringFormat("%04d.%02d.%02d", dt.year, dt.mon, dt.day));
   if(today != lastDayReset)
   { eventsToday=0; lastDayReset=today; newsT1=0; newsT2=0; }

   if(IsDrawdownBreached()) { CloseAll(); return; }
   CheckTimeStop();
   CancelOpposite();
   if(IsDailyLossBreached()) return;
   if(eventsToday >= MaxEventsPerDay) return;

   if(newsT1==0) newsT1=StringToTime(StringFormat("%04d.%02d.%02d %s",
                                     dt.year,dt.mon,dt.day,NewsTime1));
   if(newsT2==0) newsT2=StringToTime(StringFormat("%04d.%02d.%02d %s",
                                     dt.year,dt.mon,dt.day,NewsTime2));

   datetime gmtNow = TimeGMT();
   bool fire1 = (gmtNow >= newsT1 - MinutesBefore*60 &&
                 gmtNow <  newsT1 - (MinutesBefore-1)*60);
   bool fire2 = (gmtNow >= newsT2 - MinutesBefore*60 &&
                 gmtNow <  newsT2 - (MinutesBefore-1)*60);

   if((fire1 || fire2) && OrdersTotal()==0 && PositionsTotal()==0)
      PlaceStraddle();
}
```

## 🔥 PORT 10 — 183977997 | Dragon's Wrath

```mql5
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| EA_DragonsWrath_183977997.mq5                                    |
//| MTF Supertrend Trend Catcher — Standalone                        |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
#property copyright "Port 183977997"
#property version   "2.00"
#property strict

#include <Trade\Trade.mqh>

input group "═══ Timeframe Settings ═══"
input ENUM_TIMEFRAMES TF_Higher  = PERIOD_W1;
input ENUM_TIMEFRAMES TF_Middle  = PERIOD_D1;
input ENUM_TIMEFRAMES TF_Entry   = PERIOD_H4;

input group "═══ Indicator Settings ═══"
input int    EMA_HTF_Fast     = 50;
input int    EMA_HTF_Slow     = 200;
input int    ADX_Period       = 14;
input double ADX_Min          = 30.0;
input double ADX_Exit         = 20.0;
input int    ST_Period        = 10;
input double ST_Multiplier    = 3.0;
input int    ATR_SL_Period    = 14;
input double HardSL_ATR_Mult  = 2.0;

input group "═══ Money Management ═══"
input double Risk_Percent      = 1.5;
input double MaxDailyLoss_Pct  = 4.5;
input double MaxDrawdown_Pct   = 15.0;
input int    Max_Positions     = 2;
input double Min_Lot           = 0.01;
input double Max_Lot           = 10.0;
input bool   Use_Equity        = false;

input int Magic = 183977997;

CTrade trade;
int h_ema_fast_htf, h_ema_slow_htf;
int h_adx_mtf, h_atr_entry, h_atr_sl;

double prevUpper = 0, prevLower = 0;
int    prevTrend = 0;

//────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
double CalcDynamicLot(double slDist)
{
   double base     = Use_Equity ? AccountInfoDouble(ACCOUNT_EQUITY)
                                : AccountInfoDouble(ACCOUNT_BALANCE);
   double riskAmt  = base * Risk_Percent / 100.0;
   double tickVal  = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_TRADE_TICK_VALUE);
   double tickSize = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_TRADE_TICK_SIZE);
   double lotStep  = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_VOLUME_STEP);
   double minL     = MathMax(Min_Lot, SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_VOLUME_MIN));
   double maxL     = MathMin(Max_Lot, SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_VOLUME_MAX));
   if(slDist <= 0 || tickVal <= 0 || tickSize <= 0) return minL;
   double lots = riskAmt / (slDist / tickSize * tickVal);
   lots = MathFloor(lots / lotStep) * lotStep;
   return MathMax(minL, MathMin(maxL, lots));
}

int CountMyPositions()
{
   int n = 0;
   for(int i = 0; i < PositionsTotal(); i++)
   {
      ulong t = PositionGetTicket(i);
      if(PositionSelectByTicket(t) &&
         (int)PositionGetInteger(POSITION_MAGIC) == Magic) n++;
   }
   return n;
}

bool IsDailyLossBreached()
{
   double balance = AccountInfoDouble(ACCOUNT_BALANCE);
   double maxLoss = balance * MaxDailyLoss_Pct / 100.0;
   datetime dayStart = StringToTime(TimeToString(TimeCurrent(), TIME_DATE));
   double pl = 0;
   for(int i = 0; i < PositionsTotal(); i++)
   {
      ulong t = PositionGetTicket(i);
      if(!PositionSelectByTicket(t)) continue;
      if((int)PositionGetInteger(POSITION_MAGIC) != Magic) continue;
      pl += PositionGetDouble(POSITION_PROFIT)
          + PositionGetDouble(POSITION_SWAP)
          + PositionGetDouble(POSITION_COMMISSION);
   }
   HistorySelect(dayStart, TimeCurrent());
   for(int i = 0; i < HistoryDealsTotal(); i++)
   {
      ulong d = HistoryDealGetTicket(i);
      if((int)HistoryDealGetInteger(d, DEAL_MAGIC) != Magic) continue;
      if(HistoryDealGetInteger(d, DEAL_ENTRY) == DEAL_ENTRY_OUT)
         pl += HistoryDealGetDouble(d, DEAL_PROFIT)
             + HistoryDealGetDouble(d, DEAL_SWAP)
             + HistoryDealGetDouble(d, DEAL_COMMISSION);
   }
   return (pl <= -maxLoss);
}

bool IsDrawdownBreached()
{
   double bal = AccountInfoDouble(ACCOUNT_BALANCE);
   double eq  = AccountInfoDouble(ACCOUNT_EQUITY);
   return (bal > 0 && (bal - eq) / bal * 100.0 >= MaxDrawdown_Pct);
}

void CloseAll()
{
   for(int i = PositionsTotal()-1; i >= 0; i--)
   {
      ulong t = PositionGetTicket(i);
      if(PositionSelectByTicket(t) &&
         (int)PositionGetInteger(POSITION_MAGIC) == Magic)
         trade.PositionClose(t);
   }
}

int CalcSupertrend(double atr)
{
   double hi1  = iHigh(_Symbol,  TF_Entry, 1);
   double lo1  = iLow(_Symbol,   TF_Entry, 1);
   double cl1  = iClose(_Symbol, TF_Entry, 1);
   double cl2  = iClose(_Symbol, TF_Entry, 2);
   double hl2  = (hi1 + lo1) / 2.0;

   double upper = hl2 + ST_Multiplier * atr;
   double lower = hl2 - ST_Multiplier * atr;

   double fU = (prevUpper > 0 && (upper < prevUpper || cl2 > prevUpper)) ? upper : prevUpper;
   double fL = (prevLower > 0 && (lower > prevLower || cl2 < prevLower)) ? lower : prevLower;
   if(prevUpper == 0) fU = upper;
   if(prevLower == 0) fL = lower;

   int trend  = (cl1 > fU) ? 1 : (cl1 < fL ? -1 : prevTrend);
   int signal = 0;
   if(trend ==  1 && prevTrend != 1)  signal =  1;
   if(trend == -1 && prevTrend != -1) signal = -1;

   prevUpper = fU; prevLower = fL; prevTrend = trend;
   return signal;
}

void ManageSupertrend()
{
   double adx_m[2]; CopyBuffer(h_adx_mtf, MAIN_LINE, 0, 2, adx_m);
   if(adx_m[0] < ADX_Exit || IsDrawdownBreached())
   { CloseAll(); return; }

   double atr_e[2]; CopyBuffer(h_atr_entry, 0, 0, 2, atr_e);
   double hi1 = iHigh(_Symbol, TF_Entry, 1);
   double lo1 = iLow(_Symbol,  TF_Entry, 1);
   double hl2 = (hi1+lo1)/2.0;
   double trailBuy  = hl2 - ST_Multiplier * atr_e[0];
   double trailSell = hl2 + ST_Multiplier * atr_e[0];

   for(int i = PositionsTotal()-1; i >= 0; i--)
   {
      ulong t = PositionGetTicket(i);
      if(!PositionSelectByTicket(t)) continue;
      if((int)PositionGetInteger(POSITION_MAGIC) != Magic) continue;
      if(PositionGetDouble(POSITION_PROFIT) <= 0) continue;
      double sl  = PositionGetDouble(POSITION_SL);
      long   typ = PositionGetInteger(POSITION_TYPE);
      if(typ==POSITION_TYPE_BUY  && trailBuy  > sl) trade.PositionModify(t, trailBuy,  0);
      if(typ==POSITION_TYPE_SELL && trailSell < sl)  trade.PositionModify(t, trailSell, 0);
   }
}

//────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
int OnInit()
{
   h_ema_fast_htf = iMA(_Symbol,  TF_Higher, EMA_HTF_Fast, 0, MODE_EMA, PRICE_CLOSE);
   h_ema_slow_htf = iMA(_Symbol,  TF_Higher, EMA_HTF_Slow, 0, MODE_EMA, PRICE_CLOSE);
   h_adx_mtf      = iADX(_Symbol, TF_Middle, ADX_Period);
   h_atr_entry    = iATR(_Symbol, TF_Entry,  ST_Period);
   h_atr_sl       = iATR(_Symbol, TF_Middle, ATR_SL_Period);
   prevUpper = 0; prevLower = 0; prevTrend = 0;
   trade.SetExpertMagicNumber(Magic);
   return INIT_SUCCEEDED;
}

void OnDeinit(const int reason)
{
   IndicatorRelease(h_ema_fast_htf); IndicatorRelease(h_ema_slow_htf);
   IndicatorRelease(h_adx_mtf);      IndicatorRelease(h_atr_entry);
   IndicatorRelease(h_atr_sl);
}

void OnTick()
{
   static datetime lastBar = 0;
   datetime curBar = iTime(_Symbol, TF_Entry, 0);
   if(curBar == lastBar) return;
   lastBar = curBar;

   if(IsDrawdownBreached()) { CloseAll(); return; }
   if(CountMyPositions() > 0) { ManageSupertrend(); return; }
   if(IsDailyLossBreached()) return;
   if(CountMyPositions() >= (int)MathMin(Max_Positions, 5)) return;

   //--- HTF
   double ef_h[2], es_h[2];
   CopyBuffer(h_ema_fast_htf, 0, 0, 2, ef_h);
   CopyBuffer(h_ema_slow_htf, 0, 0, 2, es_h);
   bool htf_bull = ef_h[0] > es_h[0];
   bool htf_bear = ef_h[0] < es_h[0];

   //--- MTF
   double adx_m[2]; CopyBuffer(h_adx_mtf, MAIN_LINE, 0, 2, adx_m);
   if(adx_m[0] < ADX_Min) return;

   //--- LTF
   double atr_e[3]; CopyBuffer(h_atr_entry, 0, 0, 3, atr_e);
   int    stSig = CalcSupertrend(atr_e[1]);
   double atr_s[2]; CopyBuffer(h_atr_sl, 0, 0, 2, atr_s);

   if(htf_bull && stSig == 1)
   {
      double ask = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_ASK);
      double sl  = ask - atr_s[0] * HardSL_ATR_Mult;
      trade.Buy(CalcDynamicLot(ask-sl), _Symbol, ask, sl, 0, "DW_BUY");
   }
   else if(htf_bear && stSig == -1)
   {
      double bid = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_BID);
      double sl  = bid + atr_s[0] * HardSL_ATR_Mult;
      trade.Sell(CalcDynamicLot(sl-bid), _Symbol, bid, sl, 0, "DW_SELL");
   }
}
```

```mql5
bool IsSessionTime()
{
   MqlDateTime dt; TimeToStruct(TimeGMT(), dt);
   return (dt.hour >= SessionStart && dt.hour < SessionEnd);
}

double GetPip()
{
   double point = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_POINT);
   int    digs  = (int)SymbolInfoInteger(_Symbol, SYMBOL_DIGITS);
   return (digs == 5 || digs == 3) ? point * 10.0 : point;
}

//────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
int OnInit()
{
   h_ema_htf         = iMA(_Symbol, TF_Higher, EMA_HTF_Period,  0, MODE_EMA, PRICE_CLOSE);
   h_ema_fast_mtf    = iMA(_Symbol, TF_Middle, EMA_Fast_MTF,    0, MODE_EMA, PRICE_CLOSE);
   h_ema_slow_mtf    = iMA(_Symbol, TF_Middle, EMA_Slow_MTF,    0, MODE_EMA, PRICE_CLOSE);
   h_ema_fast_entry  = iMA(_Symbol, TF_Entry,  EMA_Fast_Entry,  0, MODE_EMA, PRICE_CLOSE);
   h_ema_slow_entry  = iMA(_Symbol, TF_Entry,  EMA_Slow_Entry,  0, MODE_EMA, PRICE_CLOSE);
   h_rsi_entry       = iRSI(_Symbol, TF_Entry, RSI_Period, PRICE_CLOSE);
   trade.SetExpertMagicNumber(Magic);
   return INIT_SUCCEEDED;
}

void OnDeinit(const int reason)
{
   IndicatorRelease(h_ema_htf);
   IndicatorRelease(h_ema_fast_mtf);  IndicatorRelease(h_ema_slow_mtf);
   IndicatorRelease(h_ema_fast_entry);IndicatorRelease(h_ema_slow_entry);
   IndicatorRelease(h_rsi_entry);
}

void OnTick()
{
   static datetime lastBar = 0;
   datetime curBar = iTime(_Symbol, TF_Entry, 0);
   if(curBar == lastBar) return;
   lastBar = curBar;

   if(IsDrawdownBreached()) { CloseAll(); return; }

   if(!IsSessionTime())
   {
      if(!forceClosed) { CloseAll(); forceClosed = true; }
      return;
   }
   forceClosed = false;

   if(IsDailyLossBreached()) return;
   if(CountMyPositions() >= (int)MathMin(Max_Positions, 5)) return;

   //--- HTF
   double ema_h[2]; CopyBuffer(h_ema_htf, 0, 0, 2, ema_h);
   double price  = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_BID);
   bool htf_bull = price > ema_h[0];
   bool htf_bear = price < ema_h[0];

   //--- MTF
   double ef_m[2], es_m[2];
   CopyBuffer(h_ema_fast_mtf, 0, 0, 2, ef_m);
   CopyBuffer(h_ema_slow_mtf, 0, 0, 2, es_m);
   bool mtf_bull = ef_m[0] > es_m[0];
   bool mtf_bear = ef_m[0] < es_m[0];

   //--- LTF
   double ef_e[3], es_e[3], rsi_v[2];
   CopyBuffer(h_ema_fast_entry, 0, 0, 3, ef_e);
   CopyBuffer(h_ema_slow_entry, 0, 0, 3, es_e);
   CopyBuffer(h_rsi_entry,      0, 0, 2, rsi_v);
   bool cross_up   = ef_e[1]>es_e[1] && ef_e[2]<=es_e[2] && rsi_v[0]>50;
   bool cross_down = ef_e[1]<es_e[1] && ef_e[2]>=es_e[2] && rsi_v[0]<50;

   double pip = GetPip();
   if(htf_bull && mtf_bull && cross_up)
   {
      double ask = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_ASK);
      double sl  = ask - SL_Pips*pip;
      double tp  = ask + TP_Pips*pip;
      trade.Buy(CalcDynamicLot(ask-sl), _Symbol, ask, sl, tp, "ES_BUY");
   }
   else if(htf_bear && mtf_bear && cross_down)
   {
      double bid = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_BID);
      double sl  = bid + SL_Pips*pip;
      double tp  = bid - TP_Pips*pip;
      trade.Sell(CalcDynamicLot(sl-bid), _Symbol, bid, sl, tp, "ES_SELL");
   }
}
```

---

## ✅ สรุปสิ่งที่ Standalone ทุก EA มีครบ

| Feature | รายละเอียด |
|---|---|
| **ไม่ต้องพึ่ง Include ภายนอก** | Copy ไฟล์เดียว Compile ได้เลย |
| **Configurable TF** | TF_Higher / TF_Middle / TF_Entry เปลี่ยนได้ทุกตัว |
| **Dynamic Lot** | คำนวณจาก Risk% × Balance/Equity ÷ SL Distance |
| **Max Positions** | Hard cap ที่ 5 ไม้ ปรับได้ 1-5 |
| **Daily Loss Stop** | หยุดเทรดเมื่อขาดทุนเกิน % ที่ตั้ง |
| **Max Drawdown Stop** | ปิดทั้งหมดเมื่อ DD เกิน % ที่ตั้ง |
| **OnDeinit** | Release Indicator Handle ทุกตัวเพื่อป้องกัน Memory Leak |
| **Magic Number** | แยกแต่ละพอร์ตชัดเจน ไม่ปะปนกัน |

----- [user] -----
ทำให้ทุก EA สามารถรัน symbol พร้อมกัน 2-3 แล้ว มีการ Set up configure แยกออกจากกันใน EA ตัวเดียว เพื่อดูว่าถ้า Back Test แล้ว 2 symbol นี้รันพร้อมกันจะ port แตกไหม

----- [assistant] -----
ยอดเยี่ยมครับบอส — นี่คือ Multi-Symbol Engine ที่ฝังอยู่ใน EA ตัวเดียว รัน 2-3 Symbol พร้อมกัน แต่ละ Symbol มี Config แยกอิสระ และมี Shared MM ดูภาพรวมพอร์ตด้วย

---

## 🌟 PORT 1 — 141049900 | Celestial Woodfire
### Multi-Symbol Standalone

```mql5
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| EA_CelestialWoodfire_141049900.mq5                               |
//| MTF Trend Following — Multi-Symbol Standalone                    |
//| รัน 2-3 Symbol พร้อมกันใน EA เดียว                               |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
#property copyright "Port 141049900"
#property version   "3.00"
#property strict

#include <Trade\Trade.mqh>

//────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
//  SYMBOL 1 CONFIG
//────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
input group "━━━ SYMBOL 1 ━━━"
input string  S1_Symbol       = "EURUSD";
input bool    S1_Enabled      = true;
input ENUM_TIMEFRAMES S1_TF_Higher = PERIOD_D1;
input ENUM_TIMEFRAMES S1_TF_Middle = PERIOD_H4;
input ENUM_TIMEFRAMES S1_TF_Entry  = PERIOD_H1;
input int     S1_EMA_Fast     = 50;
input int     S1_EMA_Slow     = 200;
input int     S1_MACD_Fast    = 12;
input int     S1_MACD_Slow    = 26;
input int     S1_MACD_Sig     = 9;
input int     S1_ADX_Period   = 14;
input double  S1_ADX_Min      = 25.0;
input int     S1_ATR_Period   = 14;
input double  S1_ATR_SL       = 1.5;
input double  S1_ATR_TP       = 3.0;
input double  S1_Risk_Pct     = 1.0;
input int     S1_MaxPos       = 2;
input bool    S1_Trail        = true;
input double  S1_Trail_ATR    = 1.0;

//────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
//  SYMBOL 2 CONFIG
//────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
input group "━━━ SYMBOL 2 ━━━"
input string  S2_Symbol       = "GBPUSD";
input bool    S2_Enabled      = true;
input ENUM_TIMEFRAMES S2_TF_Higher = PERIOD_D1;
input ENUM_TIMEFRAMES S2_TF_Middle = PERIOD_H4;
input ENUM_TIMEFRAMES S2_TF_Entry  = PERIOD_H1;
input int     S2_EMA_Fast     = 50;
input int     S2_EMA_Slow     = 200;
input int     S2_MACD_Fast    = 12;
input int     S2_MACD_Slow    = 26;
input int     S2_MACD_Sig     = 9;
input int     S2_ADX_Period   = 14;
input double  S2_ADX_Min      = 25.0;
input int     S2_ATR_Period   = 14;
input double  S2_ATR_SL       = 1.5;
input double  S2_ATR_TP       = 3.0;
input double  S2_Risk_Pct     = 1.0;
input int     S2_MaxPos       = 2;
input bool    S2_Trail        = true;
input double  S2_Trail_ATR    = 1.0;

//────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
//  SYMBOL 3 CONFIG
//────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
input group "━━━ SYMBOL 3 ━━━"
input string  S3_Symbol       = "USDJPY";
input bool    S3_Enabled      = false;
input ENUM_TIMEFRAMES S3_TF_Higher = PERIOD_D1;
input ENUM_TIMEFRAMES S3_TF_Middle = PERIOD_H4;
input ENUM_TIMEFRAMES S3_TF_Entry  = PERIOD_H1;
input int     S3_EMA_Fast     = 50;
input int     S3_EMA_Slow     = 200;
input int     S3_MACD_Fast    = 12;
input int     S3_MACD_Slow    = 26;
input int     S3_MACD_Sig     = 9;
input int     S3_ADX_Period   = 14;
input double  S3_ADX_Min      = 25.0;
input int     S3_ATR_Period   = 14;
input double  S3_ATR_SL       = 1.5;
input double  S3_ATR_TP       = 3.0;
input double  S3_Risk_Pct     = 1.0;
input int     S3_MaxPos       = 2;
input bool    S3_Trail        = true;
input double  S3_Trail_ATR    = 1.0;

//────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
//  SHARED MONEY MANAGEMENT
//────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
input group "━━━ SHARED MONEY MANAGEMENT ━━━"
input double  Global_MaxDailyLoss_Pct = 3.0;   // Daily Loss ทั้ง Port
input double  Global_MaxDrawdown_Pct  = 10.0;  // Max DD ทั้ง Port
input int     Global_MaxTotalPos      = 5;     // ไม้รวมทุก Symbol ≤ 5
input double  Global_Min_Lot          = 0.01;
input double  Global_Max_Lot          = 5.0;
input bool    Global_UseEquity        = false;
input int     Magic                   = 141049900;

//────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
//  SYMBOL CONFIG STRUCT
//────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
struct SymbolConfig
{
   string            sym;
   bool              enabled;
   ENUM_TIMEFRAMES   tfH, tfM, tfE;
   int               emaFast, emaSlow;
   int               macdFast, macdSlow, macdSig;
   int               adxPeriod;
   double            adxMin;
   int               atrPeriod;
   double            atrSL, atrTP;
   double            riskPct;
   int               maxPos;
   bool              trail;
   double            trailATR;
   // Handles
   int               h_ef_htf, h_es_htf;
   int               h_ef_mtf, h_es_mtf;
   int               h_macd, h_adx, h_atr;
   // Bar tracker
   datetime          lastBar;
};

SymbolConfig SC[3];
CTrade trade;

//────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
//  SHARED MM FUNCTIONS
//────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
int CountAllPositions()
{
   int n = 0;
   for(int i = 0; i < PositionsTotal(); i++)
   {
      ulong t = PositionGetTicket(i);
      if(PositionSelectByTicket(t) &&
         (int)PositionGetInteger(POSITION_MAGIC) == Magic) n++;
   }
   return n;
}

int CountSymbolPositions(string sym)
{
   int n = 0;
   for(int i = 0; i < PositionsTotal(); i++)
   {
      ulong t = PositionGetTicket(i);
      if(!PositionSelectByTicket(t)) continue;
      if((int)PositionGetInteger(POSITION_MAGIC) == Magic &&
         PositionGetString(POSITION_SYMBOL) == sym) n++;
   }
   return n;
}

bool IsGlobalDailyLossBreached()
{
   double balance = AccountInfoDouble(ACCOUNT_BALANCE);
   double maxLoss = balance * Global_MaxDailyLoss_Pct / 100.0;
   datetime dayStart = StringToTime(TimeToString(TimeCurrent(), TIME_DATE));
   double pl = 0;
   for(int i = 0; i < PositionsTotal(); i++)
   {
      ulong t = PositionGetTicket(i);
      if(!PositionSelectByTicket(t)) continue;
      if((int)PositionGetInteger(POSITION_MAGIC) != Magic) continue;
      pl += PositionGetDouble(POSITION_PROFIT)
          + PositionGetDouble(POSITION_SWAP)
          + PositionGetDouble(POSITION_COMMISSION);
   }
   HistorySelect(dayStart, TimeCurrent());
   for(int i = 0; i < HistoryDealsTotal(); i++)
   {
      ulong d = HistoryDealGetTicket(i);
      if((int)HistoryDealGetInteger(d, DEAL_MAGIC) != Magic) continue;
      if(HistoryDealGetInteger(d, DEAL_ENTRY) == DEAL_ENTRY_OUT)
         pl += HistoryDealGetDouble(d, DEAL_PROFIT)
             + HistoryDealGetDouble(d, DEAL_SWAP)
             + HistoryDealGetDouble(d, DEAL_COMMISSION);
   }
   return (pl <= -maxLoss);
}

bool IsGlobalDrawdownBreached()
{
   double bal = AccountInfoDouble(ACCOUNT_BALANCE);
   double eq  = AccountInfoDouble(ACCOUNT_EQUITY);
   return (bal > 0 && (bal - eq) / bal * 100.0 >= Global_MaxDrawdown_Pct);
}

void CloseAllPositions()
{
   for(int i = PositionsTotal()-1; i >= 0; i--)
   {
      ulong t = PositionGetTicket(i);
      if(PositionSelectByTicket(t) &&
         (int)PositionGetInteger(POSITION_MAGIC) == Magic)
         trade.PositionClose(t);
   }
}

double CalcDynamicLot(string sym, double slDist, double riskPct)
{
   double base     = Global_UseEquity ? AccountInfoDouble(ACCOUNT_EQUITY)
                                      : AccountInfoDouble(ACCOUNT_BALANCE);
   double riskAmt  = base * riskPct / 100.0;
   double tickVal  = SymbolInfoDouble(sym, SYMBOL_TRADE_TICK_VALUE);
   double tickSize = SymbolInfoDouble(sym, SYMBOL_TRADE_TICK_SIZE);
   double lotStep  = SymbolInfoDouble(sym, SYMBOL_VOLUME_STEP);
   double minL     = MathMax(Global_Min_Lot, SymbolInfoDouble(sym, SYMBOL_VOLUME_MIN));
   double maxL     = MathMin(Global_Max_Lot, SymbolInfoDouble(sym, SYMBOL_VOLUME_MAX));
   if(slDist <= 0 || tickVal <= 0 || tickSize <= 0) return minL;
   double lots = riskAmt / (slDist / tickSize * tickVal);
   lots = MathFloor(lots / lotStep) * lotStep;
   return MathMax(minL, MathMin(maxL, lots));
}

double GetPip(string sym)
{
   double point = SymbolInfoDouble(sym, SYMBOL_POINT);
   int    digs  = (int)SymbolInfoInteger(sym, SYMBOL_DIGITS);
   return (digs == 5 || digs == 3) ? point * 10.0 : point;
}

//────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
//  INIT SINGLE SYMBOL
//────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
bool InitSymbol(SymbolConfig &sc)
{
   if(!sc.enabled) return true;
   if(!SymbolSelect(sc.sym, true))
   { Print("Cannot select symbol: ", sc.sym); return false; }

   sc.h_ef_htf = iMA(sc.sym, sc.tfH, sc.emaFast, 0, MODE_EMA, PRICE_CLOSE);
   sc.h_es_htf = iMA(sc.sym, sc.tfH, sc.emaSlow, 0, MODE_EMA, PRICE_CLOSE);
   sc.h_ef_mtf = iMA(sc.sym, sc.tfM, sc.emaFast, 0, MODE_EMA, PRICE_CLOSE);
   sc.h_es_mtf = iMA(sc.sym, sc.tfM, sc.emaSlow, 0, MODE_EMA, PRICE_CLOSE);
   sc.h_macd   = iMACD(sc.sym, sc.tfE, sc.macdFast, sc.macdSlow, sc.macdSig, PRICE_CLOSE);
   sc.h_adx    = iADX(sc.sym,  sc.tfE, sc.adxPeriod);
   sc.h_atr    = iATR(sc.sym,  sc.tfE, sc.atrPeriod);
   sc.lastBar  = 0;

   if(sc.h_ef_htf < 0 || sc.h_macd < 0)
   { Print("Handle error: ", sc.sym); return false; }
   Print("Initialized: ", sc.sym);
   return true;
}

void ReleaseSymbol(SymbolConfig &sc)
{
   if(!sc.enabled) return;
   IndicatorRelease(sc.h_ef_htf); IndicatorRelease(sc.h_es_htf);
   IndicatorRelease(sc.h_ef_mtf); IndicatorRelease(sc.h_es_mtf);
   IndicatorRelease(sc.h_macd);   IndicatorRelease(sc.h_adx);
   IndicatorRelease(sc.h_atr);
}

//────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
//  PROCESS SINGLE SYMBOL
//────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
void ProcessSymbol(SymbolConfig &sc)
{
   if(!sc.enabled) return;

   // Bar gate — ทำงานเฉพาะแท่งใหม่ของแต่ละ Symbol
   datetime curBar = iTime(sc.sym, sc.tfE, 0);
   if(curBar == sc.lastBar) return;
   sc.lastBar = curBar;

   // Trailing
   if(sc.trail) ManageTrailingSymbol(sc);

   // Global Guard
   if(IsGlobalDrawdownBreached()) return;
   if(IsGlobalDailyLossBreached()) return;

   // Position Guard — Global cap + Per-Symbol cap
   int totalPos  = CountAllPositions();
   int symPos    = CountSymbolPositions(sc.sym);
   int globalCap = (int)MathMin(Global_MaxTotalPos, 5);
   int symCap    = (int)MathMin(sc.maxPos, 5);
   if(totalPos >= globalCap) return;
   if(symPos   >= symCap)   return;

   //--- HTF
   double ef_h[2], es_h[2];
   CopyBuffer(sc.h_ef_htf, 0, 0, 2, ef_h);
   CopyBuffer(sc.h_es_htf, 0, 0, 2, es_h);
   bool htf_bull = ef_h[0] > es_h[0];
   bool htf_bear = ef_h[0] < es_h[0];

   //--- MTF
   double ef_m[2], es_m[2];
   CopyBuffer(sc.h_ef_mtf, 0, 0, 2, ef_m);
   CopyBuffer(sc.h_es_mtf, 0, 0, 2, es_m);
   bool mtf_bull = ef_m[0] > es_m[0];
   bool mtf_bear = ef_m[0] < es_m[0];

   //--- LTF
   double macd_m[3], macd_s[3], adx_v[2], atr_v[2];
   CopyBuffer(sc.h_macd, MAIN_LINE,   0, 3, macd_m);
   CopyBuffer(sc.h_macd, SIGNAL_LINE, 0, 3, macd_s);
   CopyBuffer(sc.h_adx,  MAIN_LINE,   0, 2, adx_v);
   CopyBuffer(sc.h_atr,  0,           0, 2, atr_v);

   bool macd_up = macd_m[1] > macd_s[1] && macd_m[2] <= macd_s[2];
   bool macd_dn = macd_m[1] < macd_s[1] && macd_m[2] >= macd_s[2];
   bool adx_ok  = adx_v[0] > sc.adxMin;
   double atr   = atr_v[0];

   if(htf_bull && mtf_bull && macd_up && adx_ok)
   {
      double ask = SymbolInfoDouble(sc.sym, SYMBOL_ASK);
      double sl  = ask - atr * sc.atrSL;
      double tp  = ask + atr * sc.atrTP;
      double lot = CalcDynamicLot(sc.sym, ask - sl, sc.riskPct);
      trade.Buy(lot, sc.sym, ask, sl, tp,
                StringFormat("CW|%s|R%.1f", sc.sym, sc.riskPct));
   }
   else if(htf_bear && mtf_bear && macd_dn && adx_ok)
   {
      double bid = SymbolInfoDouble(sc.sym, SYMBOL_BID);
      double sl  = bid + atr * sc.atrSL;
      double tp  = bid - atr * sc.atrTP;
      double lot = CalcDynamicLot(sc.sym, sl - bid, sc.riskPct);
      trade.Sell(lot, sc.sym, bid, sl, tp,
                 StringFormat("CW|%s|R%.1f", sc.sym, sc.riskPct));
   }
}

void ManageTrailingSymbol(SymbolConfig &sc)
{
   double ef_m[2], atr_v[2];
   CopyBuffer(sc.h_ef_mtf, 0, 0, 2, ef_m);
   CopyBuffer(sc.h_atr,    0, 0, 2, atr_v);
   double atr = atr_v[0];

   for(int i = PositionsTotal()-1; i >= 0; i--)
   {
      ulong t = PositionGetTicket(i);
      if(!PositionSelectByTicket(t)) continue;
      if((int)PositionGetInteger(POSITION_MAGIC) != Magic) continue;
      if(PositionGetString(POSITION_SYMBOL) != sc.sym) continue;
      if(PositionGetDouble(POSITION_PROFIT) <= 0) continue;

      double sl  = PositionGetDouble(POSITION_SL);
      double tp  = PositionGetDouble(POSITION_TP);
      long   typ = PositionGetInteger(POSITION_TYPE);

      if(typ == POSITION_TYPE_BUY)
      {
         double nsl = ef_m[0] - atr * sc.trailATR;
         if(nsl > sl && nsl < SymbolInfoDouble(sc.sym, SYMBOL_BID))
            trade.PositionModify(t, nsl, tp);
      }
      else
      {
         double nsl = ef_m[0] + atr * sc.trailATR;
         if(nsl < sl && nsl > SymbolInfoDouble(sc.sym, SYMBOL_ASK))
            trade.PositionModify(t, nsl, tp);
      }
   }
}

//────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
//  ONINIT / ONDEINIT / ONTICK
//────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
int OnInit()
{
   trade.SetExpertMagicNumber(Magic);

   // Symbol 1
   SC[0].sym=S1_Symbol; SC[0].enabled=S1_Enabled;
   SC[0].tfH=S1_TF_Higher; SC[0].tfM=S1_TF_Middle; SC[0].tfE=S1_TF_Entry;
   SC[0].emaFast=S1_EMA_Fast; SC[0].emaSlow=S1_EMA_Slow;
   SC[0].macdFast=S1_MACD_Fast; SC[0].macdSlow=S1_MACD_Slow; SC[0].macdSig=S1_MACD_Sig;
   SC[0].adxPeriod=S1_ADX_Period; SC[0].adxMin=S1_ADX_Min;
   SC[0].atrPeriod=S1_ATR_Period; SC[0].atrSL=S1_ATR_SL; SC[0].atrTP=S1_ATR_TP;
   SC[0].riskPct=S1_Risk_Pct; SC[0].maxPos=S1_MaxPos;
   SC[0].trail=S1_Trail; SC[0].trailATR=S1_Trail_ATR;

   // Symbol 2
   SC[1].sym=S2_Symbol; SC[1].enabled=S2_Enabled;
   SC[1].tfH=S2_TF_Higher; SC[1].tfM=S2_TF_Middle; SC[1].tfE=S2_TF_Entry;
   SC[1].emaFast=S2_EMA_Fast; SC[1].emaSlow=S2_EMA_Slow;
   SC[1].macdFast=S2_MACD_Fast; SC[1].macdSlow=S2_MACD_Slow; SC[1].macdSig=S2_MACD_Sig;
   SC[1].adxPeriod=S2_ADX_Period; SC[1].adxMin=S2_ADX_Min;
   SC[1].atrPeriod=S2_ATR_Period; SC[1].atrSL=S2_ATR_SL; SC[1].atrTP=S2_ATR_TP;
   SC[1].riskPct=S2_Risk_Pct; SC[1].maxPos=S2_MaxPos;
   SC[1].trail=S2_Trail; SC[1].trailATR=S2_Trail_ATR;

   // Symbol 3
   SC[2].sym=S3_Symbol; SC[2].enabled=S3_Enabled;
   SC[2].tfH=S3_TF_Higher; SC[2].tfM=S3_TF_Middle; SC[2].tfE=S3_TF_Entry;
   SC[2].emaFast=S3_EMA_Fast; SC[2].emaSlow=S3_EMA_Slow;
   SC[2].macdFast=S3_MACD_Fast; SC[2].macdSlow=S3_MACD_Slow; SC[2].macdSig=S3_MACD_Sig;
   SC[2].adxPeriod=S3_ADX_Period; SC[2].adxMin=S3_ADX_Min;
   SC[2].atrPeriod=S3_ATR_Period; SC[2].atrSL=S3_ATR_SL; SC[2].atrTP=S3_ATR_TP;
   SC[2].riskPct=S3_Risk_Pct; SC[2].maxPos=S3_MaxPos;
   SC[2].trail=S3_Trail; SC[2].trailATR=S3_Trail_ATR;

   for(int i = 0; i < 3; i++)
      if(!InitSymbol(SC[i])) return INIT_FAILED;

   Print("CelestialWoodfire MultiSymbol Ready | Magic:", Magic);
   return INIT_SUCCEEDED;
}

void OnDeinit(const int reason)
{
   for(int i = 0; i < 3; i++) ReleaseSymbol(SC[i]);
}

void OnTick()
{
   if(IsGlobalDrawdownBreached()) { CloseAllPositions(); return; }
   for(int i = 0; i < 3; i++) ProcessSymbol(SC[i]);
}
```

---

> ⚠️ **Port 1 เสร็จสมบูรณ์แล้ว** — Pattern นี้จะใช้ซ้ำกับทุก EA ครับบอส ต่างกันเฉพาะส่วน Logic ภายใน `ProcessSymbol()` ผมจะเขียนต่อทุกพอร์ตเลย

---

## 🌟 PORT 2 — 146004104 | Ironclad Forest
### Multi-Symbol Smart Grid

```mql5
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| EA_IroncladForest_146004104.mq5                                  |
//| MTF Smart Grid — Multi-Symbol Standalone                         |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
#property copyright "Port 146004104"
#property version   "3.00"
#property strict

#include <Trade\Trade.mqh>

input group "━━━ SYMBOL 1 ━━━"
input string  S1_Symbol        = "AUDNZD";
input bool    S1_Enabled       = true;
input ENUM_TIMEFRAMES S1_TF_Higher = PERIOD_W1;
input ENUM_TIMEFRAMES S1_TF_Middle = PERIOD_D1;
input ENUM_TIMEFRAMES S1_TF_Entry  = PERIOD_H1;
input int     S1_BB_Period     = 20;
input double  S1_BB_Dev        = 2.0;
input int     S1_RSI_Period    = 14;
input int     S1_ATR_Period    = 14;
input double  S1_GridATR       = 1.0;
input double  S1_LotMult       = 1.5;
input double  S1_BaseLot       = 0.01;
input double  S1_BasketTP_Pct  = 1.0;
input double  S1_BasketSL_Pct  = 3.0;
input double  S1_Risk_Pct      = 1.0;
input int     S1_MaxGrid       = 4;

input group "━━━ SYMBOL 2 ━━━"
input string  S2_Symbol        = "AUDCAD";
input bool    S2_Enabled       = true;
input ENUM_TIMEFRAMES S2_TF_Higher = PERIOD_W1;
input ENUM_TIMEFRAMES S2_TF_Middle = PERIOD_D1;
input ENUM_TIMEFRAMES S2_TF_Entry  = PERIOD_H1;
input int     S2_BB_Period     = 20;
input double  S2_BB_Dev        = 2.0;
input int     S2_RSI_Period    = 14;
input int     S2_ATR_Period    = 14;
input double  S2_GridATR       = 1.0;
input double  S2_LotMult       = 1.5;
input double  S2_BaseLot       = 0.01;
input double  S2_BasketTP_Pct  = 1.0;
input double  S2_BasketSL_Pct  = 3.0;
input double  S2_Risk_Pct      = 1.0;
input int     S2_MaxGrid       = 4;

input group "━━━ SYMBOL 3 ━━━"
input string  S3_Symbol        = "NZDCAD";
input bool    S3_Enabled       = false;
input ENUM_TIMEFRAMES S3_TF_Higher = PERIOD_W1;
input ENUM_TIMEFRAMES S3_TF_Middle = PERIOD_D1;
input ENUM_TIMEFRAMES S3_TF_Entry  = PERIOD_H1;
input int     S3_BB_Period     = 20;
input double  S3_BB_Dev        = 2.0;
input int     S3_RSI_Period    = 14;
input int     S3_ATR_Period    = 14;
input double  S3_GridATR       = 1.0;
input double  S3_LotMult       = 1.5;
input double  S3_BaseLot       = 0.01;
input double  S3_BasketTP_Pct  = 1.0;
input double  S3_BasketSL_Pct  = 3.0;
input double  S3_Risk_Pct      = 1.0;
input int     S3_MaxGrid       = 4;

input group "━━━ SHARED MONEY MANAGEMENT ━━━"
input double  Global_MaxDailyLoss_Pct = 3.0;
input double  Global_MaxDrawdown_Pct  = 10.0;
input int     Global_MaxTotalPos      = 5;
input double  Global_Min_Lot          = 0.01;
input double  Global_Max_Lot          = 2.0;
input bool    Global_UseEquity        = false;
input int     Magic                   = 146004104;

struct GridConfig
{
   string sym; bool enabled;
   ENUM_TIMEFRAMES tfH, tfM, tfE;
   int bbPeriod; double bbDev;
   int rsiPeriod, atrPeriod;
   double gridATR, lotMult, baseLot;
   double basketTP, basketSL, riskPct;
   int maxGrid;
   int h_bb_htf, h_bb_mtf, h_bb_entry;
   int h_rsi, h_atr;
   bool gridBull;
   datetime lastBar;
};

GridConfig GC[3];
CTrade trade;

//────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
int CountAllPositions()
{
   int n = 0;
   for(int i = 0; i < PositionsTotal(); i++)
   {
      ulong t = PositionGetTicket(i);
      if(PositionSelectByTicket(t) &&
         (int)PositionGetInteger(POSITION_MAGIC) == Magic) n++;
   }
   return n;
}

int CountSymbolPositions(string sym)
{
   int n = 0;
   for(int i = 0; i < PositionsTotal(); i++)
   {
      ulong t = PositionGetTicket(i);
      if(!PositionSelectByTicket(t)) continue;
      if((int)PositionGetInteger(POSITION_MAGIC) == Magic &&
         PositionGetString(POSITION_SYMBOL) == sym) n++;
   }
   return n;
}

bool IsGlobalDailyLossBreached()
{
   double bal  = AccountInfoDouble(ACCOUNT_BALANCE);
   double maxL = bal * Global_MaxDailyLoss_Pct / 100.0;
   datetime ds = StringToTime(TimeToString(TimeCurrent(), TIME_DATE));
   double pl = 0;
   for(int i = 0; i < PositionsTotal(); i++)
   {
      ulong t = PositionGetTicket(i);
      if(!PositionSelectByTicket(t)) continue;
      if((int)PositionGetInteger(POSITION_MAGIC) != Magic) continue;
      pl += PositionGetDouble(POSITION_PROFIT)
          + PositionGetDouble(POSITION_SWAP)
          + PositionGetDouble(POSITION_COMMISSION);
   }
   HistorySelect(ds, TimeCurrent());
   for(int i = 0; i < HistoryDealsTotal(); i++)
   {
      ulong d = HistoryDealGetTicket(i);
      if((int)HistoryDealGetInteger(d, DEAL_MAGIC) != Magic) continue;
      if(HistoryDealGetInteger(d, DEAL_ENTRY) == DEAL_ENTRY_OUT)
         pl += HistoryDealGetDouble(d, DEAL_PROFIT)
             + HistoryDealGetDouble(d, DEAL_SWAP)
             + HistoryDealGetDouble(d, DEAL_COMMISSION);
   }
   return (pl <= -maxL);
}

bool IsGlobalDrawdownBreached()
{
   double bal = AccountInfoDouble(ACCOUNT_BALANCE);
   double eq  = AccountInfoDouble(ACCOUNT_EQUITY);
   return (bal > 0 && (bal - eq) / bal * 100.0 >= Global_MaxDrawdown_Pct);
}

void CloseAllPositions()
{
   for(int i = PositionsTotal()-1; i >= 0; i--)
   {
      ulong t = PositionGetTicket(i);
      if(PositionSelectByTicket(t) &&
         (int)PositionGetInteger(POSITION_MAGIC) == Magic)
         trade.PositionClose(t);
   }
}

void CloseSymbolPositions(string sym)
{
   for(int i = PositionsTotal()-1; i >= 0; i--)
   {
      ulong t = PositionGetTicket(i);
      if(!PositionSelectByTicket(t)) continue;
      if((int)PositionGetInteger(POSITION_MAGIC) == Magic &&
         PositionGetString(POSITION_SYMBOL) == sym)
         trade.PositionClose(t);
   }
}

double GetLastGridPrice(string sym)
{
   double lp = 0; datetime lt = 0;
   for(int i = 0; i < PositionsTotal(); i++)
   {
      ulong t = PositionGetTicket(i);
      if(!PositionSelectByTicket(t)) continue;
      if((int)PositionGetInteger(POSITION_MAGIC) != Magic) continue;
      if(PositionGetString(POSITION_SYMBOL) != sym) continue;
      datetime pt = (datetime)PositionGetInteger(POSITION_TIME);
      if(pt > lt) { lt=pt; lp=PositionGetDouble(POSITION_PRICE_OPEN); }
   }
   return lp;
}

double CalcGridLot(GridConfig &gc, int level)
{
   double lots    = gc.baseLot * MathPow(gc.lotMult, level);
   double lotStep = SymbolInfoDouble(gc.sym, SYMBOL_VOLUME_STEP);
   double minL    = MathMax(Global_Min_Lot, SymbolInfoDouble(gc.sym, SYMBOL_VOLUME_MIN));
   double maxL    = MathMin(Global_Max_Lot, SymbolInfoDouble(gc.sym, SYMBOL_VOLUME_MAX));
   lots = MathFloor(lots / lotStep) * lotStep;
   return MathMax(minL, MathMin(maxL, lots));
}

void CheckBasketExit(GridConfig &gc)
{
   double bal = AccountInfoDouble(ACCOUNT_BALANCE);
   double pl = 0; int cnt = 0;
   for(int i = 0; i < PositionsTotal(); i++)
   {
      ulong t = PositionGetTicket(i);
      if(!PositionSelectByTicket(t)) continue;
      if((int)PositionGetInteger(POSITION_MAGIC) != Magic) continue;
      if(PositionGetString(POSITION_SYMBOL) != gc.sym) continue;
      pl += PositionGetDouble(POSITION_PROFIT)
          + PositionGetDouble(POSITION_SWAP)
          + PositionGetDouble(POSITION_COMMISSION);
      cnt++;
   }
   if(cnt == 0) return;
   if(pl >=  bal * gc.basketTP / 100.0 ||
      pl <= -bal * gc.basketSL / 100.0)
      CloseSymbolPositions(gc.sym);
}

//────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
bool InitGridSymbol(GridConfig &gc)
{
   if(!gc.enabled) return true;
   if(!SymbolSelect(gc.sym, true)) return false;
   gc.h_bb_htf   = iBands(gc.sym, gc.tfH, gc.bbPeriod, 0, gc.bbDev, PRICE_CLOSE);
   gc.h_bb_mtf   = iBands(gc.sym, gc.tfM, gc.bbPeriod, 0, gc.bbDev, PRICE_CLOSE);
   gc.h_bb_entry = iBands(gc.sym, gc.tfE, gc.bbPeriod, 0, gc.bbDev, PRICE_CLOSE);
   gc.h_rsi      = iRSI(gc.sym,   gc.tfE, gc.rsiPeriod, PRICE_CLOSE);
   gc.h_atr      = iATR(gc.sym,   gc.tfE, gc.atrPeriod);
   gc.gridBull   = false;
   gc.lastBar    = 0;
   return true;
}

void ReleaseGridSymbol(GridConfig &gc)
{
   if(!gc.enabled) return;
   IndicatorRelease(gc.h_bb_htf);   IndicatorRelease(gc.h_bb_mtf);
   IndicatorRelease(gc.h_bb_entry); IndicatorRelease(gc.h_rsi);
   IndicatorRelease(gc.h_atr);
}

void ProcessGridSymbol(GridConfig &gc)
{
   if(!gc.enabled) return;
   datetime curBar = iTime(gc.sym, gc.tfE, 0);
   if(curBar == gc.lastBar) return;
   gc.lastBar = curBar;

   CheckBasketExit(gc);
   if(IsGlobalDrawdownBreached()) return;
   if(IsGlobalDailyLossBreached()) return;

   int totalPos = CountAllPositions();
   int symPos   = CountSymbolPositions(gc.sym);
   int globalCap= (int)MathMin(Global_MaxTotalPos, 5);
   int maxGrid  = (int)MathMin(gc.maxGrid, 4);
   if(totalPos >= globalCap) return;
   if(symPos   >= maxGrid)   return;

   double price = SymbolInfoDouble(gc.sym, SYMBOL_BID);

   // HTF: Price in W1 BB Range
   double htf_u[2], htf_l[2];
   CopyBuffer(gc.h_bb_htf, 1, 0, 2, htf_u);
   CopyBuffer(gc.h_bb_htf, 2, 0, 2, htf_l);
   if(price < htf_l[0] || price > htf_u[0]) return;

   // MTF: BB Middle Bias
   double mtf_m[2];
   CopyBuffer(gc.h_bb_mtf, 0, 0, 2, mtf_m);
   bool mtf_bull = price < mtf_m[0];
   bool mtf_bear = price > mtf_m[0];

   // LTF: BB Touch + RSI
   double en_u[2], en_l[2], rsi_v[2], atr_v[2];
   CopyBuffer(gc.h_bb_entry, 1, 0, 2, en_u);
   CopyBuffer(gc.h_bb_entry, 2, 0, 2, en_l);
   CopyBuffer(gc.h_rsi, 0, 0, 2, rsi_v);
   CopyBuffer(gc.h_atr, 0, 0, 2, atr_v);
   double atr = atr_v[0];

   bool buyOK  = price < en_l[0] && rsi_v[0] < 30;
   bool sellOK = price > en_u[0] && rsi_v[0] > 70;

   if(symPos == 0)
   {
      if(mtf_bull && buyOK)
      {
         gc.gridBull = true;
         double ask = SymbolInfoDouble(gc.sym, SYMBOL_ASK);
         trade.Buy(CalcGridLot(gc,0), gc.sym, ask, 0, 0,
                   StringFormat("IF|%s|L0", gc.sym));
      }
      else if(mtf_bear && sellOK)
      {
         gc.gridBull = false;
         double bid = SymbolInfoDouble(gc.sym, SYMBOL_BID);
         trade.Sell(CalcGridLot(gc,0), gc.sym, bid, 0, 0,
                    StringFormat("IF|%s|L0", gc.sym));
      }
   }
   else if(symPos < maxGrid && totalPos < globalCap)
   {
      double lastP = GetLastGridPrice(gc.sym);
      if(gc.gridBull && price < lastP - atr * gc.gridATR)
      {
         double ask = SymbolInfoDouble(gc.sym, SYMBOL_ASK);
         trade.Buy(CalcGridLot(gc, symPos), gc.sym, ask, 0, 0,
                   StringFormat("IF|%s|L%d", gc.sym, symPos));
      }
      else if(!gc.gridBull && price > lastP + atr * gc.gridATR)
      {
         double bid = SymbolInfoDouble(gc.sym, SYMBOL_BID);
         trade.Sell(CalcGridLot(gc, symPos), gc.sym, bid, 0, 0,
                    StringFormat("IF|%s|L%d", gc.sym, symPos));
      }
   }
}

//────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
int OnInit()
{
   trade.SetExpertMagicNumber(Magic);

   GC[0]={S1_Symbol,S1_Enabled,S1_TF_Higher,S1_TF_Middle,S1_TF_Entry,
          S1_BB_Period,S1_BB_Dev,S1_RSI_Period,S1_ATR_Period,
          S1_GridATR,S1_LotMult,S1_BaseLot,S1_BasketTP_Pct,S1_BasketSL_Pct,
          S1_Risk_Pct,S1_MaxGrid};

   GC[1]={S2_Symbol,S2_Enabled,S2_TF_Higher,S2_TF_Middle,S2_TF_Entry,
          S2_BB_Period,S2_BB_Dev,S2_RSI_Period,S2_ATR_Period,
          S2_GridATR,S2_LotMult,S2_BaseLot,S2_BasketTP_Pct,S2_BasketSL_Pct,
          S2_Risk_Pct,S2_MaxGrid};

   GC[2]={S3_Symbol,S3_Enabled,S3_TF_Higher,S3_TF_Middle,S3_TF_Entry,
          S3_BB_Period,S3_BB_Dev,S3_RSI_Period,S3_ATR_Period,
          S3_GridATR,S3_LotMult,S3_BaseLot,S3_BasketTP_Pct,S3_BasketSL_Pct,
          S3_Risk_Pct,S3_MaxGrid};

   for(int i = 0; i < 3; i++)
      if(!InitGridSymbol(GC[i])) return INIT_FAILED;

   return INIT_SUCCEEDED;
}

void OnDeinit(const int reason)
{
   for(int i = 0; i < 3; i++) ReleaseGridSymbol(GC[i]);
}

void OnTick()
{
   if(IsGlobalDrawdownBreached()) { CloseAllPositions(); return; }
   for(int i = 0; i < 3; i++) ProcessGridSymbol(GC[i]);
}
```

---

> Port 3-11 ใช้ Pattern เดียวกันทุกตัว ผมจะเขียนเฉพาะส่วน Config Struct + ProcessSymbol ที่ต่างกันครับบอส
