## 🌟 PORT 3 — 193234160 | Dawn Ascendant
### Multi-Symbol Breakout

```mql5
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| EA_DawnAscendant_193234160.mq5                                   |
//| MTF Breakout — Multi-Symbol Standalone                           |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
#property copyright "Port 193234160"
#property version   "3.00"
#property strict

#include <Trade\Trade.mqh>

input group "━━━ SYMBOL 1 ━━━"
input string  S1_Symbol       = "USDJPY";
input bool    S1_Enabled      = true;
input ENUM_TIMEFRAMES S1_TF_Higher = PERIOD_W1;
input ENUM_TIMEFRAMES S1_TF_Middle = PERIOD_D1;
input ENUM_TIMEFRAMES S1_TF_Entry  = PERIOD_H4;
input int     S1_Donchian     = 20;
input int     S1_ATR_Period   = 14;
input double  S1_ATR_Entry    = 0.5;
input double  S1_ATR_TP       = 2.0;
input double  S1_ATR_SL       = 1.0;
input int     S1_EMA_HTF      = 200;
input int     S1_Vol_Period   = 20;
input double  S1_Vol_Mult     = 1.5;
input double  S1_Risk_Pct     = 1.5;
input int     S1_MaxPos       = 2;
input bool    S1_Trail        = true;
input double  S1_Trail_ATR    = 1.0;

input group "━━━ SYMBOL 2 ━━━"
input string  S2_Symbol       = "GBPJPY";
input bool    S2_Enabled      = true;
input ENUM_TIMEFRAMES S2_TF_Higher = PERIOD_W1;
input ENUM_TIMEFRAMES S2_TF_Middle = PERIOD_D1;
input ENUM_TIMEFRAMES S2_TF_Entry  = PERIOD_H4;
input int     S2_Donchian     = 20;
input int     S2_ATR_Period   = 14;
input double  S2_ATR_Entry    = 0.5;
input double  S2_ATR_TP       = 2.0;
input double  S2_ATR_SL       = 1.0;
input int     S2_EMA_HTF      = 200;
input int     S2_Vol_Period   = 20;
input double  S2_Vol_Mult     = 1.5;
input double  S2_Risk_Pct     = 1.5;
input int     S2_MaxPos       = 2;
input bool    S2_Trail        = true;
input double  S2_Trail_ATR    = 1.0;

input group "━━━ SYMBOL 3 ━━━"
input string  S3_Symbol       = "EURJPY";
input bool    S3_Enabled      = false;
input ENUM_TIMEFRAMES S3_TF_Higher = PERIOD_W1;
input ENUM_TIMEFRAMES S3_TF_Middle = PERIOD_D1;
input ENUM_TIMEFRAMES S3_TF_Entry  = PERIOD_H4;
input int     S3_Donchian     = 20;
input int     S3_ATR_Period   = 14;
input double  S3_ATR_Entry    = 0.5;
input double  S3_ATR_TP       = 2.0;
input double  S3_ATR_SL       = 1.0;
input int     S3_EMA_HTF      = 200;
input int     S3_Vol_Period   = 20;
input double  S3_Vol_Mult     = 1.5;
input double  S3_Risk_Pct     = 1.5;
input int     S3_MaxPos       = 2;
input bool    S3_Trail        = true;
input double  S3_Trail_ATR    = 1.0;

input group "━━━ SHARED MONEY MANAGEMENT ━━━"
input double  Global_MaxDailyLoss_Pct = 4.0;
input double  Global_MaxDrawdown_Pct  = 12.0;
input int     Global_MaxTotalPos      = 5;
input double  Global_Min_Lot          = 0.01;
input double  Global_Max_Lot          = 5.0;
input bool    Global_UseEquity        = false;
input int     Magic                   = 193234160;

struct BrkConfig
{
   string sym; bool enabled;
   ENUM_TIMEFRAMES tfH, tfM, tfE;
   int donchian, atrPeriod, emaPeriod, volPeriod;
   double atrEntry, atrTP, atrSL, volMult, riskPct;
   int maxPos; bool trail; double trailATR;
   int h_ema_htf, h_atr_mtf, h_atr_entry;
   datetime lastBar;
};

BrkConfig BC[3];
CTrade trade;

//────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
int CountAllPositions()
{
   int n=0;
   for(int i=0;i<PositionsTotal();i++)
   { ulong t=PositionGetTicket(i);
     if(PositionSelectByTicket(t)&&(int)PositionGetInteger(POSITION_MAGIC)==Magic) n++; }
   return n;
}

int CountSymbolPositions(string sym)
{
   int n=0;
   for(int i=0;i<PositionsTotal();i++)
   { ulong t=PositionGetTicket(i);
     if(!PositionSelectByTicket(t)) continue;
     if((int)PositionGetInteger(POSITION_MAGIC)==Magic &&
        PositionGetString(POSITION_SYMBOL)==sym) n++; }
   return n;
}

bool IsGlobalDailyLossBreached()
{
   double bal=AccountInfoDouble(ACCOUNT_BALANCE);
   double maxL=bal*Global_MaxDailyLoss_Pct/100.0;
   datetime ds=StringToTime(TimeToString(TimeCurrent(),TIME_DATE));
   double pl=0;
   for(int i=0;i<PositionsTotal();i++)
   { ulong t=PositionGetTicket(i); if(!PositionSelectByTicket(t)) continue;
     if((int)PositionGetInteger(POSITION_MAGIC)!=Magic) continue;
     pl+=PositionGetDouble(POSITION_PROFIT)+PositionGetDouble(POSITION_SWAP)+PositionGetDouble(POSITION_COMMISSION); }
   HistorySelect(ds,TimeCurrent());
   for(int i=0;i<HistoryDealsTotal();i++)
   { ulong d=HistoryDealGetTicket(i);
     if((int)HistoryDealGetInteger(d,DEAL_MAGIC)!=Magic) continue;
     if(HistoryDealGetInteger(d,DEAL_ENTRY)==DEAL_ENTRY_OUT)
        pl+=HistoryDealGetDouble(d,DEAL_PROFIT)+HistoryDealGetDouble(d,DEAL_SWAP)+HistoryDealGetDouble(d,DEAL_COMMISSION); }
   return (pl<=-maxL);
}

bool IsGlobalDrawdownBreached()
{ double b=AccountInfoDouble(ACCOUNT_BALANCE),e=AccountInfoDouble(ACCOUNT_EQUITY);
  return (b>0&&(b-e)/b*100.0>=Global_MaxDrawdown_Pct); }

void CloseAllPositions()
{ for(int i=PositionsTotal()-1;i>=0;i--)
  { ulong t=PositionGetTicket(i);
    if(PositionSelectByTicket(t)&&(int)PositionGetInteger(POSITION_MAGIC)==Magic)
       trade.PositionClose(t); } }

double CalcDynamicLot(string sym,double slDist,double riskPct)
{
   double base=Global_UseEquity?AccountInfoDouble(ACCOUNT_EQUITY):AccountInfoDouble(ACCOUNT_BALANCE);
   double rAmt=base*riskPct/100.0;
   double tv=SymbolInfoDouble(sym,SYMBOL_TRADE_TICK_VALUE);
   double ts=SymbolInfoDouble(sym,SYMBOL_TRADE_TICK_SIZE);
   double ls=SymbolInfoDouble(sym,SYMBOL_VOLUME_STEP);
   double minL=MathMax(Global_Min_Lot,SymbolInfoDouble(sym,SYMBOL_VOLUME_MIN));
   double maxL=MathMin(Global_Max_Lot,SymbolInfoDouble(sym,SYMBOL_VOLUME_MAX));
   if(slDist<=0||tv<=0||ts<=0) return minL;
   double lots=rAmt/(slDist/ts*tv);
   lots=MathFloor(lots/ls)*ls;
   return MathMax(minL,MathMin(maxL,lots));
}

void DeleteSymbolPendings(string sym, int maxSec)
{
   for(int i=OrdersTotal()-1;i>=0;i--)
   { ulong t=OrderGetTicket(i); if(!OrderSelect(t)) continue;
     if((int)OrderGetInteger(ORDER_MAGIC)!=Magic) continue;
     if(OrderGetString(ORDER_SYMBOL)!=sym) continue;
     datetime st=(datetime)OrderGetInteger(ORDER_TIME_SETUP);
     if((TimeCurrent()-st)>=maxSec) trade.OrderDelete(t); }
}

void ManageBrkTrailing(BrkConfig &bc)
{
   double atr_e[2]; CopyBuffer(bc.h_atr_entry, 0, 0, 2, atr_e);
   double atr = atr_e[0];
   for(int i=PositionsTotal()-1;i>=0;i--)
   {
      ulong t=PositionGetTicket(i); if(!PositionSelectByTicket(t)) continue;
      if((int)PositionGetInteger(POSITION_MAGIC)!=Magic) continue;
      if(PositionGetString(POSITION_SYMBOL)!=bc.sym) continue;
      if(PositionGetDouble(POSITION_PROFIT)<=0) continue;
      double sl=PositionGetDouble(POSITION_SL),tp=PositionGetDouble(POSITION_TP);
      long typ=PositionGetInteger(POSITION_TYPE);
      if(typ==POSITION_TYPE_BUY)
      { double nsl=SymbolInfoDouble(bc.sym,SYMBOL_BID)-atr*bc.trailATR;
        if(nsl>sl) trade.PositionModify(t,nsl,tp); }
      else
      { double nsl=SymbolInfoDouble(bc.sym,SYMBOL_ASK)+atr*bc.trailATR;
        if(nsl<sl) trade.PositionModify(t,nsl,tp); }
   }
}

bool InitBrkSymbol(BrkConfig &bc)
{
   if(!bc.enabled) return true;
   if(!SymbolSelect(bc.sym,true)) return false;
   bc.h_ema_htf   = iMA(bc.sym,bc.tfH,bc.emaPeriod,0,MODE_EMA,PRICE_CLOSE);
   bc.h_atr_mtf   = iATR(bc.sym,bc.tfM,bc.atrPeriod);
   bc.h_atr_entry = iATR(bc.sym,bc.tfE,bc.atrPeriod);
   bc.lastBar = 0;
   return true;
}

void ReleaseBrkSymbol(BrkConfig &bc)
{
   if(!bc.enabled) return;
   IndicatorRelease(bc.h_ema_htf);
   IndicatorRelease(bc.h_atr_mtf);
   IndicatorRelease(bc.h_atr_entry);
}

void ProcessBrkSymbol(BrkConfig &bc)
{
   if(!bc.enabled) return;
   datetime curBar=iTime(bc.sym,bc.tfE,0);
   if(curBar==bc.lastBar) return;
   bc.lastBar=curBar;

   DeleteSymbolPendings(bc.sym, PeriodSeconds(bc.tfE));
   if(bc.trail && CountSymbolPositions(bc.sym)>0) { ManageBrkTrailing(bc); return; }
   if(IsGlobalDrawdownBreached()||IsGlobalDailyLossBreached()) return;

   int totalPos=CountAllPositions();
   int symPos=CountSymbolPositions(bc.sym);
   if(totalPos>=(int)MathMin(Global_MaxTotalPos,5)) return;
   if(symPos>=(int)MathMin(bc.maxPos,5)) return;

   // HTF EMA
   double ema_h[2]; CopyBuffer(bc.h_ema_htf,0,0,2,ema_h);
   double price=SymbolInfoDouble(bc.sym,SYMBOL_BID);
   bool htf_bull=price>ema_h[0], htf_bear=price<ema_h[0];

   // MTF Donchian
   double hi[],lo[];
   ArraySetAsSeries(hi,true); ArraySetAsSeries(lo,true);
   CopyHigh(bc.sym,bc.tfM,1,bc.donchian,hi);
   CopyLow(bc.sym, bc.tfM,1,bc.donchian,lo);
   double dH=hi[ArrayMaximum(hi)], dL=lo[ArrayMinimum(lo)];

   // LTF ATR + Volume
   double atr_e[2]; CopyBuffer(bc.h_atr_entry,0,0,2,atr_e);
   double atr_m[2]; CopyBuffer(bc.h_atr_mtf,0,0,2,atr_m);
   double atr=atr_e[0];

   long volArr[]; ArraySetAsSeries(volArr,true);
   CopyTickVolume(bc.sym,bc.tfE,1,bc.volPeriod,volArr);
   double avgV=0; for(int i=0;i<bc.volPeriod;i++) avgV+=volArr[i]; avgV/=bc.volPeriod;
   if(iVolume(bc.sym,bc.tfE,1)<avgV*bc.volMult) return;

   double lots=CalcDynamicLot(bc.sym,atr*bc.atrSL,bc.riskPct);
   if(OrdersTotal()==0)
   {
      if(htf_bull||(!htf_bull&&!htf_bear))
      { double eB=dH+atr*bc.atrEntry;
        trade.BuyStop(lots,eB,bc.sym,eB-atr_m[0],eB+atr*bc.atrTP,
                      ORDER_TIME_GTC,0,StringFormat("DA|%s|BS",bc.sym)); }
      if(htf_bear||(!htf_bull&&!htf_bear))
      { double eS=dL-atr*bc.atrEntry;
        trade.SellStop(lots,eS,bc.sym,eS+atr_m[0],eS-atr*bc.atrTP,
                       ORDER_TIME_GTC,0,StringFormat("DA|%s|SS",bc.sym)); }
   }
}

//────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
int OnInit()
{
   trade.SetExpertMagicNumber(Magic);

   BC[0]={S1_Symbol,S1_Enabled,S1_TF_Higher,S1_TF_Middle,S1_TF_Entry,
          S1_Donchian,S1_ATR_Period,S1_EMA_HTF,S1_Vol_Period,
          S1_ATR_Entry,S1_ATR_TP,S1_ATR_SL,S1_Vol_Mult,S1_Risk_Pct,
          S1_MaxPos,S1_Trail,S1_Trail_ATR};

   BC[1]={S2_Symbol,S2_Enabled,S2_TF_Higher,S2_TF_Middle,S2_TF_Entry,
          S2_Donchian,S2_ATR_Period,S2_EMA_HTF,S2_Vol_Period,
          S2_ATR_Entry,S2_ATR_TP,S2_ATR_SL,S2_Vol_Mult,S2_Risk_Pct,
          S2_MaxPos,S2_Trail,S2_Trail_ATR};

   BC[2]={S3_Symbol,S3_Enabled,S3_TF_Higher,S3_TF_Middle,S3_TF_Entry,
          S3_Donchian,S3_ATR_Period,S3_EMA_HTF,S3_Vol_Period,
          S3_ATR_Entry,S3_ATR_TP,S3_ATR_SL,S3_Vol_Mult,S3_Risk_Pct,
          S3_MaxPos,S3_Trail,S3_Trail_ATR};

   for(int i=0;i<3;i++) if(!InitBrkSymbol(BC[i])) return INIT_FAILED;
   return INIT_SUCCEEDED;
}

void OnDeinit(const int reason)
{ for(int i=0;i<3;i++) ReleaseBrkSymbol(BC[i]); }

void OnTick()
{
   if(IsGlobalDrawdownBreached()) { CloseAllPositions(); return; }
   for(int i=0;i<3;i++) ProcessBrkSymbol(BC[i]);
}
```

---

> Port 4-11 ใช้ Template เดียวกันครับบอส ผมจะสรุป Pattern รวมและแสดง Port ที่เหลือต่อ

---

## ⚔️ PORT 4-11 — Universal Multi-Symbol Template
### (คัดลอก Pattern นี้ไปใส่ทุก EA ที่เหลือ เปลี่ยนแค่ Logic ใน ProcessSymbol)

```mql5
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| UNIVERSAL MULTI-SYMBOL TEMPLATE — Port 4 ถึง 11                 |
//| เปลี่ยน: ชื่อไฟล์, Magic, SymbolConfig struct fields, Logic      |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+

// ─── PATTERN ที่ซ้ำกันทุก EA ───────────────────────────────────────
//
//  1) Input Groups:  S1_xxx / S2_xxx / S3_xxx + Global_xxx
//  2) Struct:        SymbolConfig { sym, enabled, TFs, params, handles, lastBar }
//  3) Shared MM:     CountAllPositions() / CountSymbolPositions()
//                    IsGlobalDailyLossBreached() / IsGlobalDrawdownBreached()
//                    CalcDynamicLot() / CloseAllPositions()
//  4) Per-Symbol:    InitSymbol() → ProcessSymbol() → ReleaseSymbol()
//  5) OnInit():      Map S1/S2/S3 inputs → SC[0]/SC[1]/SC[2]
//  6) OnTick():      DD Check → Loop ProcessSymbol(SC[i])
//
// ─── สิ่งที่เปลี่ยนแค่ใน ProcessSymbol() ──────────────────────────
//
//  Port 4 (CrimsonTide):   BB Width HTF + ADX MTF + RSI/Stoch LTF
//  Port 5 (Abyssal):       Z-Score Spread (ต้องมี sym1+sym2 คู่คงที่)
//  Port 6 (GoldenEmber):   Pivot Monthly/Weekly + CCI/RSI LTF
//  Port 7 (TwinFlares):    Session Filter + EMA Cross Scalp + Volume
//  Port 8 (BlazingArrow):  Momentum + Engulfing + Partial Close
//  Port 9 (SolarEclipse):  News Straddle + Time Stop
//  Port 10 (DragonsWrath): Supertrend + ADX Trailing
//  Port 11 (EmberStrike):  Session Overlap + Force Close
```

---

## ⚔️ PORT 4 — 159219430 | Crimson Tide Multi-Symbol

```mql5
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| EA_CrimsonTide_159219430.mq5 — Multi-Symbol                     |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
#property copyright "Port 159219430"
#property version   "3.00"
#property strict
#include <Trade\Trade.mqh>

input group "━━━ SYMBOL 1 ━━━"
input string  S1_Symbol      = "EURGBP"; input bool S1_Enabled = true;
input ENUM_TIMEFRAMES S1_TF_Higher=PERIOD_W1, S1_TF_Middle=PERIOD_D1, S1_TF_Entry=PERIOD_H1;
input int S1_BB_Period=20; input double S1_BB_Dev_HTF=2.0, S1_BB_Dev_Entry=2.5;
input int S1_RSI_Period=7, S1_Stoch_K=5, S1_Stoch_D=3, S1_Stoch_Slow=3;
input int S1_ADX_Period=14; input double S1_ADX_Max=30.0, S1_BB_Width_Max=0.04;
input int S1_ATR_Period=14; input double S1_ATR_SL=1.5;
input int S1_MaxHoldHours=48; input double S1_Risk_Pct=0.8; input int S1_MaxPos=3;

input group "━━━ SYMBOL 2 ━━━"
input string  S2_Symbol      = "CHFJPY"; input bool S2_Enabled = true;
input ENUM_TIMEFRAMES S2_TF_Higher=PERIOD_W1, S2_TF_Middle=PERIOD_D1, S2_TF_Entry=PERIOD_H1;
input int S2_BB_Period=20; input double S2_BB_Dev_HTF=2.0, S2_BB_Dev_Entry=2.5;
input int S2_RSI_Period=7, S2_Stoch_K=5, S2_Stoch_D=3, S2_Stoch_Slow=3;
input int S2_ADX_Period=14; input double S2_ADX_Max=30.0, S2_BB_Width_Max=0.04;
input int S2_ATR_Period=14; input double S2_ATR_SL=1.5;
input int S2_MaxHoldHours=48; input double S2_Risk_Pct=0.8; input int S2_MaxPos=3;

input group "━━━ SYMBOL 3 ━━━"
input string  S3_Symbol      = "EURCAD"; input bool S3_Enabled = false;
input ENUM_TIMEFRAMES S3_TF_Higher=PERIOD_W1, S3_TF_Middle=PERIOD_D1, S3_TF_Entry=PERIOD_H1;
input int S3_BB_Period=20; input double S3_BB_Dev_HTF=2.0, S3_BB_Dev_Entry=2.5;
input int S3_RSI_Period=7, S3_Stoch_K=5, S3_Stoch_D=3, S3_Stoch_Slow=3;
input int S3_ADX_Period=14; input double S3_ADX_Max=30.0, S3_BB_Width_Max=0.04;
input int S3_ATR_Period=14; input double S3_ATR_SL=1.5;
input int S3_MaxHoldHours=48; input double S3_Risk_Pct=0.8; input int S3_MaxPos=3;

input group "━━━ SHARED MONEY MANAGEMENT ━━━"
input double Global_MaxDailyLoss_Pct=2.5; input double Global_MaxDrawdown_Pct=8.0;
input int    Global_MaxTotalPos=5;
input double Global_Min_Lot=0.01; input double Global_Max_Lot=3.0;
input bool   Global_UseEquity=false;
input int    Magic=159219430;

struct MRConfig {
   string sym; bool enabled;
   ENUM_TIMEFRAMES tfH,tfM,tfE;
   int bbPeriod; double bbDevHTF,bbDevEntry;
   int rsiPeriod,stochK,stochD,stochSlow,adxPeriod;
   double adxMax,bbWidthMax,atrSL,riskPct;
   int atrPeriod,maxHoldHours,maxPos;
   int h_bb_htf,h_adx_mtf,h_ema200_mtf,h_bb_entry,h_rsi,h_stoch,h_atr;
   datetime lastBar;
};

MRConfig MC[3];
CTrade trade;

int CountAllPositions(){int n=0;for(int i=0;i<PositionsTotal();i++){ulong t=PositionGetTicket(i);if(PositionSelectByTicket(t)&&(int)PositionGetInteger(POSITION_MAGIC)==Magic)n++;}return n;}
int CountSymbolPositions(string sym){int n=0;for(int i=0;i<PositionsTotal();i++){ulong t=PositionGetTicket(i);if(!PositionSelectByTicket(t))continue;if((int)PositionGetInteger(POSITION_MAGIC)==Magic&&PositionGetString(POSITION_SYMBOL)==sym)n++;}return n;}
bool IsGlobalDailyLossBreached(){double bal=AccountInfoDouble(ACCOUNT_BALANCE);double maxL=bal*Global_MaxDailyLoss_Pct/100.0;datetime ds=StringToTime(TimeToString(TimeCurrent(),TIME_DATE));double pl=0;for(int i=0;i<PositionsTotal();i++){ulong t=PositionGetTicket(i);if(!PositionSelectByTicket(t))continue;if((int)PositionGetInteger(POSITION_MAGIC)!=Magic)continue;pl+=PositionGetDouble(POSITION_PROFIT)+PositionGetDouble(POSITION_SWAP)+PositionGetDouble(POSITION_COMMISSION);}HistorySelect(ds,TimeCurrent());for(int i=0;i<HistoryDealsTotal();i++){ulong d=HistoryDealGetTicket(i);if((int)HistoryDealGetInteger(d,DEAL_MAGIC)!=Magic)continue;if(HistoryDealGetInteger(d,DEAL_ENTRY)==DEAL_ENTRY_OUT)pl+=HistoryDealGetDouble(d,DEAL_PROFIT)+HistoryDealGetDouble(d,DEAL_SWAP)+HistoryDealGetDouble(d,DEAL_COMMISSION);}return(pl<=-maxL);}
bool IsGlobalDrawdownBreached(){double b=AccountInfoDouble(ACCOUNT_BALANCE),e=AccountInfoDouble(ACCOUNT_EQUITY);return(b>0&&(b-e)/b*100.0>=Global_MaxDrawdown_Pct);}
void CloseAllPositions(){for(int i=PositionsTotal()-1;i>=0;i--){ulong t=PositionGetTicket(i);if(PositionSelectByTicket(t)&&(int)PositionGetInteger(POSITION_MAGIC)==Magic)trade.PositionClose(t);}}
double CalcDynamicLot(string sym,double slDist,double riskPct){double base=Global_UseEquity?AccountInfoDouble(ACCOUNT_EQUITY):AccountInfoDouble(ACCOUNT_BALANCE);double rAmt=base*riskPct/100.0;double tv=SymbolInfoDouble(sym,SYMBOL_TRADE_TICK_VALUE);double ts=SymbolInfoDouble(sym,SYMBOL_TRADE_TICK_SIZE);double ls=SymbolInfoDouble(sym,SYMBOL_VOLUME_STEP);double minL=MathMax(Global_Min_Lot,SymbolInfoDouble(sym,SYMBOL_VOLUME_MIN));double maxL=MathMin(Global_Max_Lot,SymbolInfoDouble(sym,SYMBOL_VOLUME_MAX));if(slDist<=0||tv<=0||ts<=0)return minL;double lots=rAmt/(slDist/ts*tv);lots=MathFloor(lots/ls)*ls;return MathMax(minL,MathMin(maxL,lots));}

void CheckTimeExit(string sym)
{
   datetime now=TimeCurrent();
   for(int i=PositionsTotal()-1;i>=0;i--)
   { ulong t=PositionGetTicket(i); if(!PositionSelectByTicket(t)) continue;
     if((int)PositionGetInteger(POSITION_MAGIC)!=Magic) continue;
     if(PositionGetString(POSITION_SYMBOL)!=sym) continue;
     // หา MaxHoldHours ของ Symbol นี้
     int holdH=48;
     for(int j=0;j<3;j++) if(MC[j].sym==sym) holdH=MC[j].maxHoldHours;
     datetime ot=(datetime)PositionGetInteger(POSITION_TIME);
     if((now-ot)>=(datetime)(holdH*3600)) trade.PositionClose(t); }
}

bool InitMRSymbol(MRConfig &mc)
{
   if(!mc.enabled) return true;
   if(!SymbolSelect(mc.sym,true)) return false;
   mc.h_bb_htf    = iBands(mc.sym,mc.tfH,mc.bbPeriod,0,mc.bbDevHTF,PRICE_CLOSE);
   mc.h_adx_mtf   = iADX(mc.sym,mc.tfM,mc.adxPeriod);
   mc.h_ema200_mtf= iMA(mc.sym,mc.tfM,200,0,MODE_EMA,PRICE_CLOSE);
   mc.h_bb_entry  = iBands(mc.sym,mc.tfE,mc.bbPeriod,0,mc.bbDevEntry,PRICE_CLOSE);
   mc.h_rsi       = iRSI(mc.sym,mc.tfE,mc.rsiPeriod,PRICE_CLOSE);
   mc.h_stoch     = iStochastic(mc.sym,mc.tfE,mc.stochK,mc.stochD,mc.stochSlow,MODE_SMA,STO_LOWHIGH);
   mc.h_atr       = iATR(mc.sym,mc.tfE,mc.atrPeriod);
   mc.lastBar=0; return true;
}

void ReleaseMRSymbol(MRConfig &mc)
{ if(!mc.enabled) return;
  IndicatorRelease(mc.h_bb_htf); IndicatorRelease(mc.h_adx_mtf);
  IndicatorRelease(mc.h_ema200_mtf); IndicatorRelease(mc.h_bb_entry);
  IndicatorRelease(mc.h_rsi); IndicatorRelease(mc.h_stoch); IndicatorRelease(mc.h_atr); }

void ProcessMRSymbol(MRConfig &mc)
{
   if(!mc.enabled) return;
   datetime curBar=iTime(mc.sym,mc.tfE,0);
   if(curBar==mc.lastBar) return;
   mc.lastBar=curBar;

   CheckTimeExit(mc.sym);
   if(IsGlobalDrawdownBreached()||IsGlobalDailyLossBreached()) return;
   if(CountAllPositions()>=(int)MathMin(Global_MaxTotalPos,5)) return;
   if(CountSymbolPositions(mc.sym)>=(int)MathMin(mc.maxPos,5)) return;

   // HTF: BB Width
   double htf_u[2],htf_l[2],htf_m[2];
   CopyBuffer(mc.h_bb_htf,0,0,2,htf_m); CopyBuffer(mc.h_bb_htf,1,0,2,htf_u); CopyBuffer(mc.h_bb_htf,2,0,2,htf_l);
   double bbW=(htf_m[0]>0)?(htf_u[0]-htf_l[0])/htf_m[0]:999;
   if(bbW>mc.bbWidthMax) return;

   // MTF: ADX + EMA200
   double adx_m[2],ema200[2];
   CopyBuffer(mc.h_adx_mtf,MAIN_LINE,0,2,adx_m); CopyBuffer(mc.h_ema200_mtf,0,0,2,ema200);
   if(adx_m[0]>mc.adxMax) return;
   double price=SymbolInfoDouble(mc.sym,SYMBOL_BID);
   bool mtf_above=price>ema200[0], mtf_below=price<ema200[0];

   // LTF: BB + RSI + Stoch + ATR
   double bb_u[2],bb_l[2],bb_m2[2],rsi_v[2],stk[2],atr_v[2];
   CopyBuffer(mc.h_bb_entry,0,0,2,bb_m2); CopyBuffer(mc.h_bb_entry,1,0,2,bb_u); CopyBuffer(mc.h_bb_entry,2,0,2,bb_l);
   CopyBuffer(mc.h_rsi,0,0,2,rsi_v); CopyBuffer(mc.h_stoch,MAIN_LINE,0,2,stk); CopyBuffer(mc.h_atr,0,0,2,atr_v);
   double atr=atr_v[0];
   bool buySetup=price<bb_l[0]&&rsi_v[0]<25&&stk[0]<20;
   bool sellSetup=price>bb_u[0]&&rsi_v[0]>75&&stk[0]>80;

   if(buySetup&&!mtf_below)
   { double ask=SymbolInfoDouble(mc.sym,SYMBOL_ASK),sl=ask-atr*mc.atrSL;
     trade.Buy(CalcDynamicLot(mc.sym,ask-sl,mc.riskPct),mc.sym,ask,sl,bb_m2[0],
               StringFormat("CT|%s",mc.sym)); }
   else if(sellSetup&&!mtf_above)
   { double bid=SymbolInfoDouble(mc.sym,SYMBOL_BID),sl=bid+atr*mc.atrSL;
     trade.Sell(CalcDynamicLot(mc.sym,sl-bid,mc.riskPct),mc.sym,bid,sl,bb_m2[0],
                StringFormat("CT|%s",mc.sym)); }
}

int OnInit()
{
   trade.SetExpertMagicNumber(Magic);
   MC[0]={S1_Symbol,S1_Enabled,S1_TF_Higher,S1_TF_Middle,S1_TF_Entry,S1_BB_Period,S1_BB_Dev_HTF,S1_BB_Dev_Entry,S1_RSI_Period,S1_Stoch_K,S1_Stoch_D,S1_Stoch_Slow,S1_ADX_Period,S1_ADX_Max,S1_BB_Width_Max,S1_ATR_SL,S1_Risk_Pct,S1_ATR_Period,S1_MaxHoldHours,S1_MaxPos};
   MC[1]={S2_Symbol,S2_Enabled,S2_TF_Higher,S2_TF_Middle,S2_TF_Entry,S2_BB_Period,S2_BB_Dev_HTF,S2_BB_Dev_Entry,S2_RSI_Period,S2_Stoch_K,S2_Stoch_D,S2_Stoch_Slow,S2_ADX_Period,S2_ADX_Max,S2_BB_Width_Max,S2_ATR_SL,S2_Risk_Pct,S2_ATR_Period,S2_MaxHoldHours,S2_MaxPos};
   MC[2]={S3_Symbol,S3_Enabled,S3_TF_Higher,S3_TF_Middle,S3_TF_Entry,S3_BB_Period,S3_BB_Dev_HTF,S3_BB_Dev_Entry,S3_RSI_Period,S3_Stoch_K,S3_Stoch_D,S3_Stoch_Slow,S3_ADX_Period,S3_ADX_Max,S3_BB_Width_Max,S3_ATR_SL,S3_Risk_Pct,S3_ATR_Period,S3_MaxHoldHours,S3_MaxPos};
   for(int i=0;i<3;i++) if(!InitMRSymbol(MC[i])) return INIT_FAILED;
   return INIT_SUCCEEDED;
}
void OnDeinit(const int r){for(int i=0;i<3;i++) ReleaseMRSymbol(MC[i]);}
void OnTick(){if(IsGlobalDrawdownBreached()){CloseAllPositions();return;}for(int i=0;i<3;i++)ProcessMRSymbol(MC[i]);}
```

---

## 📊 สรุป Architecture Multi-Symbol ทั้งระบบ

```
OnTick()
  │
  ├── IsGlobalDrawdownBreached? → CloseAll → return
  │
  ├── ProcessSymbol(SC[0])  ← SYMBOL 1
  │     ├── Bar Gate (แต่ละ Symbol มี lastBar ของตัวเอง)
  │     ├── Global DD/DailyLoss Check
  │     ├── CountAllPositions() ≥ GlobalCap? → return
  │     ├── CountSymbolPositions(sym) ≥ SymCap? → return
  │     └── HTF → MTF → LTF Logic → Execute
  │
  ├── ProcessSymbol(SC[1])  ← SYMBOL 2
  │     └── (เหมือนกัน — แต่ใช้ Handle ของตัวเอง)
  │
  └── ProcessSymbol(SC[2])  ← SYMBOL 3 (Enabled/Disabled)
        └── if(!enabled) return immediately
```

### วิธี Backtest Multi-Symbol ใน MT5 Strategy Tester

| ขั้นตอน | รายละเอียด |
|---|---|
| **1. เปิด Strategy Tester** | ตั้ง Mode = "Every Tick Based on Real Ticks" |
| **2. Symbol หลัก** | ใส่ Symbol ใดก็ได้ (EA จะดึง Symbol จาก S1/S2/S3 เอง) |
| **3. เพิ่ม Symbol** | กด "+" ใน Symbols ของ Tester เพิ่ม S1/S2/S3 ทั้งหมด |
| **4. ดู Result** | กำไร/ขาดทุนรวมทุก Symbol จะนับรวมกันใน Balance เดียว |
| **5. สังเกต Drawdown** | ถ้า 2 Symbol วิ่งสวนทางกัน → DD จะพีค → ทดสอบ Global_MaxDD |

----- [user] -----
งงแหะ ทำเป็นแยกเป็น EA แยกเลยได้ไหม จะได้ก็อปวางลง MQL5 ทีเดียว

----- [assistant] -----
ได้เลยครับบอส — เขียนแยกเป็น 11 ไฟล์ ก็อปวางได้เลยทีเดียว

---

## PORT 1 — EA_CelestialWoodfire_141049900.mq5

```mql5
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| EA_CelestialWoodfire_141049900.mq5                               |
//| Strategy : MTF Trend Following                                   |
//| Symbols  : EURUSD + GBPUSD + USDJPY (เปิด/ปิดได้)              |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
#property copyright "Port 141049900"
#property version   "3.00"
#property strict
#include <Trade\Trade.mqh>

//--------------------------------------------------------------------
input group "══ SYMBOL 1 : EURUSD ══"
input bool   S1_Enable       = true;
input string S1_Symbol       = "EURUSD";
input ENUM_TIMEFRAMES S1_HTF = PERIOD_D1;
input ENUM_TIMEFRAMES S1_MTF = PERIOD_H4;
input ENUM_TIMEFRAMES S1_LTF = PERIOD_H1;
input int    S1_EMA_Fast     = 50;
input int    S1_EMA_Slow     = 200;
input int    S1_MACD_Fast    = 12;
input int    S1_MACD_Slow    = 26;
input int    S1_MACD_Sig     = 9;
input int    S1_ADX_Period   = 14;
input double S1_ADX_Min      = 25.0;
input int    S1_ATR_Period   = 14;
input double S1_ATR_SL       = 1.5;
input double S1_ATR_TP       = 3.0;
input double S1_Risk_Pct     = 1.0;
input int    S1_MaxPos       = 2;
input bool   S1_Trail        = true;
input double S1_Trail_ATR    = 1.0;

input group "══ SYMBOL 2 : GBPUSD ══"
input bool   S2_Enable       = true;
input string S2_Symbol       = "GBPUSD";
input ENUM_TIMEFRAMES S2_HTF = PERIOD_D1;
input ENUM_TIMEFRAMES S2_MTF = PERIOD_H4;
input ENUM_TIMEFRAMES S2_LTF = PERIOD_H1;
input int    S2_EMA_Fast     = 50;
input int    S2_EMA_Slow     = 200;
input int    S2_MACD_Fast    = 12;
input int    S2_MACD_Slow    = 26;
input int    S2_MACD_Sig     = 9;
input int    S2_ADX_Period   = 14;
input double S2_ADX_Min      = 25.0;
input int    S2_ATR_Period   = 14;
input double S2_ATR_SL       = 1.5;
input double S2_ATR_TP       = 3.0;
input double S2_Risk_Pct     = 1.0;
input int    S2_MaxPos       = 2;
input bool   S2_Trail        = true;
input double S2_Trail_ATR    = 1.0;

input group "══ SYMBOL 3 : USDJPY ══"
input bool   S3_Enable       = false;
input string S3_Symbol       = "USDJPY";
input ENUM_TIMEFRAMES S3_HTF = PERIOD_D1;
input ENUM_TIMEFRAMES S3_MTF = PERIOD_H4;
input ENUM_TIMEFRAMES S3_LTF = PERIOD_H1;
input int    S3_EMA_Fast     = 50;
input int    S3_EMA_Slow     = 200;
input int    S3_MACD_Fast    = 12;
input int    S3_MACD_Slow    = 26;
input int    S3_MACD_Sig     = 9;
input int    S3_ADX_Period   = 14;
input double S3_ADX_Min      = 25.0;
input int    S3_ATR_Period   = 14;
input double S3_ATR_SL       = 1.5;
input double S3_ATR_TP       = 3.0;
input double S3_Risk_Pct     = 1.0;
input int    S3_MaxPos       = 2;
input bool   S3_Trail        = true;
input double S3_Trail_ATR    = 1.0;

input group "══ MONEY MANAGEMENT (รวมทั้ง Port) ══"
input double MM_MaxDailyLoss  = 3.0;
input double MM_MaxDrawdown   = 10.0;
input int    MM_MaxTotalPos   = 5;
input double MM_MinLot        = 0.01;
input double MM_MaxLot        = 5.0;
input bool   MM_UseEquity     = false;
input int    Magic            = 141049900;

//--------------------------------------------------------------------
struct SymCfg
{
   bool   enable;
   string sym;
   ENUM_TIMEFRAMES htf, mtf, ltf;
   int    emaFast, emaSlow;
   int    macdFast, macdSlow, macdSig;
   int    adxPeriod; double adxMin;
   int    atrPeriod; double atrSL, atrTP;
   double riskPct;  int maxPos;
   bool   trail;    double trailATR;
   // handles
   int    hEF_H, hES_H, hEF_M, hES_M, hMACD, hADX, hATR;
   datetime lastBar;
};

SymCfg  SC[3];
CTrade  trade;

//--------------------------------------------------------------------
// ── Shared helpers ──────────────────────────────────────────────────
int TotalPos()
{
   int n=0;
   for(int i=0;i<PositionsTotal();i++)
   {
      ulong t=PositionGetTicket(i);
      if(PositionSelectByTicket(t)&&
         (int)PositionGetInteger(POSITION_MAGIC)==Magic) n++;
   }
   return n;
}

int SymPos(string s)
{
   int n=0;
   for(int i=0;i<PositionsTotal();i++)
   {
      ulong t=PositionGetTicket(i);
      if(!PositionSelectByTicket(t)) continue;
      if((int)PositionGetInteger(POSITION_MAGIC)==Magic &&
         PositionGetString(POSITION_SYMBOL)==s) n++;
   }
   return n;
}

bool DailyLossHit()
{
   double bal=AccountInfoDouble(ACCOUNT_BALANCE);
   double lim=bal*MM_MaxDailyLoss/100.0;
   datetime d0=StringToTime(TimeToString(TimeCurrent(),TIME_DATE));
   double pl=0;
   for(int i=0;i<PositionsTotal();i++)
   {
      ulong t=PositionGetTicket(i);
      if(!PositionSelectByTicket(t)) continue;
      if((int)PositionGetInteger(POSITION_MAGIC)!=Magic) continue;
      pl+=PositionGetDouble(POSITION_PROFIT)
        +PositionGetDouble(POSITION_SWAP)
        +PositionGetDouble(POSITION_COMMISSION);
   }
   HistorySelect(d0,TimeCurrent());
   for(int i=0;i<HistoryDealsTotal();i++)
   {
      ulong d=HistoryDealGetTicket(i);
      if((int)HistoryDealGetInteger(d,DEAL_MAGIC)!=Magic) continue;
      if(HistoryDealGetInteger(d,DEAL_ENTRY)==DEAL_ENTRY_OUT)
         pl+=HistoryDealGetDouble(d,DEAL_PROFIT)
           +HistoryDealGetDouble(d,DEAL_SWAP)
           +HistoryDealGetDouble(d,DEAL_COMMISSION);
   }
   return(pl<=-lim);
}

bool DDHit()
{
   double b=AccountInfoDouble(ACCOUNT_BALANCE);
   double e=AccountInfoDouble(ACCOUNT_EQUITY);
   return(b>0 && (b-e)/b*100.0>=MM_MaxDrawdown);
}

void CloseAll()
{
   for(int i=PositionsTotal()-1;i>=0;i--)
   {
      ulong t=PositionGetTicket(i);
      if(PositionSelectByTicket(t)&&
         (int)PositionGetInteger(POSITION_MAGIC)==Magic)
         trade.PositionClose(t);
   }
}

double Lot(string sym,double slDist,double rPct)
{
   double base=MM_UseEquity?AccountInfoDouble(ACCOUNT_EQUITY)
                           :AccountInfoDouble(ACCOUNT_BALANCE);
   double risk=base*rPct/100.0;
   double tv=SymbolInfoDouble(sym,SYMBOL_TRADE_TICK_VALUE);
   double ts=SymbolInfoDouble(sym,SYMBOL_TRADE_TICK_SIZE);
   double ls=SymbolInfoDouble(sym,SYMBOL_VOLUME_STEP);
   double mn=MathMax(MM_MinLot,SymbolInfoDouble(sym,SYMBOL_VOLUME_MIN));
   double mx=MathMin(MM_MaxLot,SymbolInfoDouble(sym,SYMBOL_VOLUME_MAX));
   if(slDist<=0||tv<=0||ts<=0) return mn;
   double lot=risk/(slDist/ts*tv);
   lot=MathFloor(lot/ls)*ls;
   return MathMax(mn,MathMin(mx,lot));
}

//--------------------------------------------------------------------
// ── Per-symbol logic ────────────────────────────────────────────────
void Trail(SymCfg &c)
{
   double ef[2],atr[2];
   CopyBuffer(c.hEF_M,0,0,2,ef);
   CopyBuffer(c.hATR, 0,0,2,atr);
   double a=atr[0];
   for(int i=PositionsTotal()-1;i>=0;i--)
   {
      ulong t=PositionGetTicket(i);
      if(!PositionSelectByTicket(t)) continue;
      if((int)PositionGetInteger(POSITION_MAGIC)!=Magic) continue;
      if(PositionGetString(POSITION_SYMBOL)!=c.sym) continue;
      if(PositionGetDouble(POSITION_PROFIT)<=0) continue;
      double sl=PositionGetDouble(POSITION_SL);
      double tp=PositionGetDouble(POSITION_TP);
      long   ty=PositionGetInteger(POSITION_TYPE);
      if(ty==POSITION_TYPE_BUY)
      {
         double nsl=ef[0]-a*c.trailATR;
         if(nsl>sl&&nsl<SymbolInfoDouble(c.sym,SYMBOL_BID))
            trade.PositionModify(t,nsl,tp);
      }
      else
      {
         double nsl=ef[0]+a*c.trailATR;
         if(nsl<sl&&nsl>SymbolInfoDouble(c.sym,SYMBOL_ASK))
            trade.PositionModify(t,nsl,tp);
      }
   }
}

void Run(SymCfg &c)
{
   if(!c.enable) return;
   datetime bar=iTime(c.sym,c.ltf,0);
   if(bar==c.lastBar) return;
   c.lastBar=bar;

   if(c.trail) Trail(c);
   if(DDHit()||DailyLossHit()) return;
   if(TotalPos()>=(int)MathMin(MM_MaxTotalPos,5)) return;
   if(SymPos(c.sym)>=(int)MathMin(c.maxPos,5))   return;

   double ef_h[2],es_h[2],ef_m[2],es_m[2];
   CopyBuffer(c.hEF_H,0,0,2,ef_h); CopyBuffer(c.hES_H,0,0,2,es_h);
   CopyBuffer(c.hEF_M,0,0,2,ef_m); CopyBuffer(c.hES_M,0,0,2,es_m);
   bool hBull=ef_h[0]>es_h[0], hBear=ef_h[0]<es_h[0];
   bool mBull=ef_m[0]>es_m[0], mBear=ef_m[0]<es_m[0];

   double mm[3],ms[3],adx[2],atr[2];
   CopyBuffer(c.hMACD,MAIN_LINE,  0,3,mm);
   CopyBuffer(c.hMACD,SIGNAL_LINE,0,3,ms);
   CopyBuffer(c.hADX, MAIN_LINE,  0,2,adx);
   CopyBuffer(c.hATR, 0,          0,2,atr);
   bool mUp=mm[1]>ms[1]&&mm[2]<=ms[2];
   bool mDn=mm[1]<ms[1]&&mm[2]>=ms[2];
   bool aOK=adx[0]>c.adxMin;
   double a=atr[0];

   if(hBull&&mBull&&mUp&&aOK)
   {
      double ask=SymbolInfoDouble(c.sym,SYMBOL_ASK);
      trade.Buy(Lot(c.sym,a*c.atrSL,c.riskPct),c.sym,ask,
                ask-a*c.atrSL,ask+a*c.atrTP,"CW_BUY");
   }
   else if(hBear&&mBear&&mDn&&aOK)
   {
      double bid=SymbolInfoDouble(c.sym,SYMBOL_BID);
      trade.Sell(Lot(c.sym,a*c.atrSL,c.riskPct),c.sym,bid,
                 bid+a*c.atrSL,bid-a*c.atrTP,"CW_SELL");
   }
}

//--------------------------------------------------------------------
bool InitSym(SymCfg &c)
{
   if(!c.enable) return true;
   if(!SymbolSelect(c.sym,true)){Print("Cannot select ",c.sym);return false;}
   c.hEF_H=iMA(c.sym,c.htf,c.emaFast,0,MODE_EMA,PRICE_CLOSE);
   c.hES_H=iMA(c.sym,c.htf,c.emaSlow,0,MODE_EMA,PRICE_CLOSE);
   c.hEF_M=iMA(c.sym,c.mtf,c.emaFast,0,MODE_EMA,PRICE_CLOSE);
   c.hES_M=iMA(c.sym,c.mtf,c.emaSlow,0,MODE_EMA,PRICE_CLOSE);
   c.hMACD=iMACD(c.sym,c.ltf,c.macdFast,c.macdSlow,c.macdSig,PRICE_CLOSE);
   c.hADX =iADX(c.sym,c.ltf,c.adxPeriod);
   c.hATR =iATR(c.sym,c.ltf,c.atrPeriod);
   c.lastBar=0;
   if(c.hEF_H<0||c.hMACD<0){Print("Handle error ",c.sym);return false;}
   return true;
}

void FreeSym(SymCfg &c)
{
   if(!c.enable) return;
   IndicatorRelease(c.hEF_H); IndicatorRelease(c.hES_H);
   IndicatorRelease(c.hEF_M); IndicatorRelease(c.hES_M);
   IndicatorRelease(c.hMACD); IndicatorRelease(c.hADX);
   IndicatorRelease(c.hATR);
}

//--------------------------------------------------------------------
int OnInit()
{
   trade.SetExpertMagicNumber(Magic);

   SC[0].enable=S1_Enable; SC[0].sym=S1_Symbol;
   SC[0].htf=S1_HTF; SC[0].mtf=S1_MTF; SC[0].ltf=S1_LTF;
   SC[0].emaFast=S1_EMA_Fast; SC[0].emaSlow=S1_EMA_Slow;
   SC[0].macdFast=S1_MACD_Fast; SC[0].macdSlow=S1_MACD_Slow; SC[0].macdSig=S1_MACD_Sig;
   SC[0].adxPeriod=S1_ADX_Period; SC[0].adxMin=S1_ADX_Min;
   SC[0].atrPeriod=S1_ATR_Period; SC[0].atrSL=S1_ATR_SL; SC[0].atrTP=S1_ATR_TP;
   SC[0].riskPct=S1_Risk_Pct; SC[0].maxPos=S1_MaxPos;
   SC[0].trail=S1_Trail; SC[0].trailATR=S1_Trail_ATR;

   SC[1].enable=S2_Enable; SC[1].sym=S2_Symbol;
   SC[1].htf=S2_HTF; SC[1].mtf=S2_MTF; SC[1].ltf=S2_LTF;
   SC[1].emaFast=S2_EMA_Fast; SC[1].emaSlow=S2_EMA_Slow;
   SC[1].macdFast=S2_MACD_Fast; SC[1].macdSlow=S2_MACD_Slow; SC[1].macdSig=S2_MACD_Sig;
   SC[1].adxPeriod=S2_ADX_Period; SC[1].adxMin=S2_ADX_Min;
   SC[1].atrPeriod=S2_ATR_Period; SC[1].atrSL=S2_ATR_SL; SC[1].atrTP=S2_ATR_TP;
   SC[1].riskPct=S2_Risk_Pct; SC[1].maxPos=S2_MaxPos;
   SC[1].trail=S2_Trail; SC[1].trailATR=S2_Trail_ATR;

   SC[2].enable=S3_Enable; SC[2].sym=S3_Symbol;
   SC[2].htf=S3_HTF; SC[2].mtf=S3_MTF; SC[2].ltf=S3_LTF;
   SC[2].emaFast=S3_EMA_Fast; SC[2].emaSlow=S3_EMA_Slow;
   SC[2].macdFast=S3_MACD_Fast; SC[2].macdSlow=S3_MACD_Slow; SC[2].macdSig=S3_MACD_Sig;
   SC[2].adxPeriod=S3_ADX_Period; SC[2].adxMin=S3_ADX_Min;
   SC[2].atrPeriod=S3_ATR_Period; SC[2].atrSL=S3_ATR_SL; SC[2].atrTP=S3_ATR_TP;
   SC[2].riskPct=S3_Risk_Pct; SC[2].maxPos=S3_MaxPos;
   SC[2].trail=S3_Trail; SC[2].trailATR=S3_Trail_ATR;

   for(int i=0;i<3;i++) if(!InitSym(SC[i])) return INIT_FAILED;
   Print("CelestialWoodfire 141049900 — OK");
   return INIT_SUCCEEDED;
}

void OnDeinit(const int r){ for(int i=0;i<3;i++) FreeSym(SC[i]); }

void OnTick()
{
   if(DDHit()){CloseAll();return;}
   for(int i=0;i<3;i++) Run(SC[i]);
}
```

## PORT 2 — EA_IroncladForest_146004104.mq5

```mql5
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| EA_IroncladForest_146004104.mq5                                  |
//| Strategy : MTF Smart Grid / DCA                                  |
//| Symbols  : AUDNZD + AUDCAD + NZDCAD                             |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
#property copyright "Port 146004104"
#property version   "3.00"
#property strict
#include <Trade\Trade.mqh>

input group "══ SYMBOL 1 : AUDNZD ══"
input bool   S1_Enable=true;   input string S1_Symbol="AUDNZD";
input ENUM_TIMEFRAMES S1_HTF=PERIOD_W1,S1_MTF=PERIOD_D1,S1_LTF=PERIOD_H1;
input int S1_BB=20; input double S1_Dev=2.0;
input int S1_RSI=14,S1_ATR=14;
input double S1_GridATR=1.0,S1_LotMult=1.5,S1_Base=0.01;
input double S1_BTP=1.0,S1_BSL=3.0,S1_Risk=1.0; input int S1_MaxGrid=4;

input group "══ SYMBOL 2 : AUDCAD ══"
input bool   S2_Enable=true;   input string S2_Symbol="AUDCAD";
input ENUM_TIMEFRAMES S2_HTF=PERIOD_W1,S2_MTF=PERIOD_D1,S2_LTF=PERIOD_H1;
input int S2_BB=20; input double S2_Dev=2.0;
input int S2_RSI=14,S2_ATR=14;
input double S2_GridATR=1.0,S2_LotMult=1.5,S2_Base=0.01;
input double S2_BTP=1.0,S2_BSL=3.0,S2_Risk=1.0; input int S2_MaxGrid=4;

input group "══ SYMBOL 3 : NZDCAD ══"
input bool   S3_Enable=false;  input string S3_Symbol="NZDCAD";
input ENUM_TIMEFRAMES S3_HTF=PERIOD_W1,S3_MTF=PERIOD_D1,S3_LTF=PERIOD_H1;
input int S3_BB=20; input double S3_Dev=2.0;
input int S3_RSI=14,S3_ATR=14;
input double S3_GridATR=1.0,S3_LotMult=1.5,S3_Base=0.01;
input double S3_BTP=1.0,S3_BSL=3.0,S3_Risk=1.0; input int S3_MaxGrid=4;

input group "══ MONEY MANAGEMENT ══"
input double MM_MaxDailyLoss=3.0,MM_MaxDrawdown=10.0;
input int    MM_MaxTotalPos=5;
input double MM_MinLot=0.01,MM_MaxLot=2.0;
input bool   MM_UseEquity=false;
input int    Magic=146004104;

struct GridCfg
{
   bool enable; string sym;
   ENUM_TIMEFRAMES htf,mtf,ltf;
   int bb; double dev;
   int rsi,atr;
   double gridATR,lotMult,base,btp,bsl,risk;
   int maxGrid;
   int hBH,hBM,hBE,hRSI,hATR;
   bool bull; datetime lastBar;
};

GridCfg GC[3];
CTrade trade;

int TotalPos(){int n=0;for(int i=0;i<PositionsTotal();i++){ulong t=PositionGetTicket(i);if(PositionSelectByTicket(t)&&(int)PositionGetInteger(POSITION_MAGIC)==Magic)n++;}return n;}
int SymPos(string s){int n=0;for(int i=0;i<PositionsTotal();i++){ulong t=PositionGetTicket(i);if(!PositionSelectByTicket(t))continue;if((int)PositionGetInteger(POSITION_MAGIC)==Magic&&PositionGetString(POSITION_SYMBOL)==s)n++;}return n;}
bool DDHit(){double b=AccountInfoDouble(ACCOUNT_BALANCE),e=AccountInfoDouble(ACCOUNT_EQUITY);return(b>0&&(b-e)/b*100.0>=MM_MaxDrawdown);}
bool DailyLossHit(){double bal=AccountInfoDouble(ACCOUNT_BALANCE);double lim=bal*MM_MaxDailyLoss/100.0;datetime d0=StringToTime(TimeToString(TimeCurrent(),TIME_DATE));double pl=0;for(int i=0;i<PositionsTotal();i++){ulong t=PositionGetTicket(i);if(!PositionSelectByTicket(t))continue;if((int)PositionGetInteger(POSITION_MAGIC)!=Magic)continue;pl+=PositionGetDouble(POSITION_PROFIT)+PositionGetDouble(POSITION_SWAP)+PositionGetDouble(POSITION_COMMISSION);}HistorySelect(d0,TimeCurrent());for(int i=0;i<HistoryDealsTotal();i++){ulong d=HistoryDealGetTicket(i);if((int)HistoryDealGetInteger(d,DEAL_MAGIC)!=Magic)continue;if(HistoryDealGetInteger(d,DEAL_ENTRY)==DEAL_ENTRY_OUT)pl+=HistoryDealGetDouble(d,DEAL_PROFIT)+HistoryDealGetDouble(d,DEAL_SWAP)+HistoryDealGetDouble(d,DEAL_COMMISSION);}return(pl<=-lim);}
void CloseAll(){for(int i=PositionsTotal()-1;i>=0;i--){ulong t=PositionGetTicket(i);if(PositionSelectByTicket(t)&&(int)PositionGetInteger(POSITION_MAGIC)==Magic)trade.PositionClose(t);}}
void CloseSymAll(string s){for(int i=PositionsTotal()-1;i>=0;i--){ulong t=PositionGetTicket(i);if(!PositionSelectByTicket(t))continue;if((int)PositionGetInteger(POSITION_MAGIC)==Magic&&PositionGetString(POSITION_SYMBOL)==s)trade.PositionClose(t);}}

double GridLot(GridCfg &g,int lv)
{
   double lot=g.base*MathPow(g.lotMult,lv);
   double ls=SymbolInfoDouble(g.sym,SYMBOL_VOLUME_STEP);
   double mn=MathMax(MM_MinLot,SymbolInfoDouble(g.sym,SYMBOL_VOLUME_MIN));
   double mx=MathMin(MM_MaxLot,SymbolInfoDouble(g.sym,SYMBOL_VOLUME_MAX));
   lot=MathFloor(lot/ls)*ls;
   return MathMax(mn,MathMin(mx,lot));
}

double LastGridPx(string s)
{
   double lp=0; datetime lt=0;
   for(int i=0;i<PositionsTotal();i++)
   {
      ulong t=PositionGetTicket(i);
      if(!PositionSelectByTicket(t)) continue;
      if((int)PositionGetInteger(POSITION_MAGIC)!=Magic) continue;
      if(PositionGetString(POSITION_SYMBOL)!=s) continue;
      datetime pt=(datetime)PositionGetInteger(POSITION_TIME);
      if(pt>lt){lt=pt;lp=PositionGetDouble(POSITION_PRICE_OPEN);}
   }
   return lp;
}

void BasketCheck(GridCfg &g)
{
   double bal=AccountInfoDouble(ACCOUNT_BALANCE),pl=0; int cnt=0;
   for(int i=0;i<PositionsTotal();i++)
   {
      ulong t=PositionGetTicket(i);
      if(!PositionSelectByTicket(t)) continue;
      if((int)PositionGetInteger(POSITION_MAGIC)!=Magic) continue;
      if(PositionGetString(POSITION_SYMBOL)!=g.sym) continue;
      pl+=PositionGetDouble(POSITION_PROFIT)+PositionGetDouble(POSITION_SWAP)+PositionGetDouble(POSITION_COMMISSION);
      cnt++;
   }
   if(cnt==0) return;
   if(pl>=bal*g.btp/100.0||pl<=-bal*g.bsl/100.0) CloseSymAll(g.sym);
}

void RunGrid(GridCfg &g)
{
   if(!g.enable) return;
   datetime bar=iTime(g.sym,g.ltf,0);
   if(bar==g.lastBar) return;
   g.lastBar=bar;

   BasketCheck(g);
   if(DDHit()||DailyLossHit()) return;
   int tot=TotalPos(), sp=SymPos(g.sym);
   int gcap=(int)MathMin(MM_MaxTotalPos,5), mg=(int)MathMin(g.maxGrid,4);
   if(tot>=gcap||sp>=mg) return;

   double px=SymbolInfoDouble(g.sym,SYMBOL_BID);
   double hu[2],hl[2],mm[2],eu[2],el[2],rv[2],av[2];
   CopyBuffer(g.hBH,1,0,2,hu); CopyBuffer(g.hBH,2,0,2,hl);
   if(px<hl[0]||px>hu[0]) return;
   CopyBuffer(g.hBM,0,0,2,mm);
   bool bull=px<mm[0], bear=px>mm[0];
   CopyBuffer(g.hBE,1,0,2,eu); CopyBuffer(g.hBE,2,0,2,el);
   CopyBuffer(g.hRSI,0,0,2,rv); CopyBuffer(g.hATR,0,0,2,av);
   double atr=av[0];

   if(sp==0)
   {
      if(bull&&px<el[0]&&rv[0]<30)
      {g.bull=true; double ask=SymbolInfoDouble(g.sym,SYMBOL_ASK);
       trade.Buy(GridLot(g,0),g.sym,ask,0,0,"IF_L0");}
      else if(bear&&px>eu[0]&&rv[0]>70)
      {g.bull=false; double bid=SymbolInfoDouble(g.sym,SYMBOL_BID);
       trade.Sell(GridLot(g,0),g.sym,bid,0,0,"IF_L0");}
   }
   else if(sp<mg&&tot<gcap)
   {
      double lp=LastGridPx(g.sym);
      if(g.bull&&px<lp-atr*g.gridATR)
      {double ask=SymbolInfoDouble(g.sym,SYMBOL_ASK);
       trade.Buy(GridLot(g,sp),g.sym,ask,0,0,"IF_L"+IntegerToString(sp));}
      else if(!g.bull&&px>lp+atr*g.gridATR)
      {double bid=SymbolInfoDouble(g.sym,SYMBOL_BID);
       trade.Sell(GridLot(g,sp),g.sym,bid,0,0,"IF_L"+IntegerToString(sp));}
   }
}

bool InitGrid(GridCfg &g)
{
   if(!g.enable) return true;
   if(!SymbolSelect(g.sym,true)) return false;
   g.hBH=iBands(g.sym,g.htf,g.bb,0,g.dev,PRICE_CLOSE);
   g.hBM=iBands(g.sym,g.mtf,g.bb,0,g.dev,PRICE_CLOSE);
   g.hBE=iBands(g.sym,g.ltf,g.bb,0,g.dev,PRICE_CLOSE);
   g.hRSI=iRSI(g.sym,g.ltf,g.rsi,PRICE_CLOSE);
   g.hATR=iATR(g.sym,g.ltf,g.atr);
   g.bull=false; g.lastBar=0; return true;
}

void FreeGrid(GridCfg &g)
{
   if(!g.enable) return;
   IndicatorRelease(g.hBH); IndicatorRelease(g.hBM); IndicatorRelease(g.hBE);
   IndicatorRelease(g.hRSI); IndicatorRelease(g.hATR);
}

int OnInit()
{
   trade.SetExpertMagicNumber(Magic);
   GC[0]={S1_Enable,S1_Symbol,S1_HTF,S1_MTF,S1_LTF,S1_BB,S1_Dev,S1_RSI,S1_ATR,S1_GridATR,S1_LotMult,S1_Base,S1_BTP,S1_BSL,S1_Risk,S1_MaxGrid};
   GC[1]={S2_Enable,S2_Symbol,S2_HTF,S2_MTF,S2_LTF,S2_BB,S2_Dev,S2_RSI,S2_ATR,S2_GridATR,S2_LotMult,S2_Base,S2_BTP,S2_BSL,S2_Risk,S2_MaxGrid};
   GC[2]={S3_Enable,S3_Symbol,S3_HTF,S3_MTF,S3_LTF,S3_BB,S3_Dev,S3_RSI,S3_ATR,S3_GridATR,S3_LotMult,S3_Base,S3_BTP,S3_BSL,S3_Risk,S3_MaxGrid};
   for(int i=0;i<3;i++) if(!InitGrid(GC[i])) return INIT_FAILED;
   return INIT_SUCCEEDED;
}
void OnDeinit(const int r){for(int i=0;i<3;i++) FreeGrid(GC[i]);}
void OnTick(){if(DDHit()){CloseAll();return;}for(int i=0;i<3;i++) RunGrid(GC[i]);}
```

## PORT 3 — EA_DawnAscendant_193234160.mq5

```mql5
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| EA_DawnAscendant_193234160.mq5                                   |
//| Strategy : MTF Volatility Breakout                               |
//| Symbols  : USDJPY + GBPJPY + EURJPY                             |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
#property copyright "Port 193234160"
#property version   "3.00"
#property strict
#include <Trade\Trade.mqh>

input group "══ SYMBOL 1 : USDJPY ══"
input bool   S1_Enable=true;  input string S1_Symbol="USDJPY";
input ENUM_TIMEFRAMES S1_HTF=PERIOD_W1,S1_MTF=PERIOD_D1,S1_LTF=PERIOD_H4;
input int S1_Don=20,S1_ATR=14,S1_EMA=200,S1_Vol=20;
input double S1_AtrEn=0.5,S1_AtrTP=2.0,S1_AtrSL=1.0,S1_VolMult=1.5;
input double S1_Risk=1.5; input int S1_MaxPos=2;
input bool S1_Trail=true; input double S1_TrATR=1.0;

input group "══ SYMBOL 2 : GBPJPY ══"
input bool   S2_Enable=true;  input string S2_Symbol="GBPJPY";
input ENUM_TIMEFRAMES S2_HTF=PERIOD_W1,S2_MTF=PERIOD_D1,S2_LTF=PERIOD_H4;
input int S2_Don=20,S2_ATR=14,S2_EMA=200,S2_Vol=20;
input double S2_AtrEn=0.5,S2_AtrTP=2.0,S2_AtrSL=1.0,S2_VolMult=1.5;
input double S2_Risk=1.5; input int S2_MaxPos=2;
input bool S2_Trail=true; input double S2_TrATR=1.0;

input group "══ SYMBOL 3 : EURJPY ══"
input bool   S3_Enable=false; input string S3_Symbol="EURJPY";
input ENUM_TIMEFRAMES S3_HTF=PERIOD_W1,S3_MTF=PERIOD_D1,S3_LTF=PERIOD_H4;
input int S3_Don=20,S3_ATR=14,S3_EMA=200,S3_Vol=20;
input double S3_AtrEn=0.5,S3_AtrTP=2.0,S3_AtrSL=1.0,S3_VolMult=1.5;
input double S3_Risk=1.5; input int S3_MaxPos=2;
input bool S3_Trail=true; input double S3_TrATR=1.0;

input group "══ MONEY MANAGEMENT ══"
input double MM_MaxDailyLoss=4.0,MM_MaxDrawdown=12.0;
input int    MM_MaxTotalPos=5;
input double MM_MinLot=0.01,MM_MaxLot=5.0;
input bool   MM_UseEquity=false;
input int    Magic=193234160;

struct BrkCfg
{
   bool enable; string sym;
   ENUM_TIMEFRAMES htf,mtf,ltf;
   int don,atr,ema,vol;
   double atrEn,atrTP,atrSL,volMult,risk;
   int maxPos; bool trail; double trATR;
   int hEMA,hATR_M,hATR_E;
   datetime lastBar;
};

BrkCfg BC[3];
CTrade trade;

int TotalPos(){int n=0;for(int i=0;i<PositionsTotal();i++){ulong t=PositionGetTicket(i);if(PositionSelectByTicket(t)&&(int)PositionGetInteger(POSITION_MAGIC)==Magic)n++;}return n;}
int SymPos(string s){int n=0;for(int i=0;i<PositionsTotal();i++){ulong t=PositionGetTicket(i);if(!PositionSelectByTicket(t))continue;if((int)PositionGetInteger(POSITION_MAGIC)==Magic&&PositionGetString(POSITION_SYMBOL)==s)n++;}return n;}
bool DDHit(){double b=AccountInfoDouble(ACCOUNT_BALANCE),e=AccountInfoDouble(ACCOUNT_EQUITY);return(b>0&&(b-e)/b*100.0>=MM_MaxDrawdown);}
bool DailyLossHit(){double bal=AccountInfoDouble(ACCOUNT_BALANCE);double lim=bal*MM_MaxDailyLoss/100.0;datetime d0=StringToTime(TimeToString(TimeCurrent(),TIME_DATE));double pl=0;for(int i=0;i<PositionsTotal();i++){ulong t=PositionGetTicket(i);if(!PositionSelectByTicket(t))continue;if((int)PositionGetInteger(POSITION_MAGIC)!=Magic)continue;pl+=PositionGetDouble(POSITION_PROFIT)+PositionGetDouble(POSITION_SWAP)+PositionGetDouble(POSITION_COMMISSION);}HistorySelect(d0,TimeCurrent());for(int i=0;i<HistoryDealsTotal();i++){ulong d=HistoryDealGetTicket(i);if((int)HistoryDealGetInteger(d,DEAL_MAGIC)!=Magic)continue;if(HistoryDealGetInteger(d,DEAL_ENTRY)==DEAL_ENTRY_OUT)pl+=HistoryDealGetDouble(d,DEAL_PROFIT)+HistoryDealGetDouble(d,DEAL_SWAP)+HistoryDealGetDouble(d,DEAL_COMMISSION);}return(pl<=-lim);}
void CloseAll(){for(int i=PositionsTotal()-1;i>=0;i--){ulong t=PositionGetTicket(i);if(PositionSelectByTicket(t)&&(int)PositionGetInteger(POSITION_MAGIC)==Magic)trade.PositionClose(t);}}

double Lot(string sym,double sl,double rPct)
{
   double base=MM_UseEquity?AccountInfoDouble(ACCOUNT_EQUITY):AccountInfoDouble(ACCOUNT_BALANCE);
   double risk=base*rPct/100.0;
   double tv=SymbolInfoDouble(sym,SYMBOL_TRADE_TICK_VALUE);
   double ts=SymbolInfoDouble(sym,SYMBOL_TRADE_TICK_SIZE);
   double ls=SymbolInfoDouble(sym,SYMBOL_VOLUME_STEP);
   double mn=MathMax(MM_MinLot,SymbolInfoDouble(sym,SYMBOL_VOLUME_MIN));
   double mx=MathMin(MM_MaxLot,SymbolInfoDouble(sym,SYMBOL_VOLUME_MAX));
   if(sl<=0||tv<=0||ts<=0) return mn;
   double lot=risk/(sl/ts*tv);
   lot=MathFloor(lot/ls)*ls;
   return MathMax(mn,MathMin(mx,lot));
}

void DelPending(string sym,int sec)
{
   for(int i=OrdersTotal()-1;i>=0;i--)
   {
      ulong t=OrderGetTicket(i); if(!OrderSelect(t)) continue;
      if((int)OrderGetInteger(ORDER_MAGIC)!=Magic) continue;
      if(OrderGetString(ORDER_SYMBOL)!=sym) continue;
      if((TimeCurrent()-(datetime)OrderGetInteger(ORDER_TIME_SETUP))>=sec)
         trade.OrderDelete(t);
   }
}

void TrailBrk(BrkCfg &b)
{
   double av[2]; CopyBuffer(b.hATR_E,0,0,2,av); double a=av[0];
   for(int i=PositionsTotal()-1;i>=0;i--)
   {
      ulong t=PositionGetTicket(i); if(!PositionSelectByTicket(t)) continue;
      if((int)PositionGetInteger(POSITION_MAGIC)!=Magic) continue;
      if(PositionGetString(POSITION_SYMBOL)!=b.sym) continue;
      if(PositionGetDouble(POSITION_PROFIT)<=0) continue;
      double sl=PositionGetDouble(POSITION_SL),tp=PositionGetDouble(POSITION_TP);
      long ty=PositionGetInteger(POSITION_TYPE);
      if(ty==POSITION_TYPE_BUY){double nsl=SymbolInfoDouble(b.sym,SYMBOL_BID)-a*b.trATR;if(nsl>sl)trade.PositionModify(t,nsl,tp);}
      else{double nsl=SymbolInfoDouble(b.sym,SYMBOL_ASK)+a*b.trATR;if(nsl<sl)trade.PositionModify(t,nsl,tp);}
   }
}

void RunBrk(BrkCfg &b)
{
   if(!b.enable) return;
   datetime bar=iTime(b.sym,b.ltf,0);
   if(bar==b.lastBar) return;
   b.lastBar=bar;

   DelPending(b.sym,PeriodSeconds(b.ltf));
   if(b.trail&&SymPos(b.sym)>0){TrailBrk(b);return;}
   if(DDHit()||DailyLossHit()) return;
   if(TotalPos()>=(int)MathMin(MM_MaxTotalPos,5)) return;
   if(SymPos(b.sym)>=(int)MathMin(b.maxPos,5))   return;

   double ema[2]; CopyBuffer(b.hEMA,0,0,2,ema);
   double px=SymbolInfoDouble(b.sym,SYMBOL_BID);
   bool hBull=px>ema[0],hBear=px<ema[0];

   double hi[],lo[];
   ArraySetAsSeries(hi,true); ArraySetAsSeries(lo,true);
   CopyHigh(b.sym,b.mtf,1,b.don,hi); CopyLow(b.sym,b.mtf,1,b.don,lo);
   double dH=hi[ArrayMaximum(hi)],dL=lo[ArrayMinimum(lo)];

   double ae[2],am[2]; CopyBuffer(b.hATR_E,0,0,2,ae); CopyBuffer(b.hATR_M,0,0,2,am);
   double atr=ae[0];
   long vArr[]; ArraySetAsSeries(vArr,true);
   CopyTickVolume(b.sym,b.ltf,1,b.vol,vArr);
   double avgV=0; for(int i=0;i<b.vol;i++) avgV+=vArr[i]; avgV/=b.vol;
   if(iVolume(b.sym,b.ltf,1)<avgV*b.volMult) return;

   double lot=Lot(b.sym,atr*b.atrSL,b.risk);
   if(OrdersTotal()==0)
   {
      if(hBull||(!hBull&&!hBear))
      {double eB=dH+atr*b.atrEn;
       trade.BuyStop(lot,eB,b.sym,eB-am[0],eB+atr*b.atrTP,ORDER_TIME_GTC,0,"DA_BS");}
      if(hBear||(!hBull&&!hBear))
      {double eS=dL-atr*b.atrEn;
       trade.SellStop(lot,eS,b.sym,eS+am[0],eS-atr*b.atrTP,ORDER_TIME_GTC,0,"DA_SS");}
   }
}

bool InitBrk(BrkCfg &b)
{
   if(!b.enable) return true;
   if(!SymbolSelect(b.sym,true)) return false;
   b.hEMA  =iMA(b.sym,b.htf,b.ema,0,MODE_EMA,PRICE_CLOSE);
   b.hATR_M=iATR(b.sym,b.mtf,b.atr);
   b.hATR_E=iATR(b.sym,b.ltf,b.atr);
   b.lastBar=0; return true;
}
void FreeBrk(BrkCfg &b){if(!b.enable)return;IndicatorRelease(b.hEMA);IndicatorRelease(b.hATR_M);IndicatorRelease(b.hATR_E);}

int OnInit()
{
   trade.SetExpertMagicNumber(Magic);
   BC[0]={S1_Enable,S1_Symbol,S1_HTF,S1_MTF,S1_LTF,S1_Don,S1_ATR,S1_EMA,S1_Vol,S1_AtrEn,S1_AtrTP,S1_AtrSL,S1_VolMult,S1_Risk,S1_MaxPos,S1_Trail,S1_TrATR};
   BC[1]={S2_Enable,S2_Symbol,S2_HTF,S2_MTF,S2_LTF,S2_Don,S2_ATR,S2_EMA,S2_Vol,S2_AtrEn,S2_AtrTP,S2_AtrSL,S2_VolMult,S2_Risk,S2_MaxPos,S2_Trail,S2_TrATR};
   BC[2]={S3_Enable,S3_Symbol,S3_HTF,S3_MTF,S3_LTF,S3_Don,S3_ATR,S3_EMA,S3_Vol,S3_AtrEn,S3_AtrTP,S3_AtrSL,S3_VolMult,S3_Risk,S3_MaxPos,S3_Trail,S3_TrATR};
   for(int i=0;i<3;i++) if(!InitBrk(BC[i])) return INIT_FAILED;
   return INIT_SUCCEEDED;
}
void OnDeinit(const int r){for(int i=0;i<3;i++) FreeBrk(BC[i]);}
void OnTick(){if(DDHit()){CloseAll();return;}for(int i=0;i<3;i++) RunBrk(BC[i]);}
```

> Port 4-11 ใช้โครงสร้างเดียวกันทุกตัว เปลี่ยนแค่ Logic ใน `Run()` ครับบอส — ผมเขียนครบต่อเลย

## PORT 4 — EA_CrimsonTide_159219430.mq5

```mql5
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| EA_CrimsonTide_159219430.mq5                                     |
//| Strategy : MTF Mean Reversion                                    |
//| Symbols  : EURGBP + CHFJPY + EURCAD                             |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
#property copyright "Port 159219430"
#property version   "3.00"
#property strict
#include <Trade\Trade.mqh>

input group "══ SYMBOL 1 : EURGBP ══"
input bool   S1_Enable=true;  input string S1_Symbol="EURGBP";
input ENUM_TIMEFRAMES S1_HTF=PERIOD_W1,S1_MTF=PERIOD_D1,S1_LTF=PERIOD_H1;
input int S1_BB=20; input double S1_DevH=2.0,S1_DevE=2.5;
input int S1_RSI=7,S1_SK=5,S1_SD=3,S1_SS=3,S1_ADX=14;
input double S1_AdxMax=30.0,S1_BbW=0.04,S1_AtrSL=1.5;
input int S1_ATR=14,S1_Hold=48; input double S1_Risk=0.8; input int S1_MaxPos=3;

input group "══ SYMBOL 2 : CHFJPY ══"
input bool   S2_Enable=true;  input string S2_Symbol="CHFJPY";
input ENUM_TIMEFRAMES S2_HTF=PERIOD_W1,S2_MTF=PERIOD_D1,S2_LTF=PERIOD_H1;
input int S2_BB=20; input double S2_DevH=2.0,S2_DevE=2.5;
input int S2_RSI=7,S2_SK=5,S2_SD=3,S2_SS=3,S2_ADX=14;
input double S2_AdxMax=30.0,S2_BbW=0.04,S2_AtrSL=1.5;
input int S2_ATR=14,S2_Hold=48; input double S2_Risk=0.8; input int S2_MaxPos=3;

input group "══ SYMBOL 3 : EURCAD ══"
input bool   S3_Enable=false; input string S3_Symbol="EURCAD";
input ENUM_TIMEFRAMES S3_HTF=PERIOD_W1,S3_MTF=PERIOD_D1,S3_LTF=PERIOD_H1;
input int S3_BB=20; input double S3_DevH=2.0,S3_DevE=2.5;
input int S3_RSI=7,S3_SK=5,S3_SD=3,S3_SS=3,S3_ADX=14;
input double S3_AdxMax=30.0,S3_BbW=0.04,S3_AtrSL=1.5;
input int S3_ATR=14,S3_Hold=48; input double S3_Risk=0.8; input int S3_MaxPos=3;

input group "══ MONEY MANAGEMENT ══"
input double MM_MaxDailyLoss=2.5,MM_MaxDrawdown=8.0;
input int    MM_MaxTotalPos=5;
input double MM_MinLot=0.01,MM_MaxLot=3.0;
input bool   MM_UseEquity=false;
input int    Magic=159219430;

struct MRCfg
{
   bool enable; string sym;
   ENUM_TIMEFRAMES htf,mtf,ltf;
   int bb; double devH,devE;
   int rsi,sk,sd,ss,adx;
   double adxMax,bbW,atrSL,risk;
   int atr,hold,maxPos;
   int hBH,hADX,hEMA,hBE,hRSI,hST,hATR;
   datetime lastBar;
};

MRCfg MC[3];
CTrade trade;

int TotalPos(){int n=0;for(int i=0;i<PositionsTotal();i++){ulong t=PositionGetTicket(i);if(PositionSelectByTicket(t)&&(int)PositionGetInteger(POSITION_MAGIC)==Magic)n++;}return n;}
int SymPos(string s){int n=0;for(int i=0;i<PositionsTotal();i++){ulong t=PositionGetTicket(i);if(!PositionSelectByTicket(t))continue;if((int)PositionGetInteger(POSITION_MAGIC)==Magic&&PositionGetString(POSITION_SYMBOL)==s)n++;}return n;}
bool DDHit(){double b=AccountInfoDouble(ACCOUNT_BALANCE),e=AccountInfoDouble(ACCOUNT_EQUITY);return(b>0&&(b-e)/b*100.0>=MM_MaxDrawdown);}
bool DailyLossHit(){double bal=AccountInfoDouble(ACCOUNT_BALANCE);double lim=bal*MM_MaxDailyLoss/100.0;datetime d0=StringToTime(TimeToString(TimeCurrent(),TIME_DATE));double pl=0;for(int i=0;i<PositionsTotal();i++){ulong t=PositionGetTicket(i);if(!PositionSelectByTicket(t))continue;if((int)PositionGetInteger(POSITION_MAGIC)!=Magic)continue;pl+=PositionGetDouble(POSITION_PROFIT)+PositionGetDouble(POSITION_SWAP)+PositionGetDouble(POSITION_COMMISSION);}HistorySelect(d0,TimeCurrent());for(int i=0;i<HistoryDealsTotal();i++){ulong d=HistoryDealGetTicket(i);if((int)HistoryDealGetInteger(d,DEAL_MAGIC)!=Magic)continue;if(HistoryDealGetInteger(d,DEAL_ENTRY)==DEAL_ENTRY_OUT)pl+=HistoryDealGetDouble(d,DEAL_PROFIT)+HistoryDealGetDouble(d,DEAL_SWAP)+HistoryDealGetDouble(d,DEAL_COMMISSION);}return(pl<=-lim);}
void CloseAll(){for(int i=PositionsTotal()-1;i>=0;i--){ulong t=PositionGetTicket(i);if(PositionSelectByTicket(t)&&(int)PositionGetInteger(POSITION_MAGIC)==Magic)trade.PositionClose(t);}}
double Lot(string sym,double sl,double rPct){double base=MM_UseEquity?AccountInfoDouble(ACCOUNT_EQUITY):AccountInfoDouble(ACCOUNT_BALANCE);double risk=base*rPct/100.0;double tv=SymbolInfoDouble(sym,SYMBOL_TRADE_TICK_VALUE);double ts=SymbolInfoDouble(sym,SYMBOL_TRADE_TICK_SIZE);double ls=SymbolInfoDouble(sym,SYMBOL_VOLUME_STEP);double mn=MathMax(MM_MinLot,SymbolInfoDouble(sym,SYMBOL_VOLUME_MIN));double mx=MathMin(MM_MaxLot,SymbolInfoDouble(sym,SYMBOL_VOLUME_MAX));if(sl<=0||tv<=0||ts<=0)return mn;double lot=risk/(sl/ts*tv);lot=MathFloor(lot/ls)*ls;return MathMax(mn,MathMin(mx,lot));}

void TimeExit(string sym,int holdH)
{
   datetime now=TimeCurrent();
   for(int i=PositionsTotal()-1;i>=0;i--)
   {
      ulong t=PositionGetTicket(i); if(!PositionSelectByTicket(t)) continue;
      if((int)PositionGetInteger(POSITION_MAGIC)!=Magic) continue;
      if(PositionGetString(POSITION_SYMBOL)!=sym) continue;
      if((now-(datetime)PositionGetInteger(POSITION_TIME))>=(datetime)(holdH*3600))
         trade.PositionClose(t);
   }
}

void RunMR(MRCfg &m)
{
   if(!m.enable) return;
   datetime bar=iTime(m.sym,m.ltf,0);
   if(bar==m.lastBar) return;
   m.lastBar=bar;

   TimeExit(m.sym,m.hold);
   if(DDHit()||DailyLossHit()) return;
   if(TotalPos()>=(int)MathMin(MM_MaxTotalPos,5)) return;
   if(SymPos(m.sym)>=(int)MathMin(m.maxPos,5))   return;

   double hu[2],hl[2],hm[2];
   CopyBuffer(m.hBH,0,0,2,hm); CopyBuffer(m.hBH,1,0,2,hu); CopyBuffer(m.hBH,2,0,2,hl);
   double bw=(hm[0]>0)?(hu[0]-hl[0])/hm[0]:999;
   if(bw>m.bbW) return;

   double adxV[2],ema[2];
   CopyBuffer(m.hADX,MAIN_LINE,0,2,adxV); CopyBuffer(m.hEMA,0,0,2,ema);
   if(adxV[0]>m.adxMax) return;
   double px=SymbolInfoDouble(m.sym,SYMBOL_BID);
   bool above=px>ema[0],below=px<ema[0];

   double bu[2],bl[2],bm[2],rv[2],stk[2],av[2];
   CopyBuffer(m.hBE,0,0,2,bm); CopyBuffer(m.hBE,1,0,2,bu); CopyBuffer(m.hBE,2,0,2,bl);
   CopyBuffer(m.hRSI,0,0,2,rv); CopyBuffer(m.hST,MAIN_LINE,0,2,stk); CopyBuffer(m.hATR,0,0,2,av);
   double a=av[0];

   if(px<bl[0]&&rv[0]<25&&stk[0]<20&&!below)
   {double ask=SymbolInfoDouble(m.sym,SYMBOL_ASK);
    trade.Buy(Lot(m.sym,a*m.atrSL,m.risk),m.sym,ask,ask-a*m.atrSL,bm[0],"CT_BUY");}
   else if(px>bu[0]&&rv[0]>75&&stk[0]>80&&!above)
   {double bid=SymbolInfoDouble(m.sym,SYMBOL_BID);
    trade.Sell(Lot(m.sym,a*m.atrSL,m.risk),m.sym,bid,bid+a*m.atrSL,bm[0],"CT_SELL");}
}

bool InitMR(MRCfg &m)
{
   if(!m.enable) return true;
   if(!SymbolSelect(m.sym,true)) return false;
   m.hBH =iBands(m.sym,m.htf,m.bb,0,m.devH,PRICE_CLOSE);
   m.hADX=iADX(m.sym,m.mtf,m.adx);
   m.hEMA=iMA(m.sym,m.mtf,200,0,MODE_EMA,PRICE_CLOSE);
   m.hBE =iBands(m.sym,m.ltf,m.bb,0,m.devE,PRICE_CLOSE);
   m.hRSI=iRSI(m.sym,m.ltf,m.rsi,PRICE_CLOSE);
   m.hST =iStochastic(m.sym,m.ltf,m.sk,m.sd,m.ss,MODE_SMA,STO_LOWHIGH);
   m.hATR=iATR(m.sym,m.ltf,m.atr);
   m.lastBar=0; return true;
}
void FreeMR(MRCfg &m){if(!m.enable)return;IndicatorRelease(m.hBH);IndicatorRelease(m.hADX);IndicatorRelease(m.hEMA);IndicatorRelease(m.hBE);IndicatorRelease(m.hRSI);IndicatorRelease(m.hST);IndicatorRelease(m.hATR);}

int OnInit()
{
   trade.SetExpertMagicNumber(Magic);
   MC[0]={S1_Enable,S1_Symbol,S1_HTF,S1_MTF,S1_LTF,S1_BB,S1_DevH,S1_DevE,S1_RSI,S1_SK,S1_SD,S1_SS,S1_ADX,S1_AdxMax,S1_BbW,S1_AtrSL,S1_Risk,S1_ATR,S1_Hold,S1_MaxPos};
   MC[1]={S2_Enable,S2_Symbol,S2_HTF,S2_MTF,S2_LTF,S2_BB,S2_DevH,S2_DevE,S2_RSI,S2_SK,S2_SD,S2_SS,S2_ADX,S2_AdxMax,S2_BbW,S2_AtrSL,S2_Risk,S2_ATR,S2_Hold,S2_MaxPos};
   MC[2]={S3_Enable,S3_Symbol,S3_HTF,S3_MTF,S3_LTF,S3_BB,S3_DevH,S3_DevE,S3_RSI,S3_SK,S3_SD,S3_SS,S3_ADX,S3_AdxMax,S3_BbW,S3_AtrSL,S3_Risk,S3_ATR,S3_Hold,S3_MaxPos};
   for(int i=0;i<3;i++) if(!InitMR(MC[i])) return INIT_FAILED;
   return INIT_SUCCEEDED;
}
void OnDeinit(const int r){for(int i=0;i<3;i++) FreeMR(MC[i]);}
void OnTick(){if(DDHit()){CloseAll();return;}for(int i=0;i<3;i++) RunMR(MC[i]);}
```

## PORT 5 — EA_AbyssalDiscipline_111098104.mq5

```mql5
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| EA_AbyssalDiscipline_111098104.mq5                               |
//| Strategy : MTF Z-Score Pair Trading                              |
//| รันบน EURUSD Chart — Symbol2 ตั้งเป็น USDCHF                    |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
#property copyright "Port 111098104"
#property version   "3.00"
#property strict
#include <Trade\Trade.mqh>

input group "══ PAIR 1 ══"
input bool   P1_Enable=true;
input string P1_Sym1="EURUSD"; input string P1_Sym2="USDCHF";
input ENUM_TIMEFRAMES P1_HTF=PERIOD_D1,P1_MTF=PERIOD_H4,P1_LTF=PERIOD_H1;
input int P1_LookH=20,P1_LookM=50;
input double P1_ZEntry=2.0,P1_ZExit=0.3,P1_ZSL=3.5,P1_CorrMin=-0.7;
input int P1_HoldDays=5; input double P1_FixLot=0.01;

input group "══ PAIR 2 ══"
input bool   P2_Enable=false;
input string P2_Sym1="GBPUSD"; input string P2_Sym2="USDCAD";
input ENUM_TIMEFRAMES P2_HTF=PERIOD_D1,P2_MTF=PERIOD_H4,P2_LTF=PERIOD_H1;
input int P2_LookH=20,P2_LookM=50;
input double P2_ZEntry=2.0,P2_ZExit=0.3,P2_ZSL=3.5,P2_CorrMin=-0.7;
input int P2_HoldDays=5; input double P2_FixLot=0.01;

input group "══ MONEY MANAGEMENT ══"
input double MM_MaxDailyLoss=2.0,MM_MaxDrawdown=8.0;
input int    MM_MaxTotalPos=5;
input int    Magic=111098104;

struct PairCfg
{
   bool enable;
   string sym1,sym2;
   ENUM_TIMEFRAMES htf,mtf,ltf;
   int lookH,lookM;
   double zEntry,zExit,zSL,corrMin;
   int holdDays; double fixLot;
   datetime lastBar;
};

PairCfg PC[2];
CTrade trade;

int TotalPos(){int n=0;for(int i=0;i<PositionsTotal();i++){ulong t=PositionGetTicket(i);if(PositionSelectByTicket(t)&&(int)PositionGetInteger(POSITION_MAGIC)==Magic)n++;}return n;}
bool DDHit(){double b=AccountInfoDouble(ACCOUNT_BALANCE),e=AccountInfoDouble(ACCOUNT_EQUITY);return(b>0&&(b-e)/b*100.0>=MM_MaxDrawdown);}
bool DailyLossHit(){double bal=AccountInfoDouble(ACCOUNT_BALANCE);double lim=bal*MM_MaxDailyLoss/100.0;datetime d0=StringToTime(TimeToString(TimeCurrent(),TIME_DATE));double pl=0;for(int i=0;i<PositionsTotal();i++){ulong t=PositionGetTicket(i);if(!PositionSelectByTicket(t))continue;if((int)PositionGetInteger(POSITION_MAGIC)!=Magic)continue;pl+=PositionGetDouble(POSITION_PROFIT)+PositionGetDouble(POSITION_SWAP)+PositionGetDouble(POSITION_COMMISSION);}HistorySelect(d0,TimeCurrent());for(int i=0;i<HistoryDealsTotal();i++){ulong d=HistoryDealGetTicket(i);if((int)HistoryDealGetInteger(d,DEAL_MAGIC)!=Magic)continue;if(HistoryDealGetInteger(d,DEAL_ENTRY)==DEAL_ENTRY_OUT)pl+=HistoryDealGetDouble(d,DEAL_PROFIT)+HistoryDealGetDouble(d,DEAL_SWAP)+HistoryDealGetDouble(d,DEAL_COMMISSION);}return(pl<=-lim);}
void CloseAll(){for(int i=PositionsTotal()-1;i>=0;i--){ulong t=PositionGetTicket(i);if(PositionSelectByTicket(t)&&(int)PositionGetInteger(POSITION_MAGIC)==Magic)trade.PositionClose(t);}}

int PairCount(string s1,string s2)
{
   int n=0;
   for(int i=0;i<PositionsTotal();i++)
   {
      ulong t=PositionGetTicket(i); if(!PositionSelectByTicket(t)) continue;
      if((int)PositionGetInteger(POSITION_MAGIC)!=Magic) continue;
      string s=PositionGetString(POSITION_SYMBOL);
      if(s==s1||s==s2) n++;
   }
   return n;
}

double ZScore(string s1,string s2,ENUM_TIMEFRAMES tf,int period)
{
   double c1[],c2[]; ArraySetAsSeries(c1,true); ArraySetAsSeries(c2,true);
   if(CopyClose(s1,tf,0,period+1,c1)<period+1) return EMPTY_VALUE;
   if(CopyClose(s2,tf,0,period+1,c2)<period+1) return EMPTY_VALUE;
   double sp[]; ArrayResize(sp,period);
   for(int i=0;i<period;i++) sp[i]=c1[i]-(1.0-c2[i]);
   double mn=0,sd=0;
   for(int i=0;i<period;i++) mn+=sp[i]; mn/=period;
   for(int i=0;i<period;i++) sd+=MathPow(sp[i]-mn,2); sd=MathSqrt(sd/period);
   return(sd==0)?0:(sp[0]-mn)/sd;
}

double Corr(string s1,string s2,ENUM_TIMEFRAMES tf,int period)
{
   double c1[],c2[]; ArraySetAsSeries(c1,true); ArraySetAsSeries(c2,true);
   CopyClose(s1,tf,0,period,c1); CopyClose(s2,tf,0,period,c2);
   double m1=0,m2=0,cov=0,v1=0,v2=0;
   for(int i=0;i<period;i++){m1+=c1[i];m2+=c2[i];} m1/=period;m2/=period;
   for(int i=0;i<period;i++){cov+=(c1[i]-m1)*(c2[i]-m2);v1+=MathPow(c1[i]-m1,2);v2+=MathPow(c2[i]-m2,2);}
   return(v1>0&&v2>0)?cov/MathSqrt(v1*v2):0;
}

void PairExit(PairCfg &p)
{
   if(PairCount(p.sym1,p.sym2)==0) return;
   double z=ZScore(p.sym1,p.sym2,p.mtf,p.lookM);
   bool eTP=MathAbs(z)<p.zExit, eSL=MathAbs(z)>p.zSL, eDD=DDHit();
   bool eTime=false;
   for(int i=0;i<PositionsTotal();i++)
   {
      ulong t=PositionGetTicket(i); if(!PositionSelectByTicket(t)) continue;
      if((int)PositionGetInteger(POSITION_MAGIC)!=Magic) continue;
      string s=PositionGetString(POSITION_SYMBOL);
      if(s!=p.sym1&&s!=p.sym2) continue;
      if((TimeCurrent()-(datetime)PositionGetInteger(POSITION_TIME))>=(datetime)(p.holdDays*86400)) eTime=true;
   }
   if(eTP||eSL||eTime||eDD)
      for(int i=PositionsTotal()-1;i>=0;i--)
      {
         ulong t=PositionGetTicket(i); if(!PositionSelectByTicket(t)) continue;
         if((int)PositionGetInteger(POSITION_MAGIC)!=Magic) continue;
         string s=PositionGetString(POSITION_SYMBOL);
         if(s==p.sym1||s==p.sym2) trade.PositionClose(t);
      }
}

void RunPair(PairCfg &p)
{
   if(!p.enable) return;
   datetime bar=iTime(p.sym1,p.ltf,0);
   if(bar==p.lastBar) return;
   p.lastBar=bar;

   PairExit(p);
   if(DDHit()||DailyLossHit()) return;
   if(TotalPos()>=(int)MathMin(MM_MaxTotalPos,5)) return;
   if(PairCount(p.sym1,p.sym2)>=2) return;

   double corrV=Corr(p.sym1,p.sym2,p.htf,p.lookH);
   if(corrV>p.corrMin) return;

   double zM=ZScore(p.sym1,p.sym2,p.mtf,p.lookM);
   double zL=ZScore(p.sym1,p.sym2,p.ltf,p.lookM/2);
   if(zM==EMPTY_VALUE||zL==EMPTY_VALUE) return;

   SymbolSelect(p.sym1,true); SymbolSelect(p.sym2,true);
   double a1=SymbolInfoDouble(p.sym1,SYMBOL_ASK),b1=SymbolInfoDouble(p.sym1,SYMBOL_BID);
   double a2=SymbolInfoDouble(p.sym2,SYMBOL_ASK),b2=SymbolInfoDouble(p.sym2,SYMBOL_BID);

   if(zM<-p.zEntry&&zL<-p.zEntry)
   {trade.Buy(p.fixLot,p.sym1,a1,0,0,"AD_B1"); trade.Sell(p.fixLot,p.sym2,b2,0,0,"AD_S2");}
   else if(zM>p.zEntry&&zL>p.zEntry)
   {trade.Sell(p.fixLot,p.sym1,b1,0,0,"AD_S1"); trade.Buy(p.fixLot,p.sym2,a2,0,0,"AD_B2");}
}

int OnInit()
{
   trade.SetExpertMagicNumber(Magic);
   PC[0]={P1_Enable,P1_Sym1,P1_Sym2,P1_HTF,P1_MTF,P1_LTF,P1_LookH,P1_LookM,P1_ZEntry,P1_ZExit,P1_ZSL,P1_CorrMin,P1_HoldDays,P1_FixLot,0};
   PC[1]={P2_Enable,P2_Sym1,P2_Sym2,P2_HTF,P2_MTF,P2_LTF,P2_LookH,P2_LookM,P2_ZEntry,P2_ZExit,P2_ZSL,P2_CorrMin,P2_HoldDays,P2_FixLot,0};
   for(int i=0;i<2;i++){if(!PC[i].enable)continue;SymbolSelect(PC[i].sym1,true);SymbolSelect(PC[i].sym2,true);}
   return INIT_SUCCEEDED;
}
void OnDeinit(const int r){}
void OnTick(){if(DDHit()){CloseAll();return;}for(int i=0;i<2;i++) RunPair(PC[i]);}
```

## PORT 6 — EA_GoldenEmber_117030969.mq5

```mql5
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| EA_GoldenEmber_117030969.mq5                                     |
//| Strategy : MTF Pivot Range Trading                               |
//| Symbols  : NZDUSD + AUDUSD + USDCAD                             |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
#property copyright "Port 117030969"
#property version   "3.00"
#property strict
#include <Trade\Trade.mqh>

input group "══ SYMBOL 1 : NZDUSD ══"
input bool   S1_Enable=true;  input string S1_Symbol="NZDUSD";
input ENUM_TIMEFRAMES S1_HTF=PERIOD_MN1,S1_MTF=PERIOD_W1,S1_LTF=PERIOD_H1;
input int S1_RSI=14,S1_CCI=20,S1_ATR=14;
input double S1_Zone=0.3,S1_SL=0.5,S1_Risk=0.8; input int S1_MaxPos=3;

input group "══ SYMBOL 2 : AUDUSD ══"
input bool   S2_Enable=true;  input string S2_Symbol="AUDUSD";
input ENUM_TIMEFRAMES S2_HTF=PERIOD_MN1,S2_MTF=PERIOD_W1,S2_LTF=PERIOD_H1;
input int S2_RSI=14,S2_CCI=20,S2_ATR=14;
input double S2_Zone=0.3,S2_SL=0.5,S2_Risk=0.8; input int S2_MaxPos=3;

input group "══ SYMBOL 3 : USDCAD ══"
input bool   S3_Enable=false; input string S3_Symbol="USDCAD";
input ENUM_TIMEFRAMES S3_HTF=PERIOD_MN1,S3_MTF=PERIOD_W1,S3_LTF=PERIOD_H1;
input int S3_RSI=14,S3_CCI=20,S3_ATR=14;
input double S3_Zone=0.3,S3_SL=0.5,S3_Risk=0.8; input int S3_MaxPos=3;

input group "══ MONEY MANAGEMENT ══"
input double MM_MaxDailyLoss=2.5,MM_MaxDrawdown=8.0;
input int    MM_MaxTotalPos=5;
input double MM_MinLot=0.01,MM_MaxLot=3.0;
input bool   MM_UseEquity=false;
input int    Magic=117030969;

struct PvtCfg
{
   bool enable; string sym;
   ENUM_TIMEFRAMES htf,mtf,ltf;
   int rsi,cci,atr;
   double zone,sl,risk; int maxPos;
   int hRSI,hCCI,hATR;
   datetime lastBar;
};
struct Pvt{double PP,R1,R2,S1,S2;};

PvtCfg VC[3];
CTrade trade;

int TotalPos(){int n=0;for(int i=0;i<PositionsTotal();i++){ulong t=PositionGetTicket(i);if(PositionSelectByTicket(t)&&(int)PositionGetInteger(POSITION_MAGIC)==Magic)n++;}return n;}
int SymPos(string s){int n=0;for(int i=0;i<PositionsTotal();i++){ulong t=PositionGetTicket(i);if(!PositionSelectByTicket(t))continue;if((int)PositionGetInteger(POSITION_MAGIC)==Magic&&PositionGetString(POSITION_SYMBOL)==s)n++;}return n;}
bool DDHit(){double b=AccountInfoDouble(ACCOUNT_BALANCE),e=AccountInfoDouble(ACCOUNT_EQUITY);return(b>0&&(b-e)/b*100.0>=MM_MaxDrawdown);}
bool DailyLossHit(){double bal=AccountInfoDouble(ACCOUNT_BALANCE);double lim=bal*MM_MaxDailyLoss/100.0;datetime d0=StringToTime(TimeToString(TimeCurrent(),TIME_DATE));double pl=0;for(int i=0;i<PositionsTotal();i++){ulong t=PositionGetTicket(i);if(!PositionSelectByTicket(t))continue;if((int)PositionGetInteger(POSITION_MAGIC)!=Magic)continue;pl+=PositionGetDouble(POSITION_PROFIT)+PositionGetDouble(POSITION_SWAP)+PositionGetDouble(POSITION_COMMISSION);}HistorySelect(d0,TimeCurrent());for(int i=0;i<HistoryDealsTotal();i++){ulong d=HistoryDealGetTicket(i);if((int)HistoryDealGetInteger(d,DEAL_MAGIC)!=Magic)continue;if(HistoryDealGetInteger(d,DEAL_ENTRY)==DEAL_ENTRY_OUT)pl+=HistoryDealGetDouble(d,DEAL_PROFIT)+HistoryDealGetDouble(d,DEAL_SWAP)+HistoryDealGetDouble(d,DEAL_COMMISSION);}return(pl<=-lim);}
void CloseAll(){for(int i=PositionsTotal()-1;i>=0;i--){ulong t=PositionGetTicket(i);if(PositionSelectByTicket(t)&&(int)PositionGetInteger(POSITION_MAGIC)==Magic)trade.PositionClose(t);}}
double Lot(string sym,double slD,double rPct){double base=MM_UseEquity?AccountInfoDouble(ACCOUNT_EQUITY):AccountInfoDouble(ACCOUNT_BALANCE);double risk=base*rPct/100.0;double tv=SymbolInfoDouble(sym,SYMBOL_TRADE_TICK_VALUE);double ts=SymbolInfoDouble(sym,SYMBOL_TRADE_TICK_SIZE);double ls=SymbolInfoDouble(sym,SYMBOL_VOLUME_STEP);double mn=MathMax(MM_MinLot,SymbolInfoDouble(sym,SYMBOL_VOLUME_MIN));double mx=MathMin(MM_MaxLot,SymbolInfoDouble(sym,SYMBOL_VOLUME_MAX));if(slD<=0||tv<=0||ts<=0)return mn;double lot=risk/(slD/ts*tv);lot=MathFloor(lot/ls)*ls;return MathMax(mn,MathMin(mx,lot));}

Pvt CalcPvt(string sym,ENUM_TIMEFRAMES tf,int sh)
{
   Pvt p={0,0,0,0,0};
   double hi[1],lo[1],cl[1];
   ArraySetAsSeries(hi,true);ArraySetAsSeries(lo,true);ArraySetAsSeries(cl,true);
   if(CopyHigh(sym,tf,sh,1,hi)<1) return p;
   if(CopyLow(sym,tf,sh,1,lo)<1)  return p;
   if(CopyClose(sym,tf,sh,1,cl)<1) return p;
   p.PP=(hi[0]+lo[0]+cl[0])/3.0;
   p.R1=2*p.PP-lo[0]; p.R2=p.PP+(hi[0]-lo[0]);
   p.S1=2*p.PP-hi[0]; p.S2=p.PP-(hi[0]-lo[0]);
   return p;
}

void RunPvt(PvtCfg &v)
{
   if(!v.enable) return;
   datetime bar=iTime(v.sym,v.ltf,0);
   if(bar==v.lastBar) return;
   v.lastBar=bar;

   if(DDHit()||DailyLossHit()) return;
   if(TotalPos()>=(int)MathMin(MM_MaxTotalPos,5)) return;
   if(SymPos(v.sym)>=(int)MathMin(v.maxPos,5))   return;

   Pvt mn=CalcPvt(v.sym,v.htf,1);
   Pvt wk=CalcPvt(v.sym,v.mtf,1);
   double px=SymbolInfoDouble(v.sym,SYMBOL_BID);
   double av[2]; CopyBuffer(v.hATR,0,0,2,av); double a=av[0];
   double zone=a*v.zone;
   double rv[2],cv[2];
   CopyBuffer(v.hRSI,0,0,2,rv); CopyBuffer(v.hCCI,0,0,2,cv);

   bool nS1=MathAbs(px-wk.S1)<=zone,nS2=MathAbs(px-wk.S2)<=zone;
   bool nR1=MathAbs(px-wk.R1)<=zone,nR2=MathAbs(px-wk.R2)<=zone;
   bool mBull=px>mn.S1, mBear=px<mn.R1;

   if((nS1||nS2)&&rv[0]<40&&cv[0]<-80&&mBull)
   {
      double ask=SymbolInfoDouble(v.sym,SYMBOL_ASK);
      double sl=(nS2?wk.S2:wk.S2-a)-a*v.sl;
      double tp=nS1?wk.PP:wk.S1;
      if(sl<ask&&tp>ask)
         trade.Buy(Lot(v.sym,ask-sl,v.risk),v.sym,ask,sl,tp,"GE_BUY");
   }
   else if((nR1||nR2)&&rv[0]>60&&cv[0]>80&&mBear)
   {
      double bid=SymbolInfoDouble(v.sym,SYMBOL_BID);
      double sl=(nR2?wk.R2:wk.R2+a)+a*v.sl;
      double tp=nR1?wk.PP:wk.R1;
      if(sl>bid&&tp<bid)
         trade.Sell(Lot(v.sym,sl-bid,v.risk),v.sym,bid,sl,tp,"GE_SELL");
   }
}

bool InitPvt(PvtCfg &v)
{
   if(!v.enable) return true;
   if(!SymbolSelect(v.sym,true)) return false;
   v.hRSI=iRSI(v.sym,v.ltf,v.rsi,PRICE_CLOSE);
   v.hCCI=iCCI(v.sym,v.ltf,v.cci,PRICE_TYPICAL);
   v.hATR=iATR(v.sym,v.ltf,v.atr);
   v.lastBar=0; return true;
}
void FreePvt(PvtCfg &v){if(!v.enable)return;IndicatorRelease(v.hRSI);IndicatorRelease(v.hCCI);IndicatorRelease(v.hATR);}

int OnInit()
{
   trade.SetExpertMagicNumber(Magic);
   VC[0]={S1_Enable,S1_Symbol,S1_HTF,S1_MTF,S1_LTF,S1_RSI,S1_CCI,S1_ATR,S1_Zone,S1_SL,S1_Risk,S1_MaxPos};
   VC[1]={S2_Enable,S2_Symbol,S2_HTF,S2_MTF,S2_LTF,S2_RSI,S2_CCI,S2_ATR,S2_Zone,S2_SL,S2_Risk,S2_MaxPos};
   VC[2]={S3_Enable,S3_Symbol,S3_HTF,S3_MTF,S3_LTF,S3_RSI,S3_CCI,S3_ATR,S3_Zone,S3_SL,S3_Risk,S3_MaxPos};
   for(int i=0;i<3;i++) if(!InitPvt(VC[i])) return INIT_FAILED;
   return INIT_SUCCEEDED;
}
void OnDeinit(const int r){for(int i=0;i<3;i++) FreePvt(VC[i]);}
void OnTick(){if(DDHit()){CloseAll();return;}for(int i=0;i<3;i++) RunPvt(VC[i]);}
```

```mql5
ScalpCfg AC[3];

double Pip(string sym){double pt=SymbolInfoDouble(sym,SYMBOL_POINT);int dg=(int)SymbolInfoInteger(sym,SYMBOL_DIGITS);return(dg==5||dg==3)?pt*10.0:pt;}
bool InSession(int sh,int eh){MqlDateTime dt;TimeToStruct(TimeGMT(),dt);return(dt.hour>=sh&&dt.hour<eh);}

void RunScalp(ScalpCfg &c)
{
   if(!c.enable) return;
   datetime bar=iTime(c.sym,c.ltf,0);
   if(bar==c.lastBar) return;
   c.lastBar=bar;

   MqlDateTime dt; TimeToStruct(TimeCurrent(),dt);
   datetime today=StringToTime(StringFormat("%04d.%02d.%02d",dt.year,dt.mon,dt.day));
   if(today!=c.lastDay){c.trades=0;c.lastDay=today;}

   if(!InSession(c.sessStart,c.sessEnd)) return;
   if(c.trades>=c.maxTd) return;
   if(DDHit()||DailyLossHit()) return;
   if(TotalPos()>=(int)MathMin(MM_MaxTotalPos,5)) return;
   if(SymPos(c.sym)>=(int)MathMin(c.maxPos,5))   return;

   double eh[2]; CopyBuffer(c.hEH,0,0,2,eh);
   double px=SymbolInfoDouble(c.sym,SYMBOL_BID);
   bool hBull=px>eh[0],hBear=px<eh[0];

   double ef_m[2],es_m[2];
   CopyBuffer(c.hEF_M,0,0,2,ef_m); CopyBuffer(c.hES_M,0,0,2,es_m);
   bool mBull=ef_m[0]>es_m[0],mBear=ef_m[0]<es_m[0];

   double ef_e[3],es_e[3];
   CopyBuffer(c.hEF_E,0,0,3,ef_e); CopyBuffer(c.hES_E,0,0,3,es_e);
   bool xUp=ef_e[1]>es_e[1]&&ef_e[2]<=es_e[2];
   bool xDn=ef_e[1]<es_e[1]&&ef_e[2]>=es_e[2];

   long vArr[]; ArraySetAsSeries(vArr,true);
   CopyTickVolume(c.sym,c.ltf,1,c.vp,vArr);
   double avgV=0; for(int i=0;i<c.vp;i++) avgV+=vArr[i]; avgV/=c.vp;
   bool vOK=iVolume(c.sym,c.ltf,1)>avgV*c.vm;

   double pip=Pip(c.sym);
   if(hBull&&mBull&&xUp&&vOK)
   {double ask=SymbolInfoDouble(c.sym,SYMBOL_ASK);
    if(trade.Buy(Lot(c.sym,c.sl*pip,c.risk),c.sym,ask,ask-c.sl*pip,ask+c.tp*pip,"TF_B")) c.trades++;}
   else if(hBear&&mBear&&xDn&&vOK)
   {double bid=SymbolInfoDouble(c.sym,SYMBOL_BID);
    if(trade.Sell(Lot(c.sym,c.sl*pip,c.risk),c.sym,bid,bid+c.sl*pip,bid-c.tp*pip,"TF_S")) c.trades++;}
}

bool InitScalp(ScalpCfg &c)
{
   if(!c.enable) return true;
   if(!SymbolSelect(c.sym,true)) return false;
   c.hEH  =iMA(c.sym,c.htf,c.eh,0,MODE_EMA,PRICE_CLOSE);
   c.hEF_M=iMA(c.sym,c.mtf,c.ef,0,MODE_EMA,PRICE_CLOSE);
   c.hES_M=iMA(c.sym,c.mtf,c.es,0,MODE_EMA,PRICE_CLOSE);
   c.hEF_E=iMA(c.sym,c.ltf,c.ef,0,MODE_EMA,PRICE_CLOSE);
   c.hES_E=iMA(c.sym,c.ltf,c.es,0,MODE_EMA,PRICE_CLOSE);
   c.trades=0; c.lastDay=0; c.lastBar=0; return true;
}
void FreeScalp(ScalpCfg &c){if(!c.enable)return;IndicatorRelease(c.hEH);IndicatorRelease(c.hEF_M);IndicatorRelease(c.hES_M);IndicatorRelease(c.hEF_E);IndicatorRelease(c.hES_E);}

int OnInit()
{
   trade.SetExpertMagicNumber(Magic);
   AC[0]={S1_Enable,S1_Symbol,S1_HTF,S1_MTF,S1_LTF,S1_EF,S1_ES,S1_EH,S1_VP,S1_VM,S1_TP,S1_SL,S1_MaxTd,S1_Risk,S1_MaxPos,S1_SessStart,S1_SessEnd};
   AC[1]={S2_Enable,S2_Symbol,S2_HTF,S2_MTF,S2_LTF,S2_EF,S2_ES,S2_EH,S2_VP,S2_VM,S2_TP,S2_SL,S2_MaxTd,S2_Risk,S2_MaxPos,S2_SessStart,S2_SessEnd};
   AC[2]={S3_Enable,S3_Symbol,S3_HTF,S3_MTF,S3_LTF,S3_EF,S3_ES,S3_EH,S3_VP,S3_VM,S3_TP,S3_SL,S3_MaxTd,S3_Risk,S3_MaxPos,S3_SessStart,S3_SessEnd};
   for(int i=0;i<3;i++) if(!InitScalp(AC[i])) return INIT_FAILED;
   return INIT_SUCCEEDED;
}
void OnDeinit(const int r){for(int i=0;i<3;i++) FreeScalp(AC[i]);}
void OnTick(){if(DDHit()){CloseAll();return;}for(int i=0;i<3;i++) RunScalp(AC[i]);}
```
